# 401: Chapter 398: Such Superb Fishing Skills—Truly Unheard Of!

D immediately called over the Dragonair that hadn't returned to Dragonite Island yet and served them portions of Paldea Seafood Rice from the pot.

The Dragonair gazed eagerly at the dish infused with the essence of the sea, their eyes sparkling with delight.

Wiggling their big noses, they leaned in to take a sniff, then happily opened their mouths wide, gobbling up a shrimp with gusto.

"Woo!"

Delicious!

At the nearby table, Koraidon and Ogerpon were also starting to enjoy this novel dinner.

A big bowl brimming with Paldea Seafood Rice, it was filled with the taste of happiness, bringing a deep sense of satisfaction both in taste and appearance.

Especially Dragonite, who, after tasting the seafood rice, immediately picked up its plate and gulped it down heartily, licking the plate clean in its entirety.

The rest of the Pokemon were eating with great joy as well.

D watched this scene with a cheerful smile; this is the true meaning of cooking!

He turned to the few Dragonair in front of him and said:

"Wait for it, tomorrow morning I'll take you to a new place for special training!"

"Woo?" The Dragonair were full of curiosity.

But D deliberately kept it a mystery: "You'll know when the time comes."

The next morning, after D watched Calyrex catalyze the World Tree in its entirety, he set about making Paldea Seafood Rice for the Dragonair.

This dish can greatly enhance the results of special training in the sea, but the effects only last for three hours.

So before heading to the Porto Marinada Town beach, he had to prepare enough Paldea Seafood Rice in advance.

Upon hearing D's plan to take them to another place for special training, the five Dragonair gathered around D early in the morning.

"We're about to set off!"

D packed the seafood rice into boxes and brought along a fishing rod and a small bucket for fish.

When the Dragonair were training in the sea, he couldn't just stand there idly.

So D planned to fish by the sea, and by the way, test the effects of Paldea Seafood Rice on humans.

This time, the trainer for the Dragonair's special training was switched from Koraidon to Dragonite by D.

Because Dragonite can create hurricanes, gathering up whirlpools in the sea to help Dragonair with their special training.

After packing up all the things needed, D mounted Koraidon, ready to set off with the Dragonair and Dragonite.

However, before Koraidon could take off, the Eevee playing in the ranch saw D getting ready to leave and surrounded him eagerly.

"Eevee! Eevee!"

Looking at the Eevee surrounding him, D said to them: "I'm going out to the seaside to help the Dragonair train."

"Eevee, Eevee!"

"You all want to come too?" D asked.

The Eevee nodded enthusiastically.

Considering this group of Eevee hadn't left the ranch since hatching, D's heart softened, and he nodded:

"Alright then, we'll all go to the beach together, but you mustn't wander off, and be good, okay?"

"Eevee!"

"Alright, come aboard!" D patted Koraidon's back.

The little Eevee quickly jumped aboard, and soon, D was covered in Eevee.

"Hold on tight, we're setting off!"

D stretched out his arms to protect the Eevee in his embrace, glanced at the Dragonite and Dragonair nearby.

"Ah-ga-sse!" Koraidon responded with a sound.

The Pokemon around him flapped their wings and flew towards Porto Marinada Town's beach with D.

About three minutes later, the group successfully arrived at the beach.

D turned to survey the surroundings.

The daytime beach wasn't crowded, with only a few scattered fishermen holding fishing rods, sitting by the sea.

The last time he came here was for the summer fireworks festival with Ogerpon and the others.

Koraidon landed on the beach, and a group of Eevee jumped off D, gazing curiously at the shimmering sea and the golden sand.

It was the first time they'd seen such a sight.

D took a small folding stool and collapsible fishing rod from his backpack, looked towards Koraidon, Glaceon, and Umbreon, and said to the three Pokemon:

"Take care of these Eevee, let them play with the sand on the beach, and make sure they don't fall into the water."

"Eevee ~"

"Ah-ga-sse!"

The Pokemon responded one by one, and then led the Eevee to build sand sculptures on the beach behind D.

Next, D took the reheated Paldea Seafood Rice from his backpack and fed it to the Dragonair.

"Dragonite, I'll leave them to you, take them to the sea and use your power to create whirlpools for them to train in."

"Awooo!" Dragonite patted its belly as if to assure the duty rested with it!

So, after the Dragonair finished the seafood rice, Dragonite led them out to the sea.

They stopped at a distance from the shore, then dove into the sea with a splash.

Sunshine, sand, sea; occasionally the call of Wingull could be heard in the distance.

D cast the fishing rod towards the sea, quietly waiting for a bite.

Behind him, the Eevee played and chased each other on the beach. D squinted his eyes, reveling in the serene fishing moment.

Suddenly, the float bobbing on the sea surface moved.

A fish was biting!

D jerked the fishing rod, and with an arc across the sky, a Fish-type Pokemon with a white body and wave-shaped yellow stripes was pulled from the water.

---

"Is it a Tynamo?" D recognized the fish-type Pokemon's appearance immediately.

The name Tynamo might not be very well-known, but its evolutionary form, Eelektross, is definitely famous.

D remembers that Lono loves using Tynamo evolving into Eelektross as a metaphor for Trainers becoming more formidable.

The Tynamo before him was struggling wildly, releasing a faint electric current to attack.

D got a mild shock from it and had no choice but to toss the Tynamo back into the sea.

At the same time, a fierce whirlpool suddenly appeared out of nowhere far from the shoreline.

Beneath the surface, hidden currents surged as five Dragonairs charged into the whirlpool, striving to overcome the force of the water.

The sea was refining their bodies; the world seemed to be filled with nothing but this whirlpool and the intense sound of water rushing past the Dragonairs' ears.

A strange feeling welled up inside the five Dragonairs.

They felt like they could clearly perceive the movement of the surrounding seawater...

This sensation compelled one Dragonair to move its body with the water.

The next moment, a violent wave burst forth from its body, seemingly sending vibrations through the surrounding water.

Water Pulse, mastered!

However, the vibrating water affected the other four Dragonairs.

The sudden attack caught the four Dragonairs off guard—they were highly focused and didn't have time to react before the whirlpool swallowed them, battering them severely with intense water currents.

The initiator of it all saw its companions getting caught by its own move, becoming anxious and instinctively trying to reach out and grab them.

"Woo!"

The Dragonair cried out and charged at its friends.

But at that moment, the Dragonair realized something.

It didn't have hands!

No—in this situation, as long as it grows hands, it can help!

With this thought, a brilliant light suddenly burst from the Dragonair.

The dense evolution light shattered the whirlpool created by Dragonite.

In the glow, the Dragonair's form rapidly began to change.

The newly evolved Dragonite quickly extended a hand, catching the four companions and gathering them in its embrace.

*

Time ticked away minute by minute. With only five minutes left in the three-hour window, D had caught a lot of fish.

The small bucket for fish was filled to the brim.

D glanced at it with sharp peripheral vision.

Five bright red Magikarps, two vacant-eyed Feebas...

Originally, there were also a few Basculin, but those Basculin had terrible tempers and started fighting as soon as they were caught, tackling D's chest.

With no other choice, D had to release them all.

Now, the remaining fish are only these weak-bodied, slow-witted Magikarps and dazed Feebas.

Compared to the aggressive Basculin, these two types of Pokemon couldn't be more different.

Even when about to be caught, they were utterly docile, not resisting at all.

Looking at these few Magikarps and Feebas, D felt like he could cosplay as the Magikarp vendor from the festival.

"Too bad I didn't catch a Gyarados or Milotic..." D muttered.

These two types of Pokemon seem hard to find near the shore; to fish them out, one would have to venture deep into the sea.

However, D was quite satisfied with the current situation.

With the boost from Paldea Seafood Rice, his fishing skills improved significantly, even though it seemed to enhance luck rather than skill—but it didn't make much difference.

With this, D could participate in the fishing contest planned for next summer!

He hadn't gone to last year's fishing contest, nor to this year's, because his fishing skills were poor; early on at the ranch, Koraidon had to help catch fish.

Yet all those fish ended up being credited to D by the townsfolk.

Not to mention, they even dubbed him the title of Fishing Master...

But now, with the added boost from Paldea Seafood Rice.

D couldn't help but imagine the scene.

At the fishing contest, he successfully caught many fish, including many rare and unique fish-type Pokemon.

The townsfolk gathered around him, praising: "Truly the Fishing Master!"

Just then, D was snapped back to reality by the movement of the bobber in front of him.

The float moved, another fish has bitten!

D suddenly started reeling in, but at first, he couldn't pull in the big guy.

This is a giant! The thought immediately crossed D's mind.

He instantly increased his strength, using all his might to wrestle with the big fish that had taken the bait.

The seasoned anglers nearby were drawn to this scene, and seeing the reaction in the sea, they realized right away that this young man had hooked a big fish.

"Come on, young man!"

"Don't give up, fishing is all about competing with the fish in patience and guts!"

"You can definitely do it!"

The anglers started cheering enthusiastically for D.

D's confidence soared, but at that moment, he suddenly felt the rod in his hand go slack.

Next, there was a disturbance on the sea surface, accompanied by a splash, a massive figure soared out from the waves.

It was a Dragonite, a plump Dragonite with an orange-yellow body.

At this moment, this Dragonite, with a delighted face, flew out of the ocean with the fish hook in its mouth.

The sunlight shone upon it, its smile was so lively and vivid.

D: "?"

The anglers behind him all had their mouths wide open in an "O" shape.

"Whoa—"

Dragonite landed.

The anglers were in utter disbelief, this young man actually caught a Dragonite!

Such advanced fishing skills... it's terrifying! Unbelievable!

D hadn't recognized this Dragonite at first, until he saw his own Dragonite flying over with four Dragonair from the sea, he finally realized what had happened.

He asked in surprise: "Dragonair?"

Dragonite spat out the fish hook and cheerfully responded: "Awoo!"

D: "..."

He turned to see the astonished anglers behind him and awkwardly explained: "This is my own Dragonite, it's just playing with me."

The anglers now also saw the Dragonite and Dragonair flying in from the sea.

One observant angler recognized the Dragonite: "That Dragonite is the town's delivery Dragonite! In that case... are you D from D Ranch?"

D nodded with a smile: "That's right!"

"That makes sense!" The angler mumbled: "I've heard of your reputation as a Fishing Master long ago, I heard that last spring, you single-handedly supplied half of the seafood market's fish in just half a month!"

D: "...those are all exaggerations, it's not that impressive!"

The anglers were so excited, they wished they could pull D in right away for a fishing competition.

But now it was already noon, and it was time for them to reel in and head home.

"Next time, when you have time, you must have a good match with us!" The anglers earnestly said.

D: "..."

The anglers left.

D finally breathed a sigh of relief, these fishing folks were a bit too much.

He turned around and released the Magikarp and Feebas he had caught back into the sea.

Then he packed up his fishing rod and asked: "How were the gains from this special training?"

"Woo!" The four Dragonair who hadn't evolved yet nodded their heads enthusiastically.

They also felt they were close to evolving!

And the already-evolved Dragonite was full of excitement.

Because starting today, it could officially join the ranch's delivery team, helping D with work just like the three senior Dragonite!

Seeing this Dragonite's demeanor, D was at a loss for words.

How could there be a Pokemon so willing to work proactively?

"Work can be really exhausting!" D said.

Dragonite No. 5 shook its head, eyes full of determination: "Awoo!"

It wasn't afraid of the hard work!

Moreover, D prepared delicious food for them every day, helping them grow stronger.

So, they also wanted to help D!

---

---

---

---

# 402: Chapter 399: Dragonite! Dragonite! Dragonite!

Today's training session is temporarily over, and D sets off with a bunch of Pokemon back to the ranch.

Before Koraidon even lands, D sees wisps of smoke coming from the chimney of his house.

He knows it must be his sister Indeedee preparing lunch for everyone.

It has to be said, having a caretaker-type Pokemon at home really lightens the load!

D simply hands over the entire lunch preparation to his sister Indeedee, and the first thing he does upon returning home is to take out a Translation Nuts Cake from the cabinet and eat it.

"Tell me, what did you all gain today?" D turns around to find Dragonair and the newly evolved Dragonite in the ranch.

To be precise, it is Dragonite No. 5.

The family Dragonite is Dragonite No. 1, and the other three working Dragonites are Nos. 2, 3, and 4 respectively.

Currently, there are a total of five Dragonites and four Dragonairs on the ranch, even for the Dragon Taming Clan, it's quite a terrifying number.

Dragonite No. 5 looks a bit goofy compared to the other Dragonites.

It scratches the back of its head and responds, "Roar!" (I've evolved into Dragonite!)

All thanks to the battle training from Koraidon over the past few months and D's guidance!

If it were left on Dragonite Island, it might take quite a long time to evolve into Dragonite.

"I know." D slightly nods and then looks to the Dragonairs nearby, "How about you all?"

The Dragonairs babble together in response, "Woo!" (We've learned new moves!)

With that, the Dragonairs in front of him start demonstrating one by one.

The first Dragonair gathers a wave around its tail and swings it vigorously, creating an atmosphere like a raging storm.

"Aqua Tail." D recognizes the move immediately and nods, "Very good."

The second Dragonair opens its mouth wide, water swirling inside, and after the energy is built up, it explodes like a cannon, sending a thick column of water blasting out.

D's eyes light up, "Hydro Pump? Impressive!"

This move is considered a super powerful move among the moves Water Type Pokemon can learn.

Not only is it powerful, but it also has tremendous explosive force, with hardly any drawbacks except its low accuracy.

Thinking of this, D looks eagerly at the remaining two Dragonairs.

The third Dragonair learned Water Pulse, and the fourth Dragonair learned Surf.

The enormous wave it summoned crashes down from above, drenching D and the surrounding Pokemon like soaked chickens.

D: "..."

The Dragonair is startled, "Woo!"

It quickly flies around D, pitifully expressing that it wasn't intentional.

"It's okay!" D grins, "Just be careful next time, you all carry on playing, Dragonite, come over and introduce the work process to this Dragonite!"

Dragonite Delivery has a new employee joining the team, and as the leader of Dragonite Delivery, Dragonite immediately runs over to introduce the details of delivery duties to the newcomer.

Meanwhile, D goes back to his room to change clothes.

Judging by the performance of the Dragonites and Dragonairs, the new dish Paldea Seafood Rice is truly effective!

It greatly enhances the results of the Pokemon's special training in the sea, truly deserving to be called the sea's blessing.

The only pity is that there aren't many Water Types in the ranch, so that dish can't be utilized by every Pokemon.

After changing clothes, D heads to the living room.

Watching the group of Eevees chasing around playfully in the living room, he suddenly remembers something.

If he remembers correctly, evolving too early is not a good situation for Pokemon.

In game terms, it's evolving before reaching the suitable level, which can lead to missing out on automatically learning certain moves.

Thinking of this, D turns around and digs out his recipe book, attempting to find a Magical Cuisine among those he's crafted before that can help Fire, Jolteon, and Vaporeon strengthen themselves.

"For Jolteon, there's the Lightning Steamed Bun made from the feathers of Zapdos." D mumbles.

This bun can help it enhance its electric power, which should make up for evolving early.

But the problem is Zapdos is currently traveling, and it's not easy for him to borrow feathers.

"Forget it, I'll not rush this matter." After some thought, D reflects.

Anyway, someday Zapdos will return to reside in the World Tree, and then he can visit Zapdos's nest every day, maybe picking up some naturally fallen feathers.

While doing so, he can feed Zapdos a Lightning Steamed Bun to enhance its power.

From what D knows, this Zapdos is a wild one, not the real Thunder God.

Its power is probably far inferior to the Zapdos seen near Shamouti Island in the Lugia movie.

If Zapdos eats the Lightning Steamed Bun, its power might draw closer to that of the Thunder God.

As for Fire and Vaporeon, their issues aren't as easily solved.

He hasn't seen Moltres, so there's no way to gather Moltres's feathers to make Flame Steamed Bun.

As for Vaporeon... this world doesn't have the existence of a Water Flow Bird.

"Oh well, I'll slowly research on my own later to see if I can come up with simple and straightforward cuisine like Grassy Seed." D thinks to himself.

---

Indeedee, D's sister, had already prepared lunch, and D put down the recipe book he was holding to call all the Pokémon back home for lunch.

*

In the evening, as the sun set behind the western hills, the Dragonite and Dragonair, who had been working (sleeping) on the ranch all day, returned to Dragonite Island carrying boxes full of Poffin and Pokeblock.

They landed at the central lake of Dragonite Island, and then sat down by the lake, starting to enjoy the delicious Poffin.

The surrounding wild Dragonite were long accustomed to this sight.

Compared to these gluttons, they were not as interested in food, but rather wanted to increase their strength to become more powerful Dragonite.

However, at this moment, several Dragonite suddenly noticed that there was an extra Dragonite among the group of working Dragonite.

This surprised them greatly.

So, three fierce-looking Dragonite gathered around, looking confused at the extra Dragonite:

"Grr?!!" (How did you evolve so quickly?!!)

Dragonite No. 5 blinked, chewing the Poffin in its mouth, and answered vaguely, "Grr～" (That's because D helped us with special training～)

D...

The three fierce-looking Dragonite knew D, the human who sent the big Dragonite to their island, the human who captured the silly Dragonite.

He had visited Dragonite Island before, but at that time, they were not very interested in that human, so they didn't interact with him.

But now, it seemed that the human was indeed impressive, and could help Dragonair evolve into Dragonite more quickly!

The "Evil Dragon" trio were shocked.

However, the next thing that happened stunned them once more.

After dinner, the remaining Dragonair began sparring with each other.

This was something they did every day upon returning to Dragonite Island to become stronger and evolve into Dragonite, and then help D with work.

The "Evil Dragon" trio had grown accustomed to this scene, having watched them from the sidelines before.

But today, the sparring among the Dragonair was somewhat different from before.

Because they were using several new moves!

The "Evil Dragon" trio asked, "Grr?" (Are those moves you just learned?)

The Dragonair nodded naively, "Grr!" (That's right! It's the result of D's special training!)

Upon hearing this, the trio widened their eyes.

That D... truly impressive!

The three Dragonite turned and went to the cliff, watched the waves crashing against it, and began whispering.

"Grr?" (Big brother, can we also ask that human for help?)

The Dragonite referred to as "big brother" looked serious, with a fierce appearance and a long scar on its face.

It was a scar left when it was attacked as an intruder by a Gyarados while fishing in the sea.

Upon hearing the question, the big brother Dragonite hesitated.

As a powerful Dragonite, it had its pride.

Just like the Gyarados that attacked it back then.

That Gyarados residing in the ocean was very strong, and the Dragonite, having just evolved, was not its match.

But its unyielding nature made it unleash formidable power, and its fearless spirit eventually scared off the Gyarados, allowing it to dominate that area of the sea.

Since then, the big brother Dragonite began pursuing the path to become stronger.

Countless battles honed its physique, and two Dragonite on Dragonite Island were moved by its spirit, choosing to follow it and become good brothers.

But recently, the big brother Dragonite felt it had reached its limit.

If it didn't break through this limit, its strength might stagnate.

And in that human named D, the big brother Dragonite saw the opportunity to surpass its limit.

That human was truly amazing, not only training the famous Dragonite on Dragonite Island to be so powerful but also helping Dratini and Dragonair evolve into Dragonite so quickly.

What's to be done?

The big brother Dragonite's heart was stirred, but it couldn't bring itself to ask for help.

It simply couldn't bring itself to seek assistance from a human...

The two younger Dragonite brothers beside it saw the expression on their big brother's face and immediately guessed his thoughts.

They exchanged glances and said in unison, "Grr!" (Then let's go together and see!)

The big brother Dragonite felt a thrill inside, but it remained silent.

The sun rose again.

On the following morning, at the crack of dawn, those working Dragonite began their journey to D Ranch.

The two younger Dragonite brothers saw this and immediately called their big brother Dragonite.

Finally, the big brother Dragonite reluctantly followed with its two younger brothers.

It wanted to see the human's strength for itself!

*

D Ranch, 6:30 am.

In an era without smartphones or fun games, D always went to bed early and got up early.

After a simple breakfast, D began working on the daily tasks at the ranch.

First, he prepared the milk and vegetables ordered by customers from town, waiting for the Dragonite to come for delivery.

As the ranch developed, the number of customers ordering from the ranch grew, from the initial three or four to nearly a hundred now.

Although production hadn't expanded at the ranch, the crops grew faster.

With the Gogoat helping cultivate, the crops in the fields now took less than half a month to mature at most.

The supplies were more than enough for the customers in town.

Just as D finished packing the milk and vegetables, the Dragonite delivery workers arrived at the ranch.

"Thanks for your hard work today!"

D smiled as he handed out the goods to them.

Then the Dragonite, having received the goods, began their journey to Porto Marinada Town, to deliver the goods to customers.

His own Dragonite also went out to deliver milk, not because it particularly liked working, but because greeting Mrs. Dantes and others in town had become a habit.

It always felt that the old lady who often baked cookies for it might worry if she didn't see it.

After seeing off the Dragonite, D planned to head to the opposite side of the river to check on the growth of the apple trees.

But just then, three Dragonite flew down from the sky.

The three Dragonite landed in the ranch, one of them bearing a long scar on its face, looking fierce and as if it came to cause trouble.

D looked at the fierce Dragonite, feeling a bit wary: "Who are you?"

The big brother Dragonite narrowed its eyes, its face fierce, and its growl was deep: "Grr..."

D: "!!!"

He cautiously observed the three Dragonite and seeing they appeared non-hostile despite their fierce looks, relaxed his guard.

D breathed a sigh of relief and asked, "Are you here to apply as delivery workers too?"

He was quite familiar with this kind of thing.

"You just need to help deliver the milk and vegetables ordered by the customers each morning, and you're free to do whatever you want with the rest of your time. As payment, I'll provide you with Pokeblocks and Poffin," D explained to the three Dragonite before him.

However... from the deep tones and bully-like demeanor of these Dragonite, it seemed they wouldn't be delivering, but rather robbing. Hopefully, they wouldn't scare the milk-ordering customers.

At that moment, some Pokémon at the ranch noticed the situation and gathered around.

Seeing the human ignoring its words and talking to himself instead, the big brother Dragonite furrowed its brow, sharply striking a nearby fruit tree with one of its Dragon Claws.

"Grr!"

D's eyes widened, and an audacious thought crossed his mind—could these three Dragonite be here to cause trouble?

In the next second, to seemingly confirm his suspicion, the fiercest looking Dragonite appeared energetic, casting its glance over the Pokémon behind D with a battle-ready demeanor.

D was taken aback, incredulously pointing to himself: "You want to battle me?"

The big brother Dragonite nodded coolly, "Grr."

————

# 403: Chapter 400: Dragonite x Scar

Battle him?

D suddenly found it hard to understand the intentions of these three Dragonites.

Why would Dragonites specifically come from Dragonite Island just to have a battle with his Pokémon?

Realizing that the intentions of these three Dragonites were not simple, D asked Pawmot to run an errand for him and bring back some Translation Nuts Cake.

"Pamo!"

Pawmot nodded and immediately turned around to fly away.

The leader Dragonite and its two underlings stood there silently with a fierce demeanor.

During the wait, no one spoke first.

Only Koraidon looked somewhat speechlessly at the three Dragonites.

Pawmot was very quick, returning in a few seconds, bringing back the Translation Nuts Cake that D wanted.

D patted its head and reached out to take the cake and eat it.

After pondering for a moment, D asked, "Why do you suddenly want to battle with me?"

The leader Dragonite said seriously, "Roar." (Because you are strong.)

D raised an eyebrow: "…So you want to battle stronger Pokémon?"

Looking at this Dragonite, it definitely wasn't as gentle and docile as other Dragonites. It must have been through many battles to become like this.

The scar on its face made it clear, a long slash running diagonally down from above its nose, adding a fierce and domineering aura to a Pokémon that is usually quite gentle.

This aura even seemed to surpass Iris's grumpy Dragonite in the anime.

Upon hearing his words, the leader Dragonite nodded, its voice very deep: "Roar." (If you win, I'll work here like the other Dragonites.)

Hearing this, D immediately understood.

This Dragonite is very strong, so it needs him to prove his strength by defeating it in a Pokémon battle.

Only then will this Dragonite acknowledge him.

But D still couldn't figure out why Dragonite was doing this.

It couldn't be just for Poffin and Pokeblock, right?

Thinking this, D asked aloud.

And the leader Dragonite's answer was straightforward.

It wanted to become stronger! So it needed a human Trainer!

Hearing this, D was shocked, quickly turning around to look around, only relaxing when he saw his own Dragonite hadn't returned yet.

He shook his head and said, "You can't say things like that recklessly. I can help you become stronger, but you don't need to consider me your Trainer, just consider me a boss!"

The leader Dragonite didn't mind and didn't linger on titles for too long.

"If I defeat you, will you stay and help me work?" D asked.

The leader Dragonite nodded, it was a very honorable Dragonite and would definitely keep its promise!

"Alright." D nodded slightly. If this Dragonite joined, it would be beneficial for the future development of Dragonite Delivery.

As for helping it become stronger… he would do his best to help later, whether through cooking or other methods.

Thinking of this, D asked the final question.

"Which of my Pokémon do you want to battle against?"

Many of the ranch's Pokémon gathered around.

There were Koraidon, Cyclizar, Flutter Mane, Pawmot, Alcremie, Eevees, Ogerpon, along with Butterfree and Garganacl.

The leader Dragonite scanned these Pokémon and finally stopped on Koraidon.

Dragon's Senses told it that this red Pokémon was the strongest.

Its aura, its gaze… all were honed through countless battles.

Following the leader Dragonite's gaze, D looked at Koraidon, internally exclaiming, good grief, it chose a big boss!

"Koraidon's strength is very strong." D reminded.

However, upon hearing this, the Dragonite's tone became even more determined.

It wanted to battle the strongest Pokémon!

D: "…"

Koraidon: "…"

"Ahem, that's the only way, then." D looked at Koraidon and said, "Koraidon, I'll leave it to you!"

"Aaagh!"

Koraidon stepped forward, staring down that wild Dragonite from a distance.

D originally thought to let the opposing Dragonite strike first, but upon reconsideration, with its personality, this approach would likely be seen as disdain, a sign of disrespect.

After some thought, D decided to let Koraidon battle seriously.

Once both Pokémon were ready, D spoke up to remind them, "Start now!"

Dragonite gave its all, immediately spreading its wings and opening its mouth wide, the purple Dragon Type Energy quickly gathering within, transforming into a Twister that poured forth.

"Dodge!" D calmly ordered.

He knew well how fast Koraidon was when it gave its all.

Sure enough, at his command, Koraidon's figure became a blur, dodging the incoming Twister, charging fiercely from the side at Dragonite.

Seeing its attack dodged, Dragonite quickly shut its mouth, its Dragon Wings vibrating fiercely, then it clenched its right fist, which crackled with electricity, heading straight for Koraidon.

The two Pokémon collided, and a powerful gust spread out from their center to the surroundings.

In the first clash, Koraidon held back slightly.

Dragonite also did not retreat, just like its unyielding nature, at this close distance, Dragonite directly used its strongest move, Outrage.

An intense purple light enveloped its body, a red glow flashed in Dragonite's eyes, and it began to unleash an unrestrained flurry of attacks towards Koraidon.

"Use Dragon Claw!" D commanded.

"Aagh." Koraidon responded.

Though the power of Outrage is immense, it has a very obvious flaw.

That is, the Pokémon can't defend while attacking and will fall into confusion after Outrage ends!

Koraidon seized this flaw, unleashing a massive Dragon Claw.

Dragonite's Outrage was directly interrupted, the powerful impact sent it flying, hitting the ground and losing its ability to battle.

The two other Dragonites accompanying the leader looked at Koraidon in shock.

D stepped forward, checking the fallen Dragonite's condition, then asked his sister Indeedee, who had hurried over, to bring a bottle of Life Sheep Milk.

During the wait, he looked at the two remaining Dragonites and asked, "Do you two also want to battle my Pokémon?"

Both Dragonites shook their heads, "Roar!" (We're with the boss, whatever the boss does, we do too!)

D was intrigued by this term, why did these three Dragonites seem like a gang?

He looked down at the unconscious Dragonite before him, reaching out to touch the scar on its face, and asked:

"How did your boss get this scar on his face?"

The two underling Dragonites eagerly began recounting their boss's heroic deeds.

"Roar!" (The boss got it fighting a Gyarados back in the day!)

"Roar!" (That Gyarados was really tough, and the boss put in a lot of effort to take it down, becoming the ruler of that sea area!)

"Roar!" (Though the boss looks fierce, the Pokémon there are all scared of him, but the boss is not a bad dragon, he never harms the Pokémon there!)

D: "…"

Evidently, these two Dragonites admired their leader immensely.

They were even deliberately mimicking the fearsome expression of the boss Dragonite.

---

---

---

---

# 404: Chapter 401: Special Tea Drink, Dragon Scale Fragrance

After drinking the Life Sheep Milk, the big brother Dragonite slowly stood up from the ground.

Its expression was solemn, and it let out a low growl: "Aowoo."

Its gaze clearly and unmistakably expressed: From now on, you are my Trainer!

The two little Dragonite brothers also came up behind the big brother Dragonite, nodding in agreement: "Aowoo!" Yes, also their Trainer!

D couldn't help but break into a sweat; how come this Dragonite comes with a buy-one-get-two-free deal?

So, when Dragonite finished its work for the day and returned to the ranch from town, this was the scene it saw—

Three wild Dragonites standing in front of D, flashing him a "pleasing" smile.

Dragonite's eyes widened; it immediately landed in front of D, blocking the view of the three wild Dragonites.

Big brother Dragonite also noticed this famously foolish Dragonite on the island.

Its gaze shifted from the human to this Dragonite, sharply sensing the hostility emanating from the latter.

Big brother Dragonite stayed silent.

Dragonite also said nothing.

The two Dragonites stood on the grass, gazing at each other, and their faces showed serious expressions.

"Ahem." Seeing D, D squeezed between the two Dragonites, blocking their view, then said to Dragonite: "Well, these three Dragonites are also here to apply for work at our ranch."

Work application? Dragonite was skeptical.

It knew the big brother Dragonite, understood its strength, and was aware of its unique pride.

Such a Dragonite couldn't be attracted by Poffin, so why would it come to the ranch to work?

"Aowoo?" Dragonite asked uncertainly.

D patted Dragonite on the shoulder: "So, according to procedure, the three of them are yours to handle next. Take them to town, show them around, and arrange some work for them!"

D wasn't wrong; these three Dragonites did come for work, but their goal wasn't Poffin—it was to become stronger.

D planned to pay these three Dragonites by helping them get stronger and informed Dragonite of this arrangement.

Dragonite thought carefully about it; working for D to gain an opportunity to increase strength?

That seemed quite nice, actually, saving a lot of Poffin!

Realizing they weren't here to compete with it for trainers, Dragonite relaxed, happily nodded in agreement: "Aowoo~"

"Go ahead then!" D said with a smile, patting its head.

So, Dragonite took the big brother Dragonite and the others to Porto Marinada Town, introducing them to the delivery process and the areas they would be responsible for.

Early next morning, D Ranch's delivery team gained three more Dragonites.

In the eyes of Porto Marinada Town's residents, the Dragonites from D Ranch were all very gentle and kind-hearted, leading them to subconsciously believe all Dragonite Pokemon were like this.

Yet today, early in the morning, these townspeople realized they were completely wrong.

When they heard the knocking as usual and opened the door to receive deliveries from Dragonite, they were confronted by a Dragonite with a fierce face, a solemn expression, and a long scar on its face.

"Ah!" A white-haired old lady was startled, retreating rapidly.

In an instant, she seemed to return to her youth.

Back then, during an outdoor adventure, she had encountered a grumpy, fierce-looking Ursaring.

That Ursaring grabbed her and beat her up badly, leaving her covered in injuries, and she stayed in the hospital for several weeks to recover.

This incident left a deep impression on her.

Seeing this human's look, big brother Dragonite quickly realized she was afraid of it.

Its brow furrowed slightly, and its expression turned even more serious.

Big brother Dragonite remained silent, placing the milk in its hand on the ground, before turning around and leaving.

Staring at the milk on the ground, the old lady snapped out of her panic.

This Dragonite... came to deliver her milk?

*

While the Dragonites were out delivering, D stayed at the ranch waiting.

Only when all the Dragonites returned safely did he relax.

"Seems like the delivery work of those three newly-joined Dragonites went quite smoothly."

D had worried their appearance would scare the townsfolk because these three Dragonites had expressions like bandits; if encountered on the road, passerby's first thought might be that the Dragonite was going to attack them!

But it seemed everything was fine, and he hadn't received complaints from the townspeople.

Now it was D's turn to fulfill his promise!

Back at the ranch, the Dragonites landed on a patch of grass.

Some were chatting with each other, some were sleeping soundly, and others were happily devouring their food.

Especially the two little Dragonite brothers accompanying the big brother Dragonite.

Big brother Dragonite wasn't very interested in tasty Poffin, but its two brothers were different.

Once they tasted the Poffin's flavor, they simply couldn't resist, fully immersing themselves in the world of Poffin.

But big brother Dragonite was different.

It had excellent self-control and pursued higher-level aims.

D glanced over and then called big brother Dragonite into the living room.

---

On the table in the living room, there's a row of fruit wines.

They can increase paralysis resistance, poison resistance, burn resistance, and freeze resistance.

"Drink all of these! This is wine I brewed myself, made from Berries. It can enhance your power." D said with a smile.

Brother Dragonite's eyes were fixed on the glasses of wine on the table, with a hint of skepticism.

How could this be possible in this world?

You don't need to battle; you can become stronger just by drinking water?

After a moment of silence, Brother Dragonite felt D had no need to deceive him.

It picked up a glass of wine from the table and started gulping it down.

Soon, after downing more than a dozen glasses of wine, Brother Dragonite felt a dizzying sensation, and his entire body felt light and floaty.

Seeing this scene, D was stunned.

These fruit wines don't seem to have a high alcohol content, right?

He drinks them like juice, and the taste and texture are very similar to the pineapple beer he drank in his past life.

Looking at the patrons of the Pokemon Tavern, they've never appeared drunk before.

Is Brother Dragonite's tolerance really that poor?

With a thud, Brother Dragonite flopped directly to the ground, passed out drunk.

D: "..."

A few Eevee were attracted by this scene, curiously gathering around Brother Dragonite, gently tapping his body with their paws.

"Eevee?"

"It's drunk." D explained.

He brought a blanket over to cover Brother Dragonite, letting him sleep there for a while.

The Eevee didn't know what it meant to be drunk, and soon, two of them jumped onto the table, sticking their noses into the glasses to sniff the aroma of the fruit wine.

D came forward and took the glasses away: "You're still young, you can't drink. Wait until you're a year old before you can have some."

One eager Eevee wanted to taste: "Eevee!" But the aroma of the fruit wine was so tempting!

Seeing that D wasn't letting them drink, one small Eevee turned its eyes toward the basement where the fruit wine was stored.

It knew that's where D kept his wine.

D immediately saw through its plan: "You can't steal wine from my cellar!"

He promptly locked the door to the cellar tightly.

*

Brother Dragonite woke up when it was already noon.

D was preparing to cook lunch. Seeing it awake, he smiled and asked: "Awake? How do you feel?"

Upon hearing this, Brother Dragonite carefully sensed the changes, and the next moment, it felt the amazing transformation happening within its body.

Its body felt stronger, brimming with energy, along with some other indescribable sensations...

Brother Dragonite's pupils slightly widened.

Those wines are truly miraculous!

"Each glass of wine contains different effects." D briefly explained the effects of those fruit wines and then said: "Your strength seems decent, but you need more practice with techniques."

From the battle between Dragonite and Koraidon, it's clear it believes in the supremacy of strength, charging without hesitation even against powerful opponents.

To help Dragonite become stronger, after lunch, D called in his own Dragonite and had the two Dragonites try a battle.

As expected, just as he guessed, Brother Dragonite relied solely on brute force in battles.

Although its displayed strength was quite good, it ultimately fell to the combination of Rain Dance and Thunder moves used by Dragonite.

After the battle ended, Brother Dragonite was full of seriousness, unable to believe that the once clumsy Dragonite had become more powerful than it!

D recalled Brother Dragonite's performance.

Since he discovered Brother Dragonite's "symptoms," the next step was to treat them appropriately.

Introducing type matchups, practicing combination moves, trying to change battle styles, learning to observe opponents' actions instead of charging blindly.

To that end, D specially brought in Koraidon and Dragonite to serve as sparring partners.

Days passed by, and in the blink of an eye, time reached September 20, 199 in the League calendar.

During this periosd, Brother Dragonite's strength steadily increased.

Now, it was no longer the reckless fighter it once was.

It learned many things from this human named D.

During this almost month-long period, Brother Dragonite also encountered many things it hadn't experienced before.

One of the biggest changes was the attitude of the townsfolk toward it.

It still remembers when it first delivered goods, people were afraid of it because of its appearance, as if it were just another wild Pokemon.

But then, as it delivered goods again and again, those humans gradually stopped fearing it and even greeted it kindly.

One time, an old woman's Sprigatito got stuck in a tree and couldn't come down, and at that time, it happened to be delivering goods to her.

Before leaving, the lonely old woman asked it to help rescue her Sprigatito.

Dragonite helped her, and in gratitude, the old woman and Sprigatito gave it many little cookies.

If initially it worked to become stronger, now Brother Dragonite found it had come to like delivering goods.

At this moment, it finally understood why other Dragonites came running to the ranch every day.

It's not just about delicious Poffins.

Aside from Poffins, there are many, many other things.

Like D, who seriously cared for every Pokemon.

---

Brother Dragonite can see that all the Pokemon here really like him.

Reflecting on the recent times when D has been working hard to help with its special training, Brother Dragonite is filled with gratitude towards D and decides to do something for him.

So, when night falls and it returns to Dragonite Island, it rides the moonlight back to its nest, rummages around, and then finds a precious scale that has fallen from its own body.

This scale is no ordinary scale, it is an Outrage scale from its body.

Once, in an accidental battle, it fell off, causing it much pain. After the battle ended, Brother Dragonite picked up the Outrage scale and treasured it.

Now, it has decided to give this precious scale to D.

The next day, when D received the gift from Brother Dragonite's hands, he was utterly shocked.

He looked at the scale in his palm and asked in disbelief: "For me?"

Brother Dragonite nodded, with a low growl: "Hoo-ooo."

D and its gaze met in the air, and after a few seconds of eye contact, he instantly understood the Dragonite's intention.

It's expressing its gratitude in its own way!

Truly a Dragonite with feelings and righteousness! D thought.

Although it looks fierce, pursuing the path of strength, it never bullies the weak and instead helps others.

D recalled an elderly woman living in town once called him specifically to thank him for his Dragonite saving Sprigatito.

D's heart was filled with affection for this Dragonite.

"I received it." D gently exhaled and then said: "From now on, live with us on the farm!"

Brother Dragonite looked at him solemnly, then responded: "Hoo-ooo."

After saying this, it went off to find D's Dragonite to hone their skills.

Meanwhile, D lowered his head to examine the crescent-shaped Dragon Scale.

The next second, the information about this Dragon Scale appeared in his mind.

[Dragonite's Outrage scale: Special item, received due to Dragonite's trust, filled with deep gratitude, containing the power of dragons, when made into a dish, some mystical effects might appear.]

D was taken aback.

Another item filled with emotion?

He remembered last time he received such a special item; it was the Friendship Berry given by the Pokemon he helped in the forest.

Those Berries were filled with the gratitude of Pokemon, and when made into dishes, they could enhance the dish's quality.

But soon, D noticed something remarkable.

This scale was not an ordinary scale but an Outrage scale from Dragonite!

For Dragon-type Pokemon, the Outrage scale growing on them is incredibly important.

And now... this Dragonite actually gave him such a precious item.

D's heart was suddenly filled with complexity.

Will the Outrage scales of Dragon-type Pokemon fall off by themselves? From his observations, they won't.

Because whether it's Koraidon or Dragonite, their Dragon Scales will not automatically fall off.

This means this Outrage scale must have fallen off by accident, like in a battle.

Back then, how painful must have Brother Dragonite been?

Thinking of this, D suddenly felt his Golden Finger was quite devilish.

Looking at this Dragon Scale which could be made into a dish, this time, he decided not to use it but to treasure the Outrage scale.

D returned to the living room with the Outrage scale, the Calyrex sitting on the sofa watching TV noticed his expression and asked in surprise: "What happened to you?"

D showed the Outrage scale in his hand: "This is Dragonite's Outrage scale, it gave me this precious scale."

Then, D introduced to Calyrex the importance of Outrage scales to Dragon-type Pokemon.

"Under normal circumstances, the Outrage scale of Dragon-type Pokemon does not fall off. Now this scale has only one possibility, which is falling off during a battle, how painful must have Dragonite been back then?"

Hearing this, Calyrex's face was solemn, and it nodded slightly, its eyes flashed blue light, activating its ability.

Then, scenes from the past and future of the Outrage scale appeared in its mind.

In the past, it saw Dragonite losing its mind due to the pain from its Outrage scale being knocked off.

In the future, it saw this Outrage scale transforming into a force entering D's body, and D started using that force to help the Dragon-type Pokemon on the farm.

Calyrex was slightly startled, and after a while, it spoke: "You can use your power to make it into a dish."

D was stunned: "What do you mean?"

Calyrex explained: "I saw this item transform into your power in the future, the scene of you using it to provide Helping Hand to the Pokemon."

Hearing this, D was utterly puzzled.

What does Calyrex mean? This scale becomes his power, and then he uses that power to help Pokemon?

Calyrex did not say much more but directly transmitted the scene it saw into D's mind.

The next second, a scene appeared in D's mind.

In the scene, he touches the scar of Brother Dragonite with his hand, and a faint light emanates from his hand.

That light flows into Brother Dragonite's body, making it look quite content.

What is... that power?

Soon, the scene vanished, and D momentarily could not regain his composure.

Although unclear about what exactly happened in the future, D obtained one piece of information from it — the effect of the dish made from this Outrage scale can provide Helping Hand to Brother Dragonite.

---

After a brief moment of thought, he made up his mind.

D got up and went to the ranch to find the elder Dragonite, asking if he could use this precious Outrage for another purpose.

The elder Dragonite nodded. Since it had already been given away, it belonged to D now, and he could use it however he wished.

Having received the elder Dragonite's consent, D returned to the house.

He initially intended to use a blender to grind the Dragon Scale into powder as he had done before, but the hardness of Dragonite's Outrage exceeded his expectations.

Thus, D had no choice but to ask Calyrex for assistance.

In the end, Calyrex used an extremely compressed Psychic power to shatter the Outrage into powder.

D did not light a fire but used this Dragon Scale Powder and black tea to brew a cup of tea.

Soon, a cup of tea with a peculiar fragrance was completed.

D looked down, and the information of this special tea appeared in his mind.

[Dragon Scale Fragrance (S+): A special tea brewed using an Outrage filled with the trust of Dragon-type Pokemon and quality black tea. It is said that the drinker will gain the friendship of dragon-type Pokemon.

Cooking Effect: Dragon Power. After drinking, the drinker will acquire a special ability, Dragon Power, effective only on humans.

Dragon Power: Greatly increases affinity with Dragon Type Pokemon, making it easier to capture Dragon Type Pokemon; can expend power to accelerate the growth of Dragon Type Pokemon, helping them become stronger.

Evaluation: It is said that only those deeply trusted by Dragon Type Pokemon can obtain this ability. Those with Dragon Power can form bonds of friendship with all Dragon Type Pokemon.]

Looking at the cup of tea in front of him, D's hand trembled.

Dragon Power... Isn't this possessed by the Dragon Taming Clan?

Moreover, even within the Dragon Taming Clan, not everyone can awaken this power. Its existence is exceedingly rare.

Those who possess this power are natural Dragon Elites.

In that instant, D thought of the Dragon Taming Clan in the Johto Region, thought of Lance, and then thought of himself and the ranch's dragon-type Pokemon.

Who is the true Dragon Taming Clan after all?

The ranch's dragon-type Pokemon didn't consist solely of Dragonite.

Besides them, there were also Koraidon and Cyclizar.

This thing has great utility!

D picked up the cup, and without worrying whether drinking tea brewed from Dragonite scale powder would harm his stomach, he directly raised the cup and drank the tea in one gulp.

Along with the tea entering his stomach, D immediately felt a clear surge of energy in his body, which he could control with his mind.

Without a doubt, that was Dragon Power.

D stood up and ran outside, immediately finding the elder Dragonite.

The elder Dragonite also looked at D, and for some reason, it suddenly felt an increased sense of closeness.

Not only that, but the surrounding Dragonair and Dragonite all felt it too.

A group of Dragon-type Pokemon curiously gathered around.

D smiled at them and then extended his hand towards the elder Dragonite, placing it on its scarred face, under its slightly confused gaze.

Dragon Power, activate!

The future foreseen by Calyrex began to unfold at this moment.

Seeing D's action, the elder Dragonite was taken aback, but soon, it felt a soothing and wondrous energy flowing into its body.

This sensation made the elder Dragonite feel unprecedentedly comfortable.

It instinctively closed its eyes, displaying a rather satisfied expression, carefully savoring the power within its body.

Its body started to heat up... Strength was continuously pouring in...

D poured all of his Dragon Power into the elder Dragonite.

Afterwards, he checked his physical state and found that apart from feeling the clear depletion of Dragon Power, there was no discomfort.

The consumed Dragon Power would gradually recover automatically, somewhat similar to mana in JRPGs.

D looked at the elder Dragonite with a smile and asked, "How do you feel?"

The elder Dragonite's gaze toward D began to turn fervent.

Seeing this gaze, the nearby Dragonite immediately grew wary, hurriedly pulling D away, and didn't forget to turn back and warn the elder Dragonite:

"Awoo!"

---

---

---

---

# 405: Chapter 402: Toward a New Era

With the Dragon Power acquired, D began using this power to nurture the Dragon Type Pokemon on the ranch.

The first batch of Dragon Type Pokemon nurtured by D were the four unevolved Dragonair.

Dragon Power is a very special ability, somewhat similar to the energy transfer in xianxia novels.

It involves consuming one's own strength to pass it onto the Dragon Type Pokemon, helping them grow better.

With the blessing of the Dragon Power, combined with the super-speed training effect brought by Paldea Seafood Rice, these four Dragonair finally evolved into Dragonite before the third last day of autumn arrived.

This allowed the Dragonite team on the ranch to become even more formidable!

D's Dragonite, along with eight working Dragonite, and the trio of elder Dragonite, now made the ranch home to twelve Dragonite in total.

Oh no, to be precise, it's thirteen, because there's still one Dragonite Egg that hasn't hatched.

Looking at this large group of Dragonite in the ranch, D felt like he could rename his ranch to Dragon Ranch.

Roughly speaking, he could even consider himself a Dragon Elite, right?

The pure dragon team alone could form two squads.

First squad: Koraidon, Cyclizar, Dragonite No.2, Dragonite No.3, Dragonite No.4, Dragonite No.5

Second squad: Dragonite, Dragonite No.6, Dragonite No.7, Dragonite No.8, Dragonite No.9, Dragonite No.10

With the increase of Dragonite, another problem arose.

That is, with the increase in the number of Pokemon, the amount of food they consume daily also increased.

Just the twelve Dragonite alone consume a lot of Poffins and Pokeblocks each day.

Pokeblocks are easy to handle, as D's home has a Berry Blender. Just set it up, add the berries according to the recipe, and Pokeblocks can be made automatically.

In about two hours, a large stack of Pokeblocks can be produced.

But Poffins are a bit more complex.

Whether it's boiling berries or pouring them into molds, everything has to be done by D himself.

Initially, with just those few Dragonite, it was manageable, but now with so many, he's overwhelmed.

Just as D was troubled by this issue, his sister Indeedee stepped up.

"Ai-yi~" (Perhaps I can help, D~)

D was a bit surprised as he ate two pieces of Translation Nuts Cake: "You know how to make Poffins?"

His sister Indeedee shook her head: "Ai-yi!" (Originally I didn't, but after watching D make Poffins every day, I slowly memorized the steps!)

If his sister Indeedee could help, he would indeed have it much easier.

The work efficiency of a Psychic Pokemon far exceeds his as a human.

"Then can you show me once?" D asked.

"Ai-yi!"

His sister Indeedee happily nodded, thrilled to be able to help D.

The two of them, human and Pokemon, went to Alcremie's Cream Workshop.

His sister Indeedee's eyes shone with a blue light, and in the next second, a magical scene unfolded.

Accompanying this light, pots, pans, and mixers automatically floated into the air, as the fridge opened and the berries and eggs floated out.

His sister Indeedee gently waved her arms, directing the kitchenware and ingredients to their respective places.

The pot automatically ignited, the berries shed their skins, eggs cracked in half, and the yolk and whites fell into a bowl with a splash.

D stared in disbelief at the spectacle before him.

The scene in front of him reminded him of two words—magic!

His sister Indeedee worked smoothly, quickly cooking the berries, mixing them with eggs and flour, and finally pouring them into molds, controlled by Telekinesis, into the oven.

After about thirty minutes, accompanied by a ding, the oven stopped.

His sister Indeedee opened the oven door, bringing out a large tray of fragrant, sweet-smelling Poffins.

D picked up one to taste.

"Ai-yi?" (How is it?)

His sister Indeedee asked nervously, worried that she might not have done it well for her first time.

"Delicious!" D nodded, highly excited internally: "Even more delicious than the Poffins I make!"

"Ai-yi!" (That's great!)

His sister Indeedee was overjoyed.

D was also very happy; this way, he wouldn't have to worry about not being able to pay the Dragonite's wages.

In fact, there is another way to solve this problem.

That is to pay the Dragonite money and let them go to town to buy what they want to eat.

But this idea is somewhat unrealistic.

Because the Dragonite don't know the prices, and the shopkeepers can't understand them.

At times like this, a Rotom Phone would be really handy.

The Rotom residing in the phone could help the Dragonite purchase items and pay for them.

It's just unknown how Nemona's father's research is progressing.

*

The weather was nice today, and after lunch, D took the Eevees to the ranch's grassy fields to bask in the sun.

He spread out a picnic cloth on the grass, with a pile of snacks on it for the Eevees to eat when they got hungry.

The Eevees weren't hungry yet and happily played chase around D.

And surrounded by them, D was using Dragon Power to nurture Cyclizar.

Now that the Dragonair have evolved, it was time to help Cyclizar get stronger.

Back then, when Pawmo ate the Lightning Steamed Bun and Dragonite ate the Storm Steamed Bun, Cyclizar looked on with envy.

---

Previously, there hadn't been much opportunity, and D felt a sense of guilt towards his Cyclizar.

Now, with the Dragon Power, he began to help Cyclizar grow.

Although the principles behind Dragon Power were unclear, it was certain that this power had a significant effect on Dragon-type Pokemon.

If this continues, then one day, Dragon-type Pokemon raised with Dragon Power could break through to Elite or even Champion level strength.

Besides accelerating the growth of Dragon-type Pokemon, Dragon Power also had the effect of connecting minds with Dragon-type Pokemon.

In the anime, Iris had used this power to communicate with Dragon-type Pokemon over distances.

Therefore, when D felt guilty, Cyclizar instantly sensed it.

"Grrr..."

Cyclizar suddenly felt an urge to cry.

So, D had never ignored it after all?

D also felt Cyclizar's emotions, and he patted Cyclizar's head, smiling and encouraging it:

"Believe in yourself. One day, you can become even stronger than Koraidon!"

Cyclizar was instantly filled with enthusiasm: "Grrr!"

Then it eagerly licked D's hand with its wet, big tongue.

D: "..."

Not far away, Koraidon, who was enjoying a leisurely afternoon on the grass, suddenly felt like someone was talking about it behind its back.

It sneezed and opened its eyes in confusion.

"Ah, grr?"

Then, Koraidon saw a pure black stretch limousine drive into the ranch.

D also noticed the car.

The Eevees around him also paused, casting curious glances that way.

Huh? Who could it be?

The answer came the next second.

The car door opened, and the excited Nemona jumped out.

From afar, D heard Nemona's voice: "Mr. D! I came to the ranch for a trial again!"

Then, the front door of the car also opened.

Nemona's father, Nikar, and an elegantly dressed old butler stepped out from the front.

"Long time no see, D!" Nikar smiled and walked over with something in hand.

"Mr. Nikar, what brings you here?" D was quite surprised. He could understand Nemona coming over, considering how much she loved Pokemon battles.

"I have something very important to discuss with you," Nikar replied.

"Wow!" At this moment, Nemona also noticed the many additional Pokemon in the ranch, and her eyes lit up: "Eevee, so many Eevees!"

Gholdengo and Ogerpon ran over together.

Nemona's gaze, which was originally on the Eevees, was immediately attracted by them: "And Ogerpon! Eh? What's that golden Pokemon?"

"Dragonite! Mr. D, you've caught other Dragonites again!"

D smiled slightly; he completely understood Nemona's feelings.

After all, in this world, no child could resist so many rare Pokemon.

Therefore, he called over Pawmot and said to it: "Pawmot, take Nemona to tour the ranch."

Pawmot looked at Nemona, remembering this human child.

"Pawmot!" Pawmot patted its chest, then led Nemona to explore other parts of the ranch.

"Great!" Nemona was completely excited.

Seeing his daughter's look, Nikar appeared helpless: "Nemona can be so rude sometimes."

D shook his head with a smile: "The first time she came to the ranch, she was very polite. Now, it's probably because she's getting more familiar with me!"

"Hahaha!" Nikar laughed heartily: "It seems Nemona really likes you!"

D smiled and then shifted to the main topic: "Mr. Nikar, what brings you to my place today?"

Hearing this, Nikar grinned and handed D the small box he was holding: "Open it and see!"

D reached out to take it, noticing the logo of Rotom Technology Company printed on the box, and felt a slight flutter in his heart.

Could it be what he thought it was?

D opened the box, revealing a Rotom Phone designed like Fuecoco, quietly lying inside.

It's a Rotom Phone!

D's eyes lit up: "Has the development been successful?"

Nikar nodded with a smile: "Yes, that's right! After several months of research by our company's scientists, we've finally created the Rotom Phone you envisioned!"

"The phone in your hand is part of the first batch of Rotom Phones. We plan to officially release them to the public by the end of this year. I have a hunch that a new era is about to dawn!" Nikar was filled with excitement.

D took the Rotom Phone out of the box.

Since he had drawn the blueprints for Phone Rotom, D was very familiar with the phone's structure and buttons.

So far, it seemed Nikar hadn't changed anything about it.

D pressed a button, and the next moment, the screen lit up.

He unlocked the phone with his finger and saw several simple apps distributed on the home screen.

D counted and discovered that it included five apps: Maps, Messaging, Phone, Camera, and Pokedex.

He tapped on the Pokedex app with his finger.

The next second, D saw the Pokemon species currently discovered in the Paldea Region.

"These are the basic apps we've developed so far. Various network companies have already learned about the upcoming release of the latest Rotom Phones, and they've started developing more apps for the phone's system," Nikar explained.

---

"At that time, you can download the app data via the internet and install them on your phone."

D nodded, as he was checking the scanning feature of the Pokedex.

Adjusting the camera, he aimed it at the Cyclizar nearby.

In the next second, an electronic voice automatically sounded.

"Cyclizar, the mount Pokemon, can carry people at a speed of 120 km/h, utilizing human body heat to warm its back and restore energy. It seems to have allowed humans to ride on its back since ancient times, as depicted in thousand-year-old murals."

Hearing that voice, D immediately flipped over the Rotom Phone and, unsurprisingly, found Rotom's pattern on its back.

"It already contains a Rotom inside. If there's anything you don't understand, you can ask it. You can consider Rotom as smart AI; it will introduce the Pokedex to you, report the weather, and provide navigation. If you don't need it, you can directly tell Rotom to stop the broadcast."

Saying this, Nikar seemed a bit troubled.

D was slightly puzzled and asked, "What's wrong?"

Nikar: "I just don't understand why the Rotom inside the phone seldom communicates with humans like the Rotom Pokedex; most of the time it only uses voice broadcast."

D thought carefully and realized it's the same in anime; he didn't know the reason, either.

But rather than this matter, he was more curious about another issue.

"How many Rotom Phones did you produce in the first batch?"

Nikar replied, "About one hundred thousand! However, not every phone has a Rotom inside, as we couldn't capture that many Rotoms."

D nodded in understanding.

Smartphones without Rotom wouldn't lack functions, but they just couldn't do voice broadcast and real-time navigation.

In that sense, the Phone Rotom inside seems exactly like smart AI such as Xiao Ai and Xiao Du.

"Is there anything else you'd like to know?" Nikar said with a smile, "Thanks to you, the Rotom Phone could be launched, helping our company secure such a large market. If there's anything you want, just say it, as long as I can make it happen!"

However, D didn't have anything particular he wanted, nor was he short of money.

After a brief contemplation, D asked, "Can I pre-order a batch of Rotom Phones from your company?"

He planned to hand these Rotom Phones to the Pokemon at the ranch.

For instance, Calyrex, Giratina, the Heroic Expedition Team travelling outside, and the Dragonites.

With Rotom Phones, Dragonite Delivery could officially be established.

Through the phone's positioning and navigation, goods from D Ranch could be delivered all over Paldea, fulfilling his initial dream.

Moreover, he could stay in touch with the traveling Mew and Teddiursa via video conferencing anytime and anywhere.

"Of course, no problem!" Nikar said with a smile, "How many do you want? I'll send them over right after I return!"

D tallied in his mind the number of Pokemon needing Rotom Phones, then said, "Fifty units."

"Alright, no problem!" Nikar readily agreed; he planned to pick out 50 Rotom Phones featuring Rotom to send over.

Additionally, Nikar's visit to the ranch today had another purpose.

He wanted to discuss with D the types and development of future mobile application markets.

D, though a bit surprised, still introduced the information he knew to Nikar.

For example, online shopping, mobile drama watching, live streaming, video conferencing, software for news from around the world, and apps for chatting and making friends with people from different regions.

Preferably something simple and direct, like WeChat.

He could conveniently create an account for Mew, Teddiursa, Calyrex, Giratina and add all the Legendary Beasts to a group.

Nikar listened attentively, occasionally his eyes lighting up.

He had a hunch, if these apps truly came to be, they would connect the entire world!

And the internet industry would officially stride into a new era!

---

---

---

---

# 406: Chapter 403: My Pokemon Became the Rainbow Hero?!

Nikar and Nemona stayed at the ranch until evening.

Nikar initially planned to head back after discussing the future developments of the Rotom Phone with D.

But unfortunately, Nemona was already having too much fun.

Throughout the afternoon, Nemona engaged in one intense Pokemon battle after another with the Pokemon on the ranch, only stopping once all the Pokemon were exhausted and she was satisfied.

"The feeling of battling is just amazing!" Nemona said excitedly.

D: "..."

He glanced at Nemona's Pokemon, feeling that Nemona had gained quite a bit of experience points today from him.

Seeing his daughter's wild demeanor, Nikar said helplessly, "Nemona's character is just like a boy's."

However, D understood: "Nemona really loves Pokemon battles."

Nikar nodded with a smile: "Yes, Nemona once told me that she wants to become a very strong Pokemon Trainer when she grows up!"

"She will definitely achieve it." D thought of Nemona in the game and said softly.

"Whew!" Nikar exhaled: "Anyway, as long as Nemona is happy, that's what matters."

As a father, no matter what his daughter wants to do in the future, he will support her unconditionally.

Saying this, Nikar glanced at the time, and then said goodbye to D:

"It's getting late, thank you very much for your hospitality, D, we'll leave you now. Once we're back, I'll arrange for someone to deliver the phone you ordered."

"Thank you." D nodded.

Nikar smiled, then looked at Nemona, who was tending to her Pokemon, and called: "Nemona, it's time to go!"

Hearing that it was time to leave, Nemona's face showed a reluctant expression.

But she also knew that after spending so much time here today, it was time to head home.

So, Nemona reluctantly put her Pokemon back into the Poke Balls and energetically waved goodbye to D.

"I'll definitely come visit again, Mr. D!"

"You're always welcome." D smiled and nodded.

"Well then, goodbye!"

Nemona followed her father back to the car, and before the car drove off, Nemona waved vigorously to D through the window.

"Pawmot, let's have another Pokemon battle next time!"

Having been dragged into battles all afternoon by Nemona: "Paw..mot."

D patted Pawmot's head and smiled as he watched the car leave the ranch.

As evening approached, Nemona and her father had just left, when Zack arrived at the ranch driving a big truck.

D helped him load the vegetables and milk onto the truck.

During checkout, Zack wiped the sweat off his face with a towel and laughed:

"The Abundant Festival is happening the day after tomorrow, don't forget to bring your Pokemon to participate!"

The Abundant Festival originally was the Harvest Festival of Porto Marinada Town, but the townspeople renamed it to honor Calyrex for saving the back mountain, using the occasion to worship and thank Calyrex for granting the land abundance.

In previous years, the Harvest Festival was held at Unity Square, but this year, due to the establishment of the Abundant Shrine, Mayor Thomas changed the venue to the back mountain.

Although there are two days left until the festival, everyone in Porto Marinada Town has already started decorating the venue for the celebration.

D knew about this because from the ranch, he could see the mountain path leading to the Abundant Shrine.

For the past two nights, the pathway has been lit, illuminating the way to the Abundant Shrine.

"Yes, we'll definitely be there." D replied with a smile.

Zack appeared eager: "It'll definitely be lively, right? I heard a lot of tourists have come specifically to Porto Marinada Town! And I hear there'll even be TV stations covering the event!"

"Certainly, I think Calyrex will be delighted to see such a scene." D said softly.

"That really makes you look forward to it!"

Thinking about the scene, Zack felt charged with energy.

Seeing the town he grew up in become more prosperous gives such a wonderful feeling!

Energized Zack left.

And D began packing Poffins and Pokeblocks into boxes and handed them to the Dragonites working at the ranch.

After a day of hard work, the Dragonites happily left the ranch, filled with joy.

Once the Dragonites were gone, the ranch immediately felt much emptier.

Reflecting on today's conversation with Nikar, D took out the Rotom Phone from his pocket and began exploring its features.

He opened the camera and pointed it at the Gogoat lounging on the grass, then pressed the shutter.

The next second, the image froze, capturing Gogoat's leisurely pose.

Hmm, the picture looks clear, now he can store Pokemon photos in the electronic album from now on.

After testing the camera feature, D opened messages and calls.

Then, he discovered something miraculous.

It doesn't require a SIM card in the Rotom Phone, which seems to have a built-in communication system, and users can view their phone number in the system settings.

It appears that as long as you know someone else's number, you can communicate with them.

He then moved on to the map feature; D opened the map, and the phone automatically located his current location - the outskirts of Porto Marinada Town.

---

The map supports zooming in and out, almost indistinguishable from the game, but it can't show detailed information about Pokemon species inhabiting certain places.

The next feature is the Rotom Pokedex. D had already tried it before and knows it contains information about Pokemon in the Paldea Region.

The last feature is the app store and website login interface.

As the name suggests, the app store is a place to download apps. However, since the phone hasn't been officially released, only a few insignificant apps can be downloaded.

The website login interface is used for logging into web pages, equipped with a specialized search engine, making it convenient for trainers to look up various information.

The most amazing thing is that the Rotom Phone doesn't need to charge!

To be precise, only phones with a Rotom inside don't need to charge.

Because the Rotom living inside the phone constantly supplies electricity as long as Rotom isn't having issues, the phone remains powered.

"Wow, such amazing Rotom Technology!" D exclaimed in wonder.

Upon closer consideration, after all, Rotom is an Electric Type Pokemon, creating electricity is an easy task for it.

*

Meanwhile, in the Johto Region, Ecruteak City.

Walking through Bellchime Trail, covered with golden sycamore leaves, ahead emerges a quiet, solemn, anciently styled tall tower.

Every floor of the tower is adorned with bells beneath the eaves. When the wind blows, the melodious sound of the bells accompanies the golden sycamore woods, drifting into the distance.

Inside Bell Tower, a few monks dressed in robes are tidying up the dust in the tower.

In an unnoticed corner, Teddiursa and Mew quietly poked their heads out.

"One me?" (Is this Bell Tower?)

Mew looked around, then nodded affirmatively: "Miu!" (This should be it!)

Seeing they've found the right spot, Teddiursa's eyes lit up, and it took out a feather radiating a rainbow-colored glow from behind, filled with mystery.

A few days ago, during their travels, they encountered this feather.

Teddiursa fully played to its Pickup trait, immediately noticing the beautiful feather that had fallen on the grass.

It then thought to pick up the feather and ask Zapdos to send it as a gift to D.

That night, a mysterious Pokemon with orange fur appeared before them.

It was a majestic Pokemon.

It wore iron rings on its sturdy limbs, bore red hexagram-shaped structures on its cheeks, had clover-like golden hair shells on its forehead, and had mane resembling flowing white smoke on its back.

The Pokemon looked at them, first showing an unexpected expression.

But soon, it introduced itself to the Heroic Expedition Team trio.

It said its name was Entei, the emissary of Ho-oh.

It also said the feather was Ho-oh's and guided them to Bell Tower in Ecruteak City, saying the feather could be used there.

Thus, after asking around, the Heroic Expedition Team arrived at this anciently styled town.

Teddiursa looked up at the tower's top: "One me..." (Seems the Pokemon said to take us to the top of the tower...)

Mew blinked and silently used Teleport to take Teddiursa outside to meet up with Zapdos.

Upon hearing of their companions' discovery, Zapdos calmly nodded and let them sit on its body.

Both Mew and Teddiursa settled in, Zapdos flapped its wings and flew towards the tower's top.

The warm orange sun shone on Zapdos, casting a moving shadow on Bellchime Trail.

A monk who was sweeping leaves on Bellchime Trail noticed the shadow. He looked up and saw an orange figure flying towards Bell Tower.

The monk was slightly stunned and then showed a delighted expression: "Lord Ho-oh! Lord Ho-oh has returned to Bell Tower!"

The monks were overjoyed, some weeping tears of joy because it had been a long time since Ho-oh returned.
