Now that Ho-oh is back at Bell Tower, does it mean it's willing to reconnect with humans?

The monks hurriedly informed the elderly in Ecruteak City who have always believed and waited for Ho-oh's return.

However, when everyone climbed to the top of Bell Tower, they didn't find Ho-oh there. Instead, they discovered three unknown Pokemon.

The monk who first saw Ho-oh looked at Zapdos, his mouth agape, and his face filled with despair: "Not Lord Ho-oh..."

Zapdos: "?"

Mew and Teddiursa curiously looked back at these humans.

Just then, one of the humans, an elderly woman with white hair, noticed the feather Teddiursa was holding.

She exclaimed: "That's Lord Ho-oh's feather!"

"One me?" Teddiursa looked at the feather in its hand.

Judging by these humans, could they be planning to seize its feather?

Teddiursa immediately showed vigilance, as the feather wasn't of use here. It still planned to give the feather to D.

So, Teddiursa and Mew immediately tried to depart with Zapdos.

But suddenly, the elderly woman stopped them.

"Please wait! I have no intention of seizing that feather!"

Upon hearing this, the three Pokemon looked at her.

The elderly woman continued: "That's Lord Ho-oh's Rainbow Feather. Since Lord Ho-oh has given its feather to you, it means you've been chosen by it."

"Those guided by the Rainbow Feather and who encounter Ho-oh will become the Rainbow Hero." The elderly woman muttered historical records.

"miu?"

"One me?"

Teddiursa and Mew quickly became attracted to the term Rainbow Hero.

Not having met Ho-oh, the elderly woman showed a lost expression.

Now, Lord Ho-oh doesn't even grant humans the qualification of Rainbow Hero but chooses these three Pokemon.

Apparently, Lord Ho-oh still cares greatly about the incident from centuries ago...

At that time, Lord Ho-oh and Lord Lugia resided in Bell Tower and Brass Tower.

Until a war broke out among humans, affecting Brass Tower, causing it to be burnt and nearby Pokemon to lose their lives.

Lord Ho-oh and Lord Lugia felt disappointed in humans. Hence, they left Ecruteak City, never to return.

Although Lord Ho-oh hasn't forgiven humans, they've always believed in Lord Ho-oh.

Now seeing Lord Ho-oh giving its Rainbow Feather to three Pokemon, she plans to follow Lord Ho-oh's directive and help the three Pokemon complete the Rainbow Hero's trial.

"Head to Mount Tensei!" guided the elderly woman: "Since Lord Ho-oh isn't willing to return to Bell Tower, surely it resides somewhere in Mount Tensei."

guided by the Rainbow Feather, head towards Mount Tensei! If you pass Lord Ho-oh's trial, you shall be called the Rainbow Hero, and Lord Ho-oh will grant you a wish.

The elderly woman slowly narrated the historical records.

*

On the evening of the following day, the fifty phones D pre-ordered were delivered by a suit-clad office worker using the Squawkabilly Flying Taxi to the ranch.

Clearly, it's an employee of Rotom Technology Company, here on a business trip to the ranch.

Once D signed for the phones, the office worker's task was done.

He didn't linger here, immediately taking the Squawkabilly Flying Taxi back.

D carried the large box of Phone Rotoms back to the living room, opened the package, took out the phones one by one, and began testing them.

Seeing all the phones functioning correctly, usable without issues, D stopped.

"Next is waiting for the phones' official release to download all the apps into them!" D thought.

D didn't plan to give the phones to Calyrex and Dragonite yet because their functions weren't yet complete, with nothing entertaining inside.

Putting away the phones, D began preparing in advance for tomorrow's Bountiful Harvest Festival.

After the Bountiful Harvest Festival, autumn will be over.

Unknowingly, the end of the year is near again; compared to last year, the ranch has gained many new companions.

This New Year will surely be lively! D thought.

Yet who knows if Teddiursa, Mew, and Zapdos will return this year.

So far, he's nearly gone two months without news from them.

Who knows what the Heroic Expedition Team is doing now, or where they've traveled to.

---

---

---

---

# 407: Chapter 404: Harvest Festival (End of This Volume)

Maybe it's because tomorrow is the Rich Harvest Festival, Porto Marinada Town has been getting more and more lively over the past few days.

D frequently saw a few trainers passing by the ranch with their Pokemon.

Without a doubt, their destination was the Abundant Shrine on the hillside.

Mayor Thomas and his team highly valued this year's Rich Harvest Festival.

From early morning, he led the townspeople to the hillside to decorate the festival venue and kept busy until nightfall.

That evening, while D was feeding the Pokemon in the ranch, he inadvertently glanced towards the hillside and saw twinkling lanterns, making it seem very lively.

It wasn't just him; the Pokemon in the ranch also noticed the scene over there.

The Pokemon led by Ogerpon seemed to be full of anticipation.

Ogerpon asked, "Boni?" (D, can we go there and play tomorrow?)

"Of course." D nodded with a smile, his gaze sweeping over the Pokemon in front of him: "Not just us, Calyrex also plans to have a look!"

Ogerpon had previously participated in local festivals in Kitakami.

Before the man passed away, during festival seasons, he and his Pokemon would wear masks and go down the mountain to celebrate with people in the town.

But after the man died, Ogerpon never officially attended a festival again; it would only wear a mask and watch the joyful people from a distance.

"Boni..." Ogerpon's mask showed expectation; he wondered what the festival here would be like?

That question was quickly answered for Ogerpon.

On September 30, 199 AL, on the last night of autumn, Porto Marinada Town's annual grand festival officially began.

A group led by Mayor Thomas passed through the lantern-lit mountain path, ascending the hillside to the Abundant Shrine to worship Calyrex.

That day, the hillside was exceptionally lively.

Trainers, having heard the news, came from all over, including several TV reporters and photographers, to report on the Legendary Pokemon Calyrex.

Stalls lined the foot of the mountain path.

Various excited pedestrians weaved through them, selecting items they liked.

There were stalls selling souvenirs, small Calyrex wood carvings, roasted corn, and even an uncle shouting about selling Magikarp.

"Magikarp, super top-notch Magikarp, extremely talented Magikarp! You won't be disappointed if you buy one, just 3000 League Coins each! Buy more, get more!"

D, adorned with Eevee, squinted at the voice he heard.

Why is there an uncle selling Magikarp here too?

Looking closely, D recognized the person as an angler he had met by the sea earlier.

With tourists aplenty around, D reminded the few Pokemon beside him, "Stay close! Don't get lost, Koraidon and Cyclizar, keep an eye on the Eevees and Alcremie for me!"

"Pawmot, could you watch Gholdengo for me, huh? Where's Gholdengo?"

D looked around and saw Gholdengo enthusiastically running to a stall selling roasted corn, handing over a few Gold Coins to the vendor, then taking a few ears of roasted corn amid the vendor's surprised gaze.

D: "..."

The Eevee hanging on D also looked eagerly at the nearby stalls selling delicious and fun things.

The most outgoing Flareon quickly leaped towards a stall selling barbecue, pointing at the sizzling meat and gesturing for D to buy it for him.

During this time, pedestrians came and went, cutting off their line of vision.

D instantly felt a headache; he went forward to buy a few skewers of barbecue, then picked up Flareon by the scruff and placed it on Koraidon's back.

"Too many people, no running around!"

However, with a lively and adventure-loving nature, how could Eevee be scared off by just the number of people?

In an unguarded moment, D lost track of two more little Eevees, separated from them by the bustling crowd.

"Paw!"

Pawmot spotted the two little Eevees in a corner, barked, then quickly went after them.

D was overwhelmed with a headache; there were just too many people now; if this continued, the other Eevees might get lost too.

To prevent such occurrences, he could only take out the Poke Ball and recall all the Eevees back into it.

After quite a while, Pawmot returned with the two Eevees.

D poked their heads, then took out the Poke Ball: "Better get back into the Poke Ball for now!"

A flash of white light later, the Eevees were retrieved into the Poke Ball.

D and the surrounding Pokemon finally breathed a sigh of relief.

"Let's keep moving!"

After browsing around the stalls for a while, D led the group of Pokemon up the mountain path to the Abundant Shrine to attend the festival.

Compared to below, there were fewer people on the mountain path.

The Abundant Shrine was built halfway up the hillside, a shrine not considered large.

At the center of the shrine stood a statue of Calyrex. In front of the statue were offerings presented by people, crops harvested from the fields.

When D arrived here, Mayor Thomas and his group were worshiping at the shrine.

A lot of reporters surrounded them.

Some reporters, facing photographers, were broadcasting live.

"Good evening, everyone! Welcome to the Paldea Evening News Channel, I'm Chloe, reporting live from a lively Rich Harvest Festival."

"According to Chloe's understanding, this festival seems to be a traditional event here in Porto Marinada Town. Can you all see it? The statue in the shrine is of the Legendary Pokemon Calyrex."

---

"Calyrex, the Legendary King of Bountiful Harvests, possesses the power to make crops ripen instantly and flowers bloom in a flash. It is said that when He descends upon a place, He will make the land abundant."

"Perhaps some viewers still remember that this spring, it was this Pokemon that helped restore vitality to this forest."

"What a magnificent Pokemon!" Reporter Chloe softly remarked in front of the camera.

And just at that moment, the photographer in charge of recording the program suddenly noticed a peculiar phenomenon above the shrine.

A flash of light streaked by, and a Pokemon with an enormous head, akin to wearing a crown, appeared above the shrine, looking down at the humans below with a face full of benevolence.

The photographer was so astonished that he gaped, quickly moving the camera.

Seeing his actions, Reporter Chloe also turned around to look.

When she saw the figure identical to the statue displayed in the shrine, her mouth formed an 'o' shape in surprise.

"Wow! That Pokemon... could it be Calyrex? Oh my god! Calyrex has appeared!"

The townspeople who were praying also saw Calyrex. Mayor Thomas, knowing that Calyrex lives in D Ranch, wasn't too surprised.

However, other uninformed townspeople were full of excitement.

The Legendary Pokemon has appeared! It must have sensed their sincerity, which is why it manifested here!

This scene was broadcasted across the entire Paldea Region.

At that moment, everyone watching the evening news witnessed the figure of Calyrex with their own eyes.

D, standing not far away with Koraidon and other Pokemon, watched the expressions on people's faces.

He could already predict the content on tomorrow's news headlines.

"Shock! The Legendary Pokemon appears at the festival, its purpose is—"

"Bonds between Pokemon and humans! The Legendary Pokemon trusts humans, forming friendships with them through the festival, watching over this land!"

Undoubtedly, this news will make more people aware of Calyrex's existence, thereby providing Him with more faith and more power.

In this era where information spreads so rapidly, if nothing unexpected happens, the deeds and legends of Calyrex will be forever passed down.

Today's events may eventually turn into a new legend, becoming an eternal story of mutual devotion between humans and Pokemon.

From now on, Calyrex will no longer have to worry about being forgotten by the world, thereby losing strength and falling into a previous state.

"Phew..." Gazing at the scene before him, D couldn't help but take a deep breath.

It's quite nostalgic, who could have imagined how things would develop to this extent today?

Initially, he only wanted to capture Calyrex's two beloved horses to participate in the town's horse racing tournament.

Little did he know, his faith inadvertently helped Calyrex and established trust with Him.

In front of the Abundant Shrine, Calyrex floated gently towards the people, looking at those who worshiped Him, grateful to Him, and trusted Him with a gentle gaze.

If a year ago today, someone told Him He would revive in the future, He would never have believed it.

But now, it indeed happened.

His little disciple traveled afar across the sea to that unfamiliar land to find Him, helping Him retrieve His beloved horses, restore His strength and memory.

And invited Him to this brand-new land, spreading His legend everywhere, making more humans aware of His existence.

All of this not only helped Him but also allowed His power to break through Himself to reach a new height.

D felt deep gratitude in Calyrex's heart.

For the humans living on this land, He was also willing to trust them, bringing abundance to them.

Thinking of this, Calyrex's gaze turned to D not far away, meeting his eyes with warmth.

Everything was conveyed without words.

D smiled slightly and said to the Pokemon beside him, "Alright, let's go take a walk down the mountain!"

The Pokemon had no objections.

On the mountain path downwards, Ogerpon looked at the brightly lit scenery around and the lively atmosphere.

This reminded it of the festival it once participated in at Kitakami Region Mossui Town.

Ogerpon thought of that deceased man. Perhaps being touched by the scene, its eyes filled with tears, and it cried out sadly.

"Bonnie..."

D and the surrounding Pokemon froze for a moment, then all turned their gaze to Ogerpon.

"Dingling?!"

Gholdengo was somewhat flustered; it couldn't understand why the happy Ogerpon suddenly started crying.

D, on the other hand, could roughly guess.

He reached out and patted Ogerpon's head, asking, "Are you thinking about the past again?"

Ogerpon looked at him sadly, "Bonnie."

D sighed, not rushing down the mountain. Instead, he took Ogerpon to sit down on a less crowded mountain path.

Today the weather was good, it was a cloudless Sunny Day, the night sky was clear, and being here, one could see an extremely beautiful starry sky.

D watched the stars with the Pokemon and said:

"In our human lives, there's a saying that when someone leaves this world, their soul ascends to become a star in the sky, watching over their loved ones, guiding them in the dark night with direction and a path forward."

---

Upon hearing these words, Ogerpon looked up at the night sky.

The stars reflected in its eyes, and Ogerpon asked, "Boni?"

D nodded, "Yes, he's definitely watching over you, protecting you, hoping you can be happy. Perhaps it was he who guided me to find you and take you away from that place."

Ogerpon's expression changed, "Boni..."

It wiped a tear from its face, trying hard to regain its composure.

Then it stood up and pulled D's hand, "Boni!"

D understood what Ogerpon meant, Ogerpon was saying: Let's go have fun!

He smiled and agreed, then took several Pokemon down the mountain.

The surrounding crowd surged, and with the appearance of Calyrex, the atmosphere of the Abundant Festival reached its peak.

Visitors heard the news of the Legendary Pokemon's appearance and rushed to the Abundant Shrine to watch.

Gradually, the crowd around the stalls began to thin out.

Only then did D release the Eevees from the Poke Balls and took them around the area.

Flareon still wanted some grilled meat, so D bought several skewers for it.

Vaporeon was very interested in fish-shaped food being sold at a stall, so D immediately bought a large bag for it.

Jolteon took a fancy to a Pikachu-shaped night light, and D bought it for him.

Glaceon and Umbreon, along with the other little Eevees, stood in front of Magikarp Uncle's stall, watching the Magikarps swimming in the water.

This time, without D's help, Gholdengo confidently patted his chest and said it would help the Eevees buy those fish!

Frightened, D quickly stopped them and picked up the Pokemon, "Those aren't ordinary fish, and you can't eat them, they're Pokemon!"

Upon hearing they couldn't be eaten, Gholdengo gave up.

Ogerpon looked around; compared to those things, it enjoyed participating in the festival with D more.

Previously, it never had the chance to participate properly; it either had to wear a mask or hide in a corner, always careful not to be discovered as a "ghost."

But now, it no longer needed a mask to hide its face, and it could freely roam the festival.

Ogerpon felt very happy about this.

Koraidon, Pawmot, Cyclizar, and Dragonite, among other Pokemon, didn't want anything, as compared to the Eevees, they were already mature large Pokemon.

Seeing this, D took out his Phone Rotom, preparing to record the moment.

He opened the video function, aimed the camera at the Pokemon, and said, "Come on, smile! I'll record a video for you!"

With a click, the smiling faces of the Pokemon were preserved in the phone amidst the lights.

When everything was over, it was already half-past nine in the evening.

The day was late, and the harvest festival was drawing to a close.

Full and tired from playing, the Eevees and Gholdengo, among other Pokemon, started to doze off. D took out the Poke Balls and gathered them inside.

Ogerpon didn't want to enter the Poke Ball; it wanted to walk home with D.

D paused for a moment, then nodded with a smile, "Alright, let's walk home together."

They left the back hill, accompanied by the night breeze and starlight, as D led the Pokemon along the country road leading to the ranch, heading towards their home.

—End of Volume 5.

---

---

---

---

# 408: Chapter 405: Time Paradox

As soon as autumn passed, the temperature dropped right away.

The Pokemon in the mountains and forests began stockpiling Berries for the winter, and the ones afraid of the cold started their annual migration, setting off for the distant southern lands.

The cold wind whistled bleakly, and yellow, withered leaves spun down from the branches.

D stopped working the fields, only planting two kinds of radishes that Spectrier and Glastrier like to eat.

He took the Pokemon along to stash the vegetables harvested earlier into the warehouse, or turn them into pickles to eat with meals.

It's worth mentioning that the Scallion Garden guarded by Farfetch'd was also affected by the season.

Those thick, sturdy green onions had grown very old from not being harvested for a long time.

Farfetch'd was very familiar with this kind of thing.

So before those green onions died of old age, it swung the leek in its hand and harvested the entire Scallion Garden one by one, then bundled them up and carried them to D so he could sell them.

After months of working out, Farfetch'd had already built a powerful physique; beneath its fine down were bulging, sturdy muscles.

The leek that once seemed ridiculously huge now felt perfectly natural in Farfetch'd's grip.

"Mm, looks like your Scallion Techniques have broken through to a new level again!"

Looking at Farfetch'd swinging its leek in front of him, D couldn't help nodding as he spoke.

Farfetch'd gripped its leek with one hand and swept it across in a burst; the fallen leaves on the ground were lifted into the air by the gust created by the swing.

Hearing D's words, Farfetch'd stopped training. Holding the leek like a rifle, it looked back with firm eyes and cried, "Gah!"

"Do you have any plans for the future?" D asked.

"Gah..." Farfetch'd thought about it carefully, then shook its head.

It wanted to stay on the ranch forever and keep training here!

"Then I'll be counting on you from now on!" D said with a laugh, rubbing Farfetch'd's head.

As he spoke, he wrapped a red wool scarf he'd bought in town around Farfetch'd's neck.

"A gift for you. This winter is probably going to be really cold," D said.

A few days ago, he'd seen on the news that a blast of strong cold air was sweeping in from the Paldea West Sea, and it wouldn't be long before it reached the Paldea Region.

Farfetch'd lowered its head to look at the scarf around its neck and looked quite happy: "Gah!"

Saying goodbye to Farfetch'd, D went back home.

It was already November, the temperature was starting to drop, and it felt like it would start snowing before long.

Last winter he went to the Galar Region in search of Calyrex; this winter he didn't really have anything special he needed to do.

So D planned to stay home the entire winter and spend it together with his Pokemon.

From what D knew, the Paldea League was also on break.

Gym Leaders like Lono and Grusha didn't have to stay at their Gyms and could use this time to travel or go back home to be with family.

After it got colder, D reopened the Slugma ventilation ducts.

As Slugma moved around inside, the warmth radiating from its body would drift through the ducts into every corner of the house, making the whole place nice and cozy.

The Eevee had thick fur, so even in this cold weather they weren't affected at all and were still happily playing outside.

In the living room, besides Calyrex watching TV with Its exclusive tea set, Sinistea and Polteageist, only the cold‑averse Cyclizar was lying in front of the toasty fireplace, Sleeping.

D took ingredients out of the fridge and started preparing lunch for the Pokemon.

He still had other things to do in the afternoon; Professor Sada had called him yesterday, saying she planned to visit the ranch this afternoon to observe how Flutter Mane was living in the outside world.

So when D finished lunch and was sitting on the sofa topping up the black tea inside Polteageist, Professor Sada arrived at the ranch with Arven.

After not seeing him for a long time, Arven didn't seem to have changed much and was still as quiet and reserved as before.

D smiled and greeted him, "Long time no see, Arven!"

Arven looked at him and answered politely, "Long time no see, Mr. D."

His little sister Indeedee brought over two cups of hot black tea for Arven and Professor Sada.

After Professor Sada took a couple of small sips, she couldn't wait any longer and wanted to go see how Flutter Mane was living on the ranch.

"Flutter Mane should be over by the Snorlax Park right now," D said as he stood up. "Come on, I'll take you there!"

The two of them rose at once and followed D across the ranch.

On the way, Arven looked left and right and suddenly noticed that a lot of Pokemon were missing from the ranch.

The Gogoat were gone, and Arboliva was missing too.

Seeing this, Arven couldn't help asking, "Mr. D, where are the Gogoat?"

"Them?" D replied. "They went to a warm place to spend the winter."

To be precise, they went to the World Tree in the Reversed World.

The World Tree had now grown very tall and massive and could easily accommodate the Gogoat and Arboliva—another hundred wouldn't be a problem either.

And since the Reversed World stayed at a constant temperature all year round, the Natural Energy released by the World Tree was extremely beneficial to Grass Type Pokemon.

So, after it got cold, D sent them to the Sky Island where the World Tree stood to train, and they'd come back in spring when the weather warmed up.

The entrance to the Reversed World was right in the ranch's lake, which also made it very convenient for milking the goats.

Hearing this answer, Arven nodded and didn't ask any more questions.

---

After crossing the river, the three arrived at Snorlax Park.

After many days, Professor Sada finally saw that special Flutter Mane again.

At this moment, there were already quite a few Pokemon gathered in Snorlax Park.

A group of Eevee was bouncing up and down on Snorlax's belly as if it were a trampoline.

Meanwhile, Flutter Mane was alone on a slide, curiously using its wing claws to slide down.

Seeing this, Professor Sada's eyes lit up.

A ferocious ancient Pokemon playing on a slide—a rare sight indeed!

D, nearby, recounted in detail the events that happened after Flutter Mane arrived at the ranch.

"Initially, Flutter Mane still retained its ancient habits. It would sneak out at night to hunt, preferring to eat raw meat, tearing chunks with its sharp teeth."

Professor Sada nodded; this was the ecological trait of Pokemon like Flutter Mane.

Affected by the ancient era's ferocity and primal energy, all ancient Pokemon retained very primitive habits.

Much like the wild men described in some adventure books.

"Later, I had Koraidon introduce it to modern life, teaching it what to do and what not to do. To change Flutter Mane's nocturnal habits, I gave it some stimulating coffee. Gradually, Flutter Mane adapted to life on the ranch."

"Did Flutter Mane make any friends?" Professor Sada asked.

D thought for a moment and then said, "Flutter Mane still prefers to be solitary, rarely acting charmingly toward me like other Pokemon. But when I pat its head and groom its fur, it does show a look of enjoyment."

"Flutter Mane gets along fairly well with the other Pokemon; it likes watching them play around, though it's closest with Koraidon."

"I understand." Professor Sada nodded lightly, taking out her camera to capture the precious moment of Flutter Mane playing on the slide.

Professor Sada stayed a while longer to observe before following D back to the living room.

Arven was very interested in the Pokemon at the ranch, so D had his brother Indeedee take him for a tour.

Meanwhile, he stayed in the living room chatting with Professor Sada.

Professor Sada took a sip of hot tea and said, "It's unusually cold this winter!"

D smiled, "Are you and Professor Turo planning to come to the surface to celebrate the New Year this year?"

"Not sure yet," Professor Sada replied, thinking about her research. She continued, "It depends on whether the research facility is busy. If we can't make it back, we'll have Arven join us."

D nodded slightly, "With you guys around, Arven will definitely be happy."

"Yes," Professor Sada nodded, "That child is the pride of Turo and me. Next year, Arven will be going to Paldea Academy, where he'll make more new friends."

That way, Arven won't feel lonely.

Hearing this, D couldn't help but feel emotional.

Time flies so fast!

When he first came, Arven was a quiet little kid, and now, in the blink of an eye, he's off to school.

Just unsure if Professor Sada's hopes can be realized.

Because, as seen in the game, Arven remained alone even while studying at Paldea Academy. Later, when Mabosstiff was injured, he roamed around seeking ways to heal it on his own.

At that time, Arven met the game's protagonist and, after an adventurous exploration of Zone Zero, became good friends with the protagonist, Nemona, and Penny.

Speaking of time, D suddenly became curious about the Time Machine in Zone Zero.

From what he knew, with the current technology in the Pokemon World, it's impossible to create a Time Machine, as time travel is the domain of Dialga and Palkia.

"Professor, can the Time Machine in Zone Zero truly achieve time travel?" D asked curiously.

Professor Sada nodded slightly, "Of course, it is thanks to its power that we summoned so many ancient and futuristic Pokemon to the present era."

"Does that mean humanity can now explore distant pasts and futures through the Time Machine?" D inquired.

"About that..." Professor Sada pondered briefly before replying, "To be honest, we currently cannot pinpoint exact times, nor can we precisely locate a specific time point. We can only summon Pokemon from general time periods."

"As for traveling back to explore..." As a researcher, Professor Sada had conducted much research in this area.

She organized her words before speaking, "While witnessing that era firsthand is Turo's and my dream, we do not intend to do so."

"Why is that?" D asked.

Professor Sada looked at him and asked, "Have you heard of the temporal paradox, where time travel or temporal traversal leads to logically contradictory conclusions?"

"Furthermore, time is a developmental line. Everything you do has cause and effect. If your actions in the past alter history, then everything in the future will change, and you'll never be able to return to the time you knew."

D listened, feeling bewildered.

Professor Sada continued, "In the past, some scientists proposed several hypotheses to solve the temporal paradox."

---

"For example, the hypothesis of 'parallel universe' is quite popular. Some scientists believe that our world has many similar clone worlds within the Cosmos."

"And when someone goes back to the past, he enters another parallel world, a future world altered by his actions. And the secondary world may collapse and disappear at some moment, perhaps the instant you yourself travel back to the past."

D: "..." I completely don't understand!

Seeing the expression on his face, Professor Sada couldn't help but laugh: "In short, you play with time, and time will play with you. Some predetermined facts simply cannot be changed."

"If you force a change, then everything in the future will collapse because of your actions. Everyone you know, all the Pokemon, and everything will be affected by the butterfly effect and change."

"For instance, if someone from the future time travels to the past to prevent their grandmother and grandfather from getting married, then the future person will disappear, having never existed."

"...It's so complex."

Looking at Professor Sada in front of him, D couldn't help but gripe in his heart: So is the outcome of the two professors dying in their research due to playing with time?

He had previously tried to alter the fate of Professor Sada and Professor Turo, but wasn't sure if he succeeded.

Professor Sada sat for a while and then prepared to leave.

There were things waiting for her to handle at the research lab, and next she planned to take Arven with her to Zone Zero.

If there's no time, then their family of three would spend the New Year there.

Seeing this, D got up to see them off.

"Be careful!" he said.

Professor Sada flashed a smile and then disappeared with Arven on that country road.

D returned to the living room, only to see Cyclizar looking lost without knowing what it was thinking.

D raised an eyebrow: "What's up? What are you thinking about?"

"Gao." Cyclizar replied.

Calyrex casually helped translate, explaining that Cyclizar was perplexed by Professor Sada's time paradox.

Cyclizar asked: "Gao?" (Does D understand what it means?)

D thought carefully and then began to explain to Cyclizar in detail what he understood it to mean.

"For example, I used to also have parents, right?"

Cyclizar nodded, everyone, every Pokemon has parents.

"If someone travels back to the past and stops my parents from falling in love, then I would never be born, and I would also be affected and disappear due to the butterfly effect... That's what Professor Sada said."

But D was somewhat uncertain, since this world indeed has the God of Time, and some scientific theories from his past life simply don't apply in this miraculous Pokemon World.

Cyclizar listened, half understanding.

D wanted to say more, but the house's video phone suddenly rang.

D went to answer it and heard the voice of Mei on the line.

Mei, the granddaughter of the Pokemon ranch store owner in town.

"Is Mr. D at home?"

"Yes, is something wrong?" D asked.

Mei replied: "It's like this, two days ago my grandfather encountered an injured Tropius in the wild jungle. That Tropius was supposed to fly south for the winter, but its wings are injured and it can't fly."

"The weather is getting colder and snow is about to fall; although its injury has healed, traveling south again at this time isn't possible. So Grandpa plans to find a Trainer to help take care of Tropius. Could you see if you have time?"

---

---

---

---

# 409: Chapter 406: The Southern Tropius, Giving Giratina a Massage

After hanging up the phone, D immediately headed to Porto Marinada Town.

When he arrived there, Amey was sorting food in the shop, and upon seeing D, she immediately took him to the location of the Tropius.

"The Tropius is temporarily residing in the ranch, but due to losing its companions, its mood has appeared quite downcast."

As they walked, Amey began to introduce the situation of the Tropius.

Upon hearing this, D nodded to indicate his understanding.

Tropius, this type of Pokemon, mostly inhabit tropical jungles, especially the Alola Region, where their numbers are extremely extensive.

However, in other regions, some Tropius still reside.

These Tropius have rather special ecological habits.

Perhaps due to the different living environments, every time the cold winter arrives, these Tropius often form large groups with other Tropius and fly to the warm south.

Only returning when the weather warms up, this is somewhat similar to swallows.

The two continued forward and after passing through a field of withered yellow grass, D finally saw the Tropius by a small lake.

The size of the Tropius in reality is very large, with a height exceeding two meters, its whole body a brownish gray, resembling coconut trees growing by the seaside.

Its neck is long, limbs extremely stout, and three green leaves grow atop its head, at a glance appearing like a tree wrapped in leaves.

The Tropius also has four giant leaves growing on its back, which are its wings, enabling it to fly by flapping them.

However, the most attention-grabbing feature is the string of banana-like yellow fruits hanging from its chin.

"This is the Tropius. Mr. D, have you seen a Tropius before?" Amey asked.

D: "I've only seen them from afar, haven't had close contact." He had previously seen Tropius over at Casseroya Lake.

The water source there is abundant, vegetation lush, air fresh, a place favored by Tropius.

"I see!" Amey lightly nodded and then began to introduce: "Tropius, this type of Pokemon, can be raised, you know!"

"The fruits that hang from their necks, a Tropius will bear fruit twice a year. I've tasted them before, and the flavor is extremely sweet and delicious."

"I heard that in the Alola Region, children often eat the fruits borne from Tropius' necks as snacks, and because these fruits are very popular, many ranches are established there to cultivate Tropius."

"But here in Paldea, due to the climate, the fruits borne by Tropius are not nearly as sweet as those in the Alola Region."

D nodded as he listened.

He glanced over at the not-so-distant Tropius and couldn't help but remark: "The size of the Tropius is really huge!" Almost comparable to an elephant.

Amey hummed in agreement and then said with a smile: "But Mr. D, don't worry! Tropius have a very gentle disposition, even wild Tropius, when encountering humans in need, will voluntarily share the fruits from their necks with them."

As she spoke, Amey approached the Tropius and gently patted its head, saying:

"Tropius, don't worry! We've already found a trainer willing to take care of you, and it's this gentleman here."

The downcast Tropius followed Amey's gaze and spotted D standing to the side.

D proactively waved at it: "Hello, Tropius, I'm D, currently managing a ranch. If you're willing, you could stay at my place this winter!"

It's a pity Tropius isn't a Dragon Type Pokemon, otherwise Dragon Power could be effective on them, D thought.

Upon hearing what he said, the Tropius raised its long neck and looked over.

It saw a very kind human, who was smiling friendlily at it.

Amey nodded: "Yes, Mr. D is a very kind person, and his ranch is home to many different Pokemon. If you were to spend the winter there, you wouldn't feel lonely."

"Oh woo..." Although Tropius still felt a bit downcast, it nodded.

This indicated its willingness to go with D to the ranch for the winter.

Because it had no other choice, previously, due to an injured wing, it had lost its ability to fly, and could only watch as other Tropius flew south, leaving it alone here.

Seeing Tropius agree, D said with a smile: "Why don't we go back now? The Pokemon at the ranch are all eagerly anticipating your arrival!"

These weren't empty words from D to deceive Tropius; before he rode on Cyclizar to come here, Alcremie, Gholdengo, and Ogerpon stopped him to ask what he was up to.

D introduced them to Tropius.

Upon hearing that this type of Pokemon has sweet fruits hanging from its neck, all the Pokemon showed expressions of anticipation, obediently waiting at home for Tropius' arrival.

Upon hearing excited Pokemon awaited its arrival, Tropius felt slightly touched, its mood not as gloomy as initially.

Just then, Tropius let out a long growl towards the sky, then flapped its giant wings, flying to D's side.

Seeing Tropius' actions, D was slightly surprised and incredulously asked: "You want me to ride on your back to go back?"

---

"Oh woo!" Tropius nodded its massive head.

Seeing this, D didn't hesitate, he took out a Poke Ball and recalled Cyclizar into it, then climbed onto Tropius' back.

One person and one Pokemon waved goodbye to Xiaomei.

Xiaomei looked very happy: "Live well, Tropius!"

"Oh woo!"

Tropius knew that the human in front of it had helped it, and it was filled with gratitude toward Xiaomei and the old man.

Before leaving, it lowered its neck and offered the bunches of bananas hanging from its neck to Xiaomei.

"Nice, it's a kind and polite Tropius." Witnessing this, D nodded internally.

He was just about to say something when he suddenly noticed that the bunches of bananas taken from Tropius' neck had regrown.

D was stunned.

What's going on? Aren't the fruits on Tropius' neck supposed to ripen twice a year?

But soon, he realized something, and a light bulb went off in his mind.

This Tropius has the Harvest ability!

Harvest, Tropius' hidden ability. In the games, it allows berries that have been used to regenerate, with a 50% chance, which increases to 100% in Sunny Day weather.

Xiaomei happily accepted Tropius' berries, and when she saw the fruits on Tropius' neck regrow, she immediately realized.

"So your ability is Harvest!" Xiaomei said to Tropius, then turned to D: "Mr. D, Tropius with the Harvest ability can occasionally have a good harvest!"

"They can use their body's energy, moisture, and sunlight to accelerate the growth of fruits, causing picked fruits to grow quickly again."

"However, this effect can only occur once in a short period, and after activation, it takes a while for Tropius' body energy to recover before it can be used again."

Upon hearing this, D said to himself that it indeed made sense.

A Tropius with the Harvest ability is the perfect combination for a ranch!

But, D was even more surprised to find that Xiaomei knew about this knowledge.

He remembered the first time he met Xiaomei, she was a shy little girl, and now, two years later, Xiaomei had grown a lot and her personality had become much more cheerful.

D praised: "You're amazing, to know even this!"

Xiaomei felt a bit embarrassed: "These are all things Grandpa taught me, I plan to become a Pokemon breeder in the future. Grandpa taught me a lot of Pokemon knowledge when he heard about it."

"A Pokemon breeder, huh?" D encouraged: "Good luck!"

Xiaomei was full of confidence: "Mm!"

After chatting simply for a while, Tropius finally flapped its wings and rose into the sky.

D pointed in the direction of the ranch, and in the next second, Tropius flew toward it.

Compared to Dragonite, Tropius was slower, but its wide elephant-like back provided a more stable ride.

Soon, one person and one Pokemon returned to the ranch.

A group of Pokemon had already gathered there in advance.

As soon as D and Tropius landed, they came to surround them.

D introduced: "This is Tropius, Tropius, these Pokemon are the ones I've just mentioned to you!"

Hearing this, Tropius curiously looked at the group of Pokemon with different species.

Alcremie, Ogerpon, Pawmot, Gholdengo, and other Pokemon all expressed their welcome to Tropius.

Perhaps feeling the enthusiasm from them, Tropius's mood was also influenced.

It squinted its eyes happily and responded: "Oh woo~!"

Next, was finding a place for Tropius to settle down on the ranch.

The temperature was dropping, and the Grass Type + Flying Type Tropius is four times weak to ice, scared of cold just like Cyclizar.

It is precisely for this reason that they migrate on a large scale before winter arrives.

The winter ranch is not suitable for it to live, and with that thought, D had his answer.

He rode Tropius and entered the Reversed World through the ranch's lake, and carried it all the way to the Sky Island where the World Tree is located.

D had cultivated many sunflowers there that could emit light and heat, allowing Grass Type Pokemon to perform Synthesis and replenish energy freely.

For Tropius, this place was the most suitable wintering location.

The World Tree now had grown immensely, visibly towering over a hundred meters tall, making even the two-meter-tall Tropius appear quite small in comparison.

Fortunately, the space in the Reversed World is infinitely large, so there's no worry about not fitting.

Looking at such a huge tree, Tropius showed a shocked expression.

"Oh woo!"

"This is the World Tree."

Saying so, D looked at the Gogoats lounging under the tree, and the Arboliva that had grown a huge stature from absorbing a lot of Natural Energy.

He stepped forward and patted the heads of these Pokemon, then introduced: "This is Tropius, if all goes well, it will be living with us from now on."

The three Gogoats, known for their leisurely and gentle temperament, cheerfully welcomed Tropius.

Arboliva stood prominently on Sky Island, resembling a large olive-laden tree at first glance.

It opened its eyes to gaze at Tropius and warmly expressed its welcome.

Tropius also noticed these magical Pokemon.

It looked at Gogoat, sensing the intense Grass Type energy emanating from them, and showed a surprised expression.

---

There are also Skiddo and Gogoat over by Casseroya Lake, but Tropius has never felt this kind of aura from them.

Wow, so awesome!

D told Gogoat and Arboliva to take good care of this new companion, then turned around, planning to head back to the ranch.

Because it was getting dark, he needed to go prepare dinner for the Pokemon.

But just before he left, Giratina, who had been napping on the neighboring Sky Island, called out to him in a gloomy tone.

Giratina: "You're getting more and more casual."

How big your guts are!

This is its turf—does he think he can come and go as he pleases?! Giratina thought irritably.

"..."

D then remembered there was still a petty dragon living in the Reversed World.

He said, "Don't you get lonely living here all by yourself as a dragon? You should make more friends."

If it stayed this grumpy, how was he supposed to hand the Rotom Phone over to Giratina later? The Rotom inside that phone would definitely get scared half to death.

Giratina refused to admit that it was actually a little lonely, so it chose to hide its true feelings behind a violent roar:

"Who's lonely?! I've been living like this for tens of thousands of years! I'm not lonely at all!"

Giratina snorted coldly, its voice icy: "Hmph, human, you're getting more and more presumptuous!"

Even as it said that, Giratina found D more and more pleasing to the eye, and there was also a kind of faintly familiar, friendly feeling coming from him.

D couldn't quite figure out Giratina's temperament. To soothe its bad mood, he thought for a moment, then floated up onto that Flower Sky Island.

Then, under Giratina's glare, D reached his hand out toward it.

Dragon Power, activate!

The next second, Giratina's eyes widened.

"You brazen human! What did you do?!"

It felt a warm energy surge into its body…

In that moment, Giratina felt its body grow warm and cozy, a sensation it had never experienced before welling up inside.

D chose to pet it with the grain: "I'm thanking you. This is an ability I just awakened; it works really well on Dragon Type Pokemon. You're a Dragon Type Pokemon too."

As he spoke, D gave Giratina a full, proper massage.

When it was over, Giratina looked as if it were drunk.

"Go on then. Since you're being so sensible, I'll let today's matter slide."

So, Dragon Power works on Legendary Pokemon too! D thought.

Though for now, the effect seemed pretty minimal.

But D had no intention of using Dragon Power to power Giratina up. For a powerhouse like Giratina, his current Dragon Power couldn't do anything on that level; it'd be like a drop in the bucket.

Being able to use Dragon Power just to raise Giratina's affection was already quite good.

After thinking it over briefly, D said goodbye to Giratina.

*

In League Calendar year 199, the snow came even earlier than last year.

Early the next morning, snowflakes began to fall from the sky.

Thankfully, D had already gotten the Pokemon on the ranch prepared in advance.

Combee and Butterfree moved into the Glass Greenhouse, while Gogoat and Arboliva and the others went under the World Tree.

The Miltank returned to the warm Pokemon House, and the Garganacl holed up in the Underground Cave.

The Wooloo were settled by D into the Miltank's Pokemon House, and Snorlax was moved by D into the living room.

With no work to do on a snowy day, D, for once, stayed home with the Pokemon.

Outside, the snow was coming down hard.

The Slugma in the room continuously sent warm air outwards, while Pawmot hovered by the vent, passing Berries in to the Slugma inside.

Several Eevee chased and wrestled with each other around the room. Alcremie and a few other Eevee were sitting on Snorlax's belly, chattering away, while two Eevee who had never seen snow before had their little faces pressed to the window, watching the snow outside.

Cyclizar and Koraidon were lying in front of the fireplace, the Indeedee Sibling keeping watch nearby. Calyrex sat on the sofa watching TV, and Flutter Mane drifted all around the rooms on the second floor.

Ogerpon and Gholdengo were upstairs sharing the mask.

Farfetch'd hugged its leek and rested to the side. Dragonite had gone back into its Poke Ball to snore away, and after finishing their work, the Dragonite laborers had each been given a big bag of Poffins by D and sent back to Dragonite Island to spend the winter.

D lay on the sofa, fiddling with the Rotom Phone, trying to communicate with the Rotom inside.

Amidst the swirling snowfall, the figure of a Zapdos cut across the sky.

On its back were two small Pokemon, both petite in size.

They were the traveling Teddiursa and Mew.

Seeing the ranch draw closer and closer, the two Pokemon's faces were filled with anticipation.

---

---

---

---

# 410: Chapter 407: The Heroic Expedition Team Returns, and the Invincible Divine Steamed Bun Is Born!

Teddiursa and Mew are back!

When D saw the three Pokemon arriving at the ranch on a snowy day, he was overwhelmed with surprise.

He quickly ushered the Pokemon into the house, brushed the snow off their bodies, and served them hot tea and soft, sweet biscuits.

The heating in the house was turned up high.

The three Pokemon of the Heroic Expedition Team sat on the floor, and only after drinking a full cup of hot tea did they start to feel their frostbitten bodies warming up, regaining a bit of energy to speak.

Teddiursa and Mew were munching on the biscuits, their mouths stuffed full like two squirrels at dinner.

Zapdos buried its head into the food bowl, enjoying the warm snacks.

After a while, Teddiursa let out a satisfied burp, then reached behind to pull out a feather shimmering with rainbow colors.

"Here...!" (This is your gift, D!)

Having eaten the Translation Nuts Cake beforehand, D instantly understood what Teddiursa was saying.

He looked at the feather, and in the next moment, the feather's details emerged in his mind.

[Rainbow Feather: Legendary Item, a feather from the Legendary Pokemon Ho-oh, said to bring happiness to humans when it shines with a rainbow glow.]

D: "!!!"

It's Ho-oh's feather!

What did Teddiursa and Mew do? This time they even brought back Ho-oh's feather?

D looked at Teddiursa and Mew with shock all over his face: "Where did this feather come from?"

Teddiursa replied joyfully: "Here!" (We found it on the grass!)

Mew nodded happily: "Miu～" (This is Ho-oh's feather～)

Teddiursa: "Here, here!" (We also became friends with Ho-oh, oh, and Marshadow!)

With the narrative from the two Pokemon, D spent five minutes finally figuring out what happened.

It turned out that during their travels, the Heroic Expedition Team suddenly found this feather, and shortly thereafter, a majestic Pokemon, resembling a big dog, appeared before them.

That Pokemon named Entei told them to follow the feather's guidance to a place called Ecruteak City.

When the Heroic Expedition Team arrived at Ecruteak City, the people there directed them to Mount Tensei to find Ho-oh.

After a few twists and turns, the Heroic Expedition Team finally reached Mount Tensei.

There, they encountered a reclusive, shadow-loving Pokemon.

Mew caught it and learned from the conversation that the shadow Pokemon's name was Marshadow, who was responsible for guiding humans chosen by Lord Ho-oh to participate in the Rainbow Hero's trial.

At that time, Marshadow told them that this had never happened before.

Ho-oh always bestowed the feather to humans, never to Pokemon.

Upon hearing this, Mew and Teddiursa promptly shook their heads to express that they weren't Normal Pokemon, they were the Heroic Expedition Team roaming the world and championing justice!

After hearing the Heroic Expedition Team's story, Marshadow spoke.

It said: "In that case, why not try placing the Rainbow Feather on that stone!"

Mew and Teddiursa did as instructed.

As soon as they placed the feather, it erupted with a beam of light shooting into the sky.

A rainbow bridge appeared from the horizon, and soon, the Pokemon named Ho-oh appeared.

Upon hearing this, D was stunned: "So you've become Rainbow Heroes?!"

The Rainbow Hero is a title given to humans chosen by Ho-oh. In the movie, Ash took this trial and became a Rainbow Hero.

Mew and Teddiursa exchanged a glance, then shook their heads: "Here, here!" (No! Ho-oh said the Rainbow Hero title is for humans!)

"Miu～" (Ho-oh only gave us the feather because of what we did. It said the feather could help us～)

D understood, surprised, as he thought his Pokemon had become Rainbow Heroes.

He calmed himself, glanced at the Rainbow Feather in his hand, and asked: "What about Ho-oh?"

The two Pokemon shook their heads.

"Here." (No idea; it just flew away afterward.)

"Miu..." (We invited Ho-oh to come to the ranch and play with us, but Ho-oh refused...)

Mew looked quite disappointed as it said this.

D: "..."

D was at a loss for words.

Seriously, the Heroic Expedition Team has turned into a Pokemon gacha machine, from Legendary Pokemon like Ho-oh to Normal Pokemon like Farfetch'd, all could be spun out.

No wonder Teddiursa is blessed with permanent Super Luck!

Ho-oh not coming was somewhat expected by D, though it's a pity; he wanted to see Ho-oh standing on the branches of the World Tree.

"Maybe Ho-oh has its own matters to attend to!" said D.

Mew asked innocently: "Miu?" (Will Ho-oh come after it finishes its tasks?)

D: "...That I can't say."

"Miu..." Mew seemed a bit downcast.

D shifted his attention to the feather.

Zapdos's feather made Lightning Steamed Bun, Lugia's feather made Storm Steamed Bun.

---

Now the question arises, what kind of dish can be created from Ho-oh's feather?

D couldn't help but start to look forward to it.

But before handling the feather, he needed to ask a few Pokemon for their opinions.

"Can I make a dish out of the feather?"

"Teddi!" (No problem, after all, it was supposed to be a gift for you, D!) Teddiursa said.

It was lying on the woolen carpet, holding its small stomach, which was bloated from eating too many cookies.

"Alright, thank you, I really like this gift." D said with a smile, then turned to find Calyrex, asking for its assistance in using Psychic to transform the feather into powder.

Calyrex gladly agreed.

After three seconds, the Rainbow Feather turned into a handful of golden-red powder akin to flour.

D took a closer look, and the information about the powder surfaced in his mind.

[Ho-oh's Feather Powder: A legendary ingredient containing abundant Divine Power, Power of Blessing, and Flame Power. Once made into a dish, it might have some magical effects.]

Seeing this, D inwardly exclaimed that it was just as he thought.

He affectionately patted the heads of Teddiursa and Mew, then said, "Wait here, I'll steam some buns for you!"

D turned and went to the kitchen to start his work.

Having had their fill, the three Pokemon joyfully looked around, greeting the Pokemon on the ranch.

Although there were many unknown Pokemon, after mutual introductions, they quickly became familiar.

When Mew and Teddiursa saw the Farfetch'd they had invited to the ranch, they were both shocked.

"Teddi!" (So strong!)

"Miu!" (Such a big change!)

"Quack!" Farfetch'd looked at them, quite pleased.

If it weren't for Mew and Teddiursa, it wouldn't be where it is today.

At that moment, Farfetch'd was immensely grateful for having encountered the Heroic Expedition Team and coming to the ranch, where it met D.

*

D was very familiar with the process of making steamed buns.

Kneading the dough, letting it rise, shaping it into buns, and steaming them.

By noon, the heavy snow outside had stopped.

The room full of Pokemon eagerly donned the scarves D bought for them and ran outside to play in the snow.

The three Pokemon of the Heroic Expedition Team also went outside.

But unlike the others, their goal was to see if the World Tree had grown.

However, as soon as they went outside, Zapdos and Mew noticed that the World Tree planted in the grass had vanished!

The Pokemon were shocked and quickly found D, chattering that the World Tree was gone!

D was just steaming the buns when he heard them and responded with a smile:

"It's not gone. It got too big for the ranch, so I moved it somewhere else."

Then D asked Indeedee to watch the pot while he took the three Pokemon across the lake into the Reversed World.

In the next moment, Zapdos, Mew, and Teddiursa found themselves in the void, looking at an extraordinarily tall and sturdy giant tree.

"Chirp!" Zapdos was visibly excited; this was the perfect dwelling it had always dreamed of!

Mew and Teddiursa also looked amazed.

At that moment, they finally understood what D meant when he said the World Tree would grow very large, and they could invite all their Pokemon friends to live there.

D brought the three Pokemon to the tree.

The excited Zapdos began flying around the World Tree.

Mew, also excited, flew onto the branches.

D, holding Teddiursa, stood under the tree, looking up at the scene.

Seeing Mew so enthusiastic, it seemed to be planning to use Secret Power on the World Tree to build a secret base for the Heroic Expedition Team there.

D said with a smile, "This isn't the World Tree's limit. In the future, it will be ten times larger than it is now."

The giant tree rose from the ground, with its own self-sustaining ecosystem inside.

D had already started planning ideas for constructing the World Tree.

The World Tree, rich with Natural Energy, would be the perfect growing place for crops and plants.

It could have its own ecosystem inside.

For example, Arboliva providing delicious olive oil, Gogoat producing goat milk, Tropius providing Berries, Garganacl producing Rock Salt, Miltank providing milk, Combee producing Honey, and Alcremie producing cream and excelling at making desserts.

Once the World Tree fully matures, D plans to help Mew and Teddiursa build an Exploration Team Union there.

The union will include shops selling supplies and healing items, a cafeteria for Pokemon, an orchard producing Berries, a farm providing wheat, dorm rooms for Pokemon, and more.

The shopkeepers for these places will be Pokemon themselves, and with so many companions, they surely won't feel lonely.

Even if D isn't around in the future, these Pokemon can sustain themselves, operating on their own, living happily ever after in the World Tree.

But it's too early to talk about these things now.

After all, the World Tree hasn't matured yet; its growth isn't even halfway there.

Leaving the three Pokemon at the World Tree, D returned to the ranch alone.

---

While the buns were still steaming, D took the Eevees out into the snow and started building snowmen with them.

Time ticked by, and soon his little sister Indeedee, who had been watching the steamer, ran out to tell D that the buns were done.

Hearing that, D told the Eevees to keep playing there on their own, then sprinted back into the house, went into the kitchen, and took the freshly steamed buns out of the pot.

Only, this batch of buns looked very different from the last two.

They were a golden-red color, strikingly similar to the colors on Ho-oh's body.

One look and you could tell this was no ordinary item!

D held his breath and looked down.

[Divine Steamed Bun (SS): Legendary Cuisine, made with powdered feathers from the mythical Pokemon Ho-oh. It's said that eating it grants Ho-oh's blessing.

Cooking Effect: 1. Holy Ash. After eating, it can instantly restore a dying Pokemon's HP to full. If a healthy Pokemon eats it, its stamina and endurance will greatly increase, making it much stronger.

2. Sacred Fire. When a Fire Type Pokemon eats it, the flames in its body will evolve into Sacred Fire. When a non–Fire Type Pokemon eats it, the power of any Fire Type Moves it uses is permanently increased by 20%.

3. Divine Blessing. After a Pokemon eats it, it will receive Ho-oh's favor and its luck will be greatly ↑ boosted. This effect only works on Mew, Teddiursa, and Zapdos.

Evaluation: A one-of-a-kind dish in this world. Legend has it that those favored by Ho-oh will obtain eternal happiness.]

As the information surfaced in his mind, D couldn't help but suck in a cold breath.

Hiss! As expected of SS-level cuisine—the effects are downright terrifying!

The first effect, Holy Ash, is different from Life Sheep Milk. It's said that this item actually originates from Ho-oh.

It truly has the power to bring the dead back to life and regrow flesh on bones, since Ho-oh really does have the ability to resurrect the dead.

In the legends of the Johto Region, three Pokemon once died in a fire at the Burned Tower.

At that time, Ho-oh descended and used its power to resurrect those three burned-to-death Pokemon.

Those three Pokemon later became Entei, Lei Gong, and Suicune. After their resurrection, they voluntarily formed Ho-oh's royal guard.

It's just a pity that none of the Pokemon on the ranch can actually trigger that part of the effect.

But unexpectedly, the latter part—greatly boosting stamina and endurance—would be extremely useful for some of the Pokemon on the ranch.

For example, the three brothers who evolved early: Flareon, Jolteon, and Vaporeon.

The Divine Steamed Bun can perfectly make up for the physical deficiencies caused by their premature evolution.

True Holy Ash Rice Bowl.

Not only that, the second effect can also help Flareon upgrade the flames in its body, turning them from ordinary fire into Sacred Fire.

Sacred Fire, Ho-oh's signature Move—whether in the anime or the games, only Ho-oh and Entei, one of the members of its royal guard, can use it.

And the reason Entei can use Sacred Fire is obviously because Ho-oh granted it a portion of its own power.

In short, the future of this "only king" looks very bright!

Besides, even non–Fire Type Pokemon can boost the power of their Fire Type Moves after eating it.

The effect is basically a piece of Charcoal that doesn't take up an item slot.

But what attracted D the most was still the third effect.

Divine Blessing, Ho-oh's favor, drastically raising a Pokemon's luck...

"An artificial Serene Grace ability, huh?" D muttered.

The Heroic Expedition Team is about to get banned!

Teddiursa already comes with a built-in high-crit-luck effect, and with Ho-oh's Divine Blessing on top to boost its luck even further, then adding its Pickup ability...

D seriously suspected that Teddiursa could now rob the Origin Creation Plate off Arceus itself without taking a scratch...

Why does this feel exactly like a Felyne using a Plundering Blade to steal materials off a Black Dragon in Monster Hunter? D couldn't help but have that thought.

The only pity is that this effect applies only to the three Pokemon of the Heroic Expedition Team.

Thinking about it carefully, though, that Rainbow Feather was a gift from Ho-oh specifically to Mew and Teddiursa's group, so having the effect limited to the three Pokemon of the Heroic Expedition Team is perfectly reasonable.

This wave is a massive power-up!

---

---

---

---

# 411: Chapter 408: Fishing Up the Little Mischief Hoopa, Tropius's Bananas!

This time D learned to be smart. Because there are so many Pokemon in the ranch, to avoid running out of food, he made the buns very small.

Plus, Ho-oh's Rainbow Feather is quite large, so D managed to steam over 20 buns in one pot.

Although it's not enough for all the Pokemon in the ranch to eat, it can still barely ensure every piece counts.

Just not sure if Ho-oh would be furious once it knows its feather was made into buns...

According to the game setting where players can summon Ho-oh using the Rainbow Feather, Ho-oh is likely able to sense the presence of its feather.

If that's the case, Ho-oh might already know about him making the feather into buns.

D suddenly felt worried, would Ho-oh come over and punish him?

But as a chef capable of making Magical Cuisine, he really couldn't hold back upon seeing that feather!

"Forget it, if Ho-oh comes over angrily, I'll just apologize," D thought, actually, he quite likes Ho-oh.

Because although Ho-oh left Ecruteak City due to human wars, it never truly abandoned humanity, always flying in the sky bestowing Rainbow Feathers to kind-hearted humans.

Thinking of this, D took the pot of buns and found the Pokemon playing in the snow.

Firstly, the Pokemon that needed these Divine Steamed Buns most were the prematurely evolved Eevee brothers.

For Flareon, the Divine Steamed Bun can enhance the flame's power within its body.

Though it doesn't work on Jolteon and Vaporeon, it still helps them enhance their physique, allowing them to grow stronger.

Holy Ash mixed with rice isn't a joke.

The three Eevees ate the buns one after another; the changes in Jolteon and Vaporeon were not too obvious, but Flareon was different.

"Ee-ee?!"

There was an exclamation, Flareon nervously turned to look around as streams of golden-red flames burst out uncontrollably from its body.

Vaporeon beside it saw this scene nervously, immediately opened its mouth and used Water Gun on Flareon's flame.

The flame was extinguished by Water Gun, billowing thick white smoke.

But soon, before the Eevees could breathe a sigh of relief, another fierce stream of fire shot high out.

Vaporeon wanted to try again, but D quickly stopped it.

"Don't worry, this won't hurt Flareon."

"Ee-ee?" Vaporeon stopped and stayed beside D to watch Flareon's changes.

Flareon's flames burned more fiercely, as if there's a volcano inside its body.

Flareon only felt its whole body filled with strength, flames gathering in its throat, tightly shut its mouth, and its cheeks were bulging with immense Fire Type Energy.

Until it could no longer hold it, Flareon in a powerful shout of "Ee-ee" suddenly sprayed those flames out.

The long flame turned into a thick fiery snake, the scorching high temperature instantly melting the snow on the ground.

Vaporeon and Jolteon looked on at the scene gobsmacked.

"Ee-ee!" Such power!

"Ee-ee!" Flareon was extremely excited.

Seeing this, D guessed the Energy Points contained in the Divine Steamed Bun ignited Flareon's flame, its body couldn't contain it, so it burst forth.

But now it seems, Flareon's transformation is complete.

As D noticed its feather's color originally orange-red becoming deeper, resembling Entei, one of the Legendary beasts.

D nodded with satisfaction, well, this way, the damage to the bodies of the three Eevees due to premature evolution has been compensated.

He bid goodbye to the Eevees and turned to head to the World Tree to feed Divine Steamed Buns to the three Pokemon of the Heroic Expedition Team.

On the way, D casually fed Koraidon, Ogerpon, Dragonite, Pawmot, Alcremie, Flutter Mane, Cyclizar, and other Pokemon one Divine Steamed Bun.

By the time he arrived at the Reversed World, he just happened to have only three buns left.

But only Zapdos could be seen here, Mew and Teddiursa were nowhere in sight.

It wasn't until D called out that those two Pokemon appeared.

Mew, Teddiursa, and Zapdos, one Pokemon for each bun.

"Done!"

After distributing the buns, D contentedly clapped his hands.

He hadn't seen Mew and Teddiursa on the tree, so he asked, "Where were you guys just now?"

"miu!"

Mew seemed very happy.

It first covered its mouth and chuckled for a while, then used the move: Teleport.

The next moment, D felt he was in a different place, surrounded not by emptiness, instead there was a wooden little room filled with toys.

Seeing those toys, D immediately realized.

"Is this the secret base you built with Secret Power?" he asked.

Clearly, this was already inside the World Tree.

"miu!" Mew nodded repeatedly, correct! This is the secret base of the Heroic Expedition Team!

D looked around, nodded and said, "Great, I'll make you two little beds to bring over here, so you can sleep on them in future."

"miu～miu～"

Upon hearing this, Mew happily flew around D.

While Teddiursa looked delighted and coaxed with a "ea-ma～".

So, he took action and in the afternoon, using the remaining wood from the ranch, D crafted two small beds suitable for Mew and Teddiursa and delivered them to their secret base.

---

The two Pokemon were very happy. That night, they stayed at the secret base and fell asleep peacefully in the company of various toys.

While Zapdos stood on the branch of the World Tree and slept.

A fixed base, stable supplies—at present, the Heroic Expedition Team has already begun to take shape.

At such times, if that Pokemon were around, it could provide more help to the Heroic Expedition Team.

Yes, that's the Light Ring's Super Demon God Hoopa.

Hoopa's rings can traverse space, so even if in the distant Sinnoh Region, as long as Hoopa is there, one can instantly travel from Paldea to there.

If Hoopa joins the Heroic Expedition Team, the journey for Teddiursa and the others would become much more convenient.

But Hoopa is currently sealed in Dahara City in the Kalos Region...

Thinking of this, early the next morning, D went to the Heroic Expedition Team and told them the story of Hoopa.

"A long, long time ago, in a desert town called Dahara City, a Pokemon named Hoopa appeared in front of the townspeople."

"Hoopa took some food but soon gave a lot of money to the town, making it increasingly prosperous. The people worshipped it very much."

"Until one day, the townspeople wanted to see how powerful it was, and demanded that Hoopa battle other Pokemon. To prove its strength, Hoopa defeated many powerful Pokemon."

"The moves it used were extremely powerful, destroying the entire town. At that time, a human arrived in Dahara City and sealed Hoopa's power, forcing Hoopa to live in a Normal way."

"Until Hoopa understood the meaning of punishment, it had to live as a small Hoopa, not only losing its freedom to traverse the rings."

The Heroic Expedition Team's three Pokemon listened quietly to the story of Hoopa.

When D finished, Teddiursa asked in confusion: "What?"

D explained: "Hoopa's character is very much like yours. Although it's mischievous and playful, it is a kind-hearted Pokemon. If you encounter Hoopa on your journey, you can invite it to join the Heroic Expedition Team."

Mew was a bit worried: "Miu?" (It's so powerful, won't it destroy our ranch?)

D: "So you need to help it understand the meaning of friends and punishment. By then, Hoopa will surely control its strength well."

But the premise is that the Heroic Expedition Team can meet Hoopa.

Upon hearing that Hoopa also needs help, Teddiursa immediately patted its chest and promised: "One one!"

No problem, they will strive to help Hoopa!

Helping Pokemon in need is the purpose of establishing the Heroic Expedition Team!

Paving way for Teddiursa and Mew, D delivered the basket of Berries carried this time to Tropius.

Although Grass-Type Tropius can use Synthesis to replenish the nutrients in its body, he also can't give it nothing to eat.

That would be too excessive.

Seeing the basket full of Berries, Tropius showed a happy expression.

It made a friendly Growl to express gratitude, then bowed its head and began to bury its head in the basket to eat the Berries.

D watched with a smile: "Eat, eat! If it's not enough, there's more on the ranch!"

With Gogoat's Grass Energy helping with cultivation, the Berry trees on the ranch produce fruit very quickly.

In just three days, the empty branches will be laden with large, plump Berries.

Nearby, the Gogoats were also happily eating Grassy Seed.

Looking at this group of well-cared-for, healthy Pokemon with glossy fur, D felt a deep sense of satisfaction from nurturing them.

Soon, Tropius finished the basket of Berries, and as D prepared to leave with the empty basket and return to the ranch, Tropius lowered its neck and gave D the bananas from its neck.

"For me?" D was a bit surprised.

Tropius happily nodded its large head.

This time, it gave away all five bananas from its neck in one go.

The Harvest ability was triggered again, and two bananas grew once more under Tropius' chin.

Seeing this, Tropius gave these two bananas to D too.

This time, perhaps because the Harvest ability was cooling down, no more fruits grew under Tropius' chin.

Although he had seen it before, witnessing the Harvest ability again, D couldn't help but feel amazed.

It's undeniable that Pokemon are indeed magical creatures!

D looked down at the bananas in the basket.

[Tropius' Bananas: Fresh fruits picked from Tropius, sweet in taste, rich in texture, can be used for cooking.]

"Thank you, I really like these fruits." D reached out to touch Tropius' head.

Then, he bid farewell to Tropius and turned back to the ranch.

"I wonder what effect Tropius' bananas will have when made into a dish."

With anticipation, D went to the kitchen to make a warm dish of fried bananas after a little thought.

First, wrap the peeled bananas in egg liquid and breadcrumbs.

Then, heat the oil in the pan, and when the oil temperature is about right, put in the prepared bananas.

The oil in the pan sizzled and danced. Soon, the color of the bananas turned golden.

D turned off the heat and used chopsticks to put the fried bananas into a white porcelain plate.

[Fried Bananas (C): A dish made from the fruit on Tropius' neck, crispy on the outside and soft inside, the sweetness of the banana balances the taste, making it sweet but not greasy to eat.

Cooking Effect: Fruit ripening, Tropius-specific, greatly accelerates the growth speed of fruits when Tropius eats.

Evaluation: As the saying goes, you are what you eat!]

D: "..."

"Accelerated the growth speed of Tropius' fruits?" D murmured.

If he remembers correctly, normally Tropius' fruits ripen twice a year.

Wild Tropius spread pollen from their neck in spring and autumn, and by summer and winter, the fruits on their neck are fully mature and can be harvested.

This growth cycle is indeed much slower compared to other Pokemon.

As it stands, Tropius needs to eat many Berries every day, producing less than it consumes.

It's really puzzling how Tropius ranches in the Alola Region make money. They can't possibly sell those bananas at a high price, can they?

D feels it's unlikely because he remembers the Pokedex clearly states that children in the Alola Region eat Tropius' bananas as snacks.

"I wonder what happens to the accelerated cooking result."

Will it grow from twice a year to three or four times a year?

Such matters remain to be investigated.

After thinking for a moment, D found Tropius, fed it fried bananas, and split the remaining fried bananas among the Pokemon on the ranch.

*

In winter, besides Zack, almost no one would go down to the countryside, let alone pass by Trainers.

No one visited the ranch, so D enjoyed leisure, and except for feeding the Pokemon, no agricultural work was needed.

Thus, D spent all day at home watching TV, surfing the internet, and grooming furry Pokemon.

Days passed quickly; in the blink of an eye, time reached the year-end of League Year 199.

On this day, the Rotom Phone developed by Rotom Technology Company finally went on sale.

This portable phone with built-in Pokedex, communication, and map navigation caused a worldwide sensation upon launch.

On the third day of the Rotom Phone launch, enthusiastic people snapped up phones from various stores.

Some purchased for work, others as cameras, and some parents bought them as gifts for children about to embark on journeys.

Those who couldn't buy a phone could only look at Rotom Technology Company with longing.

The Rotom Phone's explosive popularity shocked Rotom Technology Company's boss, Nikar.

Although he anticipated beforehand, he hadn't expected it to be so hot.

Faced with those who couldn't buy a phone, he could only hold a press conference, announcing the company's plan to quickly produce the next batch to ensure everyone could use the new phone.

Under these circumstances, internet companies in various regions also noticed this novel portable phone.

They began developing electronic apps suitable for the Rotom Phone.

Suddenly, apps from communication and social networking to news forums, and onto various video sites, live streams, and games began to emerge like mushrooms after rain.

---
