# 412: Chapter 409: The Amazing Phone, Calyrex's Online Name, and the Rotom Frightened by Giratina

It was a snowy day.

Outside the window was a world of ice and snow, the entire world enveloped in white snow.

D sat on the sofa, took out the fifty phones he had pre-ordered, and began installing software on each one.

Although the Rotom Phone had just been released, the app store was already filled with various software.

The software in this world is quite straightforwardly named, most of them using Pokemon-related content for names.

For example, Pichu TV, Pichu Live, Poke Communications, Pelipper Delivery, Pokemon Online Shopping, Pokemon Online Banking, Pokemon Forum, etc.

The icon for Pichu TV is a rectangular Pikachu headpiece with a TV screen inside, making it look just like a Pichu TV.

As the name suggests, this app is used for watching TV and movies.

Pichu Live and Pichu TV belong to the same internet company, and live streams by video hosts can be uploaded anytime for subscribers to watch on Pichu TV.

Poke Communications is a software for chatting and making friends, developed by the internet department of Rotom Technology Company based on the information provided by D.

Whether it's the interface or functions like Moments, they're very similar to WeChat, which D used in his previous life.

Both the Pelipper Delivery and Pokemon Online Shopping apps were developed by Rotom Technology Company.

One is for food delivery, the other for online shopping.

D clicked in to see that Pelipper Delivery was recruiting shop registrations and Pokemon riders.

Pokemon Online Shopping was similar; since the software was newly uploaded, not many people had downloaded it.

Pokemon Online Banking is similar to Alipay, a mobile software that users can use for payments and transfers, very convenient.

And Pokemon Forum is a place for netizens from different regions to learn about current news and communicate with each other.

D clicked in to see several randomly pushed questions from curious netizens.

For instance, a netizen named [Desert Tyrant] was asking: hello? Can anyone see this?

Clicking through the comments, there were a few replies, mostly greeting [Desert Tyrant] and expressing amazement at how miraculous the Rotom Phone and the internet were!

D: "..." Look at these people acting so naive.

He closed the software; the house had the best internet setup since they bought the computer, making both download and browsing speeds very fast.

Moreover, it's worth mentioning that the Rotom Phone is very special; as long as there's a Rotom inside, it can access the internet anytime, anywhere without Wi-Fi.

D guessed this was probably due to the unique nature of the Pokemon Rotom?

Because he noticed that phones without Rotom needed to insert a special internet card to use the internet features.

Of course, phones with built-in Rotom were also more expensive than regular phones.

It took about eight minutes for all the necessary software on the first phone to be installed.

D registered a personal account for Poke Communications for Calyrex, then handed the phone over to Calyrex.

Calyrex reached out and took it, looking curiously at the electronic device made by humans.

"Is this the phone you used to tell me about?" He asked.

D nodded with a smile and said, "That's right!"

He leaned in a bit closer, pointing to the apps on the screen and introduced them to Calyrex:

"This is Pichu TV, an app used for watching movies; with it, you can watch movies on your phone anytime without ads."

Hearing this, Calyrex's eyes brightened, and he spoke with some excitement, "Really?"

"Wanna give it a try?" D smiled. "Just a simple touch on the screen will open the app. Of course, the phone has a Pokemon named Rotom, and you can tell it to open the app for you."

"If there's anything you don't understand, you can also ask Rotom, and it will tell you everything it knows."

Upon hearing this, Calyrex let go.

The Rotom Phone floated automatically in front of him.

Calyrex tried saying, "Rotom, help me open the movie."

"Received."

Rotom's voice sounded, and in the next second, Pichu TV on the screen opened automatically, with movies and animations neatly arranged in front of Calyrex.

Then, Rotom began introducing each movie and video to Calyrex.

Calyrex tried opening a movie he had watched before.

"Play The Slugma-Powered Home."

Rotom: "Now playing The Slugma-Powered Home."

The movie indeed started playing on the screen.

Watching this scene, four big words spontaneously appeared in Calyrex's mind.

Truly magical! This phone is a great thing!

Then, D introduced the following apps to Calyrex one by one.

When introducing Poke Communications, he opened his phone and added Calyrex as a friend.

D: "From now on, with this, you can contact me anytime, whether through video calls or text messages. Oh, by the way, Rotom will assist in translating your words into human language and automatically convert them into human text for sending."

"This here is your personal homepage, where you can change your username and avatar."

---

"This is your timeline. If you make friends, you can see what they share here, or you can share your own thoughts."

Calyrex listened to D with a face full of curiosity, and in the end, started trying it out one step at a time.

Video chatting with D, having Rotom translate text messages, checking the timeline.

When the personal avatar and online name were mentioned, Calyrex suddenly asked, "What is an online name?"

D: "It's your name on the internet, a nickname that others can use to call you, of course, you can also use your real name and photo."

Calyrex listened, somewhat unsure.

Then D turned and saw it change its online name to [Farmer].

D: "..."

Alright, it does fit Calyrex's thoughts.

"I have remembered it." Calyrex nodded slightly.

"Good, if you don't understand something, you can ask the Rotom in there, and you're always welcome to communicate with me." D said.

Calyrex nodded lightly, then began to study this human-made machine.

Meanwhile, D took the remaining phones into the Reversed World.

First, he found the Heroic Expedition Team's three Pokemon, handed them a Rotom Phone, and explained the phone's functions in detail just like before.

D: "...With the Rotom Phone, you can always keep in touch with the ranch here in the future, and if you encounter any beautiful scenery, you can also take pictures and send them to me."

Saying this, D added himself to the Heroic Expedition Team's phones.

Teddiursa and Mew looked at the Rotom Phone levitating in front of them, equally curious.

They growled one by one, happily expressing that they remembered!

"Great, from now on, Rotom will also be a member of the Heroic Expedition Team, let it travel with you!" D said with a smile.

"Miu~"

Mew happily floated around the Rotom Phone.

Next, D found Giratina.

As usual, he first gave a detailed introduction to Giratina.

Giratina was originally full of disdain for this human-made thing until D said he was giving one to each Legendary Pokemon.

Giratina couldn't sit still now; if other Legendary Pokemon have it, it must have one too!

Heavy breaths sprayed from Giratina's massive nostrils.

Its scarlet eyes looked at the Rotom Phones next to D.

Seemingly sensing a terrifying aura, the quietly levitating Rotom Phones behind D suddenly stirred.

The Rotoms seemed very afraid of Giratina, strongly resisting becoming its Rotom Phone, and all hid behind D.

Seeing this scene, D was a bit dumbfounded.

Giratina also understood the intention of these useless, weak Pokemon.

Seeing no one willing to follow it, Giratina was furious, immediately forcefully taking the most timid Rotom away.

The poor Rotom was almost scared silly.

D hurriedly stopped Giratina: "Hey hey hey! Don't be so rough!"

Giratina roared furiously at Rotom: "What are you afraid of? I won't eat you!"

Shame! Absolute shame! It, the dignified master of the Reversed World, couldn't compare to those Legendary Pokemon in the outside world, and was even despised by these weak Pokemon!

D: "..."

He immediately warned: "No hurting Rotom!"

Giratina snorted coldly, its tone full of disdain: "Am I the kind of dragon to do that?"

D looked at that Rotom, seeing it was extremely scared, couldn't bear it and said: "Why not swap with another Rotom that's not afraid of you?"

Giratina was very stubborn: "No, I want it!"

D: "..."

Giratina, though temperamental, was not a dragon that would randomly harm weak Pokemon.

Seeing Giratina's determined attitude, D could only comfort that unfortunate Rotom:

"Don't be afraid, Giratina just has a bad temper, it definitely won't harm you, I promise, if it does, you can always send me a message, and I'll come and get you right away."

Hearing these words, that Rotom finally sighed lightly, clearly comforted.

"Then I hope you two get along well."

Saying this, D added Giratina as a friend, then took the remaining Phone Rotoms and left the Reversed World.

In the cold winter, D gave the working Dragonites two days off, letting them wait until the snow stopped to come to the ranch.

Without the Dragonites, D planned to give them the Rotom Phones when they come to work.

Besides, D intended to give one to Koraidon as well, but Koraidon didn't want one.

Because Koraidon felt it could be with D anytime, anywhere, and didn't need this Rotom Phone to intervene.

Seeing this, D could only give up. He placed the remaining Rotom Phones in the living room, letting any Pokemon that wanted to play with the phone have a go.

Some Pokemon were very interested in the phone, but still, some were not very interested.

D didn't force it, embracing a free-spirited approach.

He sat next to Calyrex.

One person and one Pokemon played with their phones at the same time.

D glanced over and found Calyrex listening to a detailed introduction of all the phone's functions from Rotom.

---

It's clear, Calyrex really loves his phone.

He smiled slightly, then took out his phone to check Poke Communications.

D discovered that Teddiursa and the others had already changed their online names.

Heroic Expedition Team became [Heroic Expedition Team], simple and straightforward.

Meanwhile, in the Reversed World.

After D left, Rotom was terrified, feeling helpless and alone, it simply started to play dead.

Giratina stared at it with crimson eyes.

If the enemy doesn't move, I won't move, Rotom didn't even dare to breathe.

After a long while, it heard the sound of wings flapping.

Rotom opened its eyes, only to see the terrifying giant rise and fly away.

The frightening aura instantly dissipated, allowing Rotom to breathe a sigh of relief.

As a Pokemon in this unfamiliar place, it felt aggrieved, almost wanting to cry.

After a long time, the Pokemon that had left finally flew back.

Rotom immediately lay back on the grass, continuing to play dead.

However soon, it saw several round red apples roll up beside it.

The Pokemon's cold and disdainful voice came through, "Hmph, such a small coward, I'm not going to eat you. I brought you some fruit, eat up!"

Hearing this, Rotom was stunned.

After a long time, seeing that the Pokemon truly had no intention of harming it, and had actually lain down to sleep, Rotom cautiously levitated up.

However, the next second, it saw the opponent open those terrifying blood-red eyes and look its way.

Rotom: ∑(❍ฺд❍ฺlll)

Under the deep gaze of the opponent, Rotom carefully said, "I don't know about this Rotom..."

Giratina's tone was now less aggressive, sounding rather ancient and solemn: "Then what do you eat?"

Rotom: "Electric Rotom..."

Outside, it could use solar power to recharge its electricity consumption.

But in this sunless place, Rotom had no idea what to do.

What Rotom didn't know was that there used to be one or two sunflowers on this Sky Island, but they were all crushed to death by Giratina while sleeping.

Upon hearing this, Giratina didn't ponder too much.

It directly stretched out its forelimb, touching the Rotom Phone with its golden paw.

Giratina unleashed Charge Beam.

However, the Rotom Phone was already fully charged, in just one second, Rotom felt like it was about to explode from the electric energy overload.

It hurriedly floated back, shaking its head, "I'm not hungry now, Rotom!"

Giratina said no more, closing its eyes to feign sleep.

Rotom was very responsible, realizing at this moment that this Pokemon truly wasn't a bad Pokemon. After hesitating for a while, it began to actively introduce the phone's functions to the other.

Giratina suddenly opened its eyes, looking at the electronic screen of the Rotom Phone before it.

As a Legendary Pokemon, its pride instinctively filled it with disdain for human inventions.

But actually, Giratina wasn't completely uninterested.

At that moment, it listened intently to the Phone Rotom's introductions.

Upon hearing about the possibility of video communicating with D, Giratina immediately asked for a video call with him.

Rotom obediently dialed through.

Soon, the call was answered, and D's face appeared on the screen.

It really appeared! Staring at D on the other side, Giratina was filled with shock.

Over on D's side, seeing Giratina video calling, he immediately realized it was getting on pretty well with the Phone Rotom.

So, D smiled and said a few words, then hung up the communication.

While Giratina became even more curious about the Rotom Phone.

It began constantly directing Rotom to open this app, open that app.

Giratina couldn't read, so Rotom had to translate the app's texts word by word, introducing the usage methods and purposes.

As Giratina kept exploring the new phone's features, on D's side, he wasn't idle either.

He was checking the Pokemon Shopping app, preparing to register a merchant account, and officially start operating Dragonite Delivery.

With the Rotom Phone, the Dragonites could now begin delivering across the whole Paldea Region.

Right then, the home's visual phone suddenly rang.

D went over to connect it, and saw Geeta appear on the screen.

"Long time no see, D!" Geeta's face broke into a smile.

"Is there something up, Chief?" D was a bit surprised, what could Geeta be calling about right now?

But the next second, he saw Geeta take out a Rotom Phone, waving it on the screen.

"Did you also buy this new phone? Let's add each other as Pokefriends?"

D: "..."

A minute later, staring at Geeta's friend request on the phone, D complicatedly clicked agree.

Geeta was very straightforward; her profile picture was a close-up, almost identical to the photo on her ID.

Her name was equally straightforward—[Geeta].

At this point, Geeta sent over a message.

[Geeta: That will be much more convenient, no worries about you being absent from the ranch anymore in the future! :)]

It was accompanied by a smiling emoji.

# 413: Chapter 410: Dragonite Delivery, Ranch Shop Grand Opening!

Looking at the information on the screen, D couldn't help but think: Geeta seems to accept new things pretty quickly.

Thinking this, he casually mentioned it.

Soon, Geeta replied with a cheerful smile.

[Geeta: Of course, as the chief Champion of the Paldea League, I must keep up with the times, otherwise it'll be easy to have a generation gap with you youngsters.]

D: "..."

After finishing the communication with Geeta, it wasn't long before D received several calls asking to add him as a friend.

These were from the Unova League Champion Alder, the Champion of the Galar Region Leon, and the Gym Leader of Levincia Gym, Lono.

Alder's avatar was a photo of him with Volcarona, in which Alder had an enthusiastic smile and was full of energy.

[Alder: So this is the phone you talked about back then? It's really convenient! Now I can't live without it!]

Leon's avatar was a photo of him commanding Pokemon in a shimmering spotlight.

[Leon: You're at the ranch, right? I'll be taking a vacation soon and plan to visit you, are you free?]

Lono's avatar was also a photo of herself.

[Lono: Finally added you! Hehe, I knew it, D, you also got a Rotom Phone!]

Seeing these messages, D smiled and replied to each one.

[D: Useful, isn't it?]

[D: I'm here, got time, always welcome!]

[D: Yeah, compared to the old communicators, the Rotom Phone is so convenient, I guess no one can refuse it.]

Alder and Leon exchanged a few pleasantries with him and then stopped replying, except with Lono, he chatted a bit more.

[Lono: Hehe, what are you doing now?]

[D: I'm preparing to go to the bank in town to register and link my bank card to online banking. Are you on vacation now too?]

As far as he knew, every year-end, Paldea Region Gym Leaders take a vacation, during which they can move freely without accepting challenges from Trainers at the gym.

As expected, Lono confirmed it.

[Lono: That's right, I went back to my old home a while ago, now I'm helping my mom with lunch!]

Saying this, Lono also sent over a photo.

In the photo, D saw Lono with flour all over her hands, and beside her was a very kind-looking auntie.

That must be Lono's mother.

In the games, it seems the Gym Leaders' parents never appeared...

But on second thought, a family that can raise such a cheerful and lively child like Lono must be very happy and fulfilled.

After a brief chat, D got up and went out, riding Koraidon to Porto Marinada Town, ready to handle some online banking business in town.

Meanwhile, in a mansion in Levincia City.

Lono's mother, watching her daughter's silly smile, couldn't help but feel moved, raising her slender eyebrows:

"Did you get a boyfriend?"

Lono quickly shook her head to deny: "No, no boyfriend, just a friend I knew before!"

The woman, very familiar with her daughter, said with a knowing smile: "Doesn't seem like it to me! I heard that you often travel across the whole Paldea Region to visit someone in the far west at Porto Marinada Town."

\And with Levincia City so far away from Porto Marinada Town, knowing her daughter, it was impossible for her to go there for no reason.

Lono tried to explain: "That was work arranged for us Gym Leaders by the chief!"

The woman looked on with a "go on, I'm listening" expression.

Once Lono finished, she laughed and asked: "Honestly, no feelings at all?"

Lono, caught out, could only admit: "Alright, I admit, just a little bit of feeling!"

Thus, the woman turned around and told her husband about it.

During dinner, the couple stared at Lono, their expressions rather sentimental.

"After this year, the child will be eighteen years old!"

"Tsk tsk, our daughter has grown up! She's becoming an adult before we know it."

"Time flies so fast; I still remember when she was three, she was only this tall, now she's almost becoming an adult who can stand on her own!"

Lono: "..."

Lono suddenly felt a little exasperated.

"Was it love at first sight?" the man suddenly asked.

Lono felt defeated and replied honestly: "Not really..."

Lono couldn't quite explain her feelings.

The first time they met, she thought D was an old man; the second time she saw that D had specifically cut his hair and shaved, and even asked her if he looked much younger.

At that time, Lono found D interesting, and his personality quite endearing.

Lono wasn't lying just now; she indeed has some feelings for D.

The couple, looking at their daughter's situation, smiled and encouraged her: "Go for it, Lono! If you like him, then take the initiative! Just like when I pursued your mom!"

Faced with these unconventional parents, Lono was even more exasperated.

She decided to take Bellibolt and leave tonight! Head back to her own place!

*

After finishing the online banking business in town, D rode Koraidon back to the ranch.

Looking at the long string of numbers in the online bank, D suddenly realized that unknowingly he had become a millionaire.

He felt like nothing had changed compared to before!

Life continued as usual; although life at the ranch was plain, D felt that such a simple life was all the more precious.

---

D shook his head; life still had to go on.

Now that the Rotom Phone had already hit the market, the next thing he needed to get busy with was the Dragonite Delivery Company!

Speaking of Dragonite Delivery, you had to bring up the "Pokemon Online Shopping" app, which was kind of like the TaoBao app.

The app's design concept was based on a proposal D had supplied earlier, and as far as he was concerned, all the functions that were necessary were already there on Pokemon Online Shopping.

Even though the Rotom Phone had been out for less than a week, thanks to Rotom Technology's advance groundwork, quite a few merchants had already uploaded their products to the app and officially started doing business.

D skimmed over it and found people selling MooMoo Milk, Berries, plush toys, and Poke Balls.

He tapped into the shopping interface, linked his online bank account, and tried buying twenty regular red-and-white balls.

Then two delivery options popped up below.

One was Pelipper Express, where the delivery fee was priced according to distance; the description said that after a customer finished purchasing, the merchant would have a Pelipper help deliver the goods.

All these Pelipper had undergone special training in advance; having lived year-round out at sea, they had an excellent sense of direction and would deliver the goods accurately and promptly to the customer.

The other was regular human transportation, meaning a delivery driver driving a big truck back and forth between major cities.

Compared to the first specialized service, this delivery method took longer, but it was also cheaper.

Staring at the text on the screen, D nodded thoughtfully.

He then opened the shop he had set up before and changed its name to [D Ranch].

After that, D uploaded some of the farm's crops and ranch specialties.

Besides ordinary fruits, vegetables, and MooMoo Milk, he also put up limited Life Sheep Milk, and noted its effects in the description, hoping it could be a Helping Hand to Trainers and Pokemon in need.

As for the store's delivery methods...

D checked and found that in addition to the two existing options, he could also customize his own.

So he typed in the four big characters for Dragonite Delivery and specified that once a purchase was made, a Dragonite would help deliver the goods to the customer's hands.

Compared with Pelipper and truck transport, Dragonite Delivery was much faster and very stable, so the delivery fee D set was higher than the first two.

He wasn't planning on taking any of those delivery fees; he was going to pass them all on to the Dragonite, having Rotom take them into town so they could buy whatever they wanted to eat.

With everything ready, next it was time to let the working Dragonite choose their Rotom partners.

But that might take a few days, because D had previously given the Dragonite close to a week off.

However, plans can't keep up with changes: D gave the Dragonite a vacation, but the Dragonite had ideas of their own.

The next day, the heavy snow stopped, the sun came out, and compared to the previous days the temperature was relatively mild.

Early in the morning, the working Dragonite, bored out of their minds on Dragonite Island, flew over to D Ranch.

Seeing the group of Dragonite, D was quite surprised. "What are you all doing here? Didn't I say I was giving you a few days off?"

The working Dragonite all went "Awoo awoo" nonstop.

They all expressed that it was too boring on the island and they wanted to come to the ranch to work!

D: "..." He had never seen such hardworking part-timers.

His own Dragonite just watched these working Dragonite from the side, speechless.

Work, yeah right—what it saw was that these guys just wanted to come to the ranch to eat!

D, though, didn't think that far.

Since the Dragonite had come over today, he might as well take this chance to hand out the Rotom Phones.

At the moment, counting his own Dragonite, there were 12 Dragonite at the ranch.

Thinking of this, D turned around and took out twelve Rotom Phones from the living room, then let them and the Dragonite get acquainted and pick the partner they liked.

Once all the Dragonite had chosen their Rotom partners, D began introducing the functions of the Rotom Phone and their new assignments.

The Dragonite listened half-understanding, staring blankly at the Rotom Phones floating in front of them.

D said, "Simply put, starting today, I need you to help me deliver the ranch's goods to farther places."

He had already set up a complete workflow.

Once a customer bought something from his little shop, the Rotom Phone would automatically capture the customer's information and send it to the Dragonite's partner Rotom.

At that point, those partner Rotom would automatically use satellite maps to locate the customer's position, then guide the Dragonite to set off for the destination and deliver the goods into the customer's hands.

During the trip, if they ran into any problems while delivering, Rotom would automatically contact the ranch and relay the message to D.

After hearing this detailed explanation, the Dragonite's eyes lit up.

"Awoo awoo!" They got it!

No problem, leave it to them! The Dragonite happily thumped their chests, promising they would definitely get the job done.

D nodded in satisfaction. "Then I'll be counting on you!"

It was almost New Year; he planned to officially start operations next spring when the snow melted.

D had the Dragonite stay at the ranch to get familiar with their partners first, while he headed to Porto Marinada Town to shop for New Year's supplies for this year.

League calendar year 199 was his second year in this world.

---

Over the course of two years, the ranch has gathered many Pokémon.

D thought about each Pokémon's preferences while selecting their favorite foods in his mind.

Once the shopping was done, D piled the food onto his pickup truck and drove back to the ranch.

Just as he arrived, D started directing Koraidon and Cyclizar to help move things together.

Once everything was done, D returned to the living room to have a drink of water.

To his surprise, he found that Calyrex, who had been watching TV on the sofa, was missing.

On the sofa now, there were only a few small Eevees nestled together, sleeping.

D glanced around, looked at his sibling Indeedee, and asked: "Where's Calyrex?"

Sibling Indeedee: "Aiiy..."

It pointed towards the direction of the Glass Greenhouse.

So D turned and headed to the Glass Greenhouse.

As he pushed open the glass door, he saw Calyrex sitting among a riot of flowers on the racks, playing on a smartphone.

D: "..."

Oh no, Calyrex has stopped being addicted to TV and switched to obsessing over the smartphone.

Only then did Calyrex notice D had come over.

With a reminiscent tone, He said: "If only I had a Rotom Phone to accompany me back then!"

At that time, wandering around the Crown Tundra wouldn't have made He feel so lonely.

D paused slightly, looking at Calyrex's appearance, he couldn't help but recall those lonely elderly people he saw in a past life.

Rather pitiful.

He didn't disturb Calyrex playing on the phone but turned and left the Glass Greenhouse.

*

In the evening, after dinner, sibling Indeedee and Gholdengo went to the Pokemon Tavern to start the business.

While D returned to his room, laying on the bed playing on the smartphone while hugging a fluffy Eevee.

A few unevolved Eevees cuddled up in a ball on his stomach, snug and nestled against each other.

Pawmot nestled at the foot of the bed, and Koraidon slept on the plush carpet.

Just then, the door was knocked.

D got up to open the door, only to be surprised to find sibling Indeedee coming over quickly.

"What's up?" Judging by its demeanor, there must be an urgent matter, D ate two pieces of Translation Nuts Cake.

Sibling Indeedee: "Aiiy!" (There's news about the task D had assigned to the tavern's patrons to help find Pawmot's parents!)

Hmm? Hearing these words, D quickly sat up.

The Pawmot on the bed, who was originally almost asleep, also jolted awake, excitedly asking: "Pamor pamor?" (Really?)

Sibling Indeedee gently nodded: "Aiiy~" (Yes, it was a Squawkabilly that informed me~)

Three minutes later, D and Pawmot rushed to the Pokemon Tavern.

The blue-feathered Squawkabilly was sitting at the bar drinking juice, holding a cup with its wings while chattering incessantly.

"Chirp chirp!" (Let me tell you, when I was outside, I even managed to defeat fierce Pokémon like Tauros!)

The other Pokémon patrons around listened with interest to its tales.

Halfway through, Squawkabilly suddenly noticed D and Pawmot walking in.

Pawmot immediately flew over to inquire: "Pamor pamor?" (Did you really see my parents?)

Squawkabilly stared at it for a moment, looking slightly puzzled: "Chirp chirp?" (Are you Pawmot? Your color doesn't exactly match who I've met?)

Pawmot nodded eagerly: "Pamor!" It was indeed Pawmot, undoubtedly!

Although Squawkabilly was a bit confused, it still started narrating its discovery.

A year ago, as it and its fellow Squawkabillies were soaring above a forest, they saw two Pawmots searching for their child.

Those two Pawmots even willingly climbed atop a towering tree, talking to them and asking if they had seen their child.

At that time, Squawkabilly said no, and watched them dejectedly turn and leave.

Hearing about a towering tree in that forest, Pawmot suddenly got excited.

That's right! It remembered there was such a big tree in the place it used to live!

"So, do you remember the location of that place?" D, who had not spoken, asked.

Squawkabilly proudly nodded: "Chirp chirp!" (Of course, we Squawkabilly are the world's best Pokémon, regardless of what it is, once seen, it will never be forgotten!)

---

---

---

---

# 414: Chapter 411: Reunion

This is still a bragging Squawkabilly.

D couldn't help but think.

But right now, the important thing needs attention. He continued to ask: "Can you take us over there?"

The Squawkabilly was slightly tipsy from drinking too much berry wine earlier.

Seeing this human and the Pawmot needing its help, the Squawkabilly immediately puffed out its chest and promised: "Squawk!" (Let's go! I'll take you there now!)

After all, it eats and drinks here for free every day, and it's starting to make it feel a bit embarrassed.

D: "..."

He intended to say it's too late now, but seeing the expression on Pawmot's face, he swallowed back the words he wanted to say, preparing to accompany them for a night journey.

If all goes well, they could be back before dawn tomorrow.

The Pokémon on the ranch were all asleep, and D didn't disturb them. Taking advantage of the night, he rode on Koraidon, brought along Pawmot, and followed behind Squawkabilly towards the south.

Squawkabilly had a lot to say. While leading the way ahead, it chattered non-stop.

"Squawk!" (Let me tell you, I've been to many places before. Not just forests, I've even conquered the ocean!)

"Squawk!" (The ocean, the ocean, you know? It's vast; ordinary Pokémon simply can't fly across it! Only me, the world's most exceptional Squawkabilly, can do it!)

D: "..."

Koraidon: "..."

Pawmot was quite attentive while listening to Squawkabilly brag about itself.

Perhaps because it was about to see its mom and dad, Pawmot couldn't help but reveal a childlike state of mind.

D smiled and looked at the Pawmot flying ahead.

If possible, he planned to bring Pawmot's parents to the ranch as well, allowing their family of three to reunite on the ranch.

Once the World Tree grows up and the Exploration Team Union is established, they could even let them work as clerks at the item shop, selling berries, milk, and other things to other Pokémon.

However, D wasn't certain that they'd definitely find Pawmot's parents this time.

From what Squawkabilly said earlier, it encountered Pawmot's parents a year ago.

At that time, Pawmot had just been carried away by Bombirdier, arriving at the ranch.

And now, more than a year has passed. No one can guarantee that those two Pawmots are still in that forest and haven't migrated elsewhere.

Thinking positively, at least they finally had news about Pawmot's parents this time; there's always more solutions than problems.

As long as the two Pawmots haven't been captured by a Trainer, they can definitely find them!

The wilds of the Paldea Region were shrouded in night.

The population in the Pokémon World isn't as numerous as D's previous life, which ensures there are still many undeveloped areas in the wild.

The ecology of these places is well preserved, with plenty of Pokémon residing within.

Although Squawkabilly's flying speed isn't as fast as Dragonite's, it was much quicker than Pokémon like Noctowl.

D looked at the time on the Rotom Phone, and after about an hour and a half of flight, a vast, boundless natural forest finally appeared ahead.

This forest was completely enveloped in darkness, yet through the hazy moonlight, D clearly saw a towering tree standing at the center of the forest.

At this moment, Pawmot also saw that big tree.

"Pawmo! Pawmo!"

It hurriedly called out to D twice, then impatiently flew towards that big tree.

Squawkabilly is somewhat puzzled: "Squawk?" (Why is it suddenly so excited?)

D smiled and said: "Maybe it's been a long time since it returned to its hometown?"

With that, he and Koraidon followed.

Squawkabilly also followed closely behind.

Arriving at the big tree in the woods, Pawmot had already descended onto the grass, looking left and right as if trying to identify the direction of its home.

Seeing D come down, Pawmot called out, then started running in one direction.

Halfway, Pawmot saw a clearing.

While reminiscing about its childhood, it pointed at the clearing to D and said: "Pawmo! Pawmo!"

It was on the rock there once when it was asleep and got carried away by Bombirdier!

D slightly smiled and reminisced with Pawmot: "I still remember when I first saw you, you were stealing chicken feed in the Chicken House, that time you were just a little one."

"Pawmo～" Pawmot felt a bit embarrassed.

At that time, it really was starving, plus the grain-made chicken feed was quite tasty!

Passing the clearing, Pawmot continued onward.

The vague memories in its mind began to clarify as the surrounding scenery became familiar.

Bristling underbrush, towering trees, a gently flowing stream...

Soon, Pawmot arrived before a snow-laden tree, suddenly stopping.

D looked up and noticed Pawmot's expression seemed quite emotional at the moment.

Following Pawmot's gaze, D saw a dark hollow under the big tree.

D suddenly realized that it should be Pawmot's nest here.

"Go ahead!" D gently encouraged from behind: "Your mom and dad must be waiting for you at home."

"Pawmo!" Pawmot nodded heavily, wiped its moist eyes, then crawled into the tree hole.

---

D observed the surroundings of the tree hole from outside; it wasn't dilapidated, and there were still a few small footprints left in the snow, which seemed to belong to Pawmot.

This also meant that the two Pawmots were still living here.

D breathed a sigh of relief, sincerely happy for the Pawmots.

Next, it should be a scene of a big happy reunion, right?

The long-separated parents and child meeting again, a family of three embracing and crying tears of joy.

What a warm and touching scene!

However, in the next second, D saw a flash of Spark inside the cave.

Accompanied by a somewhat panicked "Pawmo" sound, Pawmot dashed out frantically.

D: "??? "

Soon after, he saw two vigilant large Pawmots emerge from the tree hole, looking angrily at the unfamiliar Pawmot.

Pawmot hurriedly explained: "Pawmo Pawmo!" (Mom, Dad, I am your child!)

However, upon hearing this, the two large Pawmots became even more angry.

One of the Pawmots let out a threatening growl: "Pawmo!" (Imposter, our child doesn't look like you, go away! Don't pretend to be my child!)

Upon hearing this, D and Pawmot simultaneously realized something.

Pawmot looked down at its fur and finally understood why its parents wanted to attack it upon seeing it.

It quickly shook its head and tried another way to prove its identity.

"Pawmo Pawmo!" (Mom, Dad, do you remember when you took me to look for berries in the forest when I was little? I accidentally fell into the river and was nearly swept away, and it was Lombre who saved me! You even gave Lombre lots of berries!)

The two large Pawmots hesitated, their faces showing disbelief.

Pawmot pressed its advantage: "Pawmo Pawmo!" (Last year, when you were digging at home, I ran out, fell asleep on a stone, and was taken away by a Bird-type Pokemon to a very distant place!)

Upon hearing this, the hostility of the two large Pawmots dissipated. In disbelief, they tremulously asked: "Pawmo?"

Pawmot nodded vigorously: "Pawmo!"

Then, D saw the three Pawmots hugging each other tightly, crying out loud.

"Phew..." He let out a long breath, it seemed that everything had been safely resolved!

Koraidon also looked on happily at this scene: "Ahgahs~" It was happy for the Pawmots as well.

Meanwhile, the Squawkabilly beside them was shouting proudly: "Kokokyu!" (Squawkabilly is the most helpful Pokemon in the world, see that? I successfully helped the Pawmot family reunite!)

This would be a chapter in its Pokemon life written in bold colors, and once back, it would boastfully narrate this incident to all the Pokemons in the tavern!

After crying, Pawmot began explaining in detail to its parents about what happened after being taken away.

It landed in a human's ranch, was taken in by the owner, and with their help, evolved into Pawmot, becoming a very strong Pokemon.

Not only that, but it also met many companions.

The Pawmot parents, who had been staying in the forest waiting for their child to return, looked at D with gratitude.

They, along with their child, thanked D, Koraidon, and Squawkabilly beside them.

D smiled slightly: "No need to thank me, your child is excellent."

The two adult Pawmots ran back into the tree hole and brought D lots of berries.

They expressed their pure and sincere feelings in this way.

D smiled and looked at Pawmot.

Pawmot cheerfully looked at its parents and extended an invitation: "Pawmo Pawmo?" (Mom, Dad, how about living on the ranch with us?)

Hearing this, the two large Pawmots were stunned, looking at their child with confusion.

D explained: "Pawmot is inviting you to live on the ranch with it."

Pawmot: "Pawmo!" Exactly!

The two large Pawmots looked back at D.

D continued, "Don't worry, the ranch is big, it's safe there, and there's no end to berries. If you're willing to go, your family won't have to be separated ever again."

The two Pawmots looked at each other and then at their child.

Finding their child had made them happy, but leaving this place, where they had lived for so long, for somewhere else made them somewhat uneasy.

After all, normally, unless there was a drought, famine, or some natural disaster around their habitat, wild Pokemon would not easily migrate.

Pawmot keenly detected its parents' emotions, and it patted its chest, assuring them: "Pawmo Pawmo!" (I'll take good care of you!)

Having been separated from their child for so long, and now finally reunited, the two large Pawmots did not want to part with their child again so soon.

They exchanged a few glances before finally, after careful consideration, making their decision.

This time, they wanted to be with their child, protect it, and never be separated again!

So, the two Pokemons turned around and ran back into the tree hole, soon dragging out a huge pile of berries, chestnuts, and pine cones.

These were originally their stored food for the winter, but now since they were leaving, they would take them to their new home.

---

D didn't have any objections, fortunately, he had taken the parcel before leaving, which now came in handy for packing these items.

Once everything was packed, D let the two Pawmots sit on Koraidon, and together with Squawkabilly, returned to the ranch.

By the time they reached the ranch, it was already three o'clock in the morning.

It was late at night, so D let Pawmot take its parents to sleep first, and planned to introduce them to the ranch after they woke up the next day.

"Pawmot!"

Pawmot responded happily. It had grown up and was no longer the weak Pawmi it used to be.

Nowadays, it could take good care of its parents.

Pawmot took its parents and ran off.

Meanwhile, D gave Squawkabilly a lot of Berries and drinks to thank it.

Squawkabilly excitedly accepted them, grabbed the bag full of rewards with its claws, and flew away.

Only then did D return to the room with Koraidon.

Having stayed up all night, he almost fell asleep as soon as he hit the pillow.

When he opened his eyes the next day, it was surprisingly already eleven in the morning.

Today is the last day of the year 199, and also the new year in the Pokemon World.

After a simple lunch, D began preparing New Year's Eve dinner for the Pokemon in the ranch.

This year's celebration wasn't much different from last year's, except more Pokemon friends had joined the ranch.

While D was busy, those Pokemon were chasing and playing around in the living room.

Occasionally, a few Pokemon would come over to cause trouble, almost knocking over the bowl D had used to hold ingredients.

D could only drive them away to play in the living room, or have them bask in the sun by the World Tree.

Thus, a large group of Pokemon ran off cheerfully.

They ran to the Reversed World and arrived at the lush World Tree.

At the moment, the World Tree was emitting a shimmering green light, and the Pokemon stood under it, looking up at the extraordinary giant tree, letting the starlight emitted from the World Tree fall onto them.

On Flower Sky Island, Giratina was guiding Rotom on the internet.

After a few days, it had skillfully learned to use voice commands to play with the Rotom Phone.

Compared to watching TV on Pichu TV, Giratina preferred surfing the Pokemon Forum to learn about things unknown to it.

At the ranch, Pawmot was busy touring its workplace with its parents, happily introducing its chicken friends to them.

In the evening, D made a lot of delicious food for the Pokemon, enough to cover the whole table.

The Pokemon ate happily, and after filling their bellies, they lay in groups on the carpet in the room, sleeping soundly in the warm atmosphere.

Watching this scene, D smiled and exchanged a glance with his sister Indeedee, then together began to tidy up the messy dining table.

At some unknown time, snowflakes began to drift again outside the window.

On the cold winter night, the faint sound of crackling could be heard, and the snowy night was illuminated by fireworks set off by people in Porto Marinada Town celebrating the New Year.

Not knowing how long it took, the sound of fireworks gradually dimmed until it completely quieted down. Just when D thought it was all over, a bell sound suddenly came from the direction of Porto Marinada Town.

Accompanied by this ethereal transition bell sound from Porto Marinada Town.

League Era 199, thus came to an end.

---

---

---

# 415: Chapter 412: Surfing Giratina

[Happy New Year!]

At this moment, D saw several messages on his phone.

He clicked open and found that it was New Year greetings from Geeta and Alder.

D replied to each one with a smile and then opened Poke Friend Circle, where he saw photos of the Champion Gym Leaders celebrating the New Year.

The background of Geeta's photo seemed to be at the Paldea League Headquarters. Seeing this photo, D's eyebrow twitched; is this workaholic still working overtime now?

Compared to Geeta, Leon's photo was full of a sense of life.

The background of the photo seemed to be his hometown in Postwick Town.

Besides Leon, there were his grandparents, parents, and his younger brother Hop, the whole family happily gathered together having dinner.

Lono also posted in Poke Friend Circle, and D saw her complaining about how her parents left her with a bunch of Pokemon during New Year while they went out to enjoy their world as a couple.

Looking at this post, D seemed to already envision Lono with her head drooping, and the two Magnemite on her head weakly spinning around.

He smiled and liked the post, and soon received a message from Lono.

[Lono: What are you doing?]

D took a picture of the living room and sent it over.

[D: Observing these Pokemon sleep. What about you?]

[Lono: Hehe, I'm hanging out with Bellibolt and them. They're setting off fireworks in Levincia City, it's really loud outside!]

[D: The fireworks here have stopped. The ranch and town are a bit far apart, I was having dinner, didn't watch much.]

Compared to the big city Levincia City, Porto Marinada Town could be considered rural, the town's population and bustle are far less than Levincia City.

Lono didn't reply immediately, but after a while, she directly dialed a video call.

D was startled, fumbling to reduce the volume, so as not to wake the sleeping Pokemon, he quietly went outside.

The call connected, and D immediately saw the splendid fireworks blossom in the starry night sky on the screen.

Lono's voice came through the sound of fireworks: "D, look! Fireworks!"

Upon hearing this, D was slightly stunned, then realized Lono's purpose for this video call.

Indeed, it's the lively and cheerful Lono! Such things are exactly what she would do.

"I see them." D saw Levincia City shimmering with neon lights below the fireworks: "It's really lively on your side."

It's snowing in Porto Marinada Town, the ranch is still countryside, and it's all quiet.

On the other side in Levincia City, it's a sunny day with stars in the sky, and faintly, the cheering voices of people can still be heard.

The camera turned, and Lono's face appeared on the screen.

Today she wore a yellow cotton jacket with lightning patterns, replacing the Magnemite on her head with a fluffy knitted hat, even wearing thick cotton gloves on her hands.

Maybe it's a bit cold outside, Lono's cheeks were slightly red from the cold.

However, a bright smile emerged on her face, she grinned, showing her white teeth, foolishly saying: "Hehe, Rotom Phone is so convenient!"

D couldn't help but respond: "Yes, it is!"

Talking about Rotom Phone reminded D of Lono's profession as a video host in the game.

He immediately asked: "Do you have any plans for the future? Like doing some part-time work on the internet, something like a live streamer, uploading videos to Pichu TV, or starting an online store? I see lots of people starting to enter the internet industry."

"Huh?" Lono looked surprised: "How did D know I'm planning to become a live streamer?"

"Huh? You really plan on streaming?" D knowingly asked.

"That's right!" Lono nodded: "Broadcasting the intense Gym battles to everyone, it fits Lono's style! I'm going to make Levincia Gym the most popular Gym in Paldea Region!"

So that's what it was? D nodded with a smile: "Go for it! I'll be there with the Pokemon to support you!"

He planned to provide popularity and tips in the beginning for Lono, and when Lono becomes a popular live streamer, invite her to endorse the ranch's online store, do live-stream product sales, simply perfect.

Help us with the slow sales of agricultural products!

*

In the blink of an eye, winter passed and spring arrived, the snow melted, and all things began to revive.

In early spring, the Heroic Expedition Team set off once again to continue their journey.

League Year 200 seemed destined to be an extraordinary year.

Not just the phone, perhaps inspired by Rotom Phone, other communication technologies, which had been stagnant, also started to innovate and evolve.

The bulky desktop computers were retired by the times, replaced by portable and convenient tablets and laptops.

Moreover, gaming consoles appeared too.

When D saw the 3DS for sale online, he was baffled.

But soon, he accepted it.

Because if he remembers correctly, during the Sword and Shield era, the Switch is going to appear.

The previous Champion of the Galar Region, Mustard, liked playing games on the Switch very much.

Curious, D also bought a 3DS, he thought since it's a Nintendo console, there should naturally be Mario, Zelda, and the like.

However, after receiving it, he found out there weren't such games, only some racing games, Pokemon racing games, and some pretend play games.

D lost interest in playing after just one day, and finally, his brother Indeedee picked up the 3ds. It couldn't put it down, especially with the racing games on it.

Later, his sister Indeedee was also drawn to the 3ds, but unlike her brother, she preferred the house-playing mini-games.

The siblings agreed to take turns, each playing for an hour every day.

In the end, D couldn't take it anymore and had to buy another 3ds for his sister Indeedee.

The two Indeedees were very disciplined; although they loved playing games, they didn't forget their duties and still managed everything at home in an orderly fashion.

It's worth mentioning that Pawmot's parents also settled down on the ranch.

The family of three proudly became the Chicken House managers at D Ranch, herding hens there every day.

Under these circumstances, D also started focusing on running his small store.

On February 15th, year 200 of the League calendar, the shop at D Ranch officially opened.

However, he waited for a full three days without receiving a single order.

This made D a bit doubtful about life.

He carefully checked his small shop and found nothing wrong.

"Could it be because the store sells only ordinary crops and milk?" D pondered.

These crops and milk were not rare, as city dwellers could buy a whole bunch of fresh vegetables and unopened MooMoo Milk with just a ten-minute walk.

Additionally, the number of smartphone users hadn't peaked yet, and online shopping wasn't fully widespread, so this situation wasn't surprising.

With this in mind, D found the reason.

However, D was not in a hurry; if he couldn't sell them, he'd just keep them for himself.

D turned off the computer and left the online shop there. Just as he was about to go outside to check the growth of crops on the ranch, he suddenly saw his sister Indeedee running over.

"Ai eep!"

It called out, then wanted to lead D to the hatching room.

D realized something and quickly followed.

As soon as he got in, he saw cracks appearing on the Dratini Egg placed in the crib.

With a crack, a Dratini with a light pink head poked out of the eggshell.

Goodness, another Shining Dratini!

D felt his luck exploding!

The Dratini blinked its big, bright black eyes and curiously observed the surroundings.

Upon seeing D, Dratini immediately let out an affectionate growl.

"Woo～"

"This little guy knows me!" D said with a smile to his sister Indeedee.

No wonder, he had used Dragon Power to help this egg develop before. Although Dratini hadn't hatched then, it must have gotten familiar with his scent by then.

D stepped forward, and Dratini gently crawled onto his hand.

D infused it with Dragon Power, and the Dratini wore a content look, eventually falling asleep happily in D's arms after eating and drinking its fill.

D, who had experience taking care of an Eevee before, was quite familiar with how to look after a newly hatched Pokemon.

He took the Dratini downstairs, out of the room, and found the two Dragonites who had become partners, showing them the Dratini.

"Your child has hatched."

The two adult Dragonites looked down with big eyes at the Dratini in D's arms.

They instantly recognized it as their own child.

They sensed a very familiar aura from the Dratini.

"Aowoo～"

Upon seeing this, the two Dragonites let out a joyful growl.

They knew they had made the right decision leaving their kid in D's care!

D: "..."

D asked speechlessly, "Aren't you going to take it back to Dragonite Island to take care of it yourselves?"

The Dragonite couple shook their heads repeatedly: "Aowoo!"

They spent more time on the ranch than on Dragonite Island; instead of taking it back there, it's better to leave the baby on D Ranch for care.

With D taking care of it, they felt at ease.

This way, they could see their child every day.

When the child grows up, it can enjoy Poffins with them here.

Feeling the thoughts of the Dragonite couple, D couldn't help but twitch his lips.

These two Dragonites sure know how to take it easy...

But that's fine! This Dratini will continue to be taken care of by him.

*

Meanwhile, in the Reversed World, Flower Sky Island.

Giratina was browsing the Internet using Telepathy to command Rotom.

After months of getting acquainted, Giratina had become adept at operating the Rotom Phone.

And the Rotom in the phone had also grown increasingly familiar with the Giratina in front of it.

Compared to the beginning, Rotom no longer feared Giratina, but it still worried about Giratina's temper.

Because this Pokemon had such a bad temper, it would lash out at anything it didn't like.

Rotom's texting "hands" were on edge.

And today, Giratina suddenly came up with a good idea.

It instructed the Rotom Phone to search for news about those two neighbors.

Giratina: "Search for Dialga and Palkia."

Upon hearing this, Rotom knew that Giratina was up to no good again.

It nervously conducted the search, then broadcasted the information it found.

---

"Information related to Dialga and Palkia has been found, Rotom!"

"Dialga, known as the God of Time in the legends of the Sinnoh Region, possesses the power to control time. It's said that time began to flow only because of its birth, Rotom!"

"Palkia, known as the God of Space in the legends of the Sinnoh Region, has the ability to warp space. It's said that when this god was born, the barriers separating the real world vanished, thus creating boundless space, Rotom!"

"The people of the Sinnoh Region even built statues for them, and someone posted a discussion thread about Dialga and Palkia, Rotom! That person seems to really admire these two Pokemon, Rotom!"

Giratina squinted its eyes as it listened, disdain filling its heart upon hearing that those two guys were considered gods by humans. What nonsense gods?

Giratina continued: "Search for the great Giratina!"

Rotom obediently followed the request, and soon it discovered something awkward.

Giratina: "?"

Rotom: "...No information found, Rotom."

Giratina immediately widened its eyes, full of disbelief.

"Search again!"

Rotom complied, but the result was still the same.

It could only repeat: "No entries found, Rotom..."

Giratina suddenly fell silent.

No one knew what it was thinking, but Rotom could sense that Giratina was extremely angry now.

After a while, Giratina finally spoke: "Did you just say a human posted a thread about those two guys?"

Rotom replied: "That's right, Rotom!"

Giratina: "Ask him if he knows the great Giratina!"

Rotom helped Giratina inquire that netizen.

Soon, they received a reply.

[Unknown Netizen: Giratina? What is that?]

Giratina instructed Rotom to tell the other party: [Giratina is the world's most powerful god, far stronger than Dialga and Palkia!]

[Unknown Netizen: Never heard of it. Are you bragging? Are you comparing Pokemon's combat power? No matter how powerful Giratina is, could it be more impressive than Dialga and Palkia, who govern time and space? Dialga can directly manipulate the time on Giratina to return it to when it hasn't been born yet, do you understand?]

Giratina: ??!!!

Outrageous! This human... this human dares to insult it like this!

Giratina was furious and ashamed.

Seeing Giratina like this, Rotom was already trembling.

Sure enough, it heard Giratina say: "Tell him to wait for me!"

Rotom asked tremblingly: "What are you going to do, Rotom?"

Giratina: "I want to teach this ignorant human a lesson. Rotom, locate his position for me!"

"That's illegal, and I can't do such a thing, Rotom!"

Giratina snorted coldly, disdainfully: "Do human laws apply to me?"

Seeing that Rotom couldn't locate him, Giratina decided to find him on its own.

It activated its ability and observed the real world through the Reversed World.

The next moment, its gigantic body floated up, and as Giratina was about to go into the distance, Rotom quickly hopped onto its back.

Seeing Giratina's actions, Rotom was truly scared, and it quickly dialed D's number.

"D, come quickly, Giratina is about to follow the internet cables to hit people, Rotom!"

ps: There's something going on tonight, the phone might not have a signal, so updated early, don't wait tonight.

---

---

# 416: Chapter 413: Are D and Giratina Breaking Up?

When D received a call from Rotom, he was completely befuddled.

What's going on?

Is Giratina following the internet connection to attack someone? Did he hear that right?

"It's true, Rotom!" The Rotom said as it dialed a video call.

The next second, D saw the massive figure of Giratina flying through the distorted world on the screen of his Rotom Phone.

The surrounding sky islands were swiftly being left behind, and a dark aura swirled around Giratina, clearly in an extremely angry state.

D's heart skipped a beat.

He shouted for Koraidon to leap into the Reversed World to chase Giratina, while also asking Rotom for the details.

From Rotom's account, D learned that Giratina had been searching for Legendary Pokemon in the Sinnoh Region, and upon learning there were none, it was taunted by an unknown online user.

This sequence of events directly caused the irritable old dragon to go into a rage, intending to give that unknown user a harsh lesson.

Seemingly feeling that words couldn't convey what had just happened, Rotom immediately sent D a screenshot of the comment.

After reading, D was speechless for a moment.

Probably the guy on the other side never imagined he'd be going against such a Pokemon...

But the most urgent thing now is to catch up with Giratina and stop its plan.

Otherwise, the consequences will be unimaginable!

Recalling how Giratina had bombarded him with special attacks for hours in the past, D felt a headache coming on.

This irritable, vengeful, petty dragon!

Up ahead, Giratina's figure was no longer visible, but with the positioning function of the Rotom Phone, D could see Giratina was speeding away extraordinarily fast.

"Koraidon, speed up!" D commanded.

"Agh-gha-ghra!"

Koraidon seemed to also realize the gravity of the situation, responding and flying with all its might.

The speed was ridiculously fast, and everything around became a blur. Even D, with the strong physique nurtured by drinking Life Sheep Milk, found it hard to bear.

But now was clearly not the time to worry about such things.

D kept his eyes on the little red dot on the screen. With Koraidon's acceleration, the distance between the two red dots was closing very slowly.

Compared to the full-throttle Koraidon, Giratina hadn't widened the gap much, and given the Reversed World was its domain, Giratina could travel through it freely.

It was like a shadow, swiftly shuttling through the destroyed space.

One minute, two minutes, three minutes...

The originally chaotic space around started to become turbulent as Giratina flew past.

Just as the distance between the two Pokemon was about to close, a purple vortex suddenly appeared in front of Giratina.

The next second, Giratina vanished into the vortex.

When D checked Rotom's positioning, he instantly found that the distance between the two Pokemon had widened significantly.

That's probably the distance from the Sinnoh Region to the Paldea Region!

D couldn't help but curse, damn, is this the master of the Reversed World?

The ability to freely traverse the Reversed World?

Seeing that he wouldn't catch up with Giratina anytime soon, D could only attempt to communicate with it through the Rotom Phone and let Koraidon continue forward.

Realizing D's intention, the Rotom that had been staying on Giratina bravely crawled onto Giratina's head.

Then, D's voice came from it.

"Giratina, stop now!"

Giratina roared, "Don't stop me, I want to teach that daring human a lesson!"

Fearful D might use something else to threaten it, Giratina ordered Rotom to hang up the call and ignore D.

Rotom trembled with fear, but did as told.

However, it didn't turn off its real-time location.

Seeing Rotom being so obedient, Giratina was very satisfied, soothing it, "Don't be afraid! Stick with me, and I'll protect you; no one can bully you!"

Rotom was touched but still didn't turn off the location.

Meanwhile, Giratina looked at the void ahead, grinning with a face full of malice.

It's here!

That detestable human, it's time to show that guy the consequences of mocking it!

*

Meanwhile, in the Sinnoh Region, in Eterna City, in a somewhat old apartment building.

A black-haired young man was squatting on the toilet in the bathroom.

The young man's name was Ishihara, 24 years old, favorite drink was iced tea, and his past hobby was gambling coins at the game arcade.

Ever since the release of the Rotom Phone, Ishihara's hobby had become playing with the Rotom Phone and surfing the internet.

To attract traffic, gain followers, and gain more attention.

A few days ago, Ishihara specifically took pictures of two Legendary Pokemon statues on a small hill east of Eterna City and posted their legends online.

Sure enough, the legends of Dialga, the God of Time, and Palkia, the God of Space, attracted many fans to him, with everyone eagerly commenting on the post.

But this morning, a discordant voice appeared in the post.

The commenter asked if he knew Giratina, adding that Giratina was more powerful than the God of Time and the God of Space.

---

At this point, Ishihara couldn't hold back anymore. As a native of Eterna City, he had grown up hearing the legends of the Space-Time Gods.

Therefore, Ishihara directly questioned the other party, stating that the time-manipulating Dialga was practically invincible against other Pokemon.

After sending out the message, Ishihara initially thought the other party would argue with him a bit, but unexpectedly, the person disappeared entirely, not replying with a single word.

"Did I defeat them with my argument?" Otherwise, why would the person have nothing to say?

Thinking of this, Ishihara couldn't help but grin.

"Crackling!"

Suddenly, the bathroom light flickered a few times, accompanied by an eerie cold wind blowing between his legs. Ishihara instinctively clamped his legs together and quickly looked around.

What's going on? Is the bathroom light broken?!

The lights continued to flicker until finally, accompanied by a sharp sound of electricity, all the lights in the room disappeared.

Immediately, Ishihara saw a pair of scarlet eyes appearing on the mirror in front of him.

Ishihara: "!!!"

"Ahhh! A ghost!" A scream erupted suddenly, and Ishihara forgot to even pull up his pants, hurriedly fleeing toward the living room.

Before he could run outside, he was tripped by the pants lifted by the thief, falling onto the ground with a thud.

Through the mirror, Giratina saw this scene and immediately snorted disdainfully.

Wasn't he confident online? Why is his courage so small in reality?

Next, let it give a severe lesson to this clueless human!

Just then, as Ishihara climbed up from the floor, a vortex appeared in his living room.

Soon after, a massive Pokemon with blood-red eyes, surrounded by black aura and exuding an aura of destruction, flew out from the vortex, slowly landing on the floor.

Ishihara: "!!!"

So, so terrifying! He had never seen a Pokemon with such overwhelming presence!

No, something's wrong! The way this Pokemon looks at him isn't right!

Ishihara instinctively sensed danger.

Giratina's eyes were deep and gloomy. In the moonlight shining through the window, a sinister expression appeared on its face.

Ishihara was so frightened that he fell to the ground, dumbly looking up at this Pokemon filled with hostility.

Just then, D and Koraidon arrived in time. Taking advantage of the not-yet-closed portal Giratina had opened, they rushed out.

"Stop, Giratina!"

"This has nothing to do with you! Don't stop me, D!" Giratina snorted coldly, then opened its mouth, rapidly gathering fearsome energy, eventually forming a Shadow Ball that flew toward Ishihara.

Ishihara was scared to death watching this scene.

At that critical moment, Koraidon rushed forward, standing in front of Ishihara and taking the attack from the Shadow Ball.

Ishihara was scared to death.

Seeing Koraidon and D's actions, Giratina's gaze changed instantly, and it angrily roared:

"Why stop me?! This person insulted me first!"

D hadn't expected things to develop to this point.

He said in a deep voice: "I can feel he meant no harm; he simply didn't know of your existence! Also, I'm the one who gave the Rotom Phone to you causing all this, sorry, Giratina, I can't let that happen."

If Giratina harmed humans, it would be despised by even more humans, and D didn't want to see that happen.

Giratina's rage burned fiercely; it didn't want to hurt D, but D's actions made it very angry.

Besides, Giratina also felt very aggrieved.

For hundreds of years, D was the first human to treat it like this; it had even considered him a friend! But now, this so-called friend stood on the side of the human who insulted it!

Fine, very well!

"If that's the case, then you shall bear my wrath!" Giratina roared.

It didn't choose to harm D but turned its gaze toward Koraidon.

Koraidon squinted slightly.

In the next second, the two Pokemon broke through the window, flying into the night sky, clashing intensely over Eterna City at tremendous speed.

Seeing this scene, D felt his heart sink and quickly ran to the window to look up at the sky.

He couldn't help but feel disappointed; indeed, Giratina didn't regard him as a friend?

In the sky, Koraidon's crimson orb and Giratina's black orb intertwined into a spiral, crazily colliding as they continued to rise to higher places.

This scene caught the attention of the people living in Eterna City.

They all looked up to observe the night sky.

"Look! What's that?"

"It's flying so high! Are those Pokemon battling?"

"They're moving so fast!"

Someone holding binoculars watched the battle, murmuring after seeing the unfamiliar figures clearly: "What kind of Pokemon are those?"

Is Koraidon a match for Giratina?

Before today, D never had the answer in his heart, so when he saw Giratina and Koraidon start fighting, he felt exceedingly nervous.

But being too far away, he couldn't command, nor alert Koraidon not to use Fighting Moves, as Fighting Moves are ineffective against Ghost-type Giratina.

---

At this moment, Ishihara hastily put on his clothes in the living room.

He walked over to the mysterious figure standing by the window and looked up at the two Pokemon intertwined in the sky alongside him, asking in astonishment, "What on earth are those Pokemon!!"

D didn't turn around: "That's Giratina, the netizen you were arguing with on the Pokemon Forum. Your replies angered it, so it came."

Ishihara: "?"

He was completely bewildered.

Could Pokemon... also use Rotom Phones to go online?

At this moment, he heard the mysterious figure in front of him continue: "Giratina is also a [god] of your Sinnoh Region, born alongside Dialga and Palkia."

"Unlike Dialga and Palkia, who govern time and space, Giratina oversees the shadows of the world, maintaining its balance. It is also a very great [god]."

"It's just that its world is located on the reverse side of the one we live in, and it seldom appears before humans like Dialga and Palkia do."

As a forgotten god, Giratina wishes it could receive the same treatment as Dialga and Palkia.

They were born at the same time, each responsible for watching over different aspects of the world. It would be too unfair if only Giratina were forgotten.

That would only increase its resentment.

Hearing these words, Ishihara was stunned.

He didn't have time to ponder why Pokemon would play with phones, nor who the man before him truly was.

He only knew that it was his taunting replies that angered the Pokemon...

D shook his head and said, "You don't need to worry, since you didn't know before today."

"I..." Ishihara was at a loss for words.

Meanwhile, the battle in the sky was reaching its conclusion.

The battle seemed to have ended without a clear winner. After one last collision, the two balls of light began plummeting toward the ground.

Seeing this, D immediately wanted to go down and check.

But he had come in a hurry this time, without any other Pokemon.

At this moment, Ishihara took out a Poke Ball.

"Let my Staraptor take you there!"

Ishihara released two Pokemon, a Staraptor and a Murkrow.

He mounted the Murkrow, while D mounted the Staraptor.

"Thank you," he said, expressing gratitude to the unfortunate man, and then flew towards the place where Koraidon and Giratina had fallen with the help of the two Pokemon.

The place was on a plain outside of Eterna City.

When D arrived there, both Koraidon and Giratina were standing on the ground, covered in wounds, staring at each other from a distance.

Seeing this, D was filled with astonishment.

Could his Koraidon really be so strong? It could even arm-wrestle with Giratina?

"Agh..." Koraidon looked despondently at D, appearing somewhat dejected.

D could roughly guess Koraidon's thoughts and reached out to pat its head in comfort.

"You did very well."

After speaking, D looked towards the silent Giratina.

He hesitated about whether he should retrieve Giratina's Rotom Phone...

If the phone remained in Giratina's possession, perhaps incidents of traveling through the internet to hit people would occur in the future.

Giratina also silently looked at D with its blood-red eyes.

It remained silent and didn't speak either.

"I'm sorry!" Ishihara's voice came.

D turned to see him running up to Giratina, sincerely apologizing:

"I'm sorry, Giratina! This was my fault! Before today, I didn't know about you or that you were maintaining the world's balance, so please forgive me! I will never do such a thing again!"

Ishihara bowed deeply, apologizing to Giratina.

Giratina silently looked at this human for a few moments, then turned away, ignoring D and Koraidon, and opened the entrance to the Reversed World before flying in.

The Rotom belonging to Giratina showed a worried expression, glancing around before following Giratina into the Reversed World.

The vortex slowly closed, leaving D and Koraidon in the Sinnoh Region, far from the Paldea Region.

D: "..."

Koraidon also looked helpless: "Agh..."

D checked Koraidon's injuries, then went to the Pokemon Center in Eterna City, asking Miss Joy there to help heal Koraidon.

Once Koraidon had recovered, D climbed onto its back, preparing to return to the Paldea Region as quickly as possible to check on Giratina.

Before leaving, Ishihara hesitantly called out to him.

"Hey, um... could I ask for your name?"

This mysterious man seemed to know so much about the secrets of the Sinnoh Region.

The relationship between Giratina, Dialga, and Palkia—these are things that even historians who specialize in legends in the Sinnoh Region might not know, right?

Who exactly is this man? And what about that mysterious Pokemon...

Ishihara looked at the crimson Pokemon full of wildness.

D turned back to glance at him and said, "I'm D."

"Also, try to avoid getting into conflicts online in the future!"

With that, D turned around and left the Sinnoh Region on Koraidon.

*

The emergence of a mysterious Pokemon in Eterna City and the ensuing intense battle in the sky immediately caught the attention of the Sinnoh League.

The two terrifying Pokemon had left the people of Eterna City in a state of panic.

In this situation, Cynthia, the reigning Champion of the Sinnoh Region, arrived in Eterna City to investigate.

Unfortunately, she discovered nothing.

Until a passionate citizen named Ishihara arrived, providing information about the incident.

"That Pokemon was Giratina, born at the same time as Dialga and Palkia. Giratina resides on the reverse side of the world, always guarding that place and maintaining the world's balance! This was told to me by a mysterious man named D! His Pokemon even had a fierce battle with Giratina!"

Upon hearing these words and that name, Cynthia was slightly taken aback.

D... was it the same D she had met once before?

Cynthia recalled her brief encounter with him at a banquet in the Unova Region.

That man was so knowledgeable about the legends of the Sinnoh Region...

Cynthia thought back to the ancient murals she had seen in the Celestic Town ruins.

The scenes depicted on those murals suggested that the gods of the Sinnoh Region weren't just Dialga and Palkia.

But despite her investigations, Cynthia had never found any further clues.

After some cool-headed reflection, Cynthia suddenly felt it was necessary to visit the Paldea Region.

Cynthia left.

Meanwhile, Ishihara returned to his newly rented house.

His previous house had been severely damaged by Giratina, and until it was repaired, it was uninhabitable.

Sitting on the toilet, he opened his Rotom Phone and logged onto the Pokemon Forum.

The comments Giratina had replied to were still preserved in the comment section.

Ishihara clicked on the profile of the user named [Giratina the Greatest in the World], silently staring at the sparse content there.

Even after experiencing it firsthand, he still found it hard to believe that a Pokemon could use a Rotom Phone and surf the internet!

But without a doubt, this account belonged to Giratina, the internet-savvy Pokemon that was also the Legendary Pokemon of their Sinnoh Region, born alongside Dialga and Palkia.

Realizing this, Ishihara deleted his combatively toned comments and, as if driven by a strange impulse, clicked the follow button.

In the next second, the follower count on [Giratina the Greatest in the World]'s profile shifted from zero to one.

---

---

---

---

# 417: Chapter 414: Giratina's Debut is Imminent, Going Viral Across the Web!

At the same time, D also hurriedly returned to the Paldea Region.

Without time to rest, he immediately jumped into the lake at the center of the ranch, and through it, smoothly arrived in the Reversed World.

Thank goodness, Giratina did not close this entrance! Otherwise, he would have had to drink Poisonous Mushroom Soup!

Upon entering the Reversed World, D instantly breathed a sigh of relief.

He had stopped Giratina, Giratina must have been very upset then, right?

Thinking of this, D turned his head towards the direction of the Flower Sky Island.

At this moment, Giratina was resting there as usual.

The surrounding flowers, which were originally growing well, were crushed beneath it, with many casualties.

Giratina's eyes were tightly closed, its large head resting on its forelimbs, and its thick tail swaying continuously behind it, as if to display its master's unusual inner feelings.

D: "..."

He tried to step forward and landed on the Flower Sky Island, then deliberately coughed twice.

Giratina didn't even lift its eyelids, still keeping its eyes shut, looking as if it didn't want to be bothered.

—It was still angry.

Not angry at that person, but angry at him!

D immediately realized this.

At that time, as Giratina's friend, he didn't stand by Giratina's side but instead chose to block Giratina's attack for that person...

If it were him, he might also feel very disappointed.

But rationality told D that he couldn't let such things happen.

Giratina is hot-tempered, once enraged, it would really follow the internet line and eliminate a netizen's entire family.

D didn't know what to do for a moment; he couldn't just stand by and watch Giratina eliminate all the netizens it had a dispute with.

After a moment of silence, D let out a sigh, then apologized to Giratina.

"I'm sorry, it was my fault this time."

First, soothe it and then calmly explain the pros and cons to Giratina after it calms down.
