Sure enough, upon hearing this, Giratina's eyelids lifted slightly, then it turned its head, facing the back of its head towards D.

Seeing this, D reached out to it, using Dragon Power to perceive Giratina's emotions.

The next second, the power in his hand fully conveyed Giratina's current feelings to him.

It felt wronged!

D was slightly startled, then immediately understood the reason.

From the moment it was born, Giratina had been banished to this ruined world, living alone here as a dragon.

It had no friends or anyone to talk to; every day, it could only look at everything in the real world through the water surface in silence.

Initially, he had entered the Reversed World by accident. After improving the environment of the Reversed World, helping Giratina build a sunlit sky island, providing food, and using Dragon Power for massages, Giratina had already regarded him as a friend.

And now, as Giratina's friend, he stood on the opposite side.

Thinking of this, D instantly understood everything.

D felt pained! Handling such matters was really difficult.

If he chose to support Giratina, then he would have to watch Giratina hurt people, and this matter might continue indefinitely.

If he chose to stop Giratina, then Giratina would be even angrier because of his actions.

More importantly, this incident had already happened.

What should he do? D looked at the giant creature in front of him.

Deceit? Lies?

D was silent for a long time and finally decided to sincerely apologize to Giratina first and then express his true thoughts.

"For humans, everyone has a different definition of a friend."

"I don't know what others think, but in my view, friends should help each other when the other is in difficulty and also stop the other when they are about to make a mistake."

"Although that person quarreled with you, they do not deserve death... I know, I have no right to stop you, and human laws cannot restrain you, but I am just worried that if you hurt humans, people will dislike you, even though your nature isn't bad, right?"

Giratina kept its eyes closed but processed these words in its mind.

It snorted coldly in its heart; whether humans disliked it or not, it did not care!

But Giratina thought of something else again.

D's words seemed to mean that he did not want to see it go down the "wrong" path?

If that's the case, then doesn't that mean D wasn't protecting that human like it previously thought?

Giratina suddenly opened its eyes and stared at D in front of it.

From the moment they exchanged Dragon Power, not only did D feel its emotions, but Giratina also sensed D's thoughts.

D regarded it as a friend, which is why he did not want to see it hurt humans.

Moreover, Giratina felt D's intention to take responsibility for it.

It had just heard Rotom explain on its way back.

In the human world, killing a human would get someone arrested and taken into a police station.

If D took responsibility for it, then D would be caught...

Although Giratina did not care, it could break into the police station, finish off all those policemen, and then free D.

But such actions would definitely put D in a difficult position, right?

Understanding this, Giratina realized that D wasn't intentionally opposing it.

---

Its mood was great, but the tone was still cold, like that lifeless lake in the Reversed World.

"Hmph, I'll forgive you this time!"

Seeing this, D let out a sigh of relief; it seemed Giratina had listened to him.

D grinned, his mood also brightened, and he made a suggestion to Giratina.

"If someone online insults you in the future, as long as they don't go overboard, feel free to run over and scare them."

This not only taught those online trolls a lesson, making them less likely to insult people online but also helped avoid Giratina becoming a global calamity and being jointly attacked.

Though it's a deity, Giratina is also a Pokemon.

Its emotions come and go quickly, and soon it reconciled with D, forming a closer bond.

After D left the Reversed World, Giratina had Rotom perform an online search.

"Search for what it means for a human to stop their friend from killing."

Rotom, sweating: "Searching Roto... found 208 responses Roto."

Giratina: "Read them to me!"

"Got it Roto!"

Rotom read aloud word by word.

"When a human stops their friend from killing, it often means they care deeply about this friend Roto."

"Killing violates League laws. Once such an act is committed, Officer Jenny will lead the police to arrest the perpetrator. If the incident is severe, the International Police will take over Roto."

"Once caught, the murderer faces life imprisonment or even the death penalty, thus losing their life Roto."

"As a qualified friend, one would absolutely stop such things from happening, because they don't want their friend to go down that path of no return Roto."

"If you have such a friend, cherish them Roto!"

Giratina nodded repeatedly as it listened.

Indeed, D really cares about it, doesn't he?

Giratina wanted to hear more, so it asked Rotom to continue reading.

As Rotom reached the twenty-eighth response, its voice suddenly paused, then it opened the Pokemon Forum and announced:

"Someone has become your fan Roto!"

Giratina was a bit confused.

"What's a fan?"

Rotom explained: "It's someone who follows you online Roto!"

Then, Rotom explained in detail to Giratina what a fan means.

For instance, humans often develop affection, admiration, and other emotions for a person's strength, appearance, or talent.

These people regard the one they follow as an idol and can't help but pursue them.

As it spoke, Rotom opened Giratina's personal page to check the followers' information.

However, upon seeing it, Rotom realized something troublesome.

The person was the one who taunted Giratina online!

Giratina noticed Rotom's pause, squinted its eyes, and asked, "What's wrong?"

Rotom: "It's that human from before Roto! He's following you Roto!"

Seeing Rotom's reaction, Giratina quickly pieced things together.

"What does he want?" Giratina asked coldly.

Remembering how that person said it wasn't a match for Dialga made Giratina very angry.

Rotom began investigating that person's intentions.

It checked the person's comment records and posts, then noticed something peculiar.

The post where the person argued with Giratina had been deleted!

Not only that, but there was also a new introduction on the person's profile.

—[The third deity of the Sinnoh Region, the Shadow Pokemon born alongside Dialga and Palkia.]

Looking at this introduction post, both Rotom and Giratina were bewildered.

Was that Shadow Pokemon... referring to it?

The post seemed newly published, so Rotom opened it to view and repeated the contents to Giratina word by word.

[Shadow Pokemon—Giratina, the Legendary Pokemon hidden behind the world.

It's said that it was born in this world at the same time as Dialga and Palkia, with Dialga ruling over time and Palkia over space.

And Giratina went to the backside of the world, overseeing the dark matter there, supporting the reality we live in, maintaining balance.]

In the post, many people from the Sinnoh Region expressed skepticism about the content.

They grew up hearing legends about the God of Time and the God of Space but had never heard of this Pokemon, Giratina.

In response to the skepticism, the post's owner, Ishihara, who was scared by Giratina, directly shared his encounter from the day before.

Including how he was in a heated argument with a netizen only for that person to trace him to his home...

And that netizen's identity was the Legendary Pokemon, Giratina.

During this time, as a forum moderator discussing the history and Legendary Pokemon of the Sinnoh Region, Ishihara's posts attracted many enthusiasts, earning him over a thousand fans from around the world.

Now, seeing this reply, this group of netizens responded with a series of questions, replying below:

"Is this fake? Can Pokemon go online?"

"Is it made up for attention? That's too fake, right?"

"I don't believe Pokemon use Rotom Phones to surf the internet."

"Don't be so certain, someone upstairs, my Oranguru even plays 3ds fighting games with me... and I've never won a match against it."

---

Facing these voices of doubt, Ishihara directly attached a picture of his wrecked living room and a screenshot of yesterday's post replied by Giratina, and tagged Giratina.

In no time, hundreds of people flocked to Giratina's personal page.

Soon after, Rotom received one private message after another.

Rotom was stunned, and so was Giratina.

Giratina felt like those humans were there to flame it, and its face darkened, barely suppressing anger: "What are they saying?"

How dare they! If these weak humans dare to insult it again, it will hunt them down and scare them all silly one by one!

As long as it doesn't cause any fatalities, it's all good.

However, Rotom gave Giratina an unexpected answer.

Rotom: "They're asking if you are really Giratina, Loto!"

Giratina: ?

If it's not Giratina, then who could it be?

Seeing these humans doubt its identity, Giratina immediately ordered Rotom to take a photo and upload it online as proof!

Rotom obediently complied.

As a result, Giratina's personal page now had an extra photo.

The photo depicted a huge Pokemon, with grey and red stripes across its body, ghostly wings on its back, and blood-red eyes, looking exceedingly fierce.

And on the photo, as per Giratina's request, Rotom had boldly added a caption—Daddy is Giratina!

Ten minutes later, the post shot to the trending topics.

Twenty minutes later, fans of Giratina's personal page began growing rapidly.

An hour later, news of the Legendary Pokémon surfing the Pokemon Forum using a Rotom Phone became viral across the internet.

Four hours later, Giratina's follower count broke into five digits.

Sinnoh Region, Celestic Town.

Just returned to her hometown, Cynthia, who was preparing to re-examine the mural at the town's ruins, received a notification from the Sinnoh League.

She opened her Rotom Phone, logged into her Pokemon Forum account, and found the netizen named [The World's Strongest Giratina].

Clicked open the profile page, and the next moment, Cynthia was shocked to see the fierce, aggressive-looking Pokémon's photo.

Cynthia: "?"

Veilstone City, Team Galactic Base.

With a pale face and dull eyes, Cyrus stared at the photo on the phone, murmuring:

"Interesting, so there's a Pokémon that can emerge from the shadows? Giratina..."

Kanto Region, Pallet Town.

Professor Oak, filled with excitement, watched the computer screen displaying a Pokémon he had never seen before.

"Giratina... the Legendary Pokémon living on the reverse side of the world? Incredible! This Legendary Pokémon can even use a Rotom Phone to surf the internet!" With eyes blazing, Professor Oak said to his assistant by his side.

"I must get in contact with it!"

Saying that, Professor Oak immediately followed this wondrous Legendary Pokémon and sent it a private message.

In the Reversed World.

Instantly, Rotom was bombarded with thousands of private messages.

It quickly informed Giratina of these happenings: "Should we reply, Loto?"

Yet, in this situation, a now calm Giratina was filled with disdain: "Ignore them!"

Mere humans are not worthy of the divine Giratina's response.

"Got it, Loto!" Rotom promptly replied.

Giratina paused, then asked, "By the way, what's D's account name? Help me follow him!"

Rotom communicated with D's Rotom Phone, but learned from the Rotom in that phone that D was sleeping.

Tired from his overnight travel back from the Sinnoh Region, D collapsed into bed as soon as he got home.

Seeing this, Rotom directly obtained D's personal account from the other side and followed him on behalf of Giratina.

So, when D woke up from his sleep and decided to play with his phone, he received a notification that Giratina had followed him.

[The World's Strongest Giratina has followed you!]

D recognized the name, knowing it was a name Giratina had given itself.

Out of curiosity, D clicked into Giratina's personal page.

But he was caught off guard, to say the least.

Staring at Giratina's five-figure follower count, D was utterly bewildered.

What's going on?

Did some earth-shattering event happen while he was asleep?

---

---

---

---

# 418: Chapter 415: New Orders, Dragonite Delivery Action!

To get a handle on the situation, D sat up straight immediately, leaning against the headboard, scrolling through the Pokemon Forum.

Without much effort, D understood the background of the events.

Because once he clicked into the homepage of the Pokemon Forum, he saw the trending topic titled [Shocking! Legendary Pokemon Surfing the Net Using Rotom Phone!]

This trending topic even topped the list, leaving a large gap with the second place.

Well, good on you!

After reading, D exclaimed, well done!

The situation started heading towards an unpredictable outcome, like being pulled by ten thousand Tauros running wild ahead, completely out of his control.

38678.

This is the number of followers on Giratina's personal page so far, and it's only been seven hours since that post exposing Giratina was published.

Is this serendipity? D asked himself.

Giratina's explosive fame not only caused its followers to skyrocket, but also brought four to five hundred new followers to his account.

Because his account named [Winged King] is the only account that Giratina, the big internet celebrity, follows.

D has already seen many people starting to discuss what his relationship with Giratina is.

Some even sent him private messages asking for more details, asking if he is Giratina's Trainer?

D: "..."

D couldn't imagine how many private messages Giratina would receive.

Not knowing how to respond to those private messages himself, he simply ignored them all, shut off his phone, and went to the Reversed World to find Giratina.

Giratina was listening to Rotom reading those human comments aloud, and when D arrived, they ended up looking at them together.

D sat beside Giratina's big head, the Rotom Phone automatically levitating in front of them.

D glanced at the screen, seeing the private message section full of little red dots, all showing 99+, meaning the messages had exploded.

These were all private messages that people sent to Giratina.

D took a look, silently thinking to himself that it was indeed the case.

Rotom read aloud to Giratina, while D swiped the screen with his fingers to see what people actually sent to Giratina.

[Invincible Youth: Are you really a Pokemon?]

[Strongest Trainer: Giratina, please accept my challenge! I want to have a Pokemon battle with you!]

[Tropius: Amazing!]

Such comments and more.

D continued scrolling down, even saw a private message that seemed to be from Professor Oak.

[Professor Oak: Hello, Giratina! I'm Professor Oak, specializing in Pokemon research. Do you have time? Can you visit Professor Oak in Pallet Town? We'll definitely give you a warm hospitality when you come!]

D: "..." This really fits Professor Oak's character.

He clicked into that homepage, finding that this user had already been professionally certified—it really was Samuel Oak, the well-known doctor in the Pokemon Research Realm.

Goodness, Giratina, you're really famous now!

D turned his head to look at Giratina beside him.

This Pokemon, which was throwing tantrums not long ago, was now lazily shutting its eyes, listening contentedly to Rotom's reading.

"Aren't you going to reply?" D asked.

Giratina shook its head indifferently, filled with disdain: "Such humans, not worthy of my reply!"

D: "..."

That's right, it matches Giratina's character.

After all, being a Legendary Pokemon that has existed since the birth of the world, it naturally carries an air of arrogance.

Since Giratina ignored the fans, D decided to pretend not to see it too. As for his relationship with Giratina, he would let the fans imagine it themselves!

D returned to the ranch.

Dusk had arrived, with the sky outside gradually darkening.

The Pokemons were leisurely running and playing on the grass, basking in the sunset.

Having slept all afternoon, D now felt energized. He took a long deep breath, ready to prepare dinner for the Pokemons.

Walking past the living room, D noticed The King of Bountiful Harvests running back from the Glass Greenhouse, sitting elegantly on the sofa, sipping red tea while chasing a TV series on the Rotom Phone.

Seeing this scene, D couldn't help but feel a million emotions.

Giratina has become a big internet celebrity now, while The King of Bountiful Harvests is still indulging in TV dramas...

If only these two Legendary Pokemons could switch roles.

Let Giratina chase dramas, and The King of Bountiful Harvests become a big internet celebrity, thus absorbing the belief of the entire internet, spreading The King's fame across every corner of the Pokemon World.

With the support of the world's faith, The King of Bountiful Harvests would become invincible!

D felt a little tempted inside, and so that night, he created a separate forum account named [Calyrex Cultural Ambassador] and started spreading the gospel of The King of Bountiful Harvests online.

Including the legend of The King, its abilities, and so on.

Next, it was just a matter of waiting for time to ferment slowly!

*

After dinner, D returned to his room to rest.

The newly hatched Dratini followed him closely, so D placed this light pink little Dratini on his stomach to sleep.

Lying in the soft quilt, surrounded by an atmosphere of kindness and relaxation, Dratini quickly yawned and fell into a sweet dream.

The little Eevees were scattered in every corner of the room.

The number of Pokemons was so high that the bed was almost too crowded to sleep.

---

So, a few days ago, D bought each of them a small nest.

Now the Eevees run back to their small nests every day for a good sleep.

Pawmot has not been sleeping in the room these days but has instead been accompanying its parents at the ranch.

As a result, now only Koraidon, Dratini, and the Eevees are left in the room.

Lying on the bed, D pinched Dratini's big nose while opening a livestream and searching [Lono's Quirky Stories Live].

Soon, D found Lono's livestream account, and the other person was still streaming live.

D smiled slightly, did Lono think he wouldn't find her if she didn't tell him?

Maybe because she just started streaming, Lono hasn't fully adapted yet, so she doesn't really want familiar people to see.

So, a few days ago when D asked her, Lono replied mysteriously that it was confidential.

Clicking into the live room, D found there were only a few people, and Lono was welcoming a challenger to the Gym.

Although it's already eight o'clock in the evening, as one of the top cities in Paldea Region, nightlife in Levincia City is just getting started.

The battle on the screen seems to have reached a heated stage.

Both Lono and the challenger were down to their last Pokemon.

In this situation, the challenger sent out a Quaxwell, while Lono brought out her ace Pokemon, Mismagius.

As the battle began, both sides performed a Terastal.

With a dazzling flash of brilliance passing by, Mismagius became Electric Tera · Mismagius, and Quaxwell became Water Tera · Quaxwell.

D immediately saw the outcome of the battle.

The Quaxwell's level was clearly insufficient, and even though it had Terastalized, its attributes were still countered by Electric Tera · Mismagius.

Sure enough, just as he had anticipated.

Mismagius defeated the Water Tera · Quaxwell in just a minute.

The Trainer of the Quaxwell looked very dejected.

Seeing this, Lono smiled and encouraged: "Oh dear, even though you lost the match, I saw the bond between you and Quaxwell! Don't be discouraged, keep it up, you can definitely beat me next time!"

"Mm!" The Trainer was clearly encouraged.

He gratefully glanced at Lono, then turned and took his Pokemon to the Pokemon Center for treatment.

Lono then returned to the camera and said to the screen: "Hello everyone, that battle was really exciting! That challenger had a spirit just like Eelektross!"

Looking at the lively smile on Lono's face, D smiled slightly and sent out a big rocket as a reward.

Lono originally wanted to say something else, but upon seeing the reward and the name of the sender, she seemed to realize something and changed her tune:

"Haha, thank you very much for the support, but Lono has been streaming for quite a while today and now needs to rest, so that's all for today's stream, see you next time!"

With that said, the live room went dark.

D immediately received a video call from Lono.

Connecting the video, Lono seemed a bit shy because her stream had been discovered by someone she knew.

"That person just now was you, wasn't it?"

D shook his head repeatedly: "Not me, you got the wrong person."

Upon hearing this, Lono was speechless: "Then how do you know what I was just saying?"

D fell silent.

After a moment, he asked: "How did you figure it out?"

Lono: "Silly! Because all your online names are Wing King!"

D: "..."

Oops, he slipped up! He just used a unified username for convenience when registering accounts.

Others didn't notice, but Lono did.

"Alright, alright! You don't have to reward me!" Lono's face was a bit red, and she said embarrassedly: "It always feels a little weird being watched streaming by people I know..."

D didn't expect early Lono to be troubled by such issues.

He said: "But as your popularity grows, you will inevitably be seen by people you know, right?"

"That's true! Lono realized that was indeed the case, and finally sighed helplessly: "Well, I'll try to get used to it slowly!"

D smiled slightly: "That's more like it, if you want to become a celebrity, this situation is bound to happen sooner or later. When you become famous, I'll even want you to endorse me!"

Upon hearing this, Lono's attention was immediately drawn: "Endorsement?"

Talking about this, D sighed helplessly: "Yeah! I opened an online store selling agricultural products and MooMoo Fresh Milk, and I haven't received a single order yet."

Lono immediately understood what was going on.

"Although I also want to provide help, my live stream room only has over three hundred followers now, so I can't really help much..."

D quickly shook his head and said: "No worries, I believe in you, you will definitely become a very famous big streamer in the future!"

Lono paused slightly, not expecting D to believe in her so much.

This touched Lono, who was just stepping into the streaming industry.

After a brief daze, Lono flashed a bright smile: "Yes! I'll definitely promote it for you when the time comes!"

Lono plans to go back to rest, and before hanging up, she reminded D not to reward her anymore.

Because she streams not for money, but just to make the Levincia Gym famous.

---

After all, the Paldea League is very generous to the Gym Leaders and League employees, so in this case, no one would be short of money.

D could only agree.

In fact, besides turning to Lono, he could also ask the overnight sensation Giratina to endorse the ranch.

But if he did that, it would definitely attract a lot of online fans to flock over, and by then, the ranch would be overrun and turned into a tourist attraction. They can forget about continuing the peaceful life they have now.

Therefore, after much consideration, D still chose the second method.

Since he napped during the day, D found it hard to fall asleep at night.

To avoid disrupting his sleep schedule, he directly asked the Indeedee Sibling to use Hypnosis on him, and then he fell asleep immediately.

*

Meanwhile, in Levincia City.

After hanging up the phone, Lono returned to her home.

She took out her Rotom Phone, opened the Pokemon online shopping app, and tried searching for D's online store.

Lono typed "Winged King" but found nothing.

This surprised Lono.

"Huh? Isn't it Winged King this time?" What's it supposed to be?

Lono tried hard to recall what D just said, then typed "D Ranch" in the search bar.

This time the search was successful, and the interface showed more than a dozen shops.

Lono immediately spotted the [D Ranch] among them.

Looking at the shop's zero sales record, Lono couldn't help but mutter:

"I didn't expect someone as amazing as D to encounter setbacks here." He must have taken quite a hit, right?

In that case, let her lend a helping hand!

In the living room, Lono giggled, then clicked into the shop and began placing orders for the items.

Lono originally thought D would choose Pelipper Express for delivery.

But when she saw Dragonite Delivery listed as a shipping method, she was completely taken aback.

"As expected of D's style!" Lono muttered.

Dragonite's flying speed is famously fast, and even though Porto Marinada Town and Levincia City are located on the east and west ends of the Paldea Region, with Dragonite's abilities, it only takes about twenty minutes to reach.

With such speed, there's no worry that the vegetables and milk will spoil during delivery.

Realizing this, Lono added her parents' address to the delivery list, planning to buy some fresh vegetables and milk for them.

Once everything was ready, Lono made the payment.

D will definitely be very excited when he sees so many orders tomorrow, right?

*

The next morning, as soon as D woke up, he received a notification on his Rotom Phone saying that a customer had purchased farm products from the online store.

Upon hearing this, D was wide awake, fully alert.

Excitedly, he opened the Pokemon online shopping app and, sure enough, saw several orders on it.

"Three boxes of MooMoo Milk, two boxes of Berries, one jar of Rock Salt, two cans of honey, one box of eggs, two heads of cabbage... quite a lot."

D cheerfully instructed Phone Rotom to send the customer's address and other information to the working Dragonite, then went out to organize the goods, passing the items from the orders to the Dragonite for delivery to the customers.

The Dragonites' satchels are equipped with magical space technology, offering ample room to hold over a dozen boxes of MooMoo Milk.

The working Dragonites immediately set off with the goods.

Watching them depart, D was very pleased.

This is a good start!

He went back to his room, happily looking at his little shop.

However, D soon noticed something unusual.

The three orders' addresses were all in Levincia City, and the addresses and names were very similar!

D entered the three numbers into his phone, quickly discovering that one of them had already been saved in his contacts.

He took out his phone and dialed Lono's number.

Soon, the call was connected.

Lono, preparing sandwiches for Bellibolt and Mismagius, pretended nothing had happened when she saw D on the screen.

"Good morning, D! Hehe, what's the occasion for calling me today?"

---

---

---

---

# 419: Chapter 416: The Apple Garden and Applin—A New Adventure for the Heroic Expedition Team!

"Dear customer, the MooMoo Milk, berries, rock salt, honey, and other farm produce you ordered have all been delivered. You should receive them in about twenty minutes."

D imitated the tone of the customer service from his past life, smiling as he spoke.

Lono felt a bit guilty but continued to play along: "Huh? What are you talking about, D? I don't quite understand."

D: "..."

"Alright, alright, no more pretending, those three orders are yours, right?" D was a bit speechless but also felt a sense of warmth in his heart.

Lono is quite a nice person, definitely worth befriending!

With the conversation at this point, Lono dropped the act and admitted with a playful grin:

"Because the produce from your ranch is of high quality! Eating it makes you healthier, so I bought some to send over to my parents!"

By this time, the Dragonites should be near Levincia City?

Lono wanted to add something, but she saw a Dragonite with a satchel land at her doorstep guided by the Rotom Phone.

Lono moved the camera aside, stepped forward to receive the goods from the Dragonite, and then said to D, "All done! I've received the goods, hehe, Dragonite Delivery is really convenient!"

D could only sigh helplessly: "Alright!"

"Yes, yes!" Lono nodded repeatedly, worried that D might scold her, and quickly found an excuse to hang up the call.

Ten minutes later, D looked at the positive reviews on his online store and was momentarily speechless.

[User BelliboltTree: I've received the goods! MooMoo Fresh Milk is delicious, the vegetables are freshly picked! The taste of rock salt is unique too, both my Pokemon and I love it, looking forward to supporting again!]

Seems a bit like fake orders...

Meanwhile in Levincia City, as Lono was about to change her outfit and head to the gym with her Bellibolt, she suddenly received a call from her parents.

The call connected, and she immediately heard her mother's slightly anxious voice.

"Darling daughter, where did you buy those things?"

Lono was stunned, quickly asking: "What happened? Is something wrong?"

The woman recounted the incident word by word.

Ten minutes ago, a delivery Dragonite landed at their home and handed them many farm products and fresh milk.

The couple saw the name and phone number on it and knew it was their daughter who bought them.

They signed for the goods, sent off the Dragonite, and prepared to eat these fresh produce that morning.

Just then, the man noticed a special bottle of sheep milk among the goods, with a Life Sheep Milk label and a Skiddo's cartoon portrait beside it.

The man, who had never tried sheep milk, was very curious and decided to give it a taste.

However, after drinking the sheep milk, he immediately felt a surge of heat and energy throughout his body.

"Your dad has already run around the yard ten times and is now wrestling with a Machamp on the grass!"

Hearing her mother's words, Lono was momentarily dazed, then recalled the magical sheep milk sold at D Ranch.

She had once witnessed the miraculous properties of the sheep milk firsthand.

Grusha, who could no longer ski due to injury, regained the ability to ski after drinking the sheep milk.

With this in mind, Lono quickly said to her mother, "I forgot to tell you! That sheep milk is very special, meant for heavily injured people and Pokemon, healthy people who drink it will be filled with energy until they exhaust it all!"

Upon hearing this, the woman was stunned: "That shop sells such things?"

Sounds like those dubious medicine shops selling small pills to middle-aged men?

With this thought, the woman became a bit worried: "There should be no side effects, right?"

Lono reassured her: "Rest assured, absolutely no side effects! I've seen others drink it personally!"

The woman finally relaxed.

*

During the early spring, the entire ranch was brimming with vitality.

Flowers in the garden bloomed abundantly, attracting wild butterflies that flapped their wings and danced among the blooms, freely showcasing their graceful figures.

After finishing communication with Lono, D waited about twenty more minutes at the ranch, then saw the Dragonites returning from their deliveries.

D glanced at the time on his Rotom Phone, murmuring:

"It takes only 20 minutes from Porto Marinada Town to Levincia City, truly worthy of a Dragonite that can fly around Diglett in 16 hours."

He even felt that if the Dragonites exerted their full strength, the time would be even shorter.

Stacking buffs like Agility, Extreme Speed, and Dragon Dance.

Compared to regular Pelipper Express, Dragonite Delivery is a completely superior option.

Except for Lono, there were no other customers at the moment in the ranch store.

Leaving the online matters aside, D planned to check on the Pokemon in the ranch.

Spring had arrived, and there were many matters at the ranch waiting for him to handle.

Such as the upcoming spring racing, according to D's observations, Glastrier seemed very eager for this event.

Because it also wanted the "crown" that was on Spectrier's head.

Indeed, it was the champion trophy that Spectrier won at last year's horse racing competition.

It had been wearing it like a crown on its head ever since, and D once tried to help take it off for safekeeping, but was rejected by Spectrier every time.

---

Because it believes it symbolizes the Horse King and should not be handed over casually.

With no other choice, D had to give up, while beginning to worry whether Spectrier wearing that trophy long-term might cause its skull to deform into the shape of a trophy...

Thinking about this, D felt it was quite a dreadful thing.

He looked at Spectrier in the stable, feeling that things couldn't continue like this.

Thus, D turned and found Calyrex, sharing his concerns.

Upon hearing this, Calyrex said nothing but went to speak with Spectrier for a few words.

Then D saw Spectrier remove the trophy and hand it to Calyrex for safekeeping.

"It's all sorted now." Calyrex said to D, holding the trophy.

"That's good." D once again conducted a detailed inspection of Spectrier's skull, only relaxing after confirming there was no deformation caused by the trophy.

After bidding farewell to Spectrier and Glastrier, D ventured into the Reversed World, arriving beneath the World Tree.

He found the Tropius whose wings were injured last winter and inquired about its plans.

"Spring has arrived, the snow outside has melted, life begins to recover; what plans do you have next? Are you thinking of returning to Casseroya Lake to reunite with your companions, or do you plan to stay here at the ranch?"

Tropius raised its head high, letting out a melodious call: "Oh-oooh~!"

D: "Would you like to stay and live with us?"

Tropius nodded happily: "Oh-oooh!"

"Great." D smiled slightly: "Then please take care of us in the future, Tropius!"

"Oh-oooh~"

While speaking, D noticed the bananas on Tropius's neck had regrown.

If he remembered correctly, Tropius had picked all its fruits in December.

Logically, the next batch of bananas should not be harvested until summer.

But now it's spring, and they've already been harvested.

"Is it due to the accelerated effect of fried bananas, or has staying long-term near the Time Tree hastened the growth of bananas with ample natural energy?" D murmured.

Looking at it this way, Tropius's fruit growth cycle may change from twice a year to six or seven times.

Coupled with its [Harvest] ability, it might yield quite a few bananas at once; rounding up, that's one harvest per month.

"It's a pity that the effect of the Divine Steamed Bun made from Ho-oh Feather doesn't work on Tropius." D thought to himself.

[Divine Blessing, after being eaten by Pokemon, receives Ho-oh's favor, greatly increasing fortune ↑. This effect is only effective on Mew, Teddiursa, and Zapdos.]

This effect is tantamount to a man-made Serene Grace ability; if it worked on Tropius, it might trigger its Harvest ability a hundred percent, doubling banana harvests each time.

However, Divine Blessing is only effective on members of the Heroic Expedition Team favored by Ho-oh.

But one must remain content; compared to the original twice-a-year harvest, things are already much improved, aren't they?

D stretched out his hand and patted Tropius's head. As the weather gradually warmed, Gogoat and Arboliva had already left the Reversed World to reside outside.

The Heroic Expedition Team also resumed their travels.

Tropius found itself somewhat bored alone, so it followed D outside.

D settled it by the riverside, where the abundant water source and plenty of sunshine made it an ideal place for Tropius to live.

Once the World Tree fully matures, he plans to build a nest for Tropius on it.

After settling Tropius, D went across the river to the Apple Garden.

Thanks to Gogoat's cultivation, the apple saplings he had planted before had fully grown, and now red apples were hanging from them.

Judging by their size and color, they were no less impressive than the apples from Kitakami.

D picked an apple and took a bite.

In the next second, the sweet pulp exploded with rich juice in his mouth.

"Hmm, tastes sweet and crisp, nice!"

D nodded in satisfaction.

Apart from these red apples, some apples remained greenish.

D planned to harvest them in a couple of days.

Walking through the Apple Garden, looking at the apple-filled yard, D suddenly felt a bit concerned.

These apples aren't going to attract Applins, are they?

Applin, a Pokémon known as the Apple Core Pokémon, is a Grass and Dragon type.

This kind of Pokémon specializes in eating apples. From birth, it hides inside an apple, eating the flesh while growing, and usually uses the apple to disguise itself.

For farmers, Applins in normal circumstances can harm the orchard, as they are a harmful Pokémon.

If you encounter an Applin with the Gluttony ability, the entire orchard's apples might be devoured completely.

But things aren't always absolute.

In such cases, if you encounter an Applin with the Ripen ability, not only will it not eat all the apples, but it will also accelerate their growth, allowing them to ripen faster.

And once an Applin evolves into a Flapple or Appletun, the efficiency of Ripen will be greatly enhanced.

It's said that in the distant Galar Region, many orchardists raise Applins, allowing them to help the apples ripen.

Thinking of this, D carefully wandered around the Apple Garden, intending to see if any Applin had snuck into his apples to nibble on the flesh.

For identifying apples, his Golden Finger can play a significant role.

Looking out, the detailed information of almost each apple is like this:

[Apple: A common fruit, sweet and delicious, crisp, and refreshing, greatly enhanced in quality due to cultivation by Gogoats, highly nutritious, can be used for cooking.]

If you encounter an apple occupied by Applin, the detailed information will not appear.

Because at that time, the apple can no longer be used as an ingredient.

After a careful inspection, D confirmed that no Applins had come to his Apple Garden.

This left D feeling somewhat disappointed.

He quite liked Appletun, which evolved from Applin.

If he had an Appletun, he could plant an apple orchard on the World Tree and let Appletun watch over it there.

After a brief thought, D prepared to go home and use his Rotom Phone to search online for information about Applins and Appletuns, see their habitats, and then capture a few with the Ripen ability to manage the Apple Garden.

Just then, the sky suddenly darkened.

D looked up and saw the massive figure of Rayquaza slowly descending into the ranch.

D was slightly taken aback, then realized something, turning his head to look at Snorlax Park.

Sure enough, he saw Dragonite, Cyclizar, and Ogerpon there, even the Eevees and little Dratinis had gone over to join in the fun.

It seemed like they were awed by Rayquaza's majestic form, as the Eevees and Dratinis displayed gazes of admiration.

Dragonite was inviting Rayquaza to the ranch again...

Great, the cookies and Poffins he and his sister Indeedee prepared yesterday will be devoured by this big eater Rayquaza again.

D felt a headache coming on, but since Dragonite and Ogerpon were using Poffins to entertain Rayquaza, he couldn't stop them. Not letting Rayquaza eat would be too impolite.

"Sigh."

D could only accept his fate, letting out a sigh, and prepared to go home to see how many Poffins were left.

He returned to the living room, checking the ingredients in the cupboard, when he suddenly received a video call from Teddiursa and Mew.

D was slightly surprised and quickly pressed the answer button.

The next second, the three Pokémon of the Heroic Expedition Team appeared on his phone screen.

D noticed the background behind them, which seemed to be a forest.

"Looks like you have successfully reached the destination." D said with a smile.

Teddiursa looked very happy: "Eme eme!"

Its words were automatically translated by Rotom into electronic sound.

Teddiursa: "That's right! This place has many Pokémon we have never seen before!"

Mew: "We just helped a lost Wingull in the forest find its trainer!"

"Wingull?" D was a bit surprised.

Mew and Teddiursa nodded repeatedly, then turned the screen to let D see those unfamiliar Pokémon around.

Zigzagoon in the bushes, Poochyena in the clearing, Beautifly fluttering in the forest, and Taillow resting on the branches...

Looking at those familiar Pokémon on the screen, D felt a hint of realization.

Could it be that the place of adventure for the Heroic Expedition Team this time is the Hoenn Region?!

---

---

---

---

# 420: Chapter 417: Mission Assignment: Jirachi! Rumors of Paldea's Hidden Boss

Hoenn Region, the place where dreams begin.

D still remembers when he first got into Pokemon, it was during the Red, Blue, and Emerald era.

Red, Blue, Green respectively correspond to the Earth God Groudon, the Sea God Kyogre, and the Sky God Rayquaza.

"I wonder what time it is now, has the battle of the Three Gods of Hoenn happened?" D murmured in his heart,

Paldea Region is far away from Hoenn Region, he rarely hears news from over there.

But with the destructiveness and impact of Groudon and Kyogre, if that event really happened, the whole world would know about it.

Besides Groudon, Kyogre, and Rayquaza, Hoenn Region also has many Mythical Pokemon and Legendary Pokemon.

For instance, the Three Regis, Regirock, Regice, and Registeel, sleeping in Ancient Ruins.

However, the Pokemon that interests D the most is Jirachi, known as the "Seven-Night Wishing Star".

Jirachi is a very special Pokemon.

It can only wake up once every thousand years, and the waking time lasts only a brief seven days. After seven days, the Pokemon will fall asleep again until the next thousand years.

Looking at the Heroic Expedition Team on the screen, D couldn't help but fall into deep thought.

If he remembers correctly, there's a place called Forina in Hoenn Region.

It's the setting for the movie "Jirachi: Wish Maker".

And a Jirachi is sleeping in that unique terrain.

Every time the Millennial Comet arrives, Jirachi will awaken, absorb cosmic energy from the comet, then nurture the land, maintaining Forina's richness and beauty.

D wants to see if that movie will happen in this world.

Thus, he issued a second commission to the Heroic Expedition Team.

"By the way Teddiursa, Mew."

Across the screen, Teddiursa and Mew, along with Zapdos, looked over, waiting for D's next words.

"I heard that on the land you're visiting now, there's a Pokemon named Jirachi..."

D detailed the rumors of Forina and Jirachi to the three Pokemon.

After listening, Teddiursa and Mew blinked, feeling a bit sympathetic towards the Pokemon.

Teddiursa: "It's so pitiful!"

Mew also nodded: "Only able to wake for seven days, several sevens make a thousand years, Jirachi must be very lonely, right?"

All three Pokemon simultaneously looked up at D.

"D, can you help Jirachi?"

D: "..."

Is he some kind of wishing machine?

Judging by the depiction in the anime, waking every thousand years for seven days seems to be the ecological habit of this Pokemon, whether it's the one in Forina or the one in Alola, they're all like this.

D couldn't think of a good solution, unless he could develop a culinary dish useful to Jirachi, but the key is that he currently has no idea nor knows what kind of dish would have such an effect,

but looking at the three Pokemon's expectant eyes, D couldn't say he couldn't do it.

He swallowed the words and changed his tune: "I will do my best to try."

Hearing this, Mew and Teddiursa happily clapped.

Their words were translated by Rotom.

"Then we shall go find Forina and Jirachi!"

After hanging up the phone, Mew and Teddiursa looked at the Rotom Phone in front of them.

"One two one two!" (Rotom, please help us record D's commission!)

"Understood Roto!" The Rotom Phone responded.

It then opened the memo function and recorded down D's commission.

[Commission 2: Help Jirachi!

Commission content: Go to Forina to find the Mythical Pokemon Jirachi, which wakes every thousand years, then help it with D together.

Completion status: Not yet completed]

Besides this commission, there is currently one more commission in the memo.

[Commission 1: Find expedition team member

Commission content: Find a Pokemon named Hoopa, and invite it to join the Heroic Expedition Team on the journey.

Completion status: Not yet completed]

This was the plan devised by the Phone Rotom after joining to help the Heroic Expedition Team.

To prevent them from forgetting, so they can view incomplete commissions at any time, the Phone Rotom has stored all commissions submitted to the Heroic Expedition Team and tasks into the memo.

*

Near noon, D picked a basket of red apples from the Apple Garden and brought them home to make delicious apple pie and caramelized apples.

[Apple Pie (C): A delicacy made with apples, a traditional dessert from some places, Cooking Effect, increases friendliness of sweet-loving Pokemon.]

[Caramelized Apples (C+): A snack made with apples and sugar, Cooking Effect, Silk Thread enhancement lv1, enhances the toughness and resilience of thread-type moves, making them difficult to escape.]

The effect of apple pie is quite ordinary, and the effect of caramelized apples is only effective for Butterfree.

D simply glanced at it and didn't continue to pay attention to its effects, planning to eat them as regular snacks.

Besides these two desserts, D also made some meat dishes.

After all, according to his understanding, Koraidon and Cyclizar are not too interested in such sweets.

Compared to apple pie, they prefer roasted meat that is golden brown, sizzling with oil, crispy on the outside, tender on the inside, and full of meat juice.

---

Sure enough, during lunch, the two Pokémon took one look and turned away, while Alcremie, Ogerpon, and Pawmot loved the apple pie and candied apples.

Meanwhile, Koraidon and Cyclizar were focused on burying their heads in their troughs, gobbling up the sandwiches and roast meat.

Seeing them with such appetites, a smile appeared on D's face.

"You guys eat first, I'll take some candied apples to Butterfree," he said.

The Pokémon nodded, and D got up to leave the dining area, found Butterfree in the garden, and fed it the candied apples.

Butterfree usually enjoys sweet pollen and nectar, so it also took quite a liking to these syrup-made candied apples.

"Fwee~" After eating, Butterfree let out a happy growl.

D laughed and said, "This dish can make your Bug Silk tougher, do you want to give it a try?"

"Fwee?" Butterfree looked surprised.

It fluttered its wings and spit out a streak of milky white silk from its mouth.

D reached out his hand, and the Bug Silk wound around it.

He tried to pull the silk with great force but couldn't break it.

"It really has become sturdier." Looking at the silk in his hand, D thought it must be a great material for clothing.

He remembered the old lady at "The Slugma-Powered Home" used Tarountula Bug Silk to knit sweaters.

It's just that D hadn't yet mastered that skill.

*

Perhaps Lono's positive feedback had an effect; that afternoon, D Ranch's small shop finally welcomed a real customer.

When D received the notification from his Rotom Phone, he thought it was Lono buying again.

However, when he checked, he saw it was a customer from Mesagoza City.

Meanwhile, in a dormitory of Paldea Academy in Mesagoza City.

A man, about twenty years old, was browsing the Pokémon shopping app on his Rotom Phone.

The man's name was Aobara, and he was planning to buy a crate of MooMoo Fresh Milk for his dorm room from the app.

Although there were places selling MooMoo Fresh Milk in Mesagoza City, for a lazy homebody, it was just too much trouble.

In contrast, the convenience of ordering from the Pokémon shopping app stood out; after payment, a Pokémon would deliver it directly to your doorstep.

At this moment, Aobara suddenly discovered a very simple little shop.

"Huh? The shop's name is D Ranch, is it opened by a rancher?"

The name D seemed familiar to him, he couldn't quite remember where he had heard it before.

Aobara tried hard to recall, but humans tend to forget those specific things no matter how much they try.

Helpless, he had to give up on recalling and clicked open the shop's interface with his finger.

Looking at the dazzling array of goods, Aobara was a bit surprised.

Quite a lot!

From vegetables grown in fields, to fruits from fruit trees, and even milk produced by Miltank, all are available.

Not only that but there were also some very strange items for sale.

"Life Sheep Milk..." Aobara read the description word by word: "Special milk, produced by Gogoat raised on the ranch, possessing miraculous effects. Severely injured and dying Pokémon can immediately regain health by drinking it. Recommended to drink in emergencies, with a limit of two bottles per week per person..."

Reading this introduction, Aobara's first reaction was how exaggerated!

How could such sheep milk exist in this world that could instantly heal severely injured and dying Pokémon?

This shop really talks big!

However, when Aobara saw the price of this Life Sheep Milk, he was even more shocked.

My goodness! A single bottle costs thirty thousand League Coins, that's expensive!

You have to know a bottle of MooMoo Milk sells for just six hundred League Coins.

One bottle of this sheep milk can buy fifty bottles of MooMoo Milk.

As a poor student, Aobara couldn't afford such an expensive item, so he glanced at the milk prices in the store and found they were still at market price.

So, Aobara ordered a crate of MooMoo Milk.

But when he saw the delivery method supported by the shop, he was dumbfounded.

"Dragon, Dragonite Delivery?"

Does that mean the ranch is using Dragonite Pokémon for delivery?

Isn't this an overqualified use?

Dragonite, a Dragon Type Pokémon, is incredibly powerful, extremely rare in the wild, and a very scarce Pokémon.

Yet now, this shop uses such a rare Pokémon for delivery?

Aobara couldn't help but gasp, and to witness it for himself, he placed the order.

The delivery fee for Dragonite Delivery was a thousand League Coins, still within his acceptable range.

After confirming the order, Aobara expected to wait a day or two for the delivery.

However, ten minutes later, someone knocked on his window.

Aobara was startled; he lived on the fifth floor, where it was impossible for anyone to knock on the window.

Trembling, he pulled back the curtains, and saw a large orange Dragonite looking at him with big, damp eyes.

The Dragonite looked very gentle and cute, carrying a small red satchel, accompanied by a Phone Rotom.

"Dra, Dragonite!" Aobara exclaimed.

Rotom's voice came from the phone, "Dear customer, your order of MooMoo Milk has arrived, please open the window to sign, Roto!"

Only then did Aobara react. Overcome with shock, he quickly went forward and opened the window.

The Dragonite squeezed in and took out the MooMoo Milk he ordered from the satchel, placing it on the floor.

After finishing everything, Dragonite let out a howl, then turned around and flew away with Rotom.

Leaving Aobara in the room, still unable to recover from the shock.

Dragonite! It really was a Dragonite!

What kind of person is this shop's owner, to use such a rare Pokémon for delivery!

D... D Ranch...

At this moment, Aobara suddenly recalled a post he'd seen on the campus forum!

He quickly opened his Rotom Phone, logged into the campus forum, and found that post.

[Campus Legend of the Hidden Boss——Every Trainer in the Paldea Region must understand that even after challenging all Gyms and acquiring all badges, and winning the Champion battle in the Paldea League, your journey does not end.

It's said that in some place in the Paldea Region, there is a mysterious and incredibly powerful Trainer. Only by challenging and defeating this person can you become the strongest Trainer in Paldea.]

Aobara continued reading.

The original poster wrote in a follow-up comment——

[Exploring the Three Big Trials of D Ranch! This post gathers experiences from most challengers, offering guidance to help you better pass those trials.

First, the Grass Trial; during this time, D will have Gogoat take the field for battle. The challenger must defeat three Gogoat to pass.

The strength of these three Gogoat is already at Champion Level, and their mastery of Grass Energy is at an extraordinary level. They can manipulate Grass Energy, making Grassy Terrain benefit only them.

Facing them, a war of attrition is undoubtedly unwise, as far as I know, no challenger's Pokémon outlast them.

At such times, the best strategy is to utilize Type Matchups, deploying Flying, Fire, Ice, Bug, or Poison Type Pokémon to battle, allowing the Pokémon to unleash its strongest attacks to end the battle within two moves.

If you can't finish it, you'll have to face Gogoat's endless recoveries and its special move "Grass Sacred Sword".

...]

*

At night, after D bathed Koraidon and prepared to rest, he received a message from Raifort.

It was a link.

D clicked it open and saw "Campus Legend of the Paldea Hidden Boss" written in bold letters.

He raised his eyebrows in surprise, then began to read carefully.

This post seemed to be from a Trainer who came to the ranch for the trials two years ago.

The writer detailed the rules and contents of the Grass Trial and even created a specific strategy guide.

It wasn't just the Grass Trial but also Pawmot's Thunder Trial and Dragonite's Storm Trial.

D scanned through it up and down.

Hmm, very detailed, seems like the person put a lot of effort into it.

The only thing that made D speechless was the students actually referred to him as the Paldea Region's Hidden Boss...

---

---

---

---

# 421: Chapter 418: Legendary Beast Chat Group Now Serializing!

Speaking of hidden bosses in Pokemon games, we have to mention the hidden boss from HeartGold and SoulSilver, Red.

In the game, after collecting all the badges and challenging the Champion, players can go to the top of Mt. Silver to challenge this trainer with a legendary life.

He started his journey at the age of ten and became a Champion in less than a year, single-handedly facing Team Rocket and defeating their boss, Giovanni.

Afterwards, Red resigned from his role as Champion of the League, and went alone to the vast Mt. Silver to train Pokemon, disappearing from the public eye until players in the game reached the top of Mt. Silver to meet him.

D remembers, at that time all six of Red's Pokemon had surpassed level 80, with his strongest, Pikachu, reaching level 88, just 12 levels short of the max level.

Such a powerful trainer was truly a hidden boss in the game back then.

Thinking of this, D turned to look at Koraidon sleeping at the foot of the bed, and thought of Pawmo and Dragonite.

If calculated based on the game's levels, what level would his Pokemon be?

Calling him a hidden boss or something, that sounds too exaggerated.

But thinking of those "mutated" Pokemon on the ranch, D suddenly felt it might not be so exaggerated after all.

"..."

Well, he barely counts as half, I guess!

Just then, a message came through from Raifort on the screen.

[Raifort: Did you see it? You're really famous at our academy, I've heard trainers are planning to team up and challenge you!]

[D: ...]

Is this like teaming up to fight a boss in an online game?

[D: By the way, is there any progress in your research?]

[Raifort: So far, everything's going smoothly. I've found how to break the seals and pinpointed the approximate locations. However, there might be some discrepancies due to an earthquake or other reasons. But I believe it'll be done soon!]

[Raifort: At that time, I'll need your help again.]

[D: Okay.]

Even if he can't capture Chi-Yu or Ting-Lu, just going to see them and taking some photos would be great.

After ending the communication with Raifort, D casually logged into the Pokemon Forum and discovered that Giratina's fan count had exceeded six digits, reaching a hundred thousand.

In this world, Legendary Pokemon are already extremely rare, and they're often symbols of immense power.

In cities, on fields, many people are in pursuit of their tracks, but except for a few lucky ones, most chase their whole lives and never see even one, passing on with regrets.

And as a Legendary Pokemon that can use a Rotom Phone and go online, Giratina appearing at this time easily attracts attention.

It's clear that these netizens have a strong curiosity about Legendary Pokemon.

It's just... Giratina's popularity is really too exaggerated!

Seeing that number exceed 150,000, D was very envious.

While his Calyrex cultural promotion account only had over two hundred followers.

If only Giratina's followers could come to Calyrex!

No, I can't just waste Giratina's popularity like this!

So, early the next morning, D found Calyrex, who was playing on the phone, to help it register an account on the Pokemon Forum.

Then, D had Phone Rotom take a few pictures of Calyrex having afternoon tea in the garden, and uploaded them to the forum.

Facing D's actions, Calyrex was a bit puzzled: "This is...?"

D gave a mysterious smile: "You'll know soon enough."

Saying this, he turned and went to the Reversed World, finding Giratina surfing, and asked for its help in sharing Calyrex's post.

Giratina knew Calyrex because that Pokemon had once come to the Reversed World with D to help plant flowers.

"Rotom, help Calyrex to share it!" Giratina commanded in a rough voice.

"Got it, Roto!"

Phone Rotom shivered slightly and quickly handled Giratina's account to share Calyrex's post.

Before confirming to send, Rotom asked: "What content should be added, Roto?"

Giratina looked at D.

D thought carefully and said, "Just put [Hello everyone, this is my friend, Calyrex]."

"Alright, Roto!"

In the next second, the post was successfully shared.

Giratina on the side was a bit puzzled: "When did I become friends with Calyrex?"

"Whenever you want, it can happen." Saying this, D opened Poke Communications, selected Giratina and Calyrex, and created a group chat.

[The greatest Giratina in the world has joined the group chat!]

[The Farmer has joined the group chat!]

"That's fine, now you can talk directly in the group whenever there's something!" D said with a smile: "Calyrex, like you, is also a Legendary Pokemon, but its ability is to enrich the earth, I think you've already seen it."

Giratina recalled the past, nodding its big head.

D continued: "And, Calyrex's power is limitless, the more people know about it, the more power it will bring.

At that time, maybe it could directly change the natural environment of the destroyed world, making every Sky Island lush with greenery and life."

Hearing this, Giratina's eyes widened in shock: "It can do that kind of thing?"

---

"Maybe?" D chuckled, "So, you should understand why I'm asking for your help today, right."

Giratina understood instantly, as it already had many human fans.

At this moment, if Calyrex appeared, it would certainly attract many humans and become even more powerful.

Understanding this, Giratina grinned, "Then you owe me a favor this time."

D was a bit surprised, "You actually understand owing favors?"

Giratina snorted, "I am Giratina!"

Seeing this, D realized Giratina learned these things from surfing the internet.

"Alright! Then I'll use Dragon Power to give you a massage later." He said.

"A hundred thousand times!" Giratina negotiated.

Only such a grand number would be worthy of Giratina!

D: "..."

He thought to himself you might as well drain me dry, Dragon Power is limited, and after using it once, it takes a whole night to recover.

This period's Dragon Power was all used to raise Cyclizar, how could there be extra for Giratina's massage?

"Three times!" D said.

"No way, ninety thousand times!"

"Five times!"

"Fifty thousand times!"

"Fifteen at most, you're already so powerful, Dragon Power won't provide you with much help, there are many Dragon-type Pokemon in the ranch that need my help!"

Seeing D so resolute, Giratina could only say, "Alright, fifteen times it is, don't forget!"

*

A stone stirs up a thousand waves.

Accompanied by Giratina's repost, it immediately caused a sensation online.

Calyrex? Giratina's friend? Another Legendary Pokemon?

A large group of legend enthusiasts flocked to Calyrex's personal page.

Looking at the mysterious Pokemon elegantly drinking tea in the photo, these netizens all expressed doubt.

Such a coincidence quickly aroused suspicion from many.

Another internet-savvy Legendary Pokemon?

Could it be someone secretly manipulating it for traffic?

Netizens began questioning Calyrex's identity, and Giratina was also affected.

Facing human skepticism, Giratina was furious and immediately posted its second photo to prove its identity.

This time, people no longer doubted Giratina.

Because its environment and the background of the photo looked nothing like any place on this planet.

And according to the legends, whether the God of Time or the God of Space, both exist in Alternate Spaces.

As Giratina was born alongside them, it must be the same.

But Calyrex was different.

Gradually, as the skepticism grew louder, some netizens who claimed to have seen Calyrex appeared online.

They worked hard to introduce Calyrex's legend and deeds to those unfamiliar with it.

[Cherry Wine: Don't let anyone doubt The King of Bountiful Harvests!]

[Tao Tao: That's right! Calyrex is a Pokemon from Porto Marinada Town. A few years ago, a wildfire occurred on the nearby mountain, and it was Calyrex who came forward to help restore the ecosystem there!]

[Don: Correct, I previously bought Calyrex memorabilia from Porto Marinada Town, and the shopkeeper told me that Calyrex is a great Pokemon that can bring bounty to its believers, making crops instantly prosper and soil fertile!]

[Most Fond of Floette: It's true! I traveled to Porto Marinada Town last autumn and attended the local harvest festival, at that time Calyrex appeared, I have photos, check them out!]

Under this series of proofs, the skeptical voices gradually disappeared.

Looking at the people calling themselves followers of The King of Bountiful Harvests, Calyrex responded:

[Those days are in the past, now I am just a farmer.]

And it changed its personal page name to Farmer.

Compared to Giratina, who disdains human interaction and is ill-tempered, Calyrex is much gentler.

It occasionally responds to some netizens' questions.

For example, if someone asks where Calyrex lives now, Calyrex would reply: I live in a very beautiful place now.

Someone else might ask how Calyrex uses the Rotom Phone.

Calyrex would reply: A friend taught me, and the Little Rotom in the phone helps me use it better. When I encounter words I don't know, Little Rotom translates and explains them to me.

Now, netizens finally understand how these Legendary Pokemon use the Rotom Phone.

In this situation, Calyrex's fan count skyrocketed, more and more people became aware of Calyrex.

The growth speed even hinted at surpassing Giratina.

The reason being none other than The King of Bountiful Harvests is truly great and deserves human respect!

Meanwhile, in Pallet Town.

Professor Oak excitedly gazed at the photo on the Rotom Phone screen.

In the photo, a Pokemon with a very large head was sitting gracefully in the garden center, elegantly sipping a cup of tea.

Professor Oak could hardly believe it!

Within these short days, another legendary Pokemon appeared!

"Calyrex... The King of Bountiful Harvests..." Professor Oak flipped through the netizens' introductions on the screen and agreed, "It deserves that title!"

---

Professor Oak simply could not contain his excitement.

His hands trembled as he opened the private chat with Calyrex, then sent a message to the other side.

[Professor Oak: Hello, Calyrex! I am Professor Samuel Oak, a scholar among humans specializing in the study of Pokemon. I would love to make friends with you, do you have time? Would you be able to come to Professor Oak's Research Institute in Pallet Town, Kanto Region? I'll serve you the finest red tea when you arrive!]

The message sent, Professor Oak looked expectant.

The message he sent to Giratina earlier was like a stone sinking into the sea; many days had passed without a reply.

This made him uncertain if Calyrex would be like Giratina.

Professor Oak took advantage of this time to prepare a cup of instant noodles.

As soon as he returned to the laboratory, he heard notification sounds from his Rotom Phone.

Professor Oak:!!!

He hastily set down the noodles and opened his phone, only to indeed see Calyrex's reply.

[Farmer: Professor Oak, I have heard of you. I apologize, but I cannot visit your Institute in Pallet Town.]

"Huh?"

Professor Oak felt immediately disappointed.

Yet the next second, he received another message.

[Farmer: However, though I cannot come, you are welcome to visit me. My friend is also eagerly awaiting your arrival.]

Reading this message left Professor Oak completely bewildered.

Calyrex's friend? Could it be Giratina?

No, impossible, Giratina didn't know him, not even responding to his message; certainly not anticipating his visit.

Who could it be then?

Was it the human who taught Calyrex how to use the Rotom Phone?

If that's true, then it must also be who taught Giratina to use the Rotom Phone.

Could Giratina and Calyrex be Pokemon that person captured?

Professor Oak couldn't imagine what kind of Trainer could capture a Legendary Pokemon like Giratina, living in another dimension.

If the person really is human, then that guy surely isn't ordinary!

Gazing at the message on the screen, Professor Oak turned to his assistant and said, "Help me pack my bags, I'm heading to the Paldea Region!"

On February 24th in the year 200 of the League, Professor Oak, filled with anticipation, boarded an airplane bound for the other side of the Earth.

Paldea Region, Porto Marinada Town, D Ranch.

Calyrex looked at the Rotom Phone in front of him and asked, "Is this enough?"

D nodded, "That's right, Professor Oak is the most well-known professor in the Pokemon World. If you receive his help, then your name could spread to every corner of this world."

Feeling the continuous surge of power within, a gentle smile appeared on Calyrex's face:

"Actually, I'm already quite content as it is. Such a thing was unimaginable for me a few years ago."

"But now we've achieved it." D's eyes burned with determination.

One day, he will make Calyrex's name renowned in every corner of this world!

Meanwhile, in the Reversed World.

Rotom dashed frantically to report to Giratina: "Reporting Rotom! Calyrex's fans have already surpassed yours, Rotom!"

Giratina suddenly opened his blood-red eyes.

How could this be? As the strongest Legendary Pokemon, it should have the most fans! Only then would it match its grand identity!

"Rotom, keep taking pictures!"

Giratina began its operation.

# 422: Chapter 419: Inside the World Tree, Future Plans

After arranging matters on the Pokemon Forum, next will be waiting for the visit from Professor Oak.

As the number of netizens on the Pokemon Forum increased, the faith they provided to Calyrex also grew.

Although there didn't seem to be any change on the surface, D could feel the Power of Faith within Calyrex becoming increasingly vast.

Should Calyrex unleash its full force right now, D has a premonition that even Giratina might fall beneath its Glacial Lance.

After all, that's the faith of over a hundred thousand humans!

Currently, Calyrex's power has far surpassed its peak, and combined with Spectrier and Glastrier, it likely has become the strongest Pokemon at D Ranch.

Moreover, this sudden surge of faith directly replenished all the power Calyrex had expended when nurturing the World Tree last time.

So that afternoon, D took the Pokemon at the ranch along with Calyrex to the base of the World Tree.

In the ancient and ruined void, the towering and majestic World Tree soared to the sky, its immense canopy lush and expansive, the glimmering leaves blocked out the sky continually.

The trunk of the World Tree below had grown almost as large as half the ranch, surrounded by the glow of Sunflowers; the river flowing from Sky Island continually irrigated the World Tree, making it thrive.

Looking at this brand new giant tree, if D hadn't personally witnessed it, he would find it hard to imagine it grew from such a tiny seed.

At this moment, standing beneath the World Tree, D felt so insignificant.

Even the relatively large Koraidon seemed negligible in its presence.

No wonder it's the legendary tree that supports the world!

D gazed at the World Tree, and its information was fully revealed before him.

[World Tree (Mature Stage): A seedling that absorbed a large amount of Power of Grass, mutated during growth due to Gogoat's influence, with boundless Life Force brewing within, eventually emerging from the ground, said to support a world and nurture all living things with Natural Energy, requiring 48054 years to fully mature.]

According to Calyrex's original ability, it would take ten more months for this World Tree to fully mature, around the end of this year.

But now that Calyrex absorbed so much faith, D believes this timeframe will definitely shorten further.

"Begin, Calyrex!" D anticipated, curious to see what elevation the faith of one hundred thousand netizens would bring Calyrex.

"Alright."

Calyrex nodded slightly.

In the next moment, watched by Koraidon, Alcremie, Gogoat, Pawmot, and other Pokemon, Calyrex slowly extended its hands.

A gentle blue light enveloped Calyrex's entire body.

Then, the light transformed into a galaxy of pure energy, slowly flowing towards the World Tree, eventually becoming a tether connecting Calyrex and the World Tree.

As Calyrex injected power into the World Tree, a miraculous scene unfolded.

Energy ripples began spreading continuously on the trunk and canopy, resonating with a strange humming sound.

"Shaa shaa～!"

The tree's shadow swayed, the leaves rustled gently, producing a soft sound.

In the eyes of D and other Pokemon, a hole suddenly appeared on the gnarled trunk of the World Tree in front of them.

Moreover, D noticed the original trunk began gradually transitioning into another form!

This form somewhat resembled rock layers and crystals, yet still maintained the appearance of a tree.

What's going on?

After Calyrex's nurturing ended, D stepped forward, gently touching the enormous roots protruding from the ground.

"Ah gaa ss?"

Koraidon remarked in confusion, stepping forward similarly, lowering its head to inspect the roots, yet found nothing.

D fell into contemplation.

He recalled information about another World Tree in this world.

The Tree of Beginning, located in the mountains of Aurandru City in the Kanto Region, appeared tree-like but consisted of rock layers, a crystal life form capable of relying on light to supplement energy and nutrients.

Could it be that the World Tree he grew might eventually transform into that form, relying on sunlight for energy?

That would be great!

This way, even if this World Tree flies up to the sky later, there would be no worry about it withering due to lack of nutrients from the soil.

D also noticed that the description of this World Tree had changed.

[World Tree (Mature Stage): The thriving World Tree, nearing the end of its growth, transforming into another life form, constructing an internal environment, a miraculous tree, said to support a world and nurture all living things with Natural Energy, requiring 38198 years to fully mature.]

Seeing this information, D thought internally, as expected.

He glanced at the hole on the trunk, not in a hurry to explore inside, but calculating how many years the maturation period of the World Tree had shortened this time.

48054-38198=9856

"9856 years?" D murmured, "Almost double the previous duration."

---

D remembers that at the beginning, Calyrex could shorten the World Tree's growth cycle by 2,000 years at a time.

Later on, as people from Porto Marinada Town and tourists from afar heard about the legend of Calyrex, its powers increased, allowing it to shorten the growth cycle by over 4,000 years at once.

And now, the number Calyrex can shorten has doubled again, reaching nearly 10,000 years.

Standing beside him, Calyrex looked weary, but his expression was very gentle: "This is all the power I have at the moment."

Calyrex lifted his head, gazing up at the towering and majestic giant tree, feeling immensely honored and filled with anticipation.

"Do you want to take a break?" D asked.

Calyrex shook his head: "No need to rest, like you, I also want to personally enter the World Tree and take a look."

Seeing this, D didn't say much, he simply said: "Then let's go inside together!"

A few small Eevees clung to him, curiously peering at the entrance of the tree.

D, holding a pink Dratini, stepped into the entrance.

Koraidon, Cyclizar, Pawmot, Gogoats, Alcremie, Ogerpon, Flutter Mane, Gholdengo, Farfetch'd, Butterfree, the trio of Vaporeon, Jolteon, and Flareon, along with Glaceon and Umbreon followed behind.

Through the entrance, a winding and intricate passageway unfolded before D.

The passage was long and highly complex, seemingly designed to confuse intruders.

D walked for a long time, only to find himself lost.

He looked at the dead end in front of him and, feeling helpless, had to ask Calyrex to use Telekinesis to help find the way.

With the help of Calyrex, they finally found the correct path, reaching the center of the World Tree.

There, a special upward passage like an elevator awaited them.

A pale green ring of light connected to the top of the World Tree, with strange bubbles continuously rising within, seemingly allowing one to reach the top only by riding them.

D reached out and lightly touched the ring of light, and discovered that his hand passed through it.

The small Eevees hanging onto him were also curious, crowding around, jumping to the ground, and gently sniffing the aroma emanating from the light ring with their noses.

Suddenly, one of the small Eevees was accidentally bumped by its companion, sending it tumbling directly into the light ring.

"Eevee!"

The Eevee was startled and hurriedly tried to call for help, but in the next second, a transparent bubble enveloped it, lifting it towards the sky.

D, caught off guard, saw the Eevee fly away right before his eyes.

"Kora!"

The clever Koraidon pointed at the bubble, indicating that they could also ride the bubbles to catch up.

D noticed the next ascending bubble and, without saying much, nodded and led a group of Pokemon into the light ring.

After all, this is the World Tree, there couldn't possibly be any danger inside.

In the next second, several bubbles automatically rose, enveloping D and the Pawmo inside, carrying them upwards.

Along the way, D glimpsed the second layer, the third layer, the fourth layer, the fifth layer... through the pale green glow.

This left D utterly amazed.

Was the internal space of the World Tree really this vast?!

The bubble elevator ascended rapidly, and before D could figure out how many layers there were, it reached the top of the tree.

With a pop, the bubble burst, releasing D and Koraidon into a vibrant area.

As D and the Pokemon took in the view before them, they were utterly stunned.

The soil was fertile, the grass lush, and in the depths of the forest, a tranquil lake lay surrounded by mist, like an ethereal fairyland. The shores were lined with trees draped with hanging green vines and Shaman Moss.

Brightly colored flowers bloomed on the grassy shore, and a rich natural aroma filled the air, bringing a sense of relaxation and happiness.

This was the top of the World Tree, and through the dense canopy, D saw the black void of the Reversed World.

In the real world, the sky above would certainly be a clear blue or an expanse of star-filled night.
