What a beautiful scene it would be!

"Pawmot…" Pawmot mumbled.

Even without eating the Translation Nuts Cake, D could understand what it meant without any translation.

Pawmot was saying: "So beautiful..."

"Yes indeed!"

D saw the Eevee that had been carried away by the bubble standing on the grass, staring at the scene in awe.

He was in no hurry and decided to sit down on the soft grass, watching his Pokemon play and frolic in this place.

Gogoats and Tropius stood on the grass with bright eyes, occasionally bending their heads to munch the lush grass.

The Eevees ran back and forth on the grass, starting a game of hide and seek.

Vaporeon seemed very interested in the lake; it walked over, gazed at its reflection within, and then leaped in, swimming happily.

Arboliva appeared to love this place, standing tall on the grass, basking in the rich Natural Energy, and contentedly closing its eyes.

Pawmot flew around the forest, continuously exploring this place.

---

Koraidon and Cyclizar stood on either side of D, looking at everyone with him.

Calyrex slowly levitated nearby.

D said to him, "Once the World Tree is fully grown, you can come and live here. By then, I plan to transform this place into a paradise for Pokemon!"

While ascending earlier, D noticed that the World Tree has many layers inside, each with a different environment.

Some are grasslands, some forests, some valleys and caves.

Grasslands and forests are suitable for Grass Type Pokemon to dwell in, while valleys and caves are suitable for Rock Type and Ground Type Pokemon.

Water Type Pokemon could live in lakes like these.

By then, he would choose a scenic environment to build the Explorer Team Guild, Item Shop, restaurant, tavern, dessert shop, and coffee shop.

And then bring in some Pokemon to work.

For example, Pawmot's parents could serve as shop owners in the item shop; Tropius could provide food in the restaurant; Alcremie could make hot cream in the dessert shop; Rotom Phone could issue commissions at the guild.

Although this world doesn't have mysterious dungeons, commissions could involve planting Berries, cultivating crops, harvesting fields, and so on.

Along with the secret base of the Heroic Expedition Team, all the basic facilities on the World Tree would be complete.

After that, it would be time to capture more types of Pokemon and invite them to live on the World Tree.

Compared to Professor Oak's research lab, the World Tree would be the perfect backyard!

D continuously talked to Calyrex about his plan.

Calyrex listened intently, occasionally murmuring, "I very much look forward to that day."

Nearby, Koraidon nodded his large head happily. Seeing D like this made Koraidon's mood lift as well.

"Aga-sii~"

Koraidon knew that D's plans would all come true in the future.

Suddenly, Koraidon remembered something.

As the World Tree fully grows, it also attracts a particularly powerful character.

That character "forcibly" made D work for him and opened a gateway to other worlds on this great tree, allowing expedition team Pokemon to enter and rescue those worlds and assist Pokemon in need there.

Koraidon didn't want to leave D at the time, so he never went to that world, but he heard from Farfetch'd and the others who ventured there that that world also had expedition teams.

*

At night, D left the World Tree with his Pokemon and returned to the ranch.

Given Calyrex's current power, the World Tree would take only about four more months to fully grow.

While D was looking forward to it, he also had some worries.

Because he previously promised Giratina that when Arceus awakens from his slumber, he would give him a stern lesson.

Only then would Giratina help him lift the World Tree to the sky.

In the afternoon, he did some searching on his phone and, based on last year's Silver Conference contestant list, ultimately pinpointed the current timeline.

This world has Ash, who traveled through the Johto Region last year and participated in the Johto League Silver Conference.

And this year, Ash would set out to travel in the Hoenn Region.

This means that next year, he would reach Sinnoh, and Arceus would awaken at that time and start exacting revenge on humanity.

D had never seen the Pokemon referred to as God and wasn't sure about his strength.

But judging from the movie depiction, even Dialga, Palkia, and Giratina combined weren't a match for him, requiring Ash to time travel and change everything.

"The Plate, ultimately, it's about the Plate that Arceus carries." D thought.

With the Plate, Arceus could be immune to attacks of the corresponding type.

Though Pawmot has already received the King's Power from Calyrex, relying solely on him is still a bit risky!

So the question arises: could he target the Plate instead?

D thought of Teddiursa's ability and his innate luck, and the effect that guarantees escape.

Could he send Teddiursa to "Pickup" the Plate off Arceus?

If he gets it, he could immediately flee, triggering the guaranteed escape effect, which might be able to limit Arceus.

D began to devise a plan in his mind in case it was needed.

Tonight's dinner was prepared with help from his sister Indeedee.

After eating, the Pokemon at the ranch returned to their nests, each entering their dreams one after another.

D went back to his room and started playing on his phone.

However, as he played, he felt a gaze from beside him that was hard to ignore.

D turned his head and saw Koraidon looking at him with plaintive eyes, seemingly feeling neglected and a bit resentful.

D: "..."

He patted the bed, gesturing for Koraidon to come over.

Koraidon obediently walked over.

D petted his head and asked him, "Are you sure you don't want a Rotom Phone? With one, you could watch TV anytime."

"Aga-sii!"

Koraidon shook his large head, indicating he didn't need it; he just wanted to be by D's side.

D was rather helpless: "Alright then."

He wanted to continue playing on his phone, but with Koraidon looking so pitifully beside him, he couldn't focus on playing at all.

In the end, D simply tossed the phone aside and compromised: "Okay, okay, I won't play anymore, let's sleep together, alright?"

"Aga-sii!"

Koraidon immediately moved closer, happily licking with his large tongue.

D let out a sigh of resignation.

His Pokemon were so clingy. If Professor Sada ever learned Koraidon could look so pitiful and resentful, she would surely be surprised.

Wiping off Koraidon's drool from his face, D closed his eyes, starting to sleep alongside him.

---

---

---

---

# 423: Chapter 420: Professor Oak's Visit

League Year 200, February 25th, after a day's flight, the plane carrying Professor Oak finally arrived in the Paldea Region.

The Kanto Airlines plane, full of tourists, landed at Mesagoza City's airport.

Dressed casually, Professor Oak exited the cabin with a look of wonder, taking in the vibrant city.

This was his first time in the Paldea Region!

Mesagoza City's architecture was full of Paldea characteristics, and in the city center stood an ancient academy with a long history.

That was Paldea Academy.

Before arriving here, Professor Oak did his homework and gained a general understanding of the current situation in the Paldea Region.

Leaving the airport, he excitedly observed the unfamiliar Pokemon accompanying pedestrians on the street.

A grass cat Pokemon with light green fur, a hammer Pokemon with a pink body holding a small hammer, and a lizard Pokemon with tire-shaped airbags in front.

Wow! All these Pokemon aren't found in the Kanto Region!

Professor Oak took out his Rotom Phone to scan those Pokemon.

It's so convenient to have a Rotom Phone now! Back in the day, they had to hand-draw the Pokedex!

Now, you just need to scan with a phone to access the ecological data of Pokemon.

Rotom's mechanical electronic voice came from the phone.

"Sprigatito, grass cat Pokemon, its fluffy fur has components similar to plants, and it diligently washes its face to prevent dryness!"

"Tinkatuff, hammer Pokemon, attacks Bisharp groups to collect metal for making big and sturdy hammers!"

"Cyclizar, mount Pokemon, seems to have allowed humans to ride on its back since ancient times, as depicted in murals from ten thousand years ago!"

"Indeed, these are unique Pokemon to the Paldea Region!"

Having never visited the Paldea Region before, Professor Oak was deeply curious about the Pokemon species and ecology here.

Should he ask Ash to travel here, help collect the Pokedex, and capture a few Paldea Pokemon to send back to the research lab?

But Ash is currently traveling in the Hoenn Region...

"Oh well, let's wait until Ash finishes his trip in the Hoenn Region first!" Professor Oak thought to himself.

After all, the Kanto Region and the Paldea Region are located on opposite ends of this planet, with other regions in between, so a gradual journey would be better for Ash.

"Ah! I almost forgot the main reason for coming here!" Lost in thought by the Pokemon, Professor Oak slapped his forehead.

He looked down at his Rotom Phone to find a way to get from Mesagoza City to Porto Marinada Town.

Soon, Professor Oak found the answer—a Squawkabilly Flying Taxi.

Five minutes later, Professor Oak boarded the flying taxi powered by three Squawkabillies.

The Squawkabillies were tied with ropes, which connected to the flying taxi.

The taxi seats had two rows, the front for the driver, the back left for passengers.

"This mode of flying is truly unique!" Professor Oak mused.

No such transportation tool exists in the Kanto Region.

The driver in front chuckled and said, "This is your first time in the Paldea Region, isn't it?"

Professor Oak nodded, "That's right!"

"No wonder!" The driver introduced, "Squawkabilly Flying Taxi is a unique transportation tool in the Paldea Region. Not only us, but the neighboring Galar Region also uses this method! However, their driver Pokemon isn't Squawkabilly, but a Pokemon called Corviknight."

Corviknight? Professor Oak immediately thought of that large, dark-colored bird Pokemon with steel-like feathers he had seen in the city.

Professor Oak: "I noticed the Paldea Region also has Corviknight residing here. Why don't you employ them for the work?"

Considering their appearance, Corviknight seems more suited for the job, both in size and speed.

However, hearing this, the driver showed a troubled expression, "It's not that we don't want to employ Corviknight, but we can't."

"Oh?"

"Because in the Paldea Region, there's a Pokemon called Tinkaton with high intelligence. If it sees Corviknight in the sky, it will immediately attack them with a hammer."

Upon hearing this, two words popped into Professor Oak's mind: natural enemy!

Tinkaton is the natural enemy of Corviknight, just like the relationship between Caterpie and Pidgeotto!

"I see!"

No wonder the Paldea Region doesn't use Corviknight to pull flying taxis.

The driver chuckled and then focused on driving the flying taxi.

After a day of flying, Professor Oak felt a bit tired, so he leaned back in his seat and napped for a while.

When he opened his eyes again, the Squawkabilly Flying Taxi had already reached Porto Marinada Town.

He got off the taxi and walked on the street in Porto Marinada Town, taking out his phone to contact Calyrex in private chat.

Although Professor Oak wanted to go directly, unfortunately, Calyrex hadn't provided a detailed address, only mentioning Porto Marinada Town.

The message was sent, and soon a reply came from that side.

---

[Rancher: Please wait a moment.]

Professor Oak stood there waiting.

After about two minutes, a plump Pokémon with a big belly landed in front of him.

It was Dragonite!

Professor Oak was slightly surprised.

That Dragonite stared at him for a moment, then turned around and called to him.

"Awuu!"

Professor Oak was slightly taken aback, then pointed to himself and asked, "Do you want me to get on?"

"Awuu!" Dragonite nodded.

"Did your trainer send you to pick me up?" Professor Oak asked again.

Dragonite still nodded.

Seeing this, Professor Oak understood.

He said nothing more and promptly mounted Dragonite's back.

Dragonite flapped its wings and flew into the sky, heading towards the outskirts of the town.

Very soon, a ranch appeared in the fields ahead.

Dragonite landed in the ranch, and as soon as Professor Oak dismounted, he saw a familiar figure.

D, who had been waiting there, smiled and greeted him, "Long time no see, Professor Oak!"

Professor Oak was wide-eyed in disbelief.

"It's you!"

Last year at the Unova banquet, this trainer had attended with Unova Champion Alder; not only that, he had defeated the renowned Dragon Envoy Lance from the Kanto region...

Because of this, Professor Oak had a very deep impression of this trainer.

D smiled and nodded, "It's me. Hello, Professor Oak, let me formally introduce myself! I am D, the rancher of this farm, welcome to the Paldea Region."

*

At first, Professor Oak did not think there was anything special about being a rancher.

Upon D's invitation, he was ready to go to the living room to drink some tea and rest.

Along the way, Professor Oak saw a variety of Pokémon.

Various crops grew in neat rows.

And beside the fields, a perfectly bred Dragonite was using Rain Dance.

Under Dragonite's control, a rain cloud formed above the farmland, and a drizzle began to water the fields below.

"That's my Dragonite; it's currently working as the watering Pokémon on the farm," D noticed Professor Oak's gaze and introduced it to him.

Professor Oak nodded, and then he saw a large flock of black Pokémon flying overhead.

Looking up, Professor Oak saw that all those Pokémon were Dragonite, each carrying satchels!

"Those Dragonite are...?!" Professor Oak exclaimed, amazed at so many Dragonite!

He had once visited the Land of Dragons, but even there, in the Dragon's Holy Sanctuary, there weren't as many Dragonite!

D glanced up and said, "Oh, those! They are couriers from the ranch. I opened an online store a few days ago, and when customers order goods from the store, the Dragonite set out to deliver the goods to the customer."

"???"

At this moment, Professor Oak finally realized something was off.

Catching so many Dragonite and even seemingly capturing the legendary Pokémon Calyrex, this person claims to be a rancher?

Shouldn't he be a champion-caliber trainer?

In decades of life, Professor Oak had never seen such a rancher.

Not even Whitney, who opened MooMoo Ranch in Goldenrod City, was like this.

Could it be retirement?

Professor Oak knew that some trainers chose to retire in adulthood and enter other professions.

That must be it!

"You still look young, why retire so early?" Professor Oak asked confusingly.

"Retired?" D didn't react for a moment.

But soon, he understood what Professor Oak meant.

—Professor Oak thought he was a champion who retired to run a ranch.

D had encountered this many times before, and now he didn't want to explain too much, so he said:

"Compared to Pokémon battles, I'd rather live a simple life with my Pokémon here."

"I see!" Professor Oak looked at those Pokémon and said with a smile, "You must really like them."

He noticed that these Pokémon looked very spirited and were obviously well cared for by D.

"Indeed!" D smiled.

The two continued walking, and Professor Oak's attention was drawn to other Pokémon on the ranch.

"Koraidon, Cyclizar, Pawmo, Alcremie, Ogerpon, Flutter Mane, I remember them!" Professor Oak said excitedly, as D had sent them out during that previous banquet battle.

D smiled and nodded, then looked at a few Pokémon over there and introduced them to Professor Oak:

"That's Gholdengo, next to Alcremie is Garganacl, the black horse is Spectrier, and the white horse is Glastrier. They are all Calyrex's beloved steeds."

The other Pokémon were playing in Snorlax Park on the other side of the river. D wasn't in a hurry to introduce them, as Professor Oak seemed very curious about these Pokémon and would likely study them later.

D planned to introduce them to Professor Oak when the time came.

Looking at these unfamiliar Pokémon, Professor Oak was so excited he almost fainted.

So many Pokémon! Is this place heaven?

The variety of Pokémon living on this ranch surpassed even those in his research lab!

As a Pokémon ecology researcher, Professor Oak wanted to immediately start observing these Pokémon.

But reason told him there was still a more important task to do.

He hadn't met the legendary Pokémon with the title of The King of Bountiful Harvests that could browse the web using a Rotom Phone.

Seeing Professor Oak's expression, D smiled, and the duo arrived in front of the house.

D pushed open the door, "Please come in!"

"Then I'll intrude!"

Professor Oak walked in, full of excitement, and immediately noticed the Pokémon sitting upright on the sofa.

That is... Calyrex!

Professor Oak was extremely thrilled; he finally saw it!

"I believe I don't need to introduce it?" D said with a smile.

Calyrex also saw Professor Oak at that moment and elegantly nodded slightly, speaking with Telepathy, "Greetings, Professor Oak."

It spoke!

Professor Oak gasped.

But quickly, he realized that Calyrex hadn't actually spoken; it was Telepathy.

"Please have a seat!" D invited Professor Oak to sit on the sofa.

Nearby, Indeedee brought a cup of tea for the guest.

Professor Oak drank it all at once, then looked excitedly at Calyrex.

This wasn't the first time Professor Oak had seen a legendary Pokémon.

Decades ago, he had seen the Pokémon Celebi, said to freely traverse time, in a forest.

But even so, Professor Oak still felt very excited.

After all, this was an extremely rare legendary Pokémon!

"When did you meet Calyrex?" Professor Oak asked.

D thought for a moment and replied, "About the winter before last!"

The year before last was 198, so it had already been two years.

"At that time, Calyrex had lost its power and memory, wandering alone over the vast snowy plains." D continued.

Hearing about Calyrex's past, Professor Oak couldn't help but be very surprised, "How could that be?"

D asked back without answering, "Does Professor Oak know about the Galar Region?"

Galar Region... Professor Oak nodded.

"Calyrex used to live in the Galar Region," D continued, "It was the king there, revered by the people thousands of years ago. The people then built temples for it and offered sacrifices."

Hearing this, Professor Oak was slightly taken aback.

The legendary King of Bountiful Harvests was a king from ancient Galar...

This news was truly astonishing!

But soon after, Professor Oak noticed another sentence D had said earlier.

If Calyrex was so revered at that time, why did it lose its power and memory and become lost on the snowy plains?

Could someone have launched a sneak attack to seize the throne from Calyrex?

---

---

---

---

# 424: Chapter 421: The Legend of the King, Galar Region's Public Opinion Erupts!

Professor Oak's mind was racing with thoughts.

He glanced at Calyrex beside him, then couldn't wait to voice his question.

"Why did Calyrex fall into such a state?"

"That's because many things happened in the Galar Region thousands of years ago," D replied, "I wonder if you've heard of the 'Darkest Day,' Professor."

"The Darkest Day?" Professor Oak was slightly stunned.

D explained differently, "The root of the Dynamax phenomenon."

This time, Professor Oak understood.

Dynamaxing is a unique battle transformation specific to the Galar Region.

In battles, Trainers use the Dynamax Band developed by Professor Magnolia to Dynamax their Pokemon.

Dynamaxed Pokemon become gigantic, and the moves they can use change depending on their types.

Even though the Kanto Region is far from the Galar Region, Professor Oak had heard some rumors from over there.

"Related to the Dynamax?" he asked.

D nodded, continuing, "The story goes back to long, long ago."

"In the ancient times of the Galar Region, there was a place called the Crown Tundra where two Kings of Horses resided, one named Glastrier and the other named Spectrier."

"Glastrier and Spectrier were furious at humans who invaded their territory, constantly attacking humans and their companion Pokemon."

"At that time, Calyrex appeared because of this, defeating both Spectrier and Glastrier and forming deep bonds with them."

"Moreover, given the harsh climate of the Crown Tundra at that time, people's lives were difficult. Calyrex, naturally compassionate, couldn't bear to see humans suffering from hunger and cold, so it waved its hands, granting fertility to the land, allowing crops to flourish and flowers to bloom."

"The people there regarded this as a miracle. They were grateful for Calyrex's kindness and built a shrine for it and the two beloved horses, worshipping them for generations."

"At that time, riding on its beloved horse, Calyrex galloped across the Galar land. Gradually, people began to call it the King of Bountiful Harvests, seeing it as the king of the Galar Region. Under Calyrex's rule, the Galar Region once became prosperous."

Professor Oak listened intently.

Through D's words, he could almost envision the harmony between humans and Calyrex thousands of years ago.

Humans worshipped Calyrex, and Calyrex protected its people, bringing them abundance and relieving them from the suffering of hunger.

Such a thing is truly beautiful.

However, D's tone suddenly changed.

D: "But the good times didn't last. On an ordinary day, Calyrex, through its Future Sight, saw that a disaster would soon befall the Galar land."

"At that time, the Darkest Day would envelop the entire Galar Region. Under the influence of the Darkest Day, countless wild Pokemon would experience Dynamaxing, lose their reason, and commence massive destruction of everything around them."

"To prepare for the impending crisis, Calyrex sought the Pokemon Zacian and Zamazenta, known as the Hundred Battle Heroes."

"With the help of a Melmetal, the people crafted a Sword and Shield for Zacian and Zamazenta using Melmetal's steel. Calyrex bestowed its power upon Zacian and Zamazenta, transforming them into the King of Swords and the King of the Shield."

"After everything was prepared, the Darkest Day descended. Zacian and Zamazenta went to confront the threat, while the exhausted Calyrex used the last of its power to move an entire vast forest, protecting the Pokemon there from the Darkest Day."

"After doing all this, Calyrex fell into slumber, and when it awoke again, thousands of years had passed."

"How could this be…"

Hearing this, Professor Oak had even more respect for Calyrex.

Having done all that, whether for humans or Pokemon, it truly is a magnificent king!

"And what happened afterward?" Professor Oak asked again, already engrossed in the story, eager to know if the disaster had been resolved and the true nature of the Darkest Day.

"Later, Zacian and Zamazenta successfully repelled Eternatus, known as the Darkest Day. After that, not finding Calyrex, the two heroes could only retreat with the Sword and Shield back into the forest to hide until they decayed."

"Eternatus?" Professor Oak suddenly noted the name mentioned, "Is that a Pokemon too?"

D nodded, "You might say so, but it's not a Pokemon from this planet. There are rumors in the Galar Region suggesting that Eternatus fell to the Galar Region from the distant cosmos along with a meteorite."

"From the cosmos…" Professor Oak immediately thought of Pokemon like Clefairy.

But soon, Professor Oak realized something was amiss.

If Eternatus, the source of the Darkest Day, was dealt with, then logically, the phenomenon of Dynamaxing should have disappeared too!

Yet now, why can Dynamaxing still occur in the Galar Region?

Could it be…

Professor Oak's expression turned grave.

D saw his train of thought and nodded, "That's correct, Zacian and Zamazenta didn't completely defeat it, only repelled it. Afterward, Eternatus also fell into slumber; perhaps it might wake up again in the near future."

Hearing this, Professor Oak's face grew increasingly grave.

---

He took out his Rotom Phone and searched online for information about Eternatus and the King of Sword and Shield in the Galar Region.

However, he didn't find any information; he only found two humans known as the King of Swords and the King of Shield.

There was an introduction about them, supposedly the ancient royal family's members of the Galar Region who used their sword and shield to repel the Darkest Day and protect the Galar Region.

Looking at this information, Professor Oak couldn't help but be full of questions.

How is this inconsistent with what D said?

D naturally noticed Professor Oak's intentions.

However, it's impossible to find any information about Eternatus online.

The situation in the Galar Region is quite complicated.

Rose, the president of Macro Cosmos and the chairman of the Galar League, plans to use Eternatus as a power source to provide Dynamax Energy to the entire Galar Region.

Currently, Eternatus is sealed in the underground energy plant in Hammerlocke City.

Chairman Rose's intentions might be good, but his plan was shockingly audacious, and he underestimated Eternatus's power, which led to Eternatus losing control and causing the Darkest Day to shroud Galar Region once more.

Leon was gravely injured trying to stop the disaster, and in the end, it was up to Hop, Zacian, and Zamazenta to save the day and reseal Eternatus.

This plan was extremely dangerous, and naturally, Chairman Rose wouldn't disclose any information about Eternatus; he would instead seal it all away.

As the chairman of the League, it's a simple matter for Rose to do so effortlessly.

Furthermore, as the president of Macro Cosmos, nearly all enterprises in the Galar Region belong to him.

In summary, it's a monopoly.

"Why does the information say the heroes who repelled the Darkest Day were the Galar Royal Family?" Professor Oak asked questioningly.

"Because the influence of Calyrex, Zacian, and Zamazenta threatened the Galar Royal Family's status. After Calyrex fell asleep and Zacian and Zamazenta went into seclusion, they stole the achievements of these heroes, claiming publicly it was their royal family who repelled the Darkest Day," D explained.

"Information dissemination in those times was not developed; there were no phones or TVs. Even those who witnessed the battle involving Zacian and Zamazenta were very few."

"And the belief of humans can empower Calyrex. Their achievements were stolen, and when the elders who knew the truth passed away, the only ones left were the people who were kept in the dark."

"In the end, no one remembers Calyrex, the sleeping Calyrex loses its source of power, unable to awaken for a long time, and even lost its memory."

Upon hearing this, Professor Oak finally understood.

Only then did he grasp the meaning behind D's initial statement that Calyrex wanders alone across the vast snowy plains.

"Those hypocritical royals are truly detestable!" Perhaps out of respect for the heroes, Professor Oak rarely gets angry.

Pokemon shed blood and sweat to protect humans, yet those royals not only failed to thank them, but they also stole their achievements to maintain their own image!

Seeing Professor Oak's reaction, D wasn't surprised.

He smiled slightly and continued: "So, our reason for inviting you, Professor, this time is to ask you for a favor."

"What?" Professor Oak was slightly puzzled.

D said: "Please help log Calyrex's information and legends into the Pokemon Pokedex."

"Sure, no problem, leave it to me!" Professor Oak readily agreed.

As a passionate Pokemon researcher, he would never allow these Pokemon heroes to be wronged!

That afternoon, Professor Oak used D's computer and his own Rotom Phone for remote work.

He contacted Professor Oak's Research Institute located in Pallet Town and communicated with his assistant.

D didn't know what exactly Professor Oak did; he only saw that the next morning, a new Pokemon entry appeared in the Pokedex on the Rotom Phone.

[Calyrex: King Pokémon, possessing the power of healing and blessings, a Pokémon full of love and compassion, once ruled the Galar Region a long time ago.]

Following it was a research paper published by Professor Oak.

This paper was completed overnight by Professor Oak at D Ranch, and the subject of the research was Calyrex, detailing its legends and its relationship with the King of Sword and Shield, asserting that it is a great Pokémon!

Once the paper was completed, Professor Oak used his connections to publish it in a prestigious magazine in the Pokemon Research Realm.

The news sparked waves across the community.

When the surfing netizens saw this paper and the ecological information of Calyrex in the Pokedex, everyone exploded.

Netizens from Kanto, Johto, Hoenn, and Unova expressed their admiration for Calyrex, exclaiming it's truly magnificent!

As a result, Calyrex's fan base began to grow rapidly.

Meanwhile, netizens in the Galar Region noticed one thing.

The Pokedex stated Calyrex was once their Galar Region's king!

What is going on?!

The trainers and ordinary people of the Galar Region quickly erupted.

And when they clearly saw the details in the paper, they immediately understood everything.

[Yamper: Are all these details in the paper true?]

[Eevee Fan: I think there's no way it can be false, right? After all, that person is the legendary Professor Oak!]

---

[Menac's Kiss: Oh my! So you're saying it was Calyrex who prevented the disaster before?]

[Geodude: That's truly amazing! I once traveled to Freezington Village and heard the legend of Calyrex from the village head. I thought they made it up as a way to attract tourists, but it turns out to be true!]

[Wooloo Lover: Shameless Galar Royal Family! They actually stole the hero's achievements!]

[Great Sordward: Fake! It's all fake! Clearly, it was our ancestors who defeated the heroes of the Darkest Day. Zacian and Zamazenta were just Pokemon fighting alongside the heroes!]

[Great Shielbert: Exactly! It's all fake; the Galar Royal Family is the true hero of sword and shield, while Zacian and Zamazenta's true nature will only bring disaster to the Galar Region!]

[Concerned Citizen: Bah! Shameless! With your royal family's power, could you really repel the Darkest Day? Even if your identity is noble, ultimately you're just humans, relying on the power of Zacian and Zamazenta!]

[Great Sordward: Damn it! How outrageous, the dignity of royalty cannot be violated! Just wait, we will find a way to prove the truth!]

[Great Shielbert: I hope you continue to be stubborn then, we will definitely restore history!]

Their tone and replies clearly showed they were members of the Galar Royal Family, and their lofty attitude enraged many netizens, resulting in their replies being flooded with tens of thousands of insults.

The two didn't reply anymore, being berated to the point of uninstalling the Pokemon Forum in anger.

After the netizens had their fill, they finally realized something.

Did Professor Oak meet Calyrex?

Some people quickly went to Professor Oak's personal page and privately messaged him for details.

In response to these questions, Professor Oak mysteriously replied:

I'm very sorry, although I really want to tell everyone, Calyrex told me that he is no longer a king, he's just an ordinary farmer wanting a peaceful life, not wanting too many people to disturb him.

Upon seeing these replies, some netizens were stunned.

Can Calyrex speak?

Professor Oak casually explained to everyone.

[Professor Oak: Telepathy is an ability that Psychic Type Pokemon can use. They can establish telepathy with targets through psychic powers, conveying their meaning directly to the other's mind. Isn't that impressive?]

Although Professor Oak didn't disclose Calyrex's location, soon, news spread among netizens that Professor Oak had departed for the Paldea Region the day before yesterday.

This means Calyrex is now in the Paldea Region!

Some netizens from Paldea felt very proud about this.

They engaged in friendly exchanges with netizens from the Galar Region on the Pokemon Forum.

[Grass Cat Lover (Paldea): Haha, now Calyrex is our king of Paldea!]

[Machamp Muscle Man (Paldea): Someone from the agriculture bureau said that over the past two years, the overall agriculture in Paldea has seen great development, with bumper crops everywhere. Local farmers say these are gifts from Calyrex to his followers.]

[Corviknight Bro (Galar): Damn! Stop it! I'm going to go greet the royal ancestors all over again!]

[Suzie (Galar): I've decided! I'm moving to the Paldea Region!]

Suddenly, the reputation of the Galar Royal Family plummeted, becoming like a street mouse being shouted at by everyone.

Those members of the royal family and their followers could only bow their heads and act like turtles hiding in their shells.

Meanwhile, in the Galar Region, Hammerlocke City, inside the top-floor office of the Energy Mansion.

Wearing an exquisite suit, Chairman Rose calmly listened to Oleana report the situation of public opinion.

At the end, Oleana asked: "Chairman, what should we do?"

Chairman Rose gently shook his head: "Ignore it, let the royal family members have their headaches; our focus still needs to be on Eternatus."

The backward agriculture for the Galar Region is already a thing of the past.

In contrast, only by utilizing the energy from Eternatus can we bring more economic development to the Galar Region.

The potential of Dynamax battles is limitless.

Paldea Region, Paldea League Headquarters.

Geeta sat in the chair, a moderate smile at the corner of her mouth, unable to hide her happiness.

Rika: "...Chief, relax your smile."

But Geeta excitedly said: "Did you see that? Rika, there are many Trainers online saying they want to move to the Paldea Region to live, surely many talents will be attracted to come!"

"Oh-hoho, this is truly wonderful! D is indeed our lucky star in the Paldea Region!"

---

---

---

---

# 425: Chapter 422: Professor Oak's Shock and Alcremie's Metronome Trial!

Regarding the existence of Calyrex, the official personnel from the Galar region have not responded.

Besides the few initial members of the royal family who engaged in online arguments, only the regular Trainers living in the Galar region remain.

Some people feel very regretful about Calyrex's departure, while others continue to curse the Galar Royal Family.

What surprised D was that not many people considered Calyrex as an important resource for the Galar region.

Logically speaking, such a Legendary Pokémon should be crucial for a region.

But on second thought, D realized that not all Legendary Pokémon are beneficial for a region.

Like Groudon and Kyogre in the Hoenn Region, whenever they have battles, they bring all sorts of disasters to the Hoenn region.

The people there surely wish for them to stay far away.

Moreover, Legendary Pokémon inherently are independent entities, without any claim of belonging to any region or League.

This makes sense then.

As for the Galar League's reaction, it was within D's expectations.

Because as seen in the games, Chairman Rose vigorously develops Dynamax battles, attempting to utilize the energy from Eternatus to provide endless energy for the Galar region, thereby promoting industrial and economic development in Galar.

In this scenario, the agriculture represented by Calyrex isn't that important for the Galar region.

Invited by D, Professor Oak planned to stay on the ranch for a while to observe the Pokémon ecology carefully.

Thus, after logging the legends and information of Calyrex, Professor Oak shifted his attention to other Pokémon on the ranch.

On the morning of February 27th, League Year 200, Professor Oak began ecological research on the Pokémon in the ranch.

He held a telescope, hiding in the grass, observing the muscular Farfetch'd waving a gigantic leek.

The Farfetch'd easily lifted the leek taller than itself, swinging it like a sword.

Watching this scene, Professor Oak was full of amazement.

He had never seen a Farfetch'd wielding such a large leek!

Moreover, this Farfetch'd was bigger than average, probably due to years of exercise, with firm muscles beneath its white chest feathers.

The Farfetch'd had sharp eyes, with high-raised V-shaped eyebrows, suddenly swinging its leek towards the wooden dummy.

Accompanied by a series of "bang bang bang" sounds, the Farfetch'd's attacks hit the wooden dummy like a storm.

"Wow! Is this a special form of Farfetch'd? A Paldean form of Farfetch'd?" Professor Oak exclaimed.

"No, that's just a normal Farfetch'd that's been working out recently."

D happened to hear Professor Oak's remark as he passed by, looked at Professor Oak's sneaky posture, and said helplessly:

"Professor Oak, you can go next to the Pokémon to watch, no need to hide and peek."

Professor Oak chuckled, stood up, and scratched his head, explaining:

"If I'm beside them, the Pokémon won't be used to it, only like this can they show their most natural posture, hahaha, you can go ahead, don't mind me, I'm just watching casually!"

D could only nod helplessly, "Alright then, if there's anything you want to know later, we can chat."

"No problem!" Professor Oak nodded with a cheerful smile.

So D went to harvest apples in the apple garden with Koraidon and the Psyducks.

Professor Oak continued to observe the Pokémon ecology of this place.

At this time, it was early morning, and the ranch was full of vitality everywhere.

The Dragonites, along with their partner Rotoms, orderly started the delivery work.

Other Dragonites, carrying messenger bags, flew off one after another.

Meanwhile, D's lead Dragonite stood beside the fields, summoning the wind and rain, using Rain Dance to irrigate the farmland.

In the fields, a Dugtrio emerged its head from the soft soil, basking in the rain and fresh air, with all three heads showing comfortable expressions.

The diligent Combees flew out from the hive, beginning to pollinate crops and collect pollen to make honey.

This scene seemingly constituted a complete agricultural ecological chain.

"Using Dugtrio to till the land, Dragonite to water, Combee to help pollinate... fascinating!" Professor Oak was deeply interested.

Soon, when Dragonite finished watering, the rain cloud floating over the fields dissipated.

The Dugtrio, having finished breathing, swiftly dove into the ground.

Then, Professor Oak saw the three Gogoats wandering over from the pen.

Witnessing this scene made Professor Oak a little surprised.

"Huh? The Gogoats are coming over, what are they going to do?"

The next moment, Professor Oak got his answer.

The three Gogoats walked up to a spot beside the fields and stood still, not waiting for Professor Oak to react, a terrifying burst of Grass Type Energy exploded from them.

In an instant, the area around the fields was completely enveloped in a green glow, like a radiant grand stage.

The three Gogoats stood within, concentrating on cultivating the crops.

Professor Oak: !!!

---

"Wh-what's going on?" Professor Oak was utterly stunned. "Was that move Grassy Terrain?"

But why does this Grassy Terrain look a bit different from the one he knows?!

And then, the next second, something that shocked Professor Oak even more happened.

Under the irrigation of the rich Grass Type Energy released by the Gogoat outside, a carrot in the field suddenly began to swell rapidly at a speed visible to the naked eye.

In the end, that carrot burst out of the soil and grew into a gigantic carrot with a diameter of about three meters!

"Th-this… what on earth is going on?!" Professor Oak stared at the huge carrot, completely taken aback.

In contrast, those three Gogoat seemed completely used to this.

They just cast a calm glance at it and then withdrew their gaze.

After tending to the crops, the Gogoat strolled back to the pen and started eating the Grassy Seed D had prepared for them.

At this moment, Professor Oak had no mind to keep observing Pokemon ecology.

He stood up and ran to the edge of the field, staring dumbfounded at that super gigantic carrot.

Just the part protruding from the soil was already over a meter tall, making it hard to imagine how big it was in total.

Professor Oak wanted to find D to ask what was going on. He turned his head and looked around, only to see D busy on the far side of the river.

The distance was too far, so Professor Oak directly dialed D's Rotom Phone.

Very soon, the call connected. Aiming the camera at the enormous carrot, Professor Oak asked in astonishment, "Is that also Calyrex's power?!"

D immediately spotted the giant carrot and was a bit surprised in his heart—another giant crop had grown in the ranch?

Facing Professor Oak's confusion, D explained, "That's the power of the Gogoat. The Grass Energy they release has a certain chance of causing mutations in the crops. That giant carrot you're seeing is just one of the possible results."

"This is simply unbelievable!" Professor Oak muttered to himself.

He had originally thought that aside from Calyrex and Koraidon, all the other Pokemon at D Ranch were just ordinary Pokemon.

But now it seemed that every single Pokemon here was anything but ordinary!

The way those three Gogoat had just manipulated Grass Energy—sharp-eyed Professor Oak could see at a glance that they were extraordinary.

A Pokemon capable of erupting with such powerful energy in an instant had to be at least Champion Level in strength!

Three Champion Level Gogoat, used as dairy goats to produce milk in this ranch...

For a moment, Professor Oak didn't even know what to say.

Adding in the Dragonite that did deliveries, the Koraidon guarding the ranch, and the Calyrex playing on its phone, this ranch was truly full of hidden masters!

Professor Oak had never seen a Rancher like this before.

"The Paldea Region really is full of talent..."

Having witnessed the Gogoat's performance with his own eyes, Professor Oak became even more curious about the other Pokemon in this ranch.

After hanging up with D, he picked up the telescope again and resumed observing the Pokemon ecology here.

Next to the Gogoat pen, a huge Arboliva stood on the grass, spreading its arms wide as it absorbed the sunlight and dew to its heart's content, looking just like a big tree.

In front of the Pokemon House, a group of ordinary Miltank were leisurely munching on the lush pasture.

On the lawn, all sorts of Eevee were lounging and playing around. Some Eevee were energetically chasing and wrestling with each other, some were lying on the grass with their eyes half-closed in lazy comfort, and others were lying on their backs, bellies exposed, letting their whole bodies soak in the fresh air.

On another side, five Garganacl were sitting clumsily on the grass.

And in front of them, an Alcremie was going "ma-ma-ma-ma" as it delivered some long-winded lecture to them, nodding from time to time.

Mm, these Pokemon all looked quite ordinary.

For some reason, seeing this scene made Professor Oak secretly heave a sigh of relief.

It seemed those Pokemon really were just ordinary Pokemon.

Right at this moment, a girl in a Paldea Academy uniform suddenly appeared at the entrance of the ranch.

Her name was Lulu. She had come to the ranch after seeing the rumors on the school forum, specifically to challenge this extraordinary ranch.

Just as she arrived, Lulu spotted Professor Oak.

She'd thought that kindly uncle was D, so she charged up with fighting spirit and challenged him.

"Mr. D, I'm here to challenge you!"

Professor Oak: "Huh?"

Challenge? Professor Oak paused for a moment, then quickly understood what the girl meant. He pointed to the other side of the river and said kindly:

"Sorry, you've got the wrong person. D's busy over there. I'll call him over for you right away!"

Only then did Lulu realize she'd mistaken him for someone else. She hurriedly thanked Professor Oak: "Thank you so much, then I'll be in your care!"

Professor Oak chuckled: "No need to be so polite."

As he spoke, Professor Oak used his phone to call D for the girl.

After getting the message, D quickly returned with Koraidon and Cyclizar.

D looked at the girl in a school uniform, washed his hands first, then asked, "You mean, you want to take on a Trial?"

Lulu was full of fighting spirit: "That's right!"

D: "So how many Badges have you collected so far?"

Lulu was a bit dumbfounded: "Huh? I have to collect Badges before I can challenge it?"

D nodded: "That's right. Only after collecting six Badges do you qualify to challenge the Trial. As for whether you can clear it, that depends on your own effort."

"Six Badges, huh..." Lulu looked troubled. "But I'm still short two. Mr. D, can't you make an exception? I came all the way from Mesagoza City just for this!"

D: "..."

He said speechlessly, "For Trainers who haven't collected all six Badges yet, the Trials on the ranch are really quite difficult. And even with six Badges, that only gets you the right to participate."

"Honestly, I recommend that Trainers who are about to reach Gym Leader Level, or already above it in strength, come challenge instead."

At the beginning it was fine, letting those newbie Trainers battle against Gogoat. But as more and more Pokemon on the ranch grew stronger, the power gap between Pokemon became too big, and it stopped functioning as a Trial for those rookie Pokemon.

"I see..." Lulu immediately felt very dejected, but she still didn't want to give up, so she asked, "Mr. D, is there really no Trial here that I can challenge?"

She'd seen on the forums that D Ranch had several different Trials...

D was just about to say there wasn't, when he suddenly thought of Alcremie.

"There is one, but it's not quite the same as the other Trials," D said.

Hearing that, Lulu's eyes lit up: "What is it?"

"The Metronome Trial." D explained, "In this one, we can only send out Pokemon that know the move Metronome, and during the battle both Pokemon can only attack the opponent via Metronome."

"Unlike the other Trials, this one is all about testing both sides' luck. There are no strength requirements. If you want to participate, you just need to have at least one Pokemon on your team that knows Metronome."

"Metronome?" Hearing the details of this Trial, Lulu looked delighted.

She took a Poke Ball from her waist on the spot: "My Clefairy happens to know Metronome!"

With a flash of white light, an adorably shaped Clefairy appeared in front of the girl.

D raised an eyebrow and asked, "The Metronome Trial—you really want to take it?"

Lulu nodded vigorously: "I do! I'll come back for the other Trials after I've collected enough Badges!"

"Alright then. In that case, I'll accept your challenge!" D turned and called out, "Alcremie, you've got a guest here for you!"

In no time, Alcremie came running over, riding on a Garganacl.

"Maa maa!" It looked very happy, as if it had been looking forward for a long time to holding a Trial of its own like Gogoat and the others.

Professor Oak watched this scene with great interest from the side.

From the look of it, D had set up Trials on the ranch for passing Trainers to challenge?

Professor Oak had heard of such things.

Long ago, people used to call those who hosted these Trials Ranchers, and in some regions they even had unofficial titles like extra Gym Leader or extra Elite.

"How interesting."

A Metronome Trial that pits luck against luck, huh? Professor Oak had never heard of such a thing before. He laughed heartily and said, "In that case, let me be your referee!"

---

---

---

---

# 426: Chapter 423: Unknown Moves, Cynthia's Visit

Clefairy, a Fairy Type Pokemon.

It has pink fur covering its body, and its shape resembles a star, with a pair of small wings on its back.

If it evolves into Clefable, those wings will grow larger.

Before the battle started, Professor Oak looked at the Clefairy with great interest.

Perhaps due to his professional habit, Professor Oak smiled and said:

"It's Clefairy! This kind of Pokemon is quite rare!"

Because Clefairy usually lives quietly in the deep mountains with its companions, it rarely appears in front of humans.

Only on mysterious nights of a full moon do they appear in large numbers and dance under the moon.

Lulu nodded: "That's right! I'm so happy to encounter a Clefairy!"

The Clefairy heard the Trainer's words and also let out a happy cry.

"Clefairy～!"

"Hahaha! It really likes you!" laughed Professor Oak, and asked, "So you must be ready to start the battle, right?"

Lulu and Clefairy nodded seriously, looking at D and Alcremie on the opposite side, with a glow of determination in their eyes.

"Full of spirit!" D said with a smile, and indeed, many in the Pokemon World are filled with energy.

"The Pokemon in this battle are Clefairy and Alcremie. The battle rule is 1v1, and only Metronome can be used until one side loses the ability to fight, ending the battle," Professor Oak briefly explained the rules.

Seeing both sides were ready, he raised his hand high, and then suddenly swung it down: "Let the battle begin!"

"Clefairy, use Metronome!"

"Alcremie, let's go too!"

D and Lulu issued their commands simultaneously.

The next moment, the two Pokemon on the field moved their fingers in unison.

Dazzling light gradually enveloped their fingers. Moments later, the Metronome actions ended, and the two Pokemon successfully unleashed their moves.

What will it be?

Professor Oak eagerly looked at the battle field.

Alcremie was the first to move, with a beautiful moon disk appearing behind it, and starry bits floating around, together forming a cosmic scene with a mysterious aura.

The faint moonlight and starlight intertwined, pouring into Alcremie's body.

"It's Cosmic Power!" Professor Oak instantly recognized the move.

Cosmic Power enhances a Pokemon's defense. To successfully use it at the start of the battle, it seems Alcremie's luck is quite good!

And Clefairy?

Professor Oak turned his head to see Clefairy surrounded by clusters of green leaves.

It's Razor Leaf!

A Grass Type move; if all hit, the effect is quite good too!

The dense Razor Leaf successfully hit Alcremie, but due to the protection of Cosmic Power, Razor Leaf didn't cause much damage.

The first round ended; the second round officially began!

"Alcremie, continue with Metronome!" said D.

"Ma-ma!"

Lulu was not to be outdone: "Clefairy, let's go too!"

"Clefairy～!"

The two Pokemon's gazes clashed, sparking intense energy.

Fingers waved again.

This time, Clefairy was the first to unleash its move.

"Bubble Attack, huh?" Professor Oak nodded, it seems this Clefairy is also having good luck today!

Two attacking moves from Metronome so far; if this continues, Alcremie won't hold on much longer, right?

However, the next second, Professor Oak noticed an anomaly with Alcremie.

Just as the countless bubbles were about to hit Alcremie, a powerful energy suddenly erupted from Alcremie's body.

The energy surged out, forming a milky white light ball that enveloped Alcremie.

The bubbles hit the light ball, but for some reason, they all shattered and disappeared within the glow!

What move is that?

Professor Oak closely watched Alcremie's transformation, was it Aura Sphere of the Fighting Type, or Light Screen of the Psychic Type?

"Ma-ma—!" Alcremie seemed to have reached its limit.

The next moment, the light ball flew out from its body, soaring straight into the sky, suspended high above.

This scene left D slightly stunned, unable to immediately discern what move it was.

As a dazzling light flashed through, countless light bullets burst out from the light ball, the scene resembling fireworks, executing a wide-ranging strike below.

"Rumble!"

The light bullets smashed into the battle field, instantly creating a large pit.

It was as if a meteor shower had fallen; Clefairy sensed the danger and frantically ran around trying to avoid it.

However, this move was divine judgment of a god, the immense scale making it impossible for Clefairy to dodge.

A light bullet plummeted down, and with a cry of pain, Clefairy rolled its eyes back and lost the ability to fight.

Seeing Clefairy unleash an unprecedented move, Professor Oak looked on, dumbfounded.

Such terrifying power! What move could it be?!

"Judgment," D's mind echoed with the name of the move.

The exclusive move of the mythical Creator God Arceus, releasing countless meteor-like light bullets to execute judgment on foes.

---

Unexpectedly, this time Alcremie actually used this move...

D was equally taken aback by it.

Meanwhile, in a place unnoticed by anyone, a light orb fell into the lake at the ranch and entered the Reversed World through the portal opened by Giratina.

Giratina, who was listening to Rotom read comments with its eyes closed, suddenly sensed the presence of Arceus.

It jolted awake, opened its eyes, and its scarlet eyes locked onto the sky.

In the next moment, its enormous body surged out through the lake surface like a mirror.

Just as Professor Oak recovered from his shock and was about to announce Clefairy's defeat, ending the battle, his gaze caught the terrifying figure that burst from the lake.

Professor Oak: "!!!"

That majestic stature, those blood-red eyes, and that cold, ruthless expression — that Pokémon...

It's the legendary Giratina!

Professor Oak instantly recognized Giratina because he had seen its selfie on the Pokemon Forum.

"Oh my..." Professor Oak took a deep breath, never imagining Giratina would appear here.

No! He should have realized it earlier!

Professor Oak suddenly noticed something.

Giratina claimed to be friends with Calyrex, and the reason Calyrex uses a Rotom Phone is because D taught him.

Similarly, we can deduce that Giratina, who also uses a Rotom Phone to go online, is D's Pokemon!

Not only Professor Oak, but Lulu was also stunned by the sudden appearance of the Pokémon.

Giratina was vigilantly looking around.

It emerged because it sensed the aura of Arceus, but now... where is Arceus?

Giratina's gaze swept over several humans, finally stopping on D.

D couldn't help but sweat a bit.

He didn't expect Alcremie's Judgment to surprise Giratina.

Giratina must have thought Arceus descended earlier, right?

Not finding any trace of Arceus, Giratina realized it made a mistake.

It glanced at the humans below and then turned to dive back into the lake.

Professor Oak and Lulu were shocked as they gazed in that direction.

After a long time, he finally asked, "Was that Pokémon just now Giratina?"

D: "...Yes."

"That is truly incredible!" Professor Oak looked at D, murmuring.

First Calyrex, then Giratina, and capturing so many Dragonite and rare Pokémon... this Trainer is really impressive!

Noticing the look in Professor Oak's eyes, D helplessly explained, "No, that Giratina is not my Pokémon; we are just friends."

With that, D waved his hand towards the distance.

Elder Indeedee brought over a bottle of Life Sheep Milk to feed Clefairy.

Soon, Clefairy opened its eyes.

"Unfortunately, you didn't pass Alcremie's Metronome Trial." D shifted the topic to the Trial.

Lulu finally recovered, recalling the extraordinary move Alcremie used earlier, and asked, "Mr. D, what was that move just now?"

"That, huh? Honestly, I'm not too sure." D said, "There are many Pokémon and moves in this world that humans have yet to discover. Perhaps that move belongs to one of those undiscovered Pokémon?"

"I see?" Lulu murmured, "I understand now!"

Lulu looked deeply at D.

Indeed, as the school forum said, Mr. D is a Trainer with unfathomable strength!

Not only did he raise so many champion Pokémon, but he also became friends with the legendary Pokémon Giratina.

Even the top Champion might not be his match!

At this moment, Lulu wholeheartedly agreed with the forum calling D the Hidden Boss of the Paldea Region.

*

In the afternoon, Lulu left the ranch.

Before leaving, she said she would come over again to challenge the formal Trial once she collected six badges.

Watching her figure gradually fade away, D turned around, intending to return to the ranch to repair the grasslands damaged by Alcremie's Judgment, when suddenly a familiar voice sounded from behind.

"Excuse me, is this D Ranch?"

D and Professor Oak turned around to see a tall woman with long golden hair, dressed in a black coat, smiling from not far away.

Seeing the newcomer, D and Professor Oak were slightly startled.

Professor Oak was the first to react: "Miss Cynthia? Why are you here too?"

Cynthia's tone was gentle as she greeted Professor Oak, "Long time no see, Professor Oak. I came over this time because there are some matters I want to consult with this Trainer."

Saying this, Cynthia's gaze turned to D, her attitude warm and approachable, completely devoid of a superior air.

D: "..."

No need to say, he already guessed Cynthia's purpose for visiting the ranch.

Presumably, the incident of Giratina following the online signal to the Sinnoh Region had been thoroughly investigated by her.

Including his appearance that stopped Giratina's actions.

And Cynthia's visit this time is surely to learn more about Giratina.

D looked up and found Cynthia and Professor Oak looking at him.

---

"..."

Alright! Seeing Professor Oak looking so curious, he must be eager to learn about Giratina too.

Since that's the case, he might as well take this opportunity to share the legend of Giratina with the two of them.

D invited the two into his living room.

Calyrex isn't in the living room now; he must have gone to the garden to bask in the sun.

Cynthia and Professor Oak sat down on the sofa, and his sister Indeedee served tea to the guests.

"Giratina..." D uttered the name of Giratina, then organized his thoughts, wondering where to start.

Hearing this, Cynthia was slightly startled, a hint of surprise flashed across her ice-like beauty's face.

"Does Mr. D know my purpose for coming here?"

D smiled slightly: "If I'm not mistaken, you must have investigated the connection between Giratina and me, which is why you came to the Paldea Region, hoping to learn some information about it from me, right?"

After all, whether in games or anime, Cynthia's character has a strong interest in myths and history, often immersing herself in the research of ancient ruins and mythological legends.

"Then I leave it up to you!" Cynthia said politely.

Meanwhile, Professor Oak was already a bit impatient.

Seeing this, D asked: "Do you know about genes?"

"Genes?"

Both Cynthia and Professor Oak were slightly taken aback.

As a scholar researching Pokemon, Professor Oak has a certain understanding of this field.

Like fossil revival technology, which is made possible by extracting genes from ancient Pokemon.

"Humans and Pokemon both have genes, which are also known as biological blueprints. At the core, they are DNA, containing opposite information. The appearance is like two intertwined chains forming a spiral," D explained slowly.

Someone like Cyrus of Team Galactic used this principle to create the red chain to control Dialga and Palkia.

"The chain-like blueprint can restore to its original shape even if one end is damaged by copying the other end. You understand what I'm trying to express?" D asked.

Professor Oak was thoughtful, and Cynthia also looked pensive.

"Time flows in the real world, where spatial structures tend to stabilize. But behind this world, where there is no time flow, space is unstable, only Giratina exists."

Upon hearing this, Professor Oak immediately understood D's meaning.

He exclaimed: "Does this mean the other world and the world we live in are like the genes of a living being, supporting each other to prevent disappearance?"

D nodded: "Exactly, just as Dialga is the embodiment of time and Palkia embodies space, Giratina is the embodiment of the Reversed World. It controls the entire Reversed World, with both worlds entangled and dependent on each other. If an issue arises in either, the other world will also be affected."

Just like in the movie "Giratina and the Sky Warrior," where the glacier in the real world is damaged, and the Reversed World subsequently collapses.

"Is it really like this?" Cynthia pondered.

The mural in Celestic Town vaguely records related information.

Above Dialga and Palkia, there seems to be another Pokemon.

Is that Pokemon Giratina?

No... recalling the mural's content, Cynthia finds that D's description of Giratina does not match that Pokemon.

Could it mean that above Giratina, Dialga, and Palkia, another Pokemon exists?

Seeing their reactions, D began to talk seriously but nonsensically:

"These are the insights I gathered from my research into the Reversed World and Giratina. These are just my speculations, and the truth may not be like this at all."

Cynthia came out of her deep thoughts and thanked D: "Nevertheless, thank you very much for providing the information."

---

---

---

---

# 427: Chapter 424: Guiding Cynthia, Three Directions!

"Is it really like this..." Professor Oak was full of disbelief.

The relationship between Giratina and the Space-Time Gods, the connection between the Reversed World and the real world, these are all topics worth delving into.

Indeed, there are still so many undiscovered pieces of information in this world!

"Is there anything else you two want to know?" D asked.

Cynthia slightly turned her gaze, recalling the mural in Celestic Town and the Pokemon that seemed to be above Dialga and Palkia. She silently thought that perhaps D might know something.

With this thought in mind, Cynthia took the initiative to ask.

"Mr. D, is there a Pokemon that exists above Dialga, Palkia, and Giratina that hasn't been discovered by humans?"

Huh? Hearing these words, D couldn't help feeling a bit surprised.

Has Cynthia already noticed Arceus?

He looked at the Champion of the Sinnoh Region, thought for a moment, and decided there was no harm in sharing, so he began to convey what he knew to Cynthia.

"If I'm not mistaken, the one you're referring to is a Pokemon named Arceus," said D.

"Arceus?" Cynthia and Professor Oak were immediately captivated by this name.

D nodded: "In legend, it is the God that created this world. Throughout history, it has been known by many names: Creator God, Sinnoh Venerable..."

"From what I understand, when the world was initially created by Arceus, it was completely empty."

"After that, Arceus created Dialga, who governs time, and Palkia, who governs space, so that time in the world began to flow, and space gradually stabilized."

"Meanwhile, Giratina and the Reversed World, which were created at the same time, support the material world we live in."

"Once the material world was complete, Arceus created the lake guardian Pokemon that shape the spiritual world."

"They are Azelf, Mesprit, and Uxie. The union of material and spirit forms the world we live in today."

Hearing these myths she had never known, Cynthia's beautiful eyes couldn't help but shine brightly.

D laughed: "Not only in the Sinnoh Region, but legends of Arceus or related things exist in other parts of the world."

Cynthia's heart stirred, and she eagerly asked, "Where?"

Seeing her intent, D pointed Cynthia in a few directions.

"The first location is the Ruins of Alph in the Johto Region. It is said that deep within these ruins lies the entrance to the truth of creation, but only the chosen ones can find that place."

"The truth of creation..." Cynthia's heart began to race with excitement, and she asked, "Mr. D, have you ever been there?"

D nodded: "That's right. Before running this ranch, I traveled around the world and visited the Ruins of Alph."

This was his experience in the game, so it wasn't really deceiving Cynthia.

Cynthia nodded, memorizing this location, planning to visit and investigate it later.

"The second location is the secluded town of Midina in the Sinnoh Region. It has ruins dating back thousands of years, and you might find the information you seek there."

Midina, the setting of the movie "Arceus and the Jewel of Life."

Arceus was once imprisoned underground by humans using mercury. After that, the dying Arceus fell into a void, consumed by blazing anger, and entered a deep sleep, awaiting future awakening.

At that time, in the name of a god, Arceus will judge humanity.

"Midina..." Cynthia nodded again, noting this name too.

D: "The third place is Arche Valley in the Kalos Region. I once saw locals there worshiping a statue of the Creator God, and legends of Arceus are also passed down there."

The Light Ring's Super Demon God Hoopa is sealed there, living with the locals.

"I'll remember." Cynthia was eager with excitement, wishing she could rush to these places for investigation right away.

Thus, Cynthia did not linger long at the ranch.

After acquiring the information she sought, she stood up and thanked D:

"Thank you very much, Mr. D. I won't disturb you two any longer. I'm going back to the Sinnoh Region to investigate Midina."

No, she should stop over in the Johto Region first, investigate the Ruins of Alph there, and then travel north back to the Sinnoh Region, so thought Cynthia.

D: "..."

So this is what Cynthia is really like!

She really is straightforward and decisive.

He did not try to persuade her to stay, simply saying: "Then I wish you good luck."

Cynthia curved her lips into a faint smile.

Before leaving, she added D to her Poke Communications.

"After I investigate those myths and ruins, I will contact you again," Cynthia said.

D nodded: "Alright."

Cynthia left.

This noble-looking, seemingly ice-cold Champion had no airs when facing those myths and legends.

Although D anticipated this in his heart, actually interacting with Cynthia, he was still surprised by her contrasting demeanor.

After all, typically, someone who appears dignified and gentle, resembling an ice-cold beauty, usually has a rather aloof personality.

---

However, Cynthia's personality was very gentle, without any airs of a top Champion at all.

D had to admit, she really was an incredibly charming woman.

"What are you thinking about?" Professor Oak asked from the side.

Only then did D come back to his senses and answer, "I just feel Cynthia is a bit different from what I imagined."

Professor Oak was very curious. "Oh?"

"Don't you feel it, Professor?" D said. "Logically speaking, someone like Cynthia, whose strength is so unfathomable and whose bearing is so noble, would usually come off as very aloof and hard to approach."

Hearing this, Professor Oak immediately understood.

He couldn't help but laugh. "You know, you're the one who can't see what's right under his nose. Your strength is just as unfathomable, yet instead of going out there to become a Champion and make a name for yourself, you stay out in the countryside running this little ranch."

"On top of that, you even host passing Trainers and students, battle with them, and have your Pokemon act as their sparring partners. Compared to her, you're the one with absolutely no airs at all."

D: "..."

Thinking about it carefully, that did kind of make sense!

D smiled a little sheepishly.

And just then, Professor Oak's Rotom Phone started ringing.

He took out his phone, glanced at it, and answered right in front of D.

The next second, a face that felt familiar to D appeared on the screen.

"Ash, huh!" Looking at the scenery behind Ash, Professor Oak said with a smile, "Looks like you've already arrived in Rustboro City safely!"

"Yeah, that's right!" Ash took a Badge out from his badge case. "And Pikachu and I have already cleared the challenge at Rustboro Gym and won the Boulder Badge!"

"That's really great!" Professor Oak nodded with a smile. "There are still quite a few to go before you get all eight Badges, so keep it up! When you take part in the Ever Grande Conference, I'll go over there in person to cheer you on!"

At the mention of the Ever Grande Conference, Ash was immediately burning with fighting spirit. "Yeah! Pikachu and I will definitely do our best!"

"Pika～"

At this moment, Brock suddenly noticed the environment Professor Oak was in.

It seemed to be a farm, because behind Professor Oak was a large field planted with crops, and nearby were several Pokemon he had never seen before.

Having been to Professor Oak's Research Institute before, Brock could tell at a glance that this wasn't the Institute.

He opened his perpetually squinted eyes a bit and asked, "Professor Oak, are you away on a business trip?"

Professor Oak replied with a kindly smile, "No, I made a trip to the Paldea Region to visit a friend there."

As he spoke, Professor Oak turned the Rotom Phone's camera toward D and introduced him to Ash and the others:

"This is Mr. D from the Paldea Region. He's a very powerful Trainer!"

D looked toward the screen and, sure enough, saw Ash and Brock in it, as well as Norman's two kids, May and Max.

"Hello!" D waved at the screen in greeting.

This was his first time seeing Ash; faced with this anime protagonist, he couldn't help feeling a surge of curiosity.

Ash was very polite as well. "Hello, Mr. D, I'm Ash from Pallet Town!"

"Hahaha!" Professor Oak laughed heartily and said to Ash, "When you all have time in the future, you can come on a trip to the Paldea Region. There are lots of Pokemon here that you don't see in the Kanto Region!"

Hearing there were Pokemon he had never seen before, Ash's eyes lit up in an instant. He nodded seriously. "Yeah, we'll definitely go over when we get the chance!"

Professor Oak said, "Then it's settled. I'll be back at the Research Institute very soon. If you catch any new Pokemon during your travels, Ash, you can transfer them back to the Institute anytime, and I'll look after them for you."

Ash agreed.

The call ended shortly after, and Professor Oak spoke with a rather emotional tone:

"That kid is Ash. His father left home when he was very young, leaving just him and his mom to live together."

"That child has been scatterbrained since he was little. When he was five, he missed summer camp because he overslept, and when he was ten, for the same reason he missed getting his starter Pokemon. Bulbasaur, Charmander, and Squirtle were all chosen by other Trainers, and only a Pikachu was left."

"Ah, without realizing it, that kid's already been traveling for quite a long time now."

D listened quietly from the side.

Looking at Professor Oak's expression, something suddenly came to his mind.

Professor Oak knew Ash—in fact, one could say he knew Ash even before Ash was born.

Because when Professor Oak was a child, he once traveled through time with Celebi into the future, where he met Ash. They went through a tense and exciting adventure together and defeated a Pokemon Hunter.

Back then, the two of them became good friends across time.

So is that why Professor Oak later took such good care of Ash, because of that incident?

A spark of inspiration flashed through D's mind.

In his past life, there had been quite a few people online who joked about Professor Oak and Ash's mom, Hanako, saying that Professor Oak only looked after Ash so much because of Hanako.

But looking at it now, Professor Oak simply wasn't that kind of person at all.

D looked at the kindly Professor Oak in front of him.

Maybe it was just because of the friendship he once shared with Ash that he cared for him so much.

It was even possible that that special Pikachu had been prepared specifically for Ash by Professor Oak.

Because from the very beginning, he already knew what would happen later...

Thinking of this, D suddenly asked, "By the way, Professor Oak, why did you choose to build your Research Institute in a place like Pallet Town?"

If he remembered correctly, Pallet Town was just a tiny place in the Kanto Region. When Ash traveled outside and introduced himself, the response he always got from others was: "a small place, middle of nowhere, never heard of it."

Yet Professor Oak wasn't just a famous researcher; he was also a top graduate from Celadon University. He clearly could have chosen somewhere better, but he deliberately chose Pallet Town.

Hearing D's question, Professor Oak was slightly taken aback, then his face softened with nostalgia as he answered quietly:

"That's because someone [important] to me lives there. When I was a child, I once made a promise with him, a promise that we would meet again."

Professor Oak's thoughts seemed to drift back to that summer long ago.

In that forest which held the legend of the Forest God, he had met a boy named Ash.

Seeing this, D instantly knew he had guessed right.

"That friend must be very important to you, huh?" he asked.

Professor Oak nodded with a smile. "Our personalities get along quite well."

"That's wonderful," D sighed from the heart.

But thinking about it, which came first in the end? Did Professor Oak first go to Pallet Town and set up his Research Institute, or did Ash first meet Professor Oak?

If it was like Professor Sada had said before, this process seemed to be what they called a time loop.

There is no "first" or "later"; the two are each other's cause and effect. Because young Professor Oak met Ash, he later founded his Research Institute in Pallet Town.

And it was precisely because Professor Oak founded his Research Institute in Pallet Town that Ash would go on to meet the younger Professor Oak.

*

League Calendar, Year 200, March 1st, spring.

After staying at the ranch for six days, Professor Oak left the Paldea Region with a wealth of research data.

D took Koraidon to see him off, and only after sending Professor Oak onto the plane did they start heading back.

By the time D returned to the ranch, it was already evening.

Dusk had fallen, and the Pokemon on the ranch had been playing all day, so by now they were all feeling a bit tired.

But with D not home, they didn't want to go back inside the living room either, so they lined up, crouching under the eaves and staring toward the ranch gate, waiting for D to come home.

Some of the Pokemon, tired from waiting, started nodding off, their heads bobbing as they dozed.

When D flew down onto the grass on Koraidon's back and saw this scene, his heart almost melted.

Very soon, an Eevee was the first to notice him.

That Eevee's sleepy little face instantly lit up.

"Bui!"

Its cry drew the attention of the other Pokemon, and a bunch of them spotted D returning to the ranch.

They immediately ran over, happily surrounding D in a circle.

Seeing this group of Pokemon waiting for him to come home, D felt all warm inside.

Smiling, he patted each of them on the head in turn and said softly:

"Alright, if you're sleepy, go inside and get some rest."

---

---

---

---

# 428: Chapter 425: Establish the Guild—Pokemon Forest Friends Association, and Invite Pokemon Residents to Settle!

The next morning, after finishing work at the ranch, D turned his attention to the giant carrot that had been ripened by the Gogoat.

Like the giant watermelon before, this carrot also belonged to the category of giant crops.

Its color was reddish-orange, with a diameter of about one meter, and just the part protruding from the soil was already over two meters.

It's hard to imagine how big this carrot would really be.

After strolling around the field once, D instructed, "Koraidon, help me pull it out!"

"Ah gah ss!"

Koraidon responded and leaped up, landing next to the carrot, wrapping both arms around it, and exerting force.

The next second, the giant carrot was pulled out by Koraidon's brute strength and brought to D's side.

"Nice job!" D praised with a smile.

He looked up, and information about the carrot emerged in his mind.

[Giant Carrot: A type of giant crop, filled with nutrients, favored by certain Pokemon, and can be used for cooking.]

If anyone at the ranch loved carrots the most, it would be Calyrex.

Thinking briefly, D had a plan.

A carrot this huge is a waste if left here just to watch, and it'll spoil, so why not hollow it out and make carrot paste and carrot pancakes, and use the remaining carrot shell to create a little carrot hut for small-sized Pokemon like Eevee to play with.

With action in mind, D set to work immediately.
