He first scooped out the carrot flesh with a spoon and knife and set it aside, then asked Koraidon to help put the remaining carrot onto the pickup truck.

D got into the driver's seat, ready to visit Keri's cottage.

He remembered that Keri had a special material to maintain the original shape of crops and prevent them from rotting.

D had seen Keri use this method to make pumpkin lanterns before, which stayed fresh at room temperature for five months.

Driving out of the ranch, it took about ten minutes to reach Porto Marinada Town.

D parked the car in front of Keri's house; Keri was doing some woodworking at the door and was quite surprised to see him.

"Hey? How come you have time to come by today?"

D and Koraidon unloaded the carrot and explained their purpose:

"I want to ask you to help transform this giant carrot into a carrot hut for the Pokemon at the ranch to play with."

Upon hearing this, Keri understood D's intention.

The giant carrot didn't surprise her much, as she had seen a similarly giant watermelon at D Ranch before.

Come to think of it, these must be the power of Calyrex.

Keri: "Alright, no problem, it will take about three days."

"Okay, then I'll come back to pick it up in three days," D nodded.

After paying, D was about to leave but suddenly remembered another matter to attend to.

That is, ordering construction materials to bring to the World Tree for building the Exploration Team Union.

The current time is March 200, and in four months, the World Tree will be fully grown.

Though it still needs some time, as of now, the ecological environment inside the World Tree has largely stabilized and isn't likely to change much.

Over the next four months, based on the existing foundation, the internal space of the World Tree will continue to expand.

D recalled the space he visited with his Pokemon last time.

There was a vast open area, surrounded by forests and lakes, the perfect place to build the Exploration Team Union.

Therefore, D planned to set the construction site for the Union there.

But aside from this, there was another problem.

D was clueless about building houses and couldn't possibly create all those structures alone.

After brief contemplation, D turned his gaze to Keri and her Machamp assistant.

Keri was momentarily stunned and asked, "What's wrong? Is there something else?"

"I have another request for you," D said earnestly.

Surprised by his expression, Keri, nevertheless, asked, "Oh? What is it?"

D: "I need you to help me build some facilities, but not at the ranch, it's at another place."

"Are you planning to start a second ranch?" Keri was taken aback.

D: "...No, come with me and you'll understand."

Ten minutes later, Keri was levitating in the Reversed World, staring at the fragmented Sky Islands and deep voids, completely dazed.

"Oh my..." Keri muttered in shock, "What kind of place is this?"

"This is Giratina's Reversed World."

D spoke softly beside her.

His eyes glanced at the nearby Flower Sky Island; Giratina wasn't there now; perhaps it had gone to patrol its territory or to scare netizens through the web.

"Giratina?!" A huge exclamation mark appeared above Keri's head: "Is it that Legendary Pokemon from the Pokemon Forum that uses Rotom?"

"That's right." D nodded and didn't say much about the topic, instead returning to the main point: "Do you see that big tree over there?"

Keri followed his direction and indeed saw a huge tree there.

D: "I want to ask you to help me build something on that tree, something like the Pokemon Tavern at the ranch, for the Pokemon to use."

---

"Hmm... that tree is something I planted earlier. It's special; though it appears as a tree, it's hollow inside. I want to create an eco-zone there for the Pokemon in the ranch to reside."

Just then, Giratina flew back with a ferocious presence.

Its massive body and domineering aura made it hard for anyone not to notice.

Keri instantly recognized that Pokemon.

It was the Giratina surfing on the Pokemon Forum!

Giratina also noticed D and Keri, somewhat puzzled as it peered in their direction.

D explained, "This is the craftsman I invited. I'll entrust her with building some things on the tree for the time being."

Upon hearing this, Giratina nodded its enormous head and said nothing more, instead settling back on Flower Sky Island.

Seeing this, D knew Giratina had agreed.

He said to Keri, "It's all set now; you and Machamp can proceed with the construction here without concerns."

Keri: "..."

At that moment, Keri distinctly heard the thumping of her own heart.

The look she gave D changed.

Who could have imagined that their good neighbor, always kind, diligent, and low-key, was secretly such a mysterious figure!

Not only did he tame Calyrex, but he also established contact with the Legendary Pokemon Giratina, able to freely enter and exit its domain.

At this moment, Keri suddenly felt a sorrowful barrier had formed between her and D.

D noticed the look in Keri's eyes and remarked, "What's with that look?"

Keri murmured, "I'm just too shocked..."

After a while, Keri calmed her nerves, looked into D's eyes, and asked:

"Still the same, keeping the secret of Giratina's presence here, right?"

D nodded, "Thank you."

"Got it." Keri took a deep breath and quickly returned to her usual expression.

She turned her gaze to the towering tree and asked, "Tell me, what do you want to build there?"

D led Keri to the top layer of the World Tree.

Before their eyes was a vibrant natural scene.

The soil was fertile, grass lush; deep in the woods lay a tranquil lake, shrouded in mist like an ethereal fairyland. Beside the lake grew moss-covered trees, with green vines hanging from their branches.

"So beautiful..." Keri couldn't help but murmur at the sight.

D smiled gently, then looked down at the grassy ground and said to Keri:

"This is the place. By the way, Keri, have you played role-playing games?"

Keri paused, then recalled the medieval mage adventure games on her Rotom Phone, nodding, "I have. Why?"

"In those games, there's something called a guild where players can accept tasks and commissions from the bulletin board." D succinctly said, "I want to build a guild here, complete with a bar, bulletin board, and facilities."

Is D planning to play make-believe games with the Pokemon? Keri was puzzled but still nodded.

"No problem."

D continued, "Besides the guild, there's a Pokemon Tavern, Pokemon Restaurant, Pokemon Cafe, a warehouse to store Berries and food, an Item Shop to sell items, a hospital for healing injuries, and a place for Pokemon to sleep."

Keri: "..."

Indeed, D intends to play make-believe with his Pokemon, doesn't he?

"Understood."

Keri nodded, surveying the surroundings and memorizing the environment and layout.

"This afternoon, I'll start drawing the design plans, and after finishing, I'll bring them to the ranch for you to check. With this many constructions, the project is massive and will take quite a bit of time to complete. Uhm, are you in a hurry?" Keri asked.

D shook his head, "Not in a hurry."

There's still a few months before the World Tree fully grows, so Keri can build at her own pace.

"Alright, got it." Keri pondered and then devised a construction plan, "I'll bring the blueprints to you tomorrow. The store's building materials are insufficient, and I'll need to source more goods from elsewhere. Once everything is ready, I'll come over to start building."

"Alright, no problem." D nodded, not having any objections.

The two left the Reversed World, and D drove Keri back to her cottage.

On the way back to the ranch, D began selecting suitable Pokemon to work in the Exploration Team Union facilities in his mind.

The Indeedee Sibling must look after the ranch, so they are unlikely to be available for work on the World Tree; hence, other Pokemon are needed for those tasks.

When it comes to a Pokemon fit for running a Pokemon Tavern, D first thought of Oranguru.

This type of Pokemon is highly intelligent and very clever, capable of using various tools like humans.

In the anime, there's an Oranguru running a Pokemon Cafe in the forest, adept at mixing fruit juice using Berries.

Pokemon Cafe could have Spinda as a barista, and the Pokemon Restaurant could employ Mr. Mime as the chef.

D remembered that Ash's Mr. Mime was quite skilled.

Not only did it clean, but it also cooked various delicacies; it excelled in both cleaning and cooking.

---

D has already arranged the work at the Item Shop for the parents of Pawmot.

As for the hospital to treat injuries...

Several figures appeared in D's mind.

Chansey, Blissey, Wigglytuff, Audino, and Comfey.

These Pokemon are recognized as nurse assistants in the Pokemon World, always seen helping Miss Joy treat patients at various Pokemon Centers.

Apart from them, D remembered that Clefable could also serve as a nurse.

If Clefable goes to the Pokemon Center, it can also help with some simple supportive tasks.

For example, pushing medical beds, fetching medical instruments like stethoscopes. Besides, Clefable has excellent hearing, able to hear the sound of a pin drop from a kilometer away.

In such cases, it can easily hear a patient's call and go to their side.

But some of the Pokemon D imagined are not present in the Paldea Region.

To find them, one needs to go to other regions.

However, D currently doesn't have that much time to leave the ranch, otherwise, the Pokemon at the ranch would miss him.

After a little thought, D took out his Rotom Phone and delegated these tasks to the Heroic Expedition Team traveling outside.

Meanwhile, in the distant Hoenn Region.

In a vast, lush forest, the three Pokemon of the Heroic Expedition Team were sitting on a clearing, sharing Berries with a group of wild Pokemon.

Suddenly, the Rotom Phone flew up.

"New task received, Roto!"

"Miu?" Mew blinked.

Teddiursa and Zapdos also looked at the Rotom Phone.

The Rotom Phone organized D's tasks and then began reading the contents of the tasks.

[Task 3: Find Oranguru

Task details: Facilities like the Exploration Team Union, Café, and Restaurant are now being constructed on the World Tree. Please help find some staff for these facilities!

Look for Pokemon interested in cooking and Berry blending, including but not limited to Oranguru, and invite them to work on the World Tree.]

[Task 4: Find Mr. Mime

Task details: Mr. Mime is very skillful in doing housework and cooking. It can prepare food for the exploration team at the Pokemon Restaurant. If you encounter a Mr. Mime interested in cooking, invite them to the World Tree!]

[Task 5: Find Fire-starting Pokemon

Task details: Cooking and lighting a campfire both require starting a fire. Without Fire Type Pokemon, it's impossible. If you encounter any Fire Type Pokemon wanting to settle on the World Tree during your journey, invite them to live on the World Tree!]

[Task 6: Find doctor and nurse Pokemon

Task details: The Pokemon Hospital on the World Tree still lacks a few nurses and doctors. If you meet Chansey, Blissey, Wigglytuff, Audino, and Comfey, these kind-hearted Pokemon, invite them to serve as doctors on the World Tree!]

[Task 7: Find Pokemon skilled in cultivating crops, farming, and watering

Task details: A prosperous farm is being built on the World Tree. If you encounter Pokemon skilled in farming, invite them to the World Tree!

Pokemon skilled in cultivating crops: Bellossom, Chikorita, Sunflora, Petilil, etc., Grass Type Pokemon.

Skilled in farming: Diglett, Dugtrio, Mudsdale, Sandslash, Torterra, Diggersby

Skilled in watering: Squirtle, Floatzel, Poliwag, Marill, etc., Water Type Pokemon.]

---

---

---

---

# 429: Chapter 426: Treecko Forest, Guarding the Great Tree!

After sending the message, D felt like he was becoming an NPC that assigns tasks in a game.

But the Pokemon that would live on the World Tree in the future are closely connected to the Heroic Expedition Team, so it's better to let Teddiursa and Mew invite the Pokemon themselves.

Everyone is good friends, happily living together every day, farming and adventuring together.

Just imagining that kind of life is so wonderful.

After returning to the ranch, it was already noon.

D washed his hands and began preparing lunch for the Pokémon.

Today's lunch is still very sumptuous.

Pan-fried steaks, steamed sea bass, Paldea seafood rice, vegetable salad, sandwiches, paired with nutritional Pokeblocks and delicious Poffin for dessert.

This lunch set is nutritionally balanced, and caters to the preferences of almost every Pokemon on the ranch.

"Ee! Ee!"

The sound of Eevees came from the living room, and when D looked up, he saw several little Eevees chasing each other's tails, running around the couch.

One of them accidentally stepped on Koraidon's tail, and Koraidon, who was resting with eyes closed, was immediately awakened and looked at the culprit with its golden eyes.

However, the little Eevee was not afraid at all, instead, it started to chase and play with Koraidon.

Seeing this scene, Koraidon looked helpless, "Argh..."

"They're not afraid of you anymore," D said with a smile while carrying the food to the dining room.

At first, these little Eevees were afraid of Koraidon, but as they spent time together daily, they slowly realized Koraidon isn't that scary and rarely loses its temper.

Over time, they became less afraid of Koraidon.

"Argh..." Koraidon sighed resignedly, with a look of compromise, allowing the little Eevees to run around it.

As D approached, the Eevees quickly shifted their attention to him.

They gathered around, lifted their small heads, and called out "Ee! Ee!" to him.

D raised an eyebrow, "What's going on?"

The Rotom Phone helped to translate, "The Eevees are asking [Can we evolve into powerful Eevee forms now?] Rotom!"

"You want to evolve?" D turned around but didn't find those already evolved Eevees in the room.

The water, fire, and electric Eevee trio, along with Glaceon and Umbreon, after evolving, prefer the outdoor environment to the indoors.

At this time, they should be playing outside with Alcremie, right?

The remaining three unevolved Eevees all nodded their little heads, "Ee!"

Indeed! They've grown into bigger Eevees and can evolve into more powerful Eevee forms!

With that, one of the Eevees even tried to show off its strong body.

D: "..."

Calculating the time, it's been seven months since the Eevees hatched, compared to then, they have indeed grown up.

"Alright, I'll see if I can help you evolve after lunch," D promised.

"Ee!" The three Eevees exclaimed joyfully.

One Eevee sat on the side, seemingly indifferent to evolution.

Because D said that a partner Eevee that doesn't evolve is the most powerful!

Only a partner Eevee can use the super move - Extreme Evoboost!

After comforting the Eevees, D turned around and carried the remaining dishes to the dining room.

Once everything was ready, he stood under the eaves and whistled toward the ranch.

Soon, a large group of Pokemon came running back from all directions, going to their bowls and happily enjoying lunch.

*

Afternoon, in the Hoenn Region, at a place called Treecko Forest.

This is a very lush forest, and originally there was a tall giant tree growing in the center of the forest, but not long ago, the giant tree fell due to old age.

The Treecko Pokemon, which used to nest around the giant tree, also lost their home because of this.

The Heroic Expedition Team heard this news from a wild Beautifly and made a special trip here to see if they could offer some help to the poor Treecko.

Here, Mew and Teddiursa saw the withered and fallen giant tree.

"Mew..."

"miu?"

Seeing the miserable state of the big tree, the Pokémon felt compassionate, and Mew even thought of the World Tree at the ranch.

Will the World Tree also wither like this giant tree in the future? Will they also lose their home just like the Treecko living here?

Mew didn't know, Teddiursa didn't know, Zapdos didn't know.

So after some struggling, the three Pokemon decided to ask D for help.

The video call connected quickly, and D appeared on the Rotom Phone's screen.

Mew and Teddiursa squeezed their little faces together, looking at D on the other side.

"Mew-mew?" (D, will the World Tree wither in the future?)

Teddiursa's words were automatically translated by Rotom into D's ear.

D was very surprised, "Of course not, as long as the world doesn't end, the World Tree will never wither. Did you encounter something?"

Mew and Teddiursa nodded and then stepped aside, showing the withered and fallen giant tree to D.

---

D immediately understood.

"So that's how it is! But don't worry, such a thing will definitely not happen."

Teddiursa and Mew both breathed a sigh of relief.

D wanted to say more, but just then, he suddenly saw a few silhouettes flicker behind the two Pokemon in the bushes.

Accompanied by the sound of rushing through the air, several sharp Razor Leafs landed by Zapdos's feet.

"Danger!" D quickly warned.

The three Pokemon immediately became alert, and the Rotom Phone automatically scanned the surroundings.

Soon, D saw several green figures in the trees not far away.

Those are... Treecko from the Hoenn Region?!

At this time, Mew and Teddiursa also discovered the traces of those Treecko.

Teddiursa immediately began to negotiate with them: "One mo one mo!" (We are the Heroic Expedition Team traveling around, no malice!)

The Treecko still looked over with vigilance.

Seeing this situation, D instantly realized the thoughts of these Treecko.

That place was their territory, and the sudden intruders, Zapdos, Mew, and Teddiursa, were regarded as enemies invading their land.

According to the habits of Pokemon like Treecko, they would fiercely intimidate enemies invading their territory, protecting their home.

That's why Treecko are also called the Pokemon that protects the forest trees.

But judging from their recent actions, they didn't seem to be attacking but rather warning the Heroic Expedition Team.

Otherwise, with Treecko's ability, the Razor Leaf would not have missed when facing unsteady opponents.

Seeing the Treecko ignoring them, Mew also explained: "miu!" (We heard from other Pokemon that a giant tree fell here, so we came to see if we could help!)

Zapdos unfolded its wings, with currents crackling on its sharp feathers.

Seeing Zapdos's actions, the group of Treecko became more vigilant.

At this moment, an old Pokemon's growl came from the forest.

Hearing this sound, the Treecko stopped and looked back.

They saw an older Treecko slowly emerge from the bushes.

The other Treecko jumped to the ground and followed behind the older Treecko.

"Cha fu?" (Are you saying you're here to help us?)

Mew and Teddiursa nodded repeatedly.

"One mo!" (That's right! We are the Heroic Expedition Team currently traveling around the world, helping Pokemon in need!)

The Treecko looked at the giant tree: "Cha fu." (But this giant tree is already dead, it's too old to survive.)

Upon hearing this, Mew and Teddiursa looked troubled.

Mew flew forward, trying to use its power to revive the giant tree, but failed.

This left the two Pokemon at a loss for what to do.

After pondering for a moment, Teddiursa refused to give up: "One mo!" (There must be a way!)

"Excuse me." D suddenly spoke from the other side of the Rotom Phone screen, having witnessed everything.

A group of Pokemon immediately turned to look this way.

Only then did the Treecko notice that the strange Pokemon's body actually had a human inside.

D: "Teddiursa, Mew, see if you can find its seeds near the giant tree, if you can, use Calyrex's power to revive it."

Hearing this, Mew and Teddiursa's eyes lit up.

They quickly flew to the fallen giant tree, brushing the branches and checking the rotted trunk.

Soon, fortunately, Teddiursa successfully found a tree seed.

D continued: "Next, dig a soil pit in the clearing and plant the seed."

"One mo!"

Teddiursa complied, and in the watchful eyes of the Treecko, began digging a pit with its small claws.

Soon, a soil pit about ten centimeters deep was dug.

Teddiursa placed the seed inside and covered it with soil.

In the pasture, D nodded towards Calyrex.

Back when Calyrex was far away in Galar, it accelerated the maturation of the crops in his pasture.

With Calyrex's current power, such a thing is very easy.

Calyrex took a look, then closed its eyes, sensing the presence of Mew and Teddiursa, and activated its power.

The next second, a miraculous scene occurred.

The seed just buried began to grow rapidly at a visible speed, turning into a tender sapling.

The Treecko were astonished by this sight.

Before they could react, the sapling continued to grow.

In just a few breaths, a stout and towering giant tree stood before them.

Treecko:!!!

The Treecko, who had never witnessed such a scene, were stunned.

Mew and Teddiursa happily stood under the tree.

"One mo one mo!" (Now there's no problem!)

"miu~" (Now you can live under the giant tree again~)

Seeing the astonished looks of the Treecko opposite, D couldn't help but want to laugh.

Treecko, one of the Starter Pokemon from the Hoenn Region, and Ash also had a Treecko.

His Treecko later evolved into a Sceptile, achieving excellent results, even defeating Tobias's Darkrai, the Legendary Beast, in the Sinnoh Region's League Conference.

---

Although Sceptile was defeated later by the Latios Tobias sent out, it had already done a great job.

But anyway, is this what the daily life of the Heroic Expedition Team's journey is like?

Running around Helping Hand-ing Pokemon in need everywhere—it actually looks pretty fun.

D had planned to watch for a while longer, but a sudden knock sounded from outside.

Seeing that things over by Teddiursa's side were more or less settled, he hung up the call and went to open the door.

The one knocking was Mayor Thomas, sporting his white beard.

The old gentleman beamed. "Good afternoon, D!"

"Good afternoon, Mayor Thomas!" D greeted him. "Is something up?"

Mayor Thomas nodded and asked, "Here's the thing: the spring fishing tournament will be held the day after tomorrow. I was wondering, which event are you planning to enter this year?"

The spring fishing tournament...

Thinking of fishing, D immediately remembered the Paldea Seafood Rice he'd eaten before.

One of that dish's effects was to increase the eater's chance of reeling in rare fish.

"Sure!" D figured that since he'd just be lazing around at the ranch anyway, he might as well go over there and have some fun.

Mayor Thomas laughed. "Excellent. The fishing hotshots in town have been wanting to test themselves against you for ages. You skipped the last two years, and this year those guys specially came to me and asked me to see whether you'd be joining."

D: "..."

With Mayor Thomas spelling it out like that, D instantly understood what was going on.

When he first came to the ranch, he'd needed money and had Koraidon help him blast a whole bunch of fish.

The townsfolk had always assumed those fish were all caught by him, so in the minds of the people of Porto Marinada Town, Rancher D was probably some Fishing Master with god-tier technique.

The kind whose fishing skills had already reached a transcendent realm.

D was momentarily speechless.

Mayor Thomas, however, was very pleased. "The tournament will be held at Lake Verity, starting at eight in the morning the day after tomorrow. When the time comes, just use your fishing skills to completely win over those challengers!"

D: "..."

Looks like he had no choice but to bring out the Paldea Seafood Rice.

*

Meanwhile, in Treecko Forest of the Hoenn Region—

To express their gratitude to the Heroic Expedition Team, the Treecko had gathered Berries and were holding a banquet beneath the giant tree.

The elderly Treecko handed Teddiursa a Berry.

"Cha-fu!" (You're amazing! You actually managed to bring the withered giant tree back to life!)

Teddiursa took it with both paws, happily shaking its head. "Yima!" (That was all thanks to D and Calyrex!)

D? Calyrex?

Was that the human inside the Pokemon called Rotom?

Thinking that, Treecko asked directly.

Teddiursa shook its head, then nodded again. "Yima yima!" (D is D, he's the best human in the whole world! And Calyrex is also a really powerful Pokemon. He lives together with everyone at the ranch!)

Beside them, Mew bobbed its head in agreement. "Miu～"

Treecko asked again, "Cha-fu?" (Ranch?)

"Yima yima!" (That's our home! There's also an especially, especially big tree at the ranch—that's the World Tree!)

Speaking of this, Teddiursa suddenly remembered the request D had given them earlier.

It looked at the Treecko in front of it—these Pokemon were all Grass Type too, right?

So Teddiursa happily extended an invitation to the Treecko.

"Yima yima? Yima!" (The World Tree is really, really big. D said he plans to build an Explorer Team Guild there, and a Pokemon Tavern, a Pokemon farm, and a Pokemon Restaurant. Would you like to settle on the World Tree? Everyone living together, it'll be super lively!)

---

---

---

---

# 430: Chapter 427: Eevee and Baby-Doll Eyes, Evolve Now, Sylveon!

When D bid farewell to Mayor Thomas and returned to his room to dial the video call again, the matters on the Heroic Expedition Team's side had already been successfully resolved.

Through the screen of the Rotom Phone, D saw the wild Treeckos on the other side holding a banquet under the giant tree to thank the three Pokemons of the Heroic Expedition Team.

But what surprised D was that three young Treeckos suddenly stood up and walked towards Mew and Teddiursa.

Just as D was feeling puzzled, Teddiursa happily explained to him:

"Teddi Teddi!" (These Treeckos are willing to accept our invitation to live on the World Tree!)

D paused for a moment. Is it really that easy to invite three Starter Pokemon from the Hoenn Region?

Is this how the Heroic Expedition Team usually invites Pokemon residents?

D found it extremely novel.

He couldn't help but ask: "What did you say to them?"

Teddiursa replied: "Teddi, Teddi Teddi!" (I introduced them to the World Tree and Explorer Team Guild; they are very interested in the Pokemon Restaurant and Café built on the World Tree, and want to go see it for themselves!)

Upon hearing this, D immediately understood.

These are three Treeckos curious about job opportunities.

The nature of Treecko is calm and collected, able to remain composed in any situation, making them well-suited for jobs requiring patience.

D naturally welcomed their arrival.

And after the banquet on the other side was over, the three Treeckos bid farewell to other tribe members and were taken in by Mew and Teddiursa into the Poke Balls, then finally sent back to the ranch by Zapdos.

D witnessed the entire process with his own eyes.

Watching the Zapdos flying high into the sky, D thought that with Zapdos's speed, it would reach the Paldea Region by noon tomorrow at most.

*

In the afternoon, D set to work addressing the evolution issues of the Eevees.

Nowadays, the ranch already has Glaceon, Umbreon, Vaporeon, Flareon, and Jolteon.

Still lacking Espeon, Leafeon, and Sylveon to form a full Eevee army.

On this bright sunny afternoon, D had the young Eevees line up neatly on the grass, except for his partner Eevee.

The Eevees sat obediently there, their cute little faces filled with anticipation for evolution.

Ogerpon, Alcremie, Gholdengo, Cyclizar, and other Pokemons also stood curiously beside, watching the process.

"So, who wants to go first?" D's gaze swept over the three Eevees.

"Eevee!"

The Eevee on the far left promptly raised its little paw.

D looked at it and asked: "You want to evolve into a Leafeon, right?"

The Eevee nodded repeatedly, its voice soft and cute: "Eevee Eevee!"

The evolution method for Leafeon is quite simple, requiring only a Leaf Stone for direct evolution.

D had prepared the Leaf Stone for Eevee long ago, waiting for this day to come.

He took out a green stone from a small box, the Stone Man engraved with leaf patterns, looking quite unique.

The Eevees' eyes all turned towards the Leaf Stone, their inherited genetic instincts telling them that upon contact with that stone, they would evolve into another form of Eevee.

"Then let's remove the Everstone from you first!" D squatted down and took off the Everstone necklace from Eevee's neck, then placed the Leaf Stone in front of it.

"Eevee..."

Eevee looked down at the stone and reached out with its little paw to touch the Leaf Stone.

And at the moment Eevee touched the stone, a dazzling light erupted from its body.

Within the light, Eevee's appearance rapidly changed.

As the light faded, a new Eevee with a green body and exuding a fresh grass aroma appeared before everyone.

Leafeon was born!

D nodded with satisfaction, it seemed that Eevee's evolution process was fairly smooth.

Next, it was the turn of the next Eevee.

"Espeon, right?" D asked.

The Eevee gently nodded: "Eevee~"

"Hmm, let me think about what needs to be done to help you." D then fell into contemplation.

Compared to Leafeon, Espeon's evolution conditions are more demanding.

Not only does it require a certain friendliness with the Trainer, but it must also be during the day.

Is the friendliness enough?

D felt it should be maxed, after all, these Eevees were raised by him from a young age, and he had hardly left the ranch, spending almost every moment together.

Thinking slightly, D took off the Everstone from Eevee.

The warm sunlight shone on it, but nothing happened.

"It seems the conditions for evolution haven't been met yet..."

It's a pity there are no Rare Candies in this world, if there were, he could feed one to Eevee and it would directly evolve.

Since that's the case, he has to use conventional methods to achieve the goal.

Thinking of this, D called out: "Cyclizar!"

"Vroom!"

Cyclizar immediately stepped forward.

D patted its head and said: "Next, I'm going to ask you to help out, act as a sparring partner for this Eevee, train its combat skills, and help it gain experience, can you do it?"

Cyclizar immediately patted its chest, assuring: "Vroom!"

Cyclizar took Eevee to the side to impart experience.

Meanwhile, D looked at the next Eevee.

---

The Eevee, who wanted to evolve into Sylveon, met his gaze with big round eyes, full of anticipation.

D asked, "Have you mastered Baby-Doll Eyes?"

"Bui." Eevee felt a bit guilty.

D: "..."

Earlier, he had asked Alcremie to help teach Eevee the move Baby-Doll Eyes, but when he wasn't paying attention, Eevee started slacking.

"You!" D sighed helplessly, poking its little head with a finger, then turned around and shouted, "Alcremie!"

"Ma-ma!" Alcremie immediately rushed over.

"Alright! Show Eevee again how to use Baby-Doll Eyes, Eevee, watch carefully this time!" said D.

"Bui!" Eevee quickly nodded.

So, Alcremie began to demonstrate.

Compared to these freshly born Eevee, Alcremie had long been established at the ranch.

Even though it was small in stature, Alcremie still assumed the big sister role it used with Garganacl, to guide Eevee.

"Ma-ma!"

Alcremie focused intensely, staring with round eyes at the little Eevee.

D noticed when using Baby-Doll Eyes, a pink glow appeared in Alcremie's eyes, likely Fairy Type energy.

Eevee got hit by Baby-Doll Eyes, feeling dazed, it sat blankly where it was.

After quite a while, Eevee snapped out of it, quickly shaking its little head to regain clarity.

"Did you see it clearly?" asked D.

"Bui!" Eevee nodded.

Oh? So fast this time?

D felt a bit surprised: "Want to give it a try?"

Eevee nodded again, imitating Alcremie's expression as it focused fiercely, trying to make its eyes round.

However, Baby-Doll Eyes didn't come out, instead it made itself cross-eyed.

D: "..."

Its partner Eevee, witnessing the whole scene, only felt that this companion was so dumb.

It had watched many times and already learned.

Consequently, the partner Eevee ran forward while calling, "Bui bui," using Baby-Doll Eyes on Eevee.

The partner Eevee sighed, "Bui..."

Looking like it meant "You're too dumb, I learned it already!"

Witnessing this, Eevee felt like the sky was falling, tears welled in its eyes, facing the partner's "bragging," and it ran off with a "Bui."

D: "..."

The partner Eevee looked puzzled, its eyes filled with innocence.

"Bui?" What happened?

The other evolved Eevee blinked, exchanging glances at it.

"Bui..." Glaceon sighed, laying sideways on the grass speechlessly.

No hope, these Eevee all seemed quite dim-witted.

D too was momentarily at a loss for words.

He felt it necessary to give this partner Eevee a course.

The course would be called "Emotional Intelligence."

But for now, Eevee's situation was more urgent.

D let the other Pokemon play on their own, while he followed the path Eevee ran off to search for it.

Eventually, D found the distressed Eevee in the storage room behind the Pokemon Tavern.

"Alright, don't be sad." D squatted in front of Eevee, patting its little head to comfort it.

"Bui..." Eevee felt even more aggrieved, it thought it was a fool for not being able to learn Baby-Doll Eyes.

"You must understand, not all Pokemon are geniuses at first!" D began giving Eevee examples: "You know Dragonite?"

"Bui?" Eevee looked at him.

"Dragonite was originally a fool who couldn't learn any moves; later it came to the ranch and, through diligent practice every day, successfully mastered moves that it initially found difficult." D said: "In the human world, there's a saying 'diligence makes up for lack of talent.'

"It means that with diligent practice, one can compensate for natural shortcomings."

"Although I don't see you as a foolish Eevee, you just often slack off during practice."

"Bui?" Eevee quickly asked if not slacking would allow it to learn new moves.

D understood its intention and nodded: "Exactly! I believe you can definitely do it!"

"And even if you can't, it doesn't matter, at worst, we'll keep you as Eevee forever without evolving, I can still raise you for a lifetime."

"Bui..."

Upon hearing this, little Eevee was deeply moved.

But soon, it regained its determination, shaking its head and firmly declared, "Bui!"

It didn't want to stay the same! It also wanted to evolve into a stronger Sylveon, to stay with D as Sylveon!

Decided, starting today it would practice Baby-Doll Eyes diligently, no more slacking like before!

Thinking of this, Eevee never felt clearer internally.

It recalled Alcremie's teaching, deciding to begin practicing Baby-Doll Eyes right now.

First, mobilize the body's Fairy Type energy...

Eevee concentrated and began to exert effort.

D could immediately see Eevee's intentions.

He observed its movements, guiding from the side: "Make sure your mind isn't too tense, relax your expression, and gaze at your opponent with your cutest look!"

Cutest look?

Eevee tried to relax, stared with its round eyes, attempting to give D the cutest gaze.

A pink light flashed through Eevee's pupils, successfully using Baby-Doll Eyes!

Seeing this, D only felt that Eevee was truly adorable.

---

His hands began to itch, eager to lift the fluffy Eevee.

However, the next second, a brilliant white light suddenly erupted from Eevee's body.

Amidst the light, beautiful ribbons sprouted from Eevee's body.

Its fur also changed to a pure white color, while the fur on its head, ears, tail, and lower limbs turned pink.

As the light faded, a brand new Eevee appeared before D.

Sylveon, was born!

D was momentarily taken aback.

It wasn't until Sylveon wrapped its ribbons around his hand, tilting its head up to look at him happily with those blue eyes.

"Eevee!"

Sylveon's ribbons were soft, and D gently pinched them, then smiled and said, "See? I wasn't wrong, was I? You absolutely could do it. Congratulations, Sylveon."

"Eevee～" Sylveon seemed very happy.

Since the matter was resolved, there was no need to stay in the storage room.

D took Sylveon outside.

The other Eevees, upon seeing Sylveon's birth, were all surprised.

Sylveon gently greeted them, joining in to play with the group of Eevees.

D then approached his astonished partner Eevee and said to it, "Sylveon isn't dumb. It just got a bit playful, but your earlier actions hurt it, and Sylveon was really sad."

"Eevee..." The partner Eevee looked over there, it had no ill intentions, it was just a bit anxious earlier...

D patted its little head and asked, "So, do you know what to do?"

The partner Eevee looked up at D, and after a brief eye contact, the partner Eevee nodded, "Eevee!"

In the next moment, it ran towards Sylveon.

D wasn't sure what exactly they said to each other, but he was certain that Sylveon accepted the partner Eevee's apology, and they reconciled.

And at this moment, not far away, the same evolution light shone on an Eevee battling Cyclizar.

The energy of the sun surged into its body like a tide, within this energy, a ruby, dazzlingly bright, formed on Eevee's forehead.

Moments later, a brand new Eevee with light purple eyes and fur, its entire body glowing with a velvety sheen, emerged opposite Cyclizar.

Espeon, was born as well.

Thus, all evolutions of Eevee were unlocked at the ranch.

Looking at the large group of various Eevees over there, D called out to them, "Everyone, come over here!"

Immediately, an array of colorful Eevees came running, surrounding D.

D excitedly petted one and hugged another.

The Eevees obediently sat down, letting him pet them.

To the Eevees, D was the closest person in this world.

Because from the moment they hatched, the first person they saw was D.

Not only that, D would coax them to sleep, make them snacks, tell them bedtime stories, and play with them on the grass... like a father.

Of course, Glaceon and Umbreon wouldn't consider D as a father, because they came to the ranch after they grew up.

Looking at the adorable, fluffy Eevees around him, acting all charming, at this moment, D felt only one thing in his heart.

Such happiness!

---

---

---

---

# 431: Chapter 428: World Tree Candy

The evolution of Eevee made D very happy.

Next, all that's missing is a Z Ring and Eevee's exclusive Z move, and the Eevee army can be fully formed.

By using the partner Eevee and harnessing the power of Extreme Evoboost, all Eevee's strengths are merged, solving Eevee's ultimate move.

Unfortunately, both the Z Ring and exclusive Z move are specialties of Alola, and other regions don't have them.

There are many magical items in the Pokemon World.

Besides the Z Ring, there are Mega Stones and Key Stones for Mega Evolution in the Kalos region, Dynamax in the Galar Region, and Terastal in the Paldea region.

A couple of years ago, Professor Sada and Professor Turo gifted D a Tera Orb.

However, living on the ranch doesn't often require battles, so it wasn't needed, and that Tera Orb was shelved in a drawer by D.

The Dynamax Band is a specialty of the Galar Region and can only be used there; outside the Galar Region, Pokemon cannot perform Dynamax.

Among these, D's favorite is Mega Evolution, also known as Super Evolution.

This form of evolution can unleash the hidden power within a Pokemon in a short time, allowing them to exhibit powers that normal evolution cannot match.

It's worth mentioning that directly making a Pokemon undergo Mega Evolution is not feasible.

Only when Trainer and Pokemon are in sync can they surpass limits for each other's bonds.

Bonds and friendship, such things sound very exciting!

However, to D's regret, none of the many Pokemon on his ranch can undergo Mega Evolution...

Whether it's Dragonite, Butterfree, Ogerpon, or Gogoat, Cyclizar, none of them!

D felt a bit resentful inside.

Darned GF!

Why didn't they give his favorite Pseudo-legendary Pokemon Dragonite a Mega Evolution, even with later generations of Pokemon being limited by the times?

Murmuring a few words, D stood up, ready to look into recipes and see if there are any good dishes to develop.

Just as he was about to enter the house, Keri arrived at the ranch riding a Cyclizar.

She got off the ride and handed a blueprint to D.

"Here, this is the design I drew based on your requirements. See if it's okay. If confirmed, I can start construction tomorrow."

D reached out, took it, and spread open the blueprint, revealing everything in front of him.

In his sight appeared a domed wooden building, with a sign hanging above the entrance of the structure, clearly written in pencil with the words "Exploration Team Guild Headquarters".

Extending from this building, D saw various kinds of facilities on it.

An item shop selling healing medicine and various antidotes, a Pokemon Hospital treating injuries, a Pokemon Tavern for Pokemon gatherings and chats.

Also, a café for resting, a restaurant for dining, adorable dorms, a warehouse storing food and berries.

And around the guild, there are dedicated fields for cultivating crops and food.

Keri divided them into several areas, including an orchard area for berry cultivation and a farmland area for growing vegetables and fruits.

With these places, there's no need to worry about the Pokemon going hungry.

You could tell Keri put a lot of thought into it; the architectural designs she made looked very cute, perfectly matching the beautiful and dreamy nature of Pokemon, just like a fairy tale.

Keri introduced it beside him: "Although these buildings are wooden structures, the timber used is very durable Golden Wood, which, after special treatment, won't deteriorate even after a hundred years."

Golden Wood? D hadn't heard of it. It should be unique to the Pokemon World?

He nodded in satisfaction: "Let's go with this, and all other configurations must be of the highest quality."

The ranch now earns so much daily income that can't even be counted, plus the online store keeps generating continuous earnings, so using top-grade materials is no problem.

Therefore, D intends to go all out.

Keri already knew D was a mysterious yet low-profile hidden master, she nodded and agreed:

"No problem, I will do my best; shall we leave it at that?"

"Sure, thank you for your hard work!"

Keri smiled slightly with a feeling tone: "I can guarantee if Mayor Thomas and the others knew about this, they would be surprised."

D raised an eyebrow.

Keri continued: "It's surprising to have someone as impressive as you come to Porto Marinada Town, this remote countryside, to run a ranch."

D thought that he wasn't like this initially; it all developed slowly over time.

"Let's not tell Mayor Thomas and the others! It wouldn't be good if we startled the old man."

Keri nodded lightly: "Don't worry, I'll keep it secret for you, then let's leave it there; I'll come over for construction tomorrow morning."

"Okay."

With the design confirmed, the next step is construction.

Keri, while drafting construction plans, turned and left D Ranch.

The materials in the store were insufficient, she would need to order from other places later.

*

In the evening, while preparing dinner for the Pokemon, D thought back to what happened this afternoon and suddenly had an idea, could he recreate the Rare Candy from the game?

---

Rare Candy, in the game it's described as a candy full of energy, which raises a Pokemon's level by one when given to it.

If something like that existed in the real world, eating a hundred in a row, wouldn't it turn a Pokemon into one at max level straight away?

If given to Eevees, you could have nine level 100 max-level Eevees, becoming unbeatable worldwide.

With this thought, D felt a stirring inside and immediately decided to give it a try.

Although he didn't know how to make candy, the cookbook he had bought earlier in town had some instructions.

First, pour sugar, corn syrup, and water into a pot, heat over medium heat, and stir continuously.

Once it's about ready, add the golden Honey brewed by the Combees.

The final step is to pour the candy into molds to cool.

Soon, crystal-clear candies, amber-colored, appeared in front of D.

[Honey Candy (C): Candy made with honey, full of the scent of honey.

Cooking Effect: Bear Attraction lv1, when placed in the wild, it easily attracts Bear-type Pokemon, and when fed to Bear-type Pokemon, significantly increases their friendliness.

Rating: Honey-flavored candy, much loved by Bear-type Pokemon.]

D: "..."

Not Rare Candy?

D immediately felt a wave of disappointment.

There aren't many Bear-type Pokemon, besides Teddiursa and its evolution Ursaring, only Cubchoo, Beartic, Stufful, Bewear, Ursaluna, Kubfu, and the two forms of Urshifu remain.

But then, does Pancham count as a Bear-type Pokemon?

There doesn't seem to be Pancham in the Paldea Region.

Seeing that it wasn't the Rare Candy he wanted, D casually fed these Honey Candies to Alcremie, Ogerpon, and other pokemon that liked sweets.

"A Rare Candy should contain a large amount of energy." D murmured.

But the question is, what kind of energy should that be?

Can the energy from a Pokemon's body work?

After a brief thought, D decided to give it a try.

He called for his sister Indeedee and asked it to infuse Psychic Type energy into the unformed syrup.

"Aiyii?"

Although Indeedee was a bit confused, it obediently complied.

As soon as that energy entered the bowl, there was an explosion, scattering sugar and corn syrup everywhere.

D: "..."

"Aiyii!" Indeedee was startled.

It quickly grabbed a cloth to clean up the mess around them.

D crouched down to help, and soon, the kitchen was back to normal, save for a lost bowl.

"Does Pokemon energy not work?" D continued to ponder: "What about something filled with energy?"

Speaking of energetic things, the first thing D thought of was the Tree of Beginning Leaf.

He had once used a Tree of Beginning Leaf to create a Leaf Cake, which significantly boosted Gogoat, Calyrex, and Arboliva.

Calyrex said those leaves contained very rich Natural Energy.

Thinking of this, D went to the Reversed World and picked a leaf bigger than his own height.

After returning to the kitchen, D used a knife to cut it in half and gave the other unused half to the Gogoats to eat.

Then, D mashed the leaf with a machine, filtered out the juice, and mixed it into the pre-prepared syrup.

For the final step, he boiled it in a pot, then poured it into molds to cool and set.

Once everything was completed, a dark green candy appeared in front of D.

[World Tree Candy (A+): Candy made from a Tree of Beginning Leaf filled with rich Natural Energy.

Cooking Effect: Nature Power lv3, significantly enhances Grass Type Pokemon's control over Grass Type Energy, allowing them to better cultivate plants. This effect is only effective for Grass Type Pokemon and can only be consumed once.

Rating: A magical candy, somehow connected with nature!]

D: "..."

It seems he is not destined to have Rare Candy.

However, a candy that enhances a Pokemon's energy control is, in some sense, equivalent to Rare Candy.

Because the more proficient a Pokemon is with energy control, the faster it can progress, even improving attack speed and power.

In conclusion, not bad at all.

D then fed it to Leafeon, Ogerpon, Gogoat, Calyrex, Tropius, and Arboliva.

These were all the Grass Type Pokemon in the pasture.

Besides them, D also reserved three World Tree Candies to give as welcome gifts to the three Treecko who had left their hometown and settled at the World Tree.

After dinner, it was time to sleep.

D originally planned to continue sleeping with the Eevees on the bed, but after evolving, they changed their temperament and habits.

Instead of the room, Vaporeon preferred a place with clear water sources, so it left D early to sleep in a pond on the farm.

Similarly, the evolved Leafeon ran to a lush grassy area, using it as its bed.

Though he was the one who let the two Eevees sleep where they liked, D still felt a strange sadness at their departure.

---

Sigh, the kids have all grown up and have their own lives now.

However, his sadness didn't last long.

Because that lively and enthusiastic Flareon had already jumped onto his chest.

Perhaps sensing D's emotions, Flareon made a few "bui bui" sounds, indicating that it would stay with D!

The Umbreon, Jolteon, Sylveon, and Espeon, all nestled in their little spots, echoed in agreement.

Even Pawmot, Koraidon, and Dratini joined in.

D was moved and hugged the only king, kissing it on the forehead.

Flareon's eyes gleamed brightly and it looked very happy.

"Ah, gaa!"

Koraidon, who was sleeping on the floor, sharp-eyed, noticed this scene and immediately looked over with its big eyes.

Though Koraidon had a fierce appearance, its eyes were now watery, looking like a pitiful big puppy.

D, feeling helpless, could only give this big guy a kiss on the forehead.

As a result, after this kiss, all the other Pokemon also wanted a kiss.

D had to give them each a kiss, and then said to them: "No more shyness, you're all big Pokemon now, still acting cute!"

In response, the Eevees all expressed that they would always be D's babies.

The pink Dratini lying next to D's pillow nodded repeatedly.

And it, and it, it's one too!

D smiled and poked the big nose of Dratini, saying, "Time to sleep!"

All the Pokemon closed their eyes peacefully, ready to enter dreamland.

Meanwhile, D took out his phone and posted the family photo of Eevees he took today on Poke Friend Circle, with a caption expressing his happiness.

Soon, the photo received a bunch of likes.

[Lono: Wow! Have all the Eevees evolved? They look so adorable!]

[Geeta: Looks like you've created your Eevee Paradise!]

[Alder: What a coincidence! My Volcarona has started blossoming again after recovering, and soon it will become a parent too! I'll have to start taking care of Pokemon then!]

[...]

D smiled faintly and replied to each one.

He's been quite busy lately and hasn't watched Lono's livestream much. With that thought, D opened the livestream software, only to find that Lono wasn't streaming right now.

After a bit of thinking, D opened Pichu Tv, searched around, and quickly found Lono's personal account.

Most of the uploaded videos were Lono's replays, like receiving challenges from trainers, with somewhat monotonous content.

Maybe that's why Lono doesn't have many followers right now.

Lono seemed to be still awake over there, as D just clicked on the video and received Lono's greeting message.

After a brief chat, D learned that the nightlife in Levincia City had just begun, and she had just gotten off work and was preparing to go home and rest.

[Lord Wing: Is a Gym Leader's work that tiring?]

[Lono: Yeah! Lately, the Paldea Region has welcomed many trainers from other regions, and not just me, other Gym Leaders are also quite busy!]

Seeing this message, D thought to himself, could it be Calyrex attracting those people?

He had previously seen online that trainers from the Galar Region were declaring their intention to move to the Paldea Region...

Geeta must be overjoyed, right?

D could almost imagine their head Champion's excited face as trainers flock to the Paldea Region.

Geeta standing at the top of the Paldea League, with an excited smile, laughing maniacally: "Talent, haha, talent!"

D: "..." Suddenly felt that Geeta's love for talent almost rivals Steven next door with his rock collection.

---

---

---

# 432: Chapter 429: New Residents, Treecko Arrives at the World Tree!

Sure, here is the translation:

```html

Early the next morning, Keri drove the truck to the ranch.

With the help of the Machamp workers, she moved batch after batch of timber onto the World Tree.

After confirming the location for the guild, Keri's construction team officially began the construction.

Zapdos arrived at the ranch at noon.

After delivering the three Treecko, it left immediately.

The three Treecko calmly looked toward D and the Pokemon beside him.

"Welcome!"

D held a simple welcome ceremony for the Treecko with a group of Pokemon.

At the end of the ceremony, he gave the World Tree candies he made yesterday to the three Treecko as a greeting gift.

Perhaps Bear and Mew had introduced the ranch to them before coming here, so the Treecko were not too reserved.

They looked at the green candies in their palms and happily opened their mouths to eat them.

In the next second, the three Treecko widened their eyes, looking rather shocked.

"Tchafoo?!"

"Do you feel that the Grass Energy inside your body is more abundant?" D asked with a smile.

"Tchafoo!"

Though surprised, the Treecko calmly nodded their heads.

Yes! What exactly is going on here?

They cast puzzled looks at the human before them.

"Then come with me!"

D led the three Treecko across the lake and into the Reversed World.

Before the Treecko could react, they saw an incredibly tall and thick tree, even bigger than the huge tree they lived in before!

"Tchafoo..."

The three Treecko were dazed, muttering.

They couldn't imagine that such a gigantic tree would exist in this world!

If they could live on it, they would be such happy Treecko, wouldn't they?

Images of proliferating on this giant tree, nesting among the dense foliage, filled their minds with happiness.

Their eyes filled with longing and expectation.

Seeing the expression of the three Treecko, D knew they liked the World Tree very much.

"Go up and have a look!"

He led the Treecko across a few Sky Islands, entered the World Tree through the bottom hole, and took the bubble elevator unobstructedly to an area that was like an earthly paradise.

As soon as the Treecko entered, they were stunned by the fullness of energy here.

Such a rich natural atmosphere!

Suddenly, they liked this place even more.

Because no Grass Type Pokemon can refuse to live in a place abundant with natural atmosphere, where there is green grass and trees everywhere.

Not far away, Keri and her construction team were busy working, knocking here and there.

D took the Treecko around and explained to them:

"Mew and Teddiursa did not deceive you. I plan to build a bustling Pokemon Guild on this tree. Though it's only just beginning, it will be completed in a few months."

"Then you can live here peacefully and run the guild with other Pokemon."

The Treecko nodded repeatedly. They were interested in the Explorer Team Guild, so they accepted the invitation of the Heroic Expedition Team to come here.

"So where do you plan to live next? Will you go back to the ranch with me or stay directly on the World Tree?" D asked.

"Tchafoo!" The Treecko didn't hesitate to choose the World Tree.

D nodded: "Alright!"

He glanced around, noting that the World Tree was not yet fully grown, and the Berry Garden and farm had not been developed yet. Only some berries growing in the bushes were edible.

"But there's not much to eat here yet... because the Berry Garden and farm haven't been developed." D said.

However, the Treecko seemed indifferent to it.

They all expressed that they could satisfy themselves just by eating leaves!

D: "..."

Grass Type Pokemon are simple in such matters.

Some Grass Type Pokemon don't even need to eat, living solely on photosynthesis.

The Treecko had no objections, but D couldn't watch them nibble leaves to fill their stomachs, that's too pitiful.

So, after a brief consideration, D decided to let the Treecko stay here, while he returned to the ranch, drove to Porto Marinada Town, and bought a batch of Berry Seedlings from Elo Seed Store.

Next, D enlisted Dugtrio and Calyrex, took tools, and cultivated a tract of land in a pre-divided planting area.

Finally, he planted the batch of Berry Seedlings in the pits.

Once everything was done, it was time for Calyrex to showcase its skills.

With a gentle lift of its right hand, the tender and delicate Berry Seedlings began to grow rapidly.

In the blink of an eye, a dense Berry Forest appeared in front of them.

Every fruit tree had branches full of plump and weighty Berries, with shiny skins that seemed very appetizing.

Witnessing this, the three Treecko's eyes sparkled with incredible light.

"Tchafoo..." Amazing!

Thus, Calyrex's Pokemon followers increased by three.

D said with a smile: "Alright, now there's no problem. If you are hungry, you can freely pick Berries from the trees to eat."

"Tchafoo!" The Treecko nodded repeatedly.

D glanced around, then added: "By the way, there's one more thing."

"Tchafoo?"

"The World Tree is very big, apart from this layer, there are other places with considerable space, but I haven't been to those places yet. You should not go there first, lest you get lost."

"Tchafoo!"

D nodded, knowing this layer's area was already large enough for the Treecko to explore.

The World Tree was not fully grown. This layer was located at the top of the World Tree, covered by dense foliage, so even if the World Tree continues to grow, it will not change.

Other areas are currently unstable and may change later, such as expanding the space within the trunk.

Therefore, D planned to wait until the World Tree fully matured before exploring those areas, to see what the environment was like, and find suitable Pokemon habitats.

Pokemon have a wide variety of attributes and types, and they inhabit diverse environments.

Fire Type Pokemon prefer hot volcanic places, Grass Type Pokemon like areas rich in trees and grass, Water Type Pokemon prefer clean water sources.

In addition, there are Ground and Rock Pokemon who like mountains, Ice Type Pokemon who prefer icy and snowy places, etc.

Those places need to be specially created later, but D isn't in a hurry. He has plenty of time and can take his time.

---
```
