# 433: Chapter 430: Fishing Tournament, vs. Fishing Celebrity!

With the Berry Garden, Treecko can safely settle in the World Tree.

But the Berry Garden also needs Pokemon to take care of it regularly...

D thought about it carefully, then found Arboliva, who was hiding as a tree in the pasture, to assign this task to it.

Arboliva usually works with Gogoat to cultivate crops and has rich experience using Grass Energy to care for the garden.

Upon hearing D needed help, Arboliva immediately agreed and went to the World Tree to start taking care of the Berry Garden there.

Along with it went the Tropius that D had rescued earlier.

Thus, the World Tree welcomed its first batch of inhabitants.

After a brief introduction by D, Arboliva, Tropius, and the three Treecko quickly became familiar with each other.

The Treecko spent half a day exploring this piece of woodland, only returning to the guild after they became familiar with the surrounding terrain and environment.

The Treecko are Grass Type Pokemon and can somewhat help take care of crops and trees.

Thus, they surrounded Arboliva, learning from it how to use Grass Energy to tend plants and helping Arboliva take care of the Berry Garden that could bear fruit.

Arboliva is a Pokemon with a loving heart, and it taught the Treecko earnestly since they were keen on learning.

Because, barring any accidents, from now on, this place would be where they live together.

Everyone should help each other, like the Pokemon partners in the pasture, collectively building a home for everyone.

Tropius went to the tranquil and beautiful lake next to the guild.

Occasionally, when passing by, one could see Tropius lowering its long neck to drink.

The lake is the only water source nearby, frequented not just by Tropius but also Arboliva and the Treecko for water.

When Tropius saw them, it would enthusiastically share the bananas growing on its neck.

The effect of the [Fried Banana] dish combined with Tropius' Harvest ability meant the fruits quickly regrew after being given away.

Initially, the Treecko were not quite accustomed to Tropius's enthusiastic sharing.

Because in their previous habitat, it was common for Treecko to help each other.

But it wasn't common with other Pokemon.

Thus, the three Treecko awkwardly accepted the bananas from Tropius.

As a return gift, they offered the berries they found in the bushes.

Tropius happily accepted, lowering its head to gobble up the berries with its mouth.

Seeing that Tropius liked the gifts they gave, the Treecko also smiled happily.

For a moment, the atmosphere in the World Tree was so harmonious and friendly.

Even Keri was amazed by this scene.

"These Pokemon are so harmonious!" Keri couldn't help saying.

D smiled slightly: "Most Pokemon in the world are very kind-hearted. Not only do Pokemon help each other, but I've also seen wild Pokemon actively helping humans!"

For example, Zarude in the movie "Pokémon Coco" saved a human baby, Coco, from danger and adopted it.

Keri agreed: "It's wonderful to have Pokemon like these in the world!"

D nodded softly: "Yes, indeed!"

*

League Year 200, March 7th, Spring.

It's the day of the Porto Marinada Town Spring Fishing Competition.

D had received Mayor Thomas's invitation two days ago, and after finishing his work on the farm, he prepared to ride Koraidon to Casseroya Lake to participate in the fishing competition.

At 7:30 in the morning, Keri's construction team arrived at the ranch.

Seeing D and Koraidon, Keri paused, then asked, "Are you going to the fishing competition?"

"Yes!" D smiled and nodded: "I missed it the past two years. Now that I have time today, I plan to go and have a look."

"Then I wish you good luck!"

With that, Keri unloaded the prepared Carrot House from the truck.

"The Carrot House you asked me to custom-make two days ago is ready. See if you're satisfied?"

D turned his head and saw two Machamp carrying an enormous carrot approaching him.

That carrot was the giant crop he had sent to Keri's workshop for processing. After being handled, it looked completely different.

The giant carrot was cut in half, with a special material base at the bottom. The carrot had a small door and two windows on top, looking just like the carrot house out of a fairy tale where a bunny lived.

D really liked it and called over the Eevees to ask what they thought of it.

But before D could speak, he realized something was wrong.

After evolving, the Eevees had grown in size, and the current Carrot House couldn't accommodate them anymore.

At most, it could only fit two or three...

At this moment, a group of Eevees crowded at the door of the Carrot House, with the leading partner Eevee squeezing in and crouching inside.

Flareon and Jolteon followed closely, and with a Sylveon added, the Carrot House was completely packed.

---

The remaining Eevee stood outside, pacing back and forth, staring curiously at this little house made out of a giant radish.

"Can't fit all the Eevee in there..." D muttered. He thought about it for a moment, then decided to leave it as is for now.

Later he'd see if he could grow some huge pumpkins or something like that, and use one to make a pumpkin carriage big enough for all the Eevee.

"Then you guys take your time playing here, I'm heading out for a bit!" The fishing tournament was about to start; he had to go.

"Eevee!" The playful Eevee had been completely captivated by the Radish House. They answered absentmindedly, basically saying, "Yeah yeah, go ahead, we can play by ourselves!"

D: "..."

Keri covered her lips and smiled on the side.

D let out a helpless sigh and said to Keri, "I'm heading out then. Thanks for your hard work today too!"

Keri nodded lightly. "Mm, we're about to start construction as well."

The two said their goodbyes and parted ways. Keri headed to the World Tree to build the Explorer Team Guild, while D rode Koraidon and set off for Casseroya Lake.

The distance between Casseroya Lake and Porto Marinada Town wasn't far; it was just to the north of Porto Marinada Town.

At Koraidon's speed, they could get there in just a few minutes.

By the time D arrived, there was already quite a crowd gathered by Casseroya Lake.

Among them were quite a few hardcore anglers in work vests and baseball caps, fishing rods in hand.

Koraidon was extremely eye-catching. Even before D and Koraidon landed, they had already drawn a lot of attention.

Mayor Thomas had been chatting with a middle‑aged man in a floral shirt. When he saw D coming over, he immediately said with a beaming smile:

"Ah, D's here! He's a very famous fishing ace in our Porto Marinada Town!"

D had just landed when he heard this line from Mayor Thomas.

Sure enough, as soon as those words came out, all the anglers around instantly focused their gazes on him.

D: "..."

Mayor Thomas and the man in the floral shirt walked over. Mayor Thomas introduced:

"This is Sato Ken, a very famous fishing celebrity in the Paldea Region. He heard you were entering this year's fishing tournament and made a special trip from Cascarrafa City to compete with you in fishing skills!"

Sato Ken reached out his hand. "I am the man known as the one who conquered Gyarados, Sato Ken! Young friend, allow me to witness your transcendent angling skills!"

D: "?"

This—this was way too over the top, wasn't it?

Mayor Thomas chuckled as he added from the side, "When Mr. Sato Ken was out fishing at sea, he once caught a powerful Gyarados and conquered it with his own skills!"

Feeling a bit conflicted inside, D reached out to shake his hand. "Hello, Mr. Sato."

Sato Ken said excitedly, "Then next, let's have a good match!"

D: "..."

"Now that all the contestants are here, would everyone please take your positions!"

Mayor Thomas said to the crowd:

"The fishing tournament will last three hours. Within those three hours, whoever catches the most fish and the rarest fish will become the Champion of the tournament and receive the Angling Celebrity Medal awarded by the Fishing Association!"

The anglers all stared like tigers eyeing prey; it looked like they were dead set on that Angling Celebrity Medal.

D swept his gaze around and saw that there were both men and women, with ages ranging from around ten to several dozen years old.

"Then, this year's fishing tournament officially begins!"

With Mayor Thomas's command, the anglers all rushed to the shore of Casseroya Lake to claim their fishing spots.

The fishing gear and bait for the tournament were standardized, all provided by the Fishing Association, so what kind of fish you could catch came entirely down to your luck and skill.

D settled himself on a small stool, and the man who had conquered Gyarados just happened to be sitting not far to his side, separated by several dozen meters.

He picked up the rod, straightened out the line, put on the bait, then cast the line—his movements were smooth and continuous in one go.

All that was left now was to wait patiently.

Time ticked by second by second. All the anglers wore focused expressions, holding their breath and concentrating.

To outsiders, this event was extremely boring, so before long, Koraidon, who was standing beside D, let out a drowsy Yawn, then lay down by his side and began to Sleep.

No one knew how much time had passed when, all of a sudden, ripples spread across the surface of the water not far away.

D turned his head and saw Sato Ken begin reeling in at high speed.

The fish that had bitten the hook seemed to realize the danger and immediately tried to flee in the opposite direction.

Seeing this, Sato Ken instantly sank his hips, locking his lower body in place and stabilizing his center of gravity.

Man and fish immediately fell into a stalemate.

Time passed second by second. Compared to that fish, the experienced angler Sato Ken was clearly far more patient.

As soon as the tension on the line eased even a little, Sato Ken could feel that the fish was already exhausted.

Now's his chance!

His arms suddenly exploded with strength, and a splash arced across the surface of the water. A Veluza was yanked out.

"It's Veluza!"

The onlookers recognized that Pokemon at a glance and all let out cries of amazement.

"As expected of a fishing celebrity! Amazing, he actually managed to catch a bruiser like Veluza!"

Veluza, a Water Type and Psychic Type Pokemon. There were a ton of them and they were extremely aggressive, attacking any creature that entered Casseroya Lake without discrimination, so people called them the bullies of Casseroya Lake.

However, the Veluza that had been hauled up did not submit right away.

---

Its gaze locked onto the human fishing, its agile form swiftly charging towards him.

Veluza used Tackle.

Sato Ken seemed to anticipate this, and just as Veluza was about to hit him, he suddenly sidestepped and evaded, shouting loudly, followed by a swift karate chop.

"Ha!"

Veluza took a heavy blow and fell to the ground, twisting its body.

Sato Ken quickly took out a Poke Ball and captured Veluza inside.

The Poke Ball shook violently, perhaps because a lot of energy was spent in the battle just now, gradually, the shaking of the Poke Ball grew smaller and smaller, until it stopped completely.

This signaled a successful capture.

D watched the entire process in stunned silence.

Oh my! This is way too hardcore!

A fight between flesh and Pokemon?

Did this guy use the same method to conquer Gyarados back then?

Seeming to notice his stunned gaze, Sato Ken, meters away, turned his head and glanced at him, grinning:

"As a legendary fisherman, I catch not just normal fish, but all kinds of Fish-type Pokemon too! Only a legendary fisherman who can conquer all fish can become the grandmaster of fishing!"

D was speechless for a moment.

Conquer all fish... does that include the Sea God, Kyogre?

Sato Ken exclaimed excitedly: "How far can you go? Let me witness it myself!"

As he finished speaking, D's fishing float suddenly moved twice.

A fish took the bait!

D immediately refocused his attention, and at that moment, everyone's gaze around him turned towards his direction.

What kind of fish would it be?

D initially thought he would have to expend a lot of effort like Sato Ken to reel the fish in, but he was surprised to find that the fish didn't require much effort from him at all!

The transparent fishing line was pulled from the surface of the lake, and at the end of the line, was tied a small, sushi-like Pokemon.

That's... Tatsugiri?

D reeled in the hook and cradled the pale orange Tatsugiri in both hands, bewildered, staring at it.

Tatsugiri hopped twice, curiously looking at the human in front of it.

Normally, it should feel scared and turn to flee at this moment.

But the human emitted an aura that felt very congenial to it, for a moment, Tatsugiri forgot to escape, gazing with its big eyes into those of the human.

"It's Tatsugiri!"

The crowd quickly recognized the Pokemon.

Tatsugiri, a Dragon + Water-type Pokemon, known for being very cunning and deceitful, would inflate its throat pouch to mimic.

Even more remarkable, although Tatsugiri is small and lacking in strength, it knows how to use its intelligence to survive.

Even among Dragon-type Pokemon, Tatsugiri's intelligence is top-notch!

They live inside Dondozo's mouth, protecting themselves from external threats, and not only that, they command Dondozo, working together to catch prey!

And for the smart Tatsugiri, Dondozo even voluntarily regards it as a leader with respect.

"That's amazing!" someone exclaimed in astonishment.

It's known that Tatsugiri, being such a crafty Pokemon, is very difficult to catch!

Sometimes, even if you catch a hundred reckless Veluza, you might not catch a single Tatsugiri.

Upon seeing the Tatsugiri, Sato Ken couldn't help but show a serious expression.

"No wonder you're the renowned fishing champion here in Porto Marinada Town! It seems I must give it my all!"

D: "..." He could only smile politely.

Looking at the Tatsugiri in his palm, D was at a loss for words.

Because through the Dragon Power's perception, he realized that Tatsugiri had intentionally bitten his hook, allowing itself to be caught!

---

---

---

---

# 434: Chapter 431: Fishing Master and Milotic

Tatsugiri raised its head high, looking adorably at the humans in front of it.

D glanced at the Poke Ball containing the Fish-type Pokemon beside him, wondering if such a docile Tatsugiri really needed to be stored in a Poke Ball.

After thinking for a moment, he placed the Tatsugiri into a small bucket filled with water nearby.

"Stay here obediently, okay!"

With a splash, Tatsugiri slid into the water bucket.

D turned around and continued fishing, occasionally checking back to find it indeed waiting obediently in the bucket.

Tatsugiri poked half its head out of the water, peering outside.

I have to admit, Tatsugiri looks quite cute like this.

D flashed it a slight smile, then turned back to focus on fishing.

The fishing competition continued.

Suddenly, a loud splash was heard from the direction of a girl in a short skirt not far away.

The surrounding anglers turned their heads in unison to see a large, fat catfish-like Pokemon leaping out of the water.

"Oh my! Another contestant after Sato Ken and D has caught a Fish-type Pokemon!"

"Can Ming Lan handle it and conquer that Dondozo? Go Ming Lan!"

Spectators on the shore cheered for the girl named Ming Lan.

But the Dondozo was simply too strong, far beyond what she could handle.

Despite Ming Lan's efforts to reel it in, the Dondozo eventually escaped, nearly dragging her fishing rod away with it.

Witnessing this, people sighed with regret.

"What a pity!"

"Fishing requires patience and technique. You must fish without damaging your rod; brute force won't catch rare fish," Sato Ken said calmly.

Just as he finished speaking, the float on the water in front of him bobbed.

Ripples radiated from the fishing line's center, spreading outward.

Sato Ken's gaze suddenly sharpened as he maintained a steady rhythm in reeling in. Soon, he caught a regular river fish.

This time, it wasn't a rare Fish-type Pokemon, but Sato Ken wasn't discouraged. He placed the fish into the bucket, then sat back on his stool, continuing to fish.

Sato Ken looked like a master detached from worldly concerns, which left D momentarily stunned, realizing the other truly had skill.

Determined, he also decided to get serious.

Here comes a fish!

The float on the water bobbed, appearing quite heavy, suggesting a big catch!

What kind of fish would be caught this time?

Judging by the weight, could it be a Dondozo?

Recalling the Dondozo caught by the girl earlier, D prepared his stance to reel it in.

Because Dondozo is incredibly reckless, even if not too smart, it's really strong.

If he wasn't ready, Dondozo's struggles might drag him into the water.

D exerted power in his arms, starting to reel in forcefully.

To his surprise, the fish didn't struggle at all upon being hooked.

D was left bewildered.

Was it not a Dondozo?

Soon, he found the answer to his question.

What he caught wasn't a Dondozo, but a Pokemon with a pink body, chubby figure, two curly ears, and a creamy mouth.

The Pokemon was biting the hook tightly with a dazed expression, its long pink tail hanging down.

Is this... a Slowpoke?!

D was dumbfounded.

What's going on? Slowpoke isn't supposed to live on riverbanks?

Soon, others noticed the scene on his side.

"Wow! How surprising, D caught a Slowpoke!"

Some spectators attending the fishing competition for the first time looked confused: "Is Slowpoke considered a Fish-type Pokemon?"

"In a conventional sense, not really. But under the fishing competition's rules, whether you catch regular fish or Pokemon, each counts as a point! If it's rare enough, one could be worth several!"

"That's some decent luck," Sato Ken observed the Slowpoke.

According to him, Slowpokes usually spend their days idly lying by the sea and rivers, often spacing out, seldom venturing into the water.

But that doesn't mean it never happens.

Some Slowpokes float on the water with their companions, lost in thought, allowing the currents to carry them afar.

D: "..."

He helped free the Slowpoke from the hook, placing it on the grass nearby.

Throughout the process, the Slowpoke remained unresponsive, staring blankly, looking rather dumb.

In the end, it sat beside D and spaced out.

This is Slowpoke?

Its name certainly suits it...

Shaking his head, D cast his fishing rod again, ready to catch the next fish.

Time ticked by; gradually, other anglers also caught quite a few fish.

There were ordinary river fish and some Fish-type Pokemon.

Common ones in the river were Magikarp and Feebas, among others.

Though some lucky ones caught Dondozo, they all managed to escape.

Soon, three hours had passed, leaving only ten minutes.

During this time, Sato Ken and D caught several fish and Pokemon.

The fishing competition organizer glanced at the time, then announced to the crowd: "Attention all participants! There are ten minutes left until the fishing competition ends!"

Ten minutes, huh? D calculated in his mind that there might still be time to catch one last fish.

Just then, there was some commotion from Sato Ken's side.

On the water's surface, violent whirlpools appeared, as if some gigantic creature was struggling frantically.

A big catch!

Sato Ken felt the power coming from the fishing rod and his expression suddenly turned serious.

He exerted his arm forcefully, stabilizing the rod to prevent the fish from breaking free.

He then started reeling it in continuously, and when it was close enough, Sato Ken pulled with all his might.

The next moment, a massive Dondozo leaped high out of the lake.

Its figure blocked out the sun, casting Sato Ken in its shadow.

"Another Dondozo!" The onlookers widened their eyes.

"This is already the eighth contestant today to catch a Dondozo! Unfortunately, all the previous ones failed. I wonder if Mr. Sato Ken can succeed."

"His chances are high, after all, Mr. Sato Ken once conquered a wild Gyarados!"

Amid the crowd's discussions, Sato Ken's expression remained tense.

Clearly, he couldn't afford to be careless against this powerful Dondozo.

The Dondozo eventually landed on the riverside ground, angrily scanning its surroundings before opening its mouth to unleash a Water Gun attack right at its originator.

The stream of water condensed into a water column and shot out at high speed.

Fortunately, Sato Ken anticipated this and quickly sidestepped to dodge.

Taking advantage of the Dondozo's attack interval, Sato Ken swiftly stepped forward, climbed onto Dondozo's back, and grabbed its long catfish whiskers tightly, pressing it firmly underneath him.

With its whiskers restricted and stimulated, the Dondozo started thrashing wildly on the spot.

However, being on land instead of water, the Dondozo, naturally at a disadvantage, couldn't fully exert its power.

The Dondozo struggled while Sato Ken held it down firmly.

The man and the Pokemon soon fell into a stalemate.

What followed was a test of endurance between the two, to see who would hold out longer.

D was left gawking at the scene.

Is this how fishing is done? How did it turn into a battle with the fish?

Then again, the physical prowess of people in the Pokemon World is quite impressive!

As time ticked by, the Dondozo's struggles weakened until it finally lay exhausted on the grassy ground, panting heavily.

It seemed to have admitted defeat to the human...

Seeing this, Sato Ken knew he had succeeded.

He breathed a sigh of relief, took out a new Poke Ball, and captured the Dondozo with it.

Perhaps due to Dondozo's exhaustion, the Poke Ball only shook a few times before settling down.

"Clap, clap, clap!"

The nervous onlookers by the shore broke into applause.

"As expected of Mr. Sato Ken!"

"Amazing! He actually subdued the Dondozo!"

D was also greatly impressed by him. If he were to climb onto a Pokemon's back to subdue it like that, he couldn't do it.

There's still a little over a minute before the competition ends; such a short time is unlikely to be enough to catch another fish.

Moreover, since someone has already caught a Dondozo, others figured they had no chance of winning, so they began to pack up their rods and prepare for the judging later.

D also planned to do the same, but just then, the float on the water's surface suddenly bobbed again.

This means another fish has taken the bait!

D was slightly taken aback, but his hands instinctively moved to action.

With the sound of splashing, a slender figure leaped from the lake surface.

Droplets of water scattered from it, and under the sunlight, they refracted into a beautiful rainbow-like glow.

This unexpected development immediately caught everyone's attention around.

As soon as people saw clearly the silhouette that was pulled up, they jumped up in shock, lifting their heads with astonishment, their gaze incredulously fixed on that Pokemon.

It was a slender-bodied Pokemon with a creamy-colored body, eyes like enchanting rubies, and scales of blue and pink covering its body.

Its appearance was exceedingly beautiful, the scales shimmering under the sunlight, and its streamlined slender body looked so graceful and captivating.

"Muuu~"

With a melodious cry, that Pokemon broke free from the hook and returned to the lake.

"Milotic..." Sato Ken dazedly uttered the name of that Pokemon.

For every angler, Milotic is like a dreamlike presence of a Pokemon.

Because they are just too rare.

"D, quickly catch it!" Sato Ken hurriedly shouted to remind him.

The stunned D snapped back to his senses, overwhelmed by the Milotic.

Capture it? How should he capture it?

Should he really climb onto Milotic and hold it down like before?

No matter! Just throw the ball directly!

D gritted his teeth and forcefully threw the Poke Ball in his hand.

The next second, the Poke Ball hit Milotic, whose body transformed into a red stream of light, getting absorbed into the Poke Ball.

"Buzz—buzz!"

The Poke Ball, lying on the lake surface, began shaking incessantly.

One second, two seconds, three seconds...

Everyone held their breath, tensely watching the scene unfold.

D didn't hold much hope for capturing Milotic with a single throw.

Because no matter how he thought about it, it seemed impossible; he's not a prodigy at capturing Pokemon with one ball, nor does he have any bond with Milotic, as they are still strangers to each other.

---

Milotic would definitely break free from the Poke Ball, then turn around and swim away, disappearing over the lake surface.

D thought so in his heart,

however, at that moment, the Poke Ball floating on the water suddenly stopped moving.

Upon seeing that scene, the onlookers immediately let out a triumphant cheer.

"Yes! Successfully caught it!"

"Mr. D is amazing! He actually caught such a rare Pokemon like Milotic!"

Sato Ken also breathed a sigh of relief.

Compared to that Milotic, the Dondozo and Veluza he caught were somewhat inferior.

It seems the champion of this fishing contest has already emerged.

But this time, he accepted his loss gracefully!

While D's mind was buzzing, could it be that the Milotic he caught recognized him?!

What is going on?

The Poke Ball containing Milotic was washed to the shore by the waters of Casseroya Lake, and D, puzzled, walked over and picked it up.

Pressing the button on the Poke Ball, accompanied by a flash of white light, the Milotic just appeared again in front of everyone.

It didn't turn and flee, but gazed over gently.

Three exclamation marks popped over D's head suddenly.

Could the Poke Ball have a brainwashing function?!

"Milotic?" he tried to call out.

Milotic gazed at the human in front of it, with a hint of curiosity in its eyes.

He had a very strong aura of nature!

Born in nature, Milotic naturally felt an affinity for this aura.

Those chosen by nature cannot be bad people!

D now didn't have any Translation Nuts Cake to communicate with Milotic, but seeing it seemed to have no intention of leaving, he turned around, preparing to take the Pokemon he caught today for the final evaluation.

The other fishing enthusiasts around looked at that Milotic, their eyes full of envy.

"It seems everyone had a fruitful day!" Mayor Thomas smiled and walked forward, "So next, let me see what kinds of fish everyone has caught."

The final results of the fishing competition are judged based on points.

There are further subdivisions, such as ordinary fish being one point, while a Fish-type Pokemon is two points.

If the type of Fish-type Pokemon caught is rare, then the points will be even higher.

The sum of these points is the final score for these contestants.

Mayor Thomas and the judges began checking the fish caught by each contestant.

While it wasn't his turn yet, D looked down at his catch today.

Three Tatsugiri, two Chewtle, one Slowpoke, a bunch of bass and shrimp, and a Milotic.

Adding these up, they should earn quite a few points, right?

D turned to look at Sato Ken not far away.

He also caught a lot.

Namely, four Veluza, five Magikarp, one Dondozo, and a bunch of ordinary river fish.

"Contestant Sato Ken, the final score is 42 points!" Mayor Thomas checked his Fish-type Pokemon and then announced.

Compared to others, this score was already very high.

Recalling how Sato Ken used his physical prowess to conquer Pokemon earlier, D felt genuine admiration.

He truly is a dazzling Fishing Master!

And at this moment, Mayor Thomas arrived beside D.

After a careful inspection, he smilingly announced the final result:

"Contestant D, the final score is 52 points! By the way, this is the first time someone has caught a Milotic."

52 points, a whole ten points more than Sato Ken.

D unsurprisingly won this year's fishing contest, and received the champion medal awarded by the Fishing Association.

Afterwards, Sato Ken approached D.

"I concede my loss wholeheartedly." Sato Ken felt genuine admiration.

D: "..."

"No." He shook his head, "I just had better luck."

Combining Dragon Power and Paldea Seafood Rice, he had more advantages than other contestants.

It is Sato Ken who is the truly skilled Fishing Master.

After hearing those words, Sato Ken smiled and shook his head: "Sometimes, luck is also a part of strength. I'm really glad to compete with you in today's fishing contest. If there's a chance in the future, let's head into the vast ocean to fish together!"

Compared to inland lakes, the number of Fish-type Pokemon inhabiting the endless ocean is much larger.

To fishing enthusiasts, it is the best fishing spot in the world.

D: "..."

If they go to sea to fish, he's worried he might accidentally catch the little Kyogre that Lana had caught.

After all, he's already witnessed the effects of Paldea Seafood Rice firsthand.

But D still responded, "Okay."

Sato Ken then satisfiedly smiled: "Then when the time comes, let us conquer the sea's Fish-type Pokemon together!"

The fishing contest concluded, and to preserve Casseroya Lake's ecosystem, some fishermen chose to release the Pokemon they caught.

And D turned his gaze to the Pokemon he caught.

The three Tatsugiri poked their heads out from the bucket, the two Chewtle clung to the edges of the small barrel stubbornly, while Slowpoke continued to daze out there.

Milotic stood on the grass, looking over.

D smiled and said, "Thanks for today, because of your willingness to bite the hook, I was able to win the fishing competition. Now that the competition is over, if you want to return, feel free to go back!"

---

As he spoke, D placed the small bucket by the riverside.

The two Chewtle were forced to release their mouths, looking around before swimming into the lake and disappearing from sight.

The three Tatsugiri remained in the small bucket, jumping a bit, while Slowpoke continued to daydream.

It seemed these Pokemon had no intention of leaving.

If that's the case, he might as well take them back to live on the World Tree.

With this thought, D turned his gaze to Milotic.

He took out his Rotom Phone from his pocket, asking the Rotom inside for translation help.

"Milotic, do you also plan to follow me?"

"Muuu~" Milotic gently nodded.

D was even more surprised.

He asked curiously, "Why did you choose me?"

Milotic: "Muuu~"

Rotom helped translate: "Milotic said [because you have a nice smell] Roto!"

Huh? What smell?

D hurriedly lowered his head to sniff himself, but he didn't smell anything.

"What do you mean?" he asked.

Milotic hesitantly described, "Muuu..." (A bit like the wind, and somewhat like green leaves, muu, I don't know how to say...)

D felt a bit lost.

But soon, a bold guess popped into his mind.

Could it be that the scent Milotic detected is from the World Tree?

Moreover, he had eaten dishes prepared with the leaves of the World Tree before... So, could this Milotic have willingly chosen him because of this reason?

Is the attraction of the World Tree really that strong for ordinary Pokemon?

D truly hadn't anticipated this situation.

He recalled the reactions Zapdos and Calyrex had upon seeing the World Tree, and felt he found the answer.

It seemed the natural ambiance of the World Tree was indeed highly favored by Pokemon.

After a brief pondering, D asked again, "So, do you want to go back with me?"

Milotic gladly nodded.

The fishing contest had ended, and people were gradually dispersing.

D put Milotic into the Poke Ball, then picked up Slowpoke, grabbed the bucket containing the Tatsugiri, and returned to the ranch.

Upon arrival, he went directly to the World Tree, reaching the lake on its top floor.

"Come out, Milotic!"

With a flash of white light, Milotic's figure landed in the lake water.

Feeling the even denser natural aura around, Milotic was full of wonder.

Then, D released the three Tatsugiri and the Slowpoke.

Milotic, with a gentle personality, disliked fighting.

The Tatsugiri were weak in strength, rarely provoking other Pokemon.

And Slowpoke, true to its name, spent the whole day dazing by the shore, contemplating life.

These Pokemon wouldn't likely cause conflicts with others, and should easily become friends with the Heroic Expedition Team.

Moreover, Milotic and the Tatsugiri could even help with watering the crops at the World Tree.

"This is inside the World Tree," D introduced to the Pokemon before him, "I plan to build an Explorer Team Guild here where all Pokemon can coexist harmoniously and help one another. You're welcome to join!"

The Tatsugiri poked their heads out of the water, and Milotic blinked its eyes.

They seemed very curious about the so-called Explorer Team Guild.

D smiled, then called over Arboliva, saying, "Arboliva, these Pokemon are new companions, I'll leave the introduction of this place to you."

Arboliva happily agreed.

As there was still work to be done on the ranch, D left for now.

After D departed, Arboliva gently introduced the new companions to the surroundings of the World Tree.

This included the Heroic Expedition Team, the Explorer Team Guild, the Pokemon Restaurant, and the Pokemon Fruit Garden and Farmland.

Soon, the Treecko were also drawn over.

They curiously observed the new companions.

Arboliva continued introducing, "Li~Li!" (Once this place is built, everyone will live happily together~ The Treecko want to work as waiters in the café. If any of you are interested in jobs, you can help too!)

The Tatsugiri bounced excitedly on the spot.

They said, "Mi!" (We want to be the sushi shop owners!)

Milotic's eyes sparkled, finding this place wonderful since it doesn't like conflicts!

"Muuu!" (Then I'll help with the watering!) Milotic said.

Arboliva nodded gently, speaking kindly: "Li~" (From now on, we're companions, let's look after each other~)

As for Slowpoke, it continued dazing by the bank.

--

Attached is a picture of Mew's secret base.

---

# 435: Chapter 432: The Shock Caused by Dragonite Delivery and Life Sheep Milk!

With so many Pokemon joining, the World Tree over there has gradually started to operate smoothly.

When Mew and Teddiursa come back, they'll surely be surprised to see the completed expedition team base.

D eagerly anticipates the expressions of the Pokemon upon seeing that scene.

Recalling how Milotic was attracted to the World Tree today, D suddenly realized that the World Tree has immense benefits for ordinary Pokemon.

In terms of a cultivation novel's description, the World Tree is brimming with "spiritual power," rich in "immortal energy," making it a sacred place that countless cultivators dream of residing in for long-term training.

And Pokemon are akin to cultivators; for Pokemon born in nature, they will prefer places rich in natural aura.

Having realized this, D returned to the ranch, ate a few pieces of Translation Nuts Cake, and then gathered all the Pokemon in the ranch.

His gaze swept over the faces of the group of Pokemon before him, and he asked:

"The World Tree over there is almost built. Does anyone want to go and live there?"

Upon hearing this, Alcremie immediately asked.

"Mama?" (Will D live with us on the World Tree?)

D looked at Alcremie, and with a smile answered: "No, I won't be going over for now. That's a place I've specifically built for you Pokemon, where you can live comfortably."

The ranch is his lifelong passion, built up bit by bit with his own hands.

He and the Pokemon have lived here for several years, and it is full of their memories.

D has accumulated deep feelings for this place; he cannot bear to leave.

"Mama..." (I see...) Alcremie pondered carefully, then happily said: "Mama!" (If D is not going, then neither am I!)

"Bamor."

"Boni～"

"Gao!"

"Awoo～"

"Agassi."

"..."

Pawmot, Ogerpon, Cyclizar, Dragonite, Koraidon, and other Pokemon nodded in agreement alongside.

Indeed, they will not go either; wherever D is, they wish to be there as well!

Seeing this, D couldn't help but laugh: "I'm not suggesting you leave! I just thought the World Tree would be more suitable for you to live, and it's so close to the ranch, just a few steps away."

"Bamor!"

The Pokemon still didn't want to go; they wished to live with D continuously.

This deeply moved D; these adorable Pokemon… they were not raised in vain!

Since they all don't want to leave the ranch, D abandoned the idea of having them settle on the World Tree.

At the same time, he began to busy himself with Dragonite Delivery, as the development of Dragonite Delivery brought some problems in the online store's operations.

For instance, someone specifically left messages on the store's message board, asking if Dragonite Delivery supports cross-regional shipping.

At first, D replied with [Sorry, the store currently does not offer cross-regional delivery service.]

But later, more and more people inquired, eventually even jointly petitioning the owner to start cross-regional delivery, willing to pay extra postage.

D felt that other regions were full of Pokemon poachers and evil organizations, unsafe for Dragonite, so he had not started this service.

Team Rocket in Kanto and Johto Regions, Team Aqua and Team Magma in Hoenn Region, Team Galactic and Pokemon Hunters in Sinnoh Region...

Upon careful consideration, except for Team Yell in Galar and Team Star in Paldea Region, other regions' villainous organizations somewhat engage in snatching rare and precious Pokemon.

Members of Team Yell are Pokemon Trainers from Spikemuth Town and avid fans of Marnie.

These individuals, aiming to help Marnie ascend to the Champion throne, often occupy inns to hinder challengers, seize transport tools, and shout interference during Gym battles.

Even so, Team Yell never seizes others' Pokemon.

And as for Team Star, it is even more needless to say...

At this moment in time, Team Star has not even begun to appear.

Precisely because of the security issues in those regions, D has long been unwilling to agree to start cross-regional delivery service.

Now, the number of inquiries has grown, with almost all of them being from other regions.

Staring at the overnight increase of over a thousand messages on the message board, D was bewildered.

Until he inquired carefully a customer who had left a message, he learned what had happened and why suddenly so many people came.

*

As the saying goes, getting started is difficult and the initial steps are hard.

After passing the initial period of obscurity, Dragonite Delivery and D Ranch's store quickly gained a bit of fame online.

The reason being, no one had ever used a Pokemon like Dragonite for delivery before.

This shocking news spread from person to person, ultimately fermenting a bit on the Pokemon Forum.

[Shock! It's common for online stores to use Pelipper for deliveries, but have you ever seen a store using a Pokemon like Dragonite for delivery?!]

The poster detailed their experience with Dragonite Delivery.

[The poster is a homebody; generally speaking, homebodies don't like going out, I stay home every day with my Mimikyu.

Yesterday, I ran out of MooMoo Milk at home and thought of buying some online, after all, as everyone knows, Pokemon online shopping is extremely convenient; just place an order, and Pelipper will deliver the goods and letters straight to your hand.

---

Back then I casually opened a random shop that sold MooMoo Milk, bought two cases on a whim, and right when I was about to pay, I suddenly noticed the only delivery option in their shop was Dragonite Delivery!

I was totally dumbfounded, thought I'd read it wrong, or maybe the shop owner had typed the name wrong. To figure out what was going on, I casually tapped into the message board, and there I saw tons of people leaving rave reviews.

I tried paying (even though the delivery fee was a bit pricier than Pelipper Express, getting to see a Dragonite makes it worth it!), and within just a few minutes, the app backend showed the milk had already shipped.

Less than ten minutes later, before I'd even wrapped my head around it, a Dragonite with a satchel on its back knocked on my front door.

Then that Dragonite hugged the two cases of milk out and set them down on my floor… hiss, you have no idea, that speed was just ridiculous!

Not even ten minutes from placing the order and the milk was already at my doorstep, and I also got to see a Dragonite you can hardly ever spot out in the wild with my own eyes!

Dragonite Delivery gets a huge thumbs-up! It's just perfect for shut-ins like us! And honestly, the milk from that shop is really high quality; both I and my Mimikyu love it!]

Some people were skeptical about this poster's thread.

[Dragonite Delivery? Isn't this just an ad?]

In response, the OP simply told them to go verify it themselves.

Then someone else commented underneath:

[It's really convenient, but I'm super curious what kind of person the shop owner is, to actually be able to use Dragonite for deliveries. And from what I've found out, they don't just have one Dragonite.]

[Don't tell me a Dragon Master family has started moving into the internet business?]

When the Dragon Elite Hassel of the Paldea Region saw this comment, he also thought it was the old folks in his family finally taking the initiative to get in touch with this newfangled thing called the internet.

But very soon, he realized something kind of embarrassing.

Their Dragon Master family didn't have that many Dragonite.

Hassel: "..."

He carefully scrolled through the whole thread, and only when he saw the OP's reply to a certain netizen did he react.

That netizen asked what the shop was called.

The OP replied: [The shop's called D Ranch]

Hassel instantly saw the light. Oh, so it was that guy!

Then it's fine.

The four big words [Dragonite Delivery] gradually shot up the trending list on the Pokemon Forum.

More and more people started noticing this.

At the same time, in the student dorms of Paldea Academy.

Arven, who had already started the new term, looked at the comments on the Pokemon Forum and felt everyone was making a fuss over nothing.

D had only just opened the shop when he started buying from there.

Over a month ago, someone had posted about Dragonite Delivery on the campus forum.

Arven happened to see that post, casually searched it up, and discovered that it was D's shop.

Having been to D Ranch before, Arven knew very well just how high the quality of the milk there was; it tasted great too.

So from that day on, he started ordering milk from there.

Not only did he drink it himself, Mabosstiff was also very fond of it.

Besides Arven, the Glaseado Gym Leader Grusha and the corporate drone Larry were also using it.

Grusha found out about it from Lono's stream.

At the time, Lono was streaming her daily life with Bellibolt and the others; when she was drinking milk, she casually mentioned this to the viewers, saying she was really shocked that that shop actually used a Pokemon like Dragonite for delivery!

Grusha was really curious, so he clicked in and saw that it was D's shop.

And Larry also often buys things from D's shop. For someone who works as much as he does, time is money.

The time he saves can be used to rest better, then he can recharge and go complete whatever the top brass assigns him.

The delivery speed of Dragonite is dozens of times that of Pelipper.

With it, it can help him save a ton of time. Sometimes even when he's traveling for work and shopping on the road, Dragonite can still deliver the milk to him with pinpoint accuracy; it's just too convenient.

Although everyone often buys things from D's shop, none of them told D about it.

Thanks to word of mouth, Dragonite Delivery gradually became somewhat well-known in the Paldea Region.

At first, people were purely attracted by the insane efficiency of Dragonite Delivery.

But soon after, someone noticed a specialty item in that shop called [Life Sheep Milk].

According to the description, that sheep milk can instantly heal a Pokemon's injuries, and it can also have a huge effect on humans.

And it was sold in limited quantities, only 50 bottles per week!

People were intrigued by this milk.

Very soon, an enthusiastic netizen posted their purchase experience.

[That's right! Everything in the description is true. I'm telling you, the effect of this Life Sheep Milk is super expensive, but it's totally worth the price! No, I even think the owner is selling it too cheap!

Because Life Sheep Milk is just that incredible— even if a Pokemon is gravely injured and on the verge of death, as long as it drinks it, it can instantly recover its stamina. Awesome, right?

I ran into a very strong wild Pokemon while exploring in the wild, and it was thanks to this that I managed to escape.]

The second netizen continued to share.

[Hehe, let me tell you, this milk isn't just for Pokemon; humans can use it too. After you drink a bottle, your body will have endless energy, if you know what I mean.jpg]

People who had never encountered anything like this were stunned by Life Sheep Milk's effects.

There was actually something like this in the world!

Especially some people in the Johto Region.

Because if the description of that milk was true, then wouldn't its effect be exactly the same as the Holy Object [Holy Ash] left behind by the Legendary Pokemon Ho-oh?

Holy Ash, a Holy Object said to have been born from the Legendary Pokemon Ho-oh.

It can resurrect the dead and regrow flesh from bones— as long as there's still one breath left, the Holy Ash can save both Pokemon and humans.

Even in the Johto Region, there are only one or two remaining portions of Holy Ash, gifted to humans back when Ho-oh still trusted mankind and lived atop the Bell Tower.

Ordinary people can't come into contact with it at all, because all of that Holy Ash is kept by a few ancient great clans, carefully preserved by those who worship Ho-oh.

And now...

That tiny shop had suddenly started selling, in bulk, a Holy Object with the same effect as Lord Ho-oh's Holy Ash?

In that moment, Ho-oh's followers all looked at each other, and an unbelievable guess surfaced in their minds.

The Pokemon producing that sheep milk, could it possibly be, just like their Lord Ho-oh, a Legendary Pokemon?

Oh Lord Ho-oh! What's the deal with the owner of that little shop?

If their guess was true, then that person wasn't respecting that great Legendary Pokemon at all, actually putting the Holy Object it produced up for sale online!

A Holy Object bestowed by a Legendary Pokemon should be treasured and enshrined, you know!

At the same time, the people of these ancient clans also felt an emotion called curiosity toward Life Sheep Milk.

They wanted to know how Life Sheep Milk's effect compared to Holy Ash.

At that moment, countless netizens opened the Pokemon shopping app, searched for D Ranch, wanting to check out Dragonite Delivery and Life Sheep Milk.

Some netizens wanted to buy a case of the milk to try, but only when they opened the detailed page did they realize the shop didn't support cross-region delivery!

So more and more people started leaving messages on the shop's message board, jointly petitioning the owner to enable cross-region delivery.

After D finished reading the whole thread and all the messages on his ranch shop's board on his Rotom Phone, he fell completely silent.

He understood why people were shocked by Life Sheep Milk's effects, and he understood their curiosity, but why were some people using his family's milk like some kind of little miracle pill, for crying out loud?

The Life Sheep Milk his family made is honest-to-goodness, proper milk!

---

---

---

---

# 436: Chapter 433: Giratina Escorts Dragonite Delivery

When it comes to cross-regional delivery, D, after much deliberation, still felt it was important not to rush things.

The wave of popularity inevitably draws some Pokemon hunters from other regions, who intentionally place orders to lure Dragonite over, only to capture them all in one fell swoop.

Compared to humans, Pokemon are still too naïve, even Legendary Pokemon are no exception.

In the anime alone, many Legendary Pokemon have been captured by humans using technology and schemes.

Such as Sea God Lugia, Celebi, Latios, Lake Guardians, Meloetta, Suicune...

Even Time God Dialga and Space God Palkia have been controlled.

Thinking of this, D replied to those who requested cross-regional delivery.

[D Ranch (Merchant): Sorry, for now, our delivery service is limited to the Paldea Region. Distances to other regions are too great, and I'm concerned that the delivery Dragonites might encounter danger.]

This reply quickly made its way to the Pokemon Forum through screenshots.

Smart people immediately understood D's concern that the Dragonites would encounter Pokemon hunters in other regions!

There was not much the netizens could say about this reply.

In all major regions, there are cases where rare Pokemon are hunted by hunters and then sold on the black market.

Especially strong and rare Dragon-type Pokemon like Dragonite are very popular among Pokemon hunters.

Worrying about the Dragonites at home encountering danger is only natural.

But soon, a netizen raised a question, doesn't the Paldea Region have any Pokemon hunters?

This time, before D could comment, someone else replied on his behalf.

[Paldea Chief Champion Geeta: That's right, the Paldea Region has always had great security! Thanks to our hardworking Paldea League staff, who tirelessly work to maintain regional peace! We welcome everyone to travel and settle in the Paldea Region!]

Geeta shared the message.

D: "..."

Geeta's words were indeed a truth; he had never seen such a hardworking Pokemon League.

Everyone from top to bottom worked the same way, not just Larry, even Geeta often worked overtime losing track of time.

Any emergency in major cities, the Paldea League immediately sends personnel to resolve it.

Like when a giant Dragonite entered the Paldea Sea a few years back, the chief champion stepped in personally.

If there's any dangerous "villain" in the Paldea Region, it would have to be Professor Sada and Professor Turo researching at the bottom of the Paldea Giant Pit...

Due to their actions, many ferocious Paradox Pokemon later broke through the Pit's defense, intruding into the Paldea Region, causing quite a bit of trouble for Geeta and her team.

However, while Geeta is working hard on her side, he also wants to remind the Dragonites to be cautious.

As the saying goes, one should have precautions against people with bad intentions, but have no intention of harming others.

League Calendar Year 200, March 10, Spring, 6:25 am.

As the Dragonites left Dragonite Island heading towards the ranch to start a new day of work, D gathered them all on the grass.

There were eleven working Dragonites plus his own, making a total of twelve Dragonites and a newly hatched Dratini at the ranch.

The Dragonite stood by D's side, arms crossed, looking towards the working Dragonites and their work partner Rotom.

D cleared his throat twice and asked, "Ahem, has the work been going smoothly these days?"

The Dragonites nodded, happily growling: "Roar~"

The work was quite easy for them!

The Dragonites had an adorably clumsy appearance, their smiles were very cute, and D smiled as he patted their heads, saying:

"But today, there's something else I need to talk to you about."

The Dragonites blinked, looking over curiously: "Roar?"

"That is the possible dangers you might encounter during deliveries," D told them, "Humans, like Pokemon, have different personalities. Some humans are kind-hearted, giving you cookies, like Mrs. Dantes and Lady Mary in town."

"But there are others with dark intentions, who think of harming you, such as using stronger Pokemon to defeat you, then capturing you with nets or cages to sell at high prices."

Upon hearing this, the Dragonites frowned, expressing their dislike for the second type of person.

But they were not afraid.

Led by the eldest Dragonite, the trio let out a low growl: "Grrr."

Indicating that if they encountered such individuals, they would teach them a harsh lesson!

D shook his head: "You must not underestimate them."

"Roar?" The eldest Dragonite looked over in confusion.

D glanced at his own Dragonite and used it as an example.

"If Dragonite had never shown its true strength, in your eyes, it would still be the same silly Dragonite, so you would continue to see it that way, but you wouldn't realize that its strength has long since improved."

"In such situations, if you were to face it in battle, what would happen then?"

Dragonite happily agreed with D: "Roar~"

D smiled as he gently pinched its little pudgy paw.

---

Upon hearing these words, big brother Dragonite couldn't help but furrow its eyebrows, deep in thought.

Soon, a serious expression appeared on its face.

Seeing this, D finally spoke: "So, does everyone understand?"

"Roar!" The Dragonites all nodded their heads, indicating that they remembered.

Next, D looked over at the Phone Rotoms and said to them:

"I'll leave the Dragonites in your care. If someone tries to harm or capture them, you must contact me immediately, and if you can't reach me, contact Calyrex or Giratina."

The Phone Rotoms all agreed in unison, indicating they understood.

D then nodded.

He had already asked two Legendary Pokemon for help.

Both Calyrex and Giratina agreed, promising to rush to the rescue upon receiving any information.

Among them, Giratina was the most enthusiastic.

It assured D that from now on, the Dragonites would be its little brothers, and it would look after them!

If anyone dared to bully them, it would immediately travel through the internet to take those guys down, and then rescue the Dragonites.

When D heard these words from Giratina, he was initially confused until he learned from Phone Rotom that Giratina had been watching a movie about gangsters filmed by Pokemon Hollywood.

Rotom said the gangster movie told the story of a loyal human leading his followers to battle against other gangs, eventually becoming the kingpin ruling all gangs worldwide.

Giratina felt that this human matched its style, so it fantasized about becoming a Pokemon like that and having a group of followers.

Therefore, when D asked for its help, Giratina agreed without hesitation.

Although Giratina sometimes had a temper, it was reliable in this regard.

With the dual protection of Calyrex and Giratina, if anyone tried to capture the Dragonites, Giratina would immediately travel through the internet for retribution.

Thus, he could finally rest assured.

The Dragonites embarked on a new day's work, carrying goods as they followed the navigation from the Phone Rotoms to make deliveries for customers.

D then started a thorough cleaning of the house with the Indeedee Sibling.

With so many Pokemon, their toys were scattered all over the place, and sometimes Pokemon would track mud inside after playing outdoors, dirtying the living room.

As D busied himself around the house, the Eevees frolicked around him.

When D turned around, he noticed a trail of muddy footprints on the newly cleaned floor.

!!!

"You naughty Eevees, you've dirtied the floor all over, go outside and play!"

D took a deep breath, deciding to chase all the Pokemon outside, sending them off to play in the ranch.

The mischievous Eevees scampered away playfully, and Flutter Mane, who was inspecting the food in the fridge, also prepared to head outside upon hearing this.

D noticed Flutter Mane's movement, and quickly called it back: "Flutter Mane, I wasn't talking about you, you keep playing over there!"

Flutter Mane rarely landed on the floor, so it didn't dirty the floors at all.

"Mane."

Flutter Mane let out a soft response, floating back to its spot.

Seeing Flutter Mane stay, Koraidon, who was about to leave, also paused, deciding to remain in the living room.

D noticed this scene, looking as if he wanted to say something but ended up stopping.

"Aaah-sshhh?"

D sighed helplessly, "Ugh, fine, where's Cyclizar?"

Koraidon turned to look outside: "Aaah-sshhh!"

It had just seen Cyclizar, Ogerpon, and Gholdengo head over to Snorlax Park to play.

Witnessing this, D understood.

He told Koraidon, "Go help me call them all over, I need to give you guys a decent bath today."

"Aaah-sshhh!"

Koraidon responded, turning and running off.

By the time D and the Indeedee Sibling finished cleaning the house, Koraidon brought a long line of Pokemon back.

D went to the bathroom and filled several basins with water, as there were quite a few Pokemon he could only wash them one by one.

Starting with the non-furry Pokemon, as they only required a quick rinse and a splash of fragrant body wash, taking no time at all.

Koraidon, Cyclizar, Gholdengo, and Ogerpon were all done within five minutes.

The furry Pokemon, however, took more time, as they needed thorough cleaning of their fur, and after bathing, their fur had to be dried to prevent colds.

But thanks to the Slugma at home, this wasn't a problem.

Thus, after washing each Eevee, D sent them to the fireplace.

The Slugma living in the heating ducts had been called to the fireplace area by D, where its body heat could be used like a dryer.

The soaked-fur Eevees sat crouched in front of the fireplace, allowing the high temperatures emitted by Slugma to dry the water from their fur.

Perhaps the environment was too cozy, as all the Eevees lay lazily, yawning contentedly, even exposing their little bellies.

So when D came out of the bathroom, he was greeted with this sight—

A bunch of Eevees lounging in front of the fireplace, looking peaceful and content. Some leisurely swayed their tails, others lay on their paws, some were about to drift to sleep, and a few were sprawled out on their backs, displaying their half-damp bellies.

---

He immediately extended his magical hand towards these Pokemon.

Soon, cries of "Indeedee Indeedee!" echoed from the room.

*

In the afternoon, after finishing lunch, D, who had been busy all morning, felt utterly exhausted.

The weather was great today, the sun was pleasantly warm, so D decided to head over to the Wooloo Ranch, lying on a small hillside, feeling the breeze and basking in the afternoon sun.

In this relaxed environment, D soon felt completely at ease.

In that moment, it seemed as if time had slowed down, with an endless blue sky overhead and clouds like cotton candy drifting lazily in the gentle breeze.

D's heart felt an unprecedented peace.

Here, there were no bustling cities, no endless overtime, no constant traffic... only fertile farmlands, bountiful orchards, soft meadows, and various adorable Pokemon.

This kind of life felt like a dream.

This is life!

From afternoon till evening, D lay there, leaning against the soft, woolly Wooloo, with the breeze and sun accompanying him.

During this time, he saw the working Dragonites deliver goods and head out again.

At the end of the day's work, when the shop closed, the Dragonites collected their Poffins from his sister Indeedee, satisfied, and prepared to return to Dragonite Island.

Seeing this, D suddenly felt that the working Dragonites seemed to have a tough time commuting back and forth every day.

He thought for a moment and then got up to call out to the departing Dragonites.

A large group of Dragonites turned their heads, looking perplexed: "Hoo?"

D said, "Oh, I almost forgot something. The World Tree has grown up, and it has a large space inside where you can live. Would you like to stay and live on the World Tree?"

"Hoo?"

The working Dragonites knew of the World Tree, but they hadn't seen it in a long time, not quite understanding what D meant.

Seeing this, D directly took them into the Reversed World, to the World Tree, so the Dragonites could see the place with their own eyes.

Feeling the natural aura of the place, the Dragonites all showed expressions of astonishment.

"How about it? Do you want to stay and live here? That way it will be convenient for daily commutes to the ranch, without the need to travel back and forth like before," D invited.

Looking at the surroundings, the Dragonites felt a stirring in their hearts.

"Hoo~!"

Four of the Dragonites nodded without hesitation, they were the Dratini and Dragonair that had come to play at the ranch previously.

Their fondness and attachment to the ranch were much deeper than that of the big brother Dragonite trio.

The other three working Dragonites blinked and then also agreed happily.

The remaining big brother Dragonite trio hesitated for a moment, but soon they gave their response.

"Hoo." The big brother Dragonite with a scar on its face looked at D, indicating it wanted to go back to Dragonite Island.

D nodded, saying, "Alright, I'll wait for you here. No matter what decision you make, it's okay. I'll respect your choice."

The big brother Dragonite was touched. After spending time together, it finally understood why the other Dragonites liked this human so much.

Because he truly was good, not only teaching them battle techniques but also helping them grow with Dragon Power.

Moreover, he was willing to respect their opinions and care about their feelings.

Thinking of this, the big brother Dragonite looked at D, more serious than ever before.

However, at that moment, it suddenly sensed an unmistakable gaze fixed on it.

The big brother Dragonite turned its head and saw that foolish Dragonite glaring at it.

The Dragonite warned, "Hoo!" (D is my Trainer! You can only live here; you can't let D be your trainer!)

---

---

---

---

# 437: Chapter 434: Relocating Dragon Island

That evening, the working Dragonites returned to Dragonite Island, which lay upon the vast sea.

Since they had decided to stay at the World Tree to live, today they wanted to bid a proper farewell to their companions on Dragon Island.

On the endless stretch of the sea, Dragonite Island sat quietly, as if a moon hanging in the night sky.

Nurtured by Dragon Power, the island's vegetation was lush, and clusters of berries grew on the trees sprouting from the ground.

"Rustle, rustle!"

A sound came from the leaves in the forest, and a Dragonite's head poked out from among them, using its chubby paw to pick a bunch of berries, which it then placed into its mouth.

The juice from the fruit burst inside Dragonite's mouth immediately, and it chewed with great satisfaction.

Not far behind this Dragonite, lay a circular lake.

Many Dragonites, Dragonairs, and Dratinis were playing by its side.

"Awuuu~!"

A string of melodious calls came from the sky.

The Dragonites on the island all raised their heads to look up, seeing the Dragonites who had gone to help humans return to the island.

The eyes of some Dratinis and Dragonairs playing in the clear lake water suddenly lit up.

When those Dragonites landed, they gathered around, looking eagerly at the Poffin Boxes in the adult Dragonites' hands.

The eldest Dragonite, seeing this scene, skillfully distributed the Poffins to the younger Dratinis and Dragonairs.

Apart from these Dratinis and Dragonairs, some other Dragonites on the island were also attracted over.

They gazed with adorable eyes at the Poffin Boxes their companions held, as if asking: "What does that taste like?"

If it were any other day, the working Dragonites might only share one or two Poffins. But today was their last night on the island, and they would soon live comfortably on the ranch.

In that case, these delicious Poffins would be farewell gifts for their fellow Dragonites!

After all, they weren't in short supply; D and his sister Indeedee would make plenty of delicious Poffins for them, to the point where they almost got tired of them.

"Awuuu~"

Thinking of this, the working Dragonites opened the Poffin Boxes and shared the Poffins with the other Dragonites.

Gradually, more and more Dragonites were drawn in.

They happily ate the Poffins, surprised at why these Dragonites were being so generous today, giving all the Poffins to them instead of eating them themselves.

In response, one of the working Dragonites happily replied:

"Awuuu~" (Because from tomorrow, we will move to live with D~)

Hearing this, the Dragonites were shocked.

"Awuu?!"

Some Dragonites were astonished by their decision to leave Dragonite Island so soon.

Some Dragonites also realized that if they moved, they might never be able to eat such delicious Poffins again?!

This news quickly reached the ears of an elderly Dragonite on Dragonite Island.

The elder Dragonite stepped forward and asked: "Awuuu?" (Have you already decided?)

The working Dragonites nodded, excitedly telling these Dragonites about life on the ranch.

"Awuu." (D is a really nice human, and he's very capable.)

"Awuuu~" (Thanks to D, I was able to evolve into Dragonite~)

"Awuu awuu!" (I really like life there; every day, I just need to help Rotom deliver milk to get lots of food and money. Rotom said I can buy things I like with money!)

"Awuu!" (That's right! Plus, if we perform well, D will use that magical power to help us get stronger!)

The Dragonites listened quietly, their eyes full of curiosity.

These words sparked a touch of curiosity in some Dragonites who originally had no interest in the ranch or the human named D.

Yet, some stubborn Dragonites still showed concern.

"Awuu..." (But if you leave, how will I be able to eat Poffins again...)

A working Dragonite thought carefully for a moment, then replied: "Awuu!" (You can come work too!)

Another working Dragonite nodded: "Awuu!" (That's right! If you work, D will give you lots of Poffins!)

Hearing this, a small light bulb appeared above the head of the Dragonite who asked that question.

"Awuu!"

That's a great idea!

The eyes of other Dragonites, who suddenly realized they wouldn't get to eat Poffins, also lit up.

Yes! Why didn't they think of that?

If they worked too, wouldn't they get to eat delicious Poffins every day?

So, early the next morning, a large group of Dragonites set off from Dragonite Island, crossing the vast Paldea Sea to the Paldea Region.

During this journey, an ocean liner detected their presence.

The captain and first mate stood on the deck, observing the scene with binoculars.

Some tourists were attracted to the deck, and as they looked at the distant scene, they were all astonished.

So many Dragonites... were they migrating?

Migrating Dragonites were an extremely rare sight in the wild!

Many tourists believed seeing Dragonites was a good omen and began to make wishes to the Dragonites.

---

At 6:35 a.m. Paldea time, while D was feeding the Pokemon at the ranch, he felt a shadow pass over his head.

He looked up to see a large flock of Dragonite landing in the ranch.

D:!!!

Normally, Dragonite coming to work at the ranch is a usual thing.

But why so many Dragonite today?!

Startled, D quickly found the most prominent senior Dragonite and asked it what was going on.

To this, the senior Dragonite honestly replied: "Whooo." (They also want to work here.)

A nearby working Dragonite added: "Woo woo!" (That's right! D, as long as you give them Poffins, they'll be happy to work like us!)

"..."

After listening to the replies from several Dragonite, D finally figured out what was happening.

Turns out his Dragonite employees had told the other Dragonite about the ranch affairs when they returned to the island yesterday, saying goodbye, and attracted these Dragonite over.

However, D felt it was more likely because they realized they couldn't get free Poffins anymore, so they came over to work...

Looking at this scene, D was momentarily at a loss for words.

If Professor Oak were here studying the ecology of Dragonite, he would surely discover something.

That is, working at D Ranch seemed to be extremely popular among some young Dragonite.

"Alright then!" D looked at the other Dragonite, smiling to show his goodwill: "Welcome, I'm D, the owner of this ranch."

The Dragonite blinked; they knew D because he'd visited their Dragonite Island before and gave them a lot to eat.

"From now on, please take good care of each other. Help me deliver goods, and as thanks, I'll make Poffins for you."

D asked Koraidon to get a dozen idle Rotom Phones, then called the Dragonite from his household to help guide and teach these new Dragonite.

Dragonite nodded, maturely stepping forward to take D's place and began teaching the new Dragonite how to use Rotom Phones and deliver goods.

Meanwhile, some veteran Dragonite employees were providing additional support nearby.

And D counted the number of these newly joined Dragonite.

"One, two, three, four... eleven, twelve, thirteen."

"A total of thirteen Dragonite, plus twelve existing employees, there are now twenty-five Dragonite and one Dratini in the ranch."

D: "..." What an exaggerated number!

So, how many Dragonite are left on Dragonite Island now?

If he remembered correctly, there were only a total of thirty-plus Dragonite there, right?

Including Dratini and Dragonair who hadn't evolved yet, the total number probably didn't exceed fifty.

Oh my, half of Dragonite Island's Dragonite have come to his ranch!

Even D himself could hardly believe this number.

He remembered that the giant Dragonite was also living on Dragonite Island now.

The giant Dragonite originally came to the Paldea Region to look for friends, hardly finding an island of kin, and now, half the island's Dragonite had come to him...

D was momentarily speechless.

How about inviting the remaining Dragonite to settle on the World Tree?

This way, the Dragonite could gather here, even if they don't work for him.

Although D wanted to invite all the Dragonite over, the remaining Dragonite surely didn't plan to leave Dragonite Island.

Otherwise, they would've come along this morning.

Since the Dragonite didn't intend to, he couldn't force them to move.

After contemplating carefully, D gave up on this idea.

However, even though he gave up, the Dragonite had ideas of their own.

In the evening, after finishing a day's work, D brought the Dragonite to the World Tree and temporarily settled them by the lake where Milotic resided.

After that, D returned to the ranch while the Dragonite, full of curiosity, looked around, flew around, and familiarized themselves with the surroundings.

During this time, Milotic, who came earlier, along with Tropius, Tatsugiri, Arboliva, and other Pokemon noticed these twenty-plus Dragonite.

To welcome them, Arboliva generously offered Berries stored in the warehouse.

By night, Keri finished the construction and returned home with the Machamp, leaving only this group of Pokemon on the World Tree.

After discussing with everyone, Arboliva and Milotic held a campfire gathering for the Dragonite.

Treecko, Arboliva, Tatsugiri, Milotic, Dragonite, and other Pokemon sat around the fire.

Arboliva used found leaves to serve Berries, while Tropius shared its banana fruit with everyone.

The three Tatsugiri looked at the Dragonite and asked: "Mee?" (Where did you all come from?)

A Dragonite replied: "Whooo～" (From Dragonite Island, where there are many, many Dragonite～)

"Whooo!" Another Dragonite nodded happily to affirm, but soon felt a bit sad, saying, "Whooo..." (We won't be able to see them again anymore...)

"Chafu?" The Treecko looked puzzled, why?

---

Dragonite answered, "Rawr." (Because everyone stayed behind on Dragonite Island.)

This was a Dragonite that had only joined today.

Spending the night at the World Tree for the first time felt a bit strange for it, since it had grown up on Dragonite Island.

It kind of missed everyone back on Dragonite Island.

The Treecko had also left their hometown to come here, but unlike Dragonite, they were full of anticipation for life here, looking forward to the founding of the Pokemon Guild, and running a café in it.

Because of that, Treecko didn't really know how to comfort Dragonite.

The leaping campfire reflected on the Dragonites' faces, and a sad mood lingered in the air; Milotic and Tatsugiri were also feeling sorry for them.

Arboliva's gentle gaze swept over the faces of the gathered Pokemon.

It had lived with D since it was little. Though it rarely took the initiative to express itself, it had always been silently watching over D.

If it were D, at a time like this he would definitely choose to stay together with everyone, right?

Yeah! That's right!

Arboliva's expression cleared; since they were sad about being separated, wouldn't it be fine if everyone just lived together?

Thinking of this, Arboliva looked at the Dragonite and said to them, "Li, li!" (If you're missing your friends, then just invite them all to come live here on the World Tree!)

"Rawr?!" The Dragonite lifted their heads and looked over.

Arboliva gently explained, "Li." (You've seen it yourselves. There's a lot of space around the World Tree, enough to hold many Pokemon. If they come here too, then you'll never have to be apart again, just like everyone on the ranch.)

At those words, the eyes of the working Dragonite lit up all at once.

What a great idea!

This way everyone could always be together! And the World Tree was such a nice place—they were sure to fall in love with it too!

The Dragonite were overjoyed.

It was decided! Tomorrow morning they would go invite everyone over!

Having made up their minds, the Dragonites' moods brightened again.

They spent a peaceful night on the World Tree.

Early the next morning, once it was fully light, a large group of Dragonite went to find D, told him their plan, and asked if it would be okay to invite all the Dragonite on the island to come live on the World Tree.

"Rawr!"

The Dragonite looked at him expectantly, their expressions pleading.

D had never imagined it would be about this, and was a bit dumbfounded for a while before he could react.

He asked in disbelief, "You're saying you want to invite all the Dragonite on the island to come live on the World Tree?"

"Rawr!" The Dragonite nodded again and again.

"Will they come?" D was a little worried.

But the Dragonite themselves weren't worried at all.

They patted their chests, indicating that they definitely would!

Everyone on Dragon Island had lived together since they were little, catching fish in the sea every day, picking Berries in the jungle, watching the stars and the sunrise together.

This separation had been hard not only on them; everyone on Dragonite Island must also be having a rough time getting used to it.

"Then I'll go with you!"

D closed up the little ranch shop, planning to shut it for a day just to take care of the Dragonite's matter.

He mounted Koraidon, took Dragonite with him, and followed behind the working Dragonite as they crossed the ocean.

After a year, D once again returned to this lonely little Dragonite Island standing in the middle of the sea.

He followed behind the working Dragonite, with Koraidon and his Dragonite by his side.

Hearing what the Dragonite had just said, his Dragonite also felt a faint stir in its heart.

There was a time when it, too, had lived on this Dragonite Island, just like everyone else.

Back then it had been a dummy, but no one ever laughed at it or bullied it; instead, it was always the one who thought of itself as a dumb Dragonite and sulked.

Now that it had stepped out and looked back, Dragonite actually felt a trace of nostalgia for that time.

Looking at the group up ahead who wanted to compete with it for its Trainer, Dragonite decided that from now on it would allow D to teach them new Moves too!

The working Dragonite started negotiating with the other Dragonite on the island.

D watched the scene with interest, then turned to the Dragonite beside him and asked, "Did you play with them when you were little too?"

Dragonite nodded. "Rawr rawr."

Back then it had still been a Dratini. They had all been small and weak, staying in the lake every day, waiting to grow up.

At that time, some Dragonite on the island would bring Berries and fish from outside to give to them.

D nodded slightly. Looking at his own Dragonite, he suddenly felt a bit emotional. "Compared to when I first met you, you've really grown a lot more mature."
