Being praised by D, Dragonite bashfully scratched its head. "Rawr~"

D suddenly remembered something. His gaze swept over the group of Dragonite not far away and he asked, "But speaking of which, the Dragonite you used to like is in there too, right?"

At that, Dragonite's eyes widened at once.

Seeing its expression, D raised an eyebrow. "You don't like her anymore?"

Dragonite didn't know how to answer either.

D encouraged it, "You're a really amazing Dragonite now. You have to believe in yourself!"

Dragonite let out a helpless sigh. "Rawr..."

By this time, the conversation between the working Dragonite and the island Dragonite had already come to an end.

All the Dragonite turned their heads to look at D.

One of the working Dragonite happily walked up, its tone full of excitement. "Rawr!"

---

"Did they agree?" D asked.

The working Dragonite nodded.

Seeing this, D let out a sigh of relief in his heart.

His gaze was drawn to the gigantic Dragonite among the group of Dragonites.

This Dragonite had previously eaten the size-changing pumpkin he made, and now its size is identical to a regular Dragonite.

"Long time no see, Dragonite!"

The gigantic Dragonite happily responded, "Roar~"

It also remembered D, who had helped it find this place in the first place.

The gigantic Dragonite was filled with gratitude towards D.

Since the Dragonites had agreed to live on the World Tree, the next thing to consider was where to settle them within the World Tree.

If they were all placed on the Explorer Team Guild layer, it would be too crowded.

Moreover, the number of Dragonites was too large, and the Berry Garden he had previously cultivated probably couldn't feed all of them.

He would need to cultivate several more Berry Gardens...

D immediately fell into deep thought.

He looked around, observing the island where the Dragonites lived, and thought about the inside of the World Tree.

Suddenly, a good idea popped into D's mind.

Could he possibly ask Calyrex to use its Psychic abilities to teleport the entire Dragonite Island into the World Tree?

According to D's observations, the World Tree contains many layers of space. Although each layer's environment is roughly the same, he could modify it himself.

Below the Explorer Team Guild, there's a vast valley perfectly suitable for constructing a Land of Dragons for the Dragonites.

If Dragonite Island were transferred there, and some seawater was brought into the valley, followed by releasing fish, it could perfectly recreate the environment around Dragonite Island!

If the Dragonites were hungry, they could go into the water to catch fish. If they didn't want to eat fish, they could go to the Berry Garden to pick berries.

And with Calyrex's abilities, it's entirely possible to accomplish this.

Because it had once moved an entire vast forest, saving the Pokemon living there.

Thinking of this, D immediately took out his phone and contacted Calyrex.

After learning of his idea, Calyrex stated that this could indeed be achieved.

After inquiring about D's location, Calyrex used the Phone Rotom's navigation to teleport repeatedly, arriving at Dragonite Island.

"Do we need to leave first?" D asked.

Calyrex looked around, assessed the surroundings, then gently shook its head: "No need, you all can stay there."

The next second, it activated its abilities.

An overwhelming Psychic pressure emanated from Calyrex.

The Psychic energy transformed into a faint pink light, enveloping Dragonite Island and the surrounding seawater.

The powerful Psychic energy even distorted the surrounding space, but this state didn't last very long.

Once the entire island was covered by Psychic energy, a dazzling white light burst forth, and Dragonite Island vanished from the sea surface.

As for D and the Dragonites, before they could react, the surrounding scenery underwent a tremendous change.

The sea was nowhere to be seen, replaced by a mysterious and special space.

This was the inside of the World Tree.

Calyrex not only teleported Dragonite Island but also brought a large amount of seawater with it.

The blue seawater flowed into this layer, and soon, the entire region transformed into a vast ocean.

The relocated Dragonite Island quietly levitated atop this man-made "sea."

Gradually, the turbulent seawater calmed down, returning to a state of tranquility.

The Dragonites were completely stunned, looking up incredulously at the scene.

Everything around them was identical to before, as if they were still on the sea.

---

---

---

---

# 438: Chapter 435: Completion of the Land of Dragons and the Hidden Trial in the Ranch

D discovered that the World Tree, mutated from the Grass Energy of the Gogoat, is quite special.

Unlike the World Tree in the Kanto Region, this one can absorb energy through Synthesis to provide sunlight to its internal areas.

Its interior is a self-contained space; if described in one sentence, it's a mini Pokemon World ecological simulator.

As the name implies, the World Tree can simulate all external environments internally and infuse them with vitality and energy.

For instance, this ocean transferred by Calyrex, the World Tree can inject Natural Energy into it and let it flow.

Standing on Dragonite Island, D looked around the surrounding waters, contemplating the next goals in this mysterious space.

Indeed, this area is rich in water resources, which can serve both as a Land of Dragons for the Dragonites and provide a habitat for some Water Type Pokemon.

Like Lapras, Corsola, Luvdisc, and Starmie.

In this way, the ecological zone for Water Type Pokemon habitation is basically completed.

"If only I could establish a connection with Groudon and Kyogre." D thought inwardly.

If those two Pokemon are willing to settle on the World Tree, he could build two distinct ecological zones centered around them.

As the Sea God, Kyogre can endlessly create seawater to provide life to this water area, and working with the World Tree, it can revitalize this ocean with even greater vitality.

Meanwhile, the Earth God Groudon can create the earth and magma.

If Groudon were present, an ecological zone could be formed centered around it, providing homes for Fire Type Pokemon, Rock Type Pokemon, and Ground Type Pokemon in a lava ecological area.

Unfortunately, he doesn't have much chance to interact with these two Pokemon.

Moreover, Groudon and Kyogre are sworn enemies, and if they live in the same place, chaos would inevitably ensue.

After brief contemplation, D returned his thoughts to reality.

Now that Dragonite Island has been relocated, the next step is to plant fish fry there.

The ranch hasn't initiated the aquaculture industry yet, so it can't directly supply fish fry; thus, D plans to go to the town and ask Zack for information regarding purchasing fish fry.

Besides, it's March now, the time for Calyrex to accelerate the World Tree's growth.

After bidding farewell to the Dragonites, D took the World Tree elevator to leave the place and drove a pickup truck to the town. Eventually, he found Zack at Porto Marinada Market.

Upon hearing D's purpose, Zack seemed quite surprised.

"Buying fish fry? What's up? Are you planning to breed fish?"

D nodded: "Sort of, but I'm not farming fish for selling, rather to feed the Dragonites, so just regular fish fry, not Fish-type Pokemon."

"I see!" Zack knew about the Dragonite couriers from D Ranch; he nodded with a smile, "Alright, no problem! Leave this task to me, I'll contact nearby fish fry bases this afternoon."

"It's up to you then." D gifted Zack two cans of Rock Salt produced at the ranch as a token of gratitude.

Zack had helped him a lot, so he couldn't keep troubling him—doing so would be impolite.

Zack breezily presented him with a bag of cherries: "I just picked these cherries from Elif Cherry Garden, they're sweet—take them back for the Cyclizar to eat!"

Spring is the season for cherries; the cherries from Elif Cherry Garden are large and round, well-known in Porto Marinada Town.

D expressed his gratitude and then bid farewell to Zack, turning back to the ranch.

Calyrex and Koraidon were already waiting for him by the centerpiece lake of the ranch.

The group ventured into the Reversed World, where Calyrex activated its ability to accelerate the World Tree's growth.

In the next instant, the enormous World Tree emitted a glowing green light, its leaves gently swaying in the breeze, creating a rustling sound.

Meanwhile, on the World Tree.

Keri and the Machamps, who were at work, heard the sound. Looking up, they saw all the leaves above their heads emitting a soft green glow.

The surrounding vegetation, as if in resonance, also changed, becoming more lush.

In the orchard, Treeckos had just picked a batch of Berries from the trees and were preparing to store them in the tree hole together with Tropius.

However, just as they were about to leave, they suddenly saw the entire orchard enveloped in a layer of green light.

Within the glow, those fruit trees quickly bore new Berries at a visibly fast pace.

Almost in the blink of an eye, the branches of all the fruit trees were loaded again with heavy, sweet fruits.

"Chatot!!!"

The startled Treeckos accidentally dropped the leaves they were using to carry Berries, a large heap of berries tumbling to the ground.

One Treecko, witnessing this incredible scene, couldn't help but murmur: "Chatot..." So many Berries!

They can't possibly eat them all, they can't finish them!

Compared to the shocked Treeckos, Arboliva remained considerably calm.

Thinking of the Dragonites moving into the World Tree for residence, it gently suggested, "Li, Li~" (If we can't eat them all, let's share them with the Dragonites~)

---

Both Treecko Tropius and Milotic Tatsugiri agreed gladly.

Meanwhile, the Dragonites mentioned by Arboliva had also noticed the strange changes happening on this tree.

On Dragonite Island, a large group of Dragonites raised their heads to look at the sky, while some Dragonites looked around confusedly.

The surrounding waters were emitting a faint green glow at this moment, as if something they didn't know about was quietly changing.

The Dragonites were quite puzzled by this situation.

But soon, a clever Dragonite leapt into the sea, intending to see what was happening underwater.

With a splash, the Dragonite disappeared beneath the green-glowing surface of the sea.

It quickly reached the seabed.

The seabed was also undergoing strange changes, where the original grasslands and bushes had vanished, replaced by patches of sea grass, seaweed, and kelp.

There was even a beautiful coral reef!

"Rawr!"

The Dragonite was greatly shocked. It grabbed a long piece of kelp with its large claws and swam to the surface, showcasing it to its companions.

Soon, all the Dragonites knew what kind of changes were happening in this place.

*

Outside the World Tree, as the light on the leaves gradually dimmed, Calyrex's urging of growth reached its conclusion.

D cast his gaze towards the World Tree, and information about it automatically appeared in his mind.

[World Tree (Mature Stage): The thriving World Tree is reaching the end of its growth, transforming into another form of life, creating an internal environment. A mystical tree that is said to sustain a world by nurturing all creatures with natural energy. It requires 27,198 more years to fully mature.]

"It's shortened by ten thousand years again, at this rate, it will mature in three months." D muttered.

It's March now, and in three months, it will be June, just perfect.

"You've worked hard!" D said to Calyrex.

With a smile, Calyrex looked up at the towering tree before him and said to D:

"I just sensed that something is changing inside the World Tree. You might want to take a look."

"Hmm?" D was slightly taken aback, changes? What changes?

To this, Calyrex shook its head saying, "I am not sure either, but I can feel that it is different from before."

D nodded thoughtfully, if that's the case, he might as well investigate!

Accompanied by a group of Pokemon, D once again entered the World Tree. Riding the tree's elevator, he first arrived at the Land of Dragons where the Dragonites resided.

As D reached here, he saw many Dragonites playing on the island's beach with seaweed, coral, and kelp in their Dragon Claws.

D was a bit confused.

Where did the seaweed and coral come from? Does the World Tree have these things?

Or did Calyrex transfer them along with the seawater?

However, Calyrex stated that it was not their doing.

Since that's the case, he might as well go check it out!

D took off his clothes and dove into the water with a splash.

The Dragonites playing on the island saw this and thought D had fallen into the water, so they all jumped in after him.

Before D could figure out what was happening, several Dragonites approached behind him, one of them wrapped its chubby claws around him, attempting to drag him out of the ocean.

D: "?"

Through Dragon Power, he quickly sensed the Dragonite's thoughts.

—It thought he had fallen into the water and was attempting to save him.

D couldn't help but feel a bit speechless, but speaking of which, wild Dragonites do seem to rescue humans who fall into the ocean.

This is why ships sailing at sea often like to have Dragonite as a mascot.

D gently patted the Dragonite's arm, conveying his intention through Dragon Power.

[I didn't fall into the water, I just want to come down and see what's changing here.]

"Rawr?"

Hearing this, the Dragonite looked at D carefully and realized he did not appear to have fallen into the water, so it released its grip.

[Do you know what just happened here?] D asked.

His delivery Dragonite and other working Dragonites had gone out for delivery and did not come today, so he took this opportunity to seek help from these Dragonites.

Upon hearing D's words, the Dragonite was very happy.

It hugged D and swam towards the seabed with the other Dragonites, then pointed to the underwater sea grass and seaweed, and said to D: "Rawr!" (These have grown!)

D:!

Looking at the green sea grass swaying with the ocean currents on the seabed, D was momentarily stunned.

He even saw an expanse of coral reef not far away.

What is going on?!

Could it be that when Calyrex urged the World Tree's growth, it caused a natural evolution within its interior, merging with these waters?

After thinking it over, D believed this was the only possibility.

To think it was possible...

This is truly amazing!

D felt a surge of wonder on his face, amazed by the power of the World Tree.

He wanted to look further, but he was running out of oxygen and had to signal the Dragonite to take him back to the surface, returning to Dragonite Island.

Lying on the beach, D took deep breaths of the fresh air.

---

Several Dragonites gathered around, bringing their heads close and observing D with big eyes.

"Alright, I'm fine, just held my breath for too long." D smiled and patted one of the Dragonite's heads.

"Roar~" The Dragonites let out happy growls.

After resting for a while, D sat up, walked over to Koraidon and Calyrex, looked at the Dragonites, and said with a smile:

"The ocean has completely merged with the World Tree. From today, you can live here without worries."

"Roar~!" The Dragonites also discovered this and expressed their happiness.

D then scanned the open spaces on Dragonite Island, searching for spots suitable for farmland development, and planning to create a Berry Garden there so the Dragonites could be self-sufficient.

By using Rain Dance for watering and Dragon Power for nourishing the crops, this Dragon Island would soon become a paradise for Dragonites.

Before long, D found suitable land.

He decided to bring berry saplings and Dugtrio over with Zack when they found fish fry to help the Dragonites build their Berry Garden.

With the Dragonites' changes understood, the next step was the Explorer Team Guild.

D waved goodbye to the Dragonites, then took the World Tree elevator to the top floor.

There, he saw the Treeckos holding a big pile of berries, seemingly about to do something.

Surprised, D stopped them and asked: "Where are you going?"

Treecko: "Chaha!"

Beside him, Calyrex helped translate: "They say the berries in the garden are more than they can eat, and the tree holes are full, so they want to take these berries to the Dragonites."

Upon hearing this, D was slightly stunned. As far as he knew, although the Berry trees bore fruit more frequently than regular fruit trees, it shouldn't be that exaggerated, right?

"Then go for it! The Dragonites are all on the island now!" D replied.

"Chaha~" The Treeckos happily responded.

D watched as the three Treeckos carried berries past him, taking the World Tree elevator to the next level.

He, meanwhile, led a group of Pokemon to observe the changes on this level.

Compared to the Dragonites' side, the changes here were not as evident.

After a simple tour, D found that, apart from the vegetation being more lush and the natural atmosphere being thicker, not much had changed here.

During this time, D greeted Keri, who was working.

From Keri, he learned about the recent phenomena here.

The World Tree's leaves above emitted a gentle glow, the grassland's vegetation began to grow at a visible speed, and according to Machamp, the Berry Garden's trees were also crazily bearing fruit.

Berry trees crazily bearing fruit?

D raised an eyebrow, then continued with the Pokemon, reaching the Berry Garden after passing through a grove.

Now, the Berry Garden was filled with large and plump berries everywhere.

Arboliva was beside it, using Grass Energy to tend to these fruit trees.

Seeing D approaching, Arboliva paused its actions.

"Arboliva, did these berries just grow?" D asked.

"Liva!" Arboliva nodded slightly and began to explain the recent changes to D.

It turned out that when Calyrex cultivated the World Tree, the Berry Garden was also affected.

One after another, berries sprouted, and the Treeckos frantically picked them.

But just as they picked one batch, another batch of berries would grow again.

After several continuous harvests, the fruit trees in the garden finally stopped bearing fruit, leaving the Treeckos panting tiredly.

As they talked, Arboliva took D to see where they stored the berries.

It was inside tree holes located in the trees within the forest.

Since the Explorer Team's warehouse for storing berries wasn't built yet, the Pokemon had to store the berries in those tree holes for now.

D saw that the tree holes of several big trees were packed full of berries, and some had even fallen onto the grass because there was no more space.

"..."

Alright, now he understood why the Treeckos wanted to give the berries to the Dragonites.

There were simply too many berries for them to consume.

In light of this, D explained the situation to Arboliva, saying, "The warehouse will be finished soon, so soon there won't be any worries about running out of space for berries."

Keri's construction progress was fast, and with Machamp's help, the warehouse would soon be complete, after which they could start building the guild headquarters and other Pokemon facilities.

"Liva~" Arboliva nodded gently.

Finally understanding what changes had occurred on the World Tree, D exhaled in relief; fortunately, everything was progressing positively.

Before leaving, he took a trip to the lake where Milotic lived to check on Milotic and Tatsugiri.

When D arrived there, Milotic was gently swimming in the lake.

The three Tatsugiris were lounging lazily in the sun by the shore. With no danger here, they relaxed without fear of being captured by other Pokemon.

Slowpoke was still dozing by the shore.

Seeing this scene, D couldn't resist walking over, gently squeezing Slowpoke's armpits, and picking it up.

Slowpoke showed no reaction.

---

D took the opportunity to greet Milotic and the others.

"How is it? Are you getting used to living here?"

"Woo～"

Milotic let out a melodious cry, expressing its fondness for this fairy-like place.

At this moment, Milotic deeply appreciated that it took the bait back then.

Meanwhile, the Tatsugiri, petite and sushi-like in appearance, were bouncing on the grass, trying to get D's attention.

"Meep!"

D smiled and asked, "And how about you guys?"

"Meep!"

Seeing that D finally noticed them, the Tatsugiri bounced even more joyfully.

They eagerly described their dreams to D.

"Meep, meep!"

Calyrex casually helped translate, "They said they want to become sushi shop managers in the future and run a sushi shop diligently."

"What?" D was almost taken aback, looking at the three Tatsugiri with a peculiar expression: "You want to be sushi shop managers?"

"Meep!" Tatsugiri nodded excitedly.

D: "...Well, you better be careful not to stand next to the sushi, or else a customer might mistake you for sushi and eat you."

After all, Tatsugiri's appearance looks exactly like sushi, especially the orange Tatsugiri, which looks just like a salmon sushi.

"Meep!"

Tatsugiri quickly shook their heads, indicating they wouldn't!

D thought to himself that they hadn't even seen sushi yet... but the specifics could wait until the sushi shop actually opened!

Right now, he was more curious about this Slowpoke in front of him.

After a long while, seemingly realizing its position had changed, Slowpoke finally turned its head slowly.

Its turning movement was very slow, and its gaze remained vacant.

"Poke?"

D heard Slowpoke's call.

"Poke?" he repeated Slowpoke's call.

But Slowpoke didn't respond, its vacant gaze meeting his.

D: "..."

He felt that if he sat here without moving, Slowpoke could continue staring at him like this for three days and nights.

Placing Slowpoke by the lake, D reached out to touch its curled ears.

Speaking of Slowpoke, one must mention its two evolutionary forms.

Slowbro and Slowking.

Even among Pokémon around the world, Slowpoke's evolution method is very special.

Unlike other Pokémon that evolve independently, Slowpoke needs another Pokémon named Shellder to evolve.

When Shellder bites onto Slowpoke's tail, the two Pokémon combine to evolve into Slowbro.

And when Shellder bites onto Slowpoke's head, combined with the evolution item King's Rock, it can evolve into Slowking.

Slowking, as the name implies, is the king among Slowpoke.

Once, researchers discovered that Slowking's intelligence is very high, reaching levels comparable to top scientists worldwide.

Wild Slowking would even compete in intelligence with Oranguru, often resulting in a draw.

Thinking about this, D's interest was piqued.

He had always wanted to hire a high-intelligence Pokémon to open a shop here and had even given the Heroic Expedition Team a commission to find an Oranguru.

But it seemed that encountering Oranguru was proving difficult at the moment.

So the question arose: Could he help this Slowpoke evolve into Slowking to take over the work of an Oranguru?

However, for Slowpoke to evolve into Slowking, aside from needing a Pokémon named Shellder, it also requires a special item, the King's Rock.

Neither of these evolution items was available on the ranch.

After thinking it over slightly, D decided to search online later to see if he could buy a King's Rock from someone else.

As for Shellder, that was much simpler.

As long as he took Slowpoke to a coastal area with Shellder presence, Shellder would eventually nibble at its head or tail, forming a symbiotic relationship with Slowpoke.

*

In the afternoon, Zack called to inform him that he had already made contact with a fry aquaculture farm.

After hanging up the phone, D went with Zack to a fry aquaculture farm located south of Porto Marinada Town and spent a sum of money to buy a large batch of fry.

Bass, yellow croaker, saury, and so on.

Afterward, D took a trip to Porto Marinada Town and purchased a batch of Berry seedlings from Elo Seed Store.

After accomplishing all these tasks, he took Dugtrio and Calyrex and headed to Dragonite Island where the Dragonite resided.

The next steps were much simpler.

D had worked with Dugtrio lots of times; marking out the designated area so Dugtrio understood.

They burrowed underground, using Dig to transform the area into soil suitable for growing fruit trees.

Some Dragonite were attracted and stood by curiously to observe.

While Dugtrio was tilling the land, D rode his Dragonite and started releasing the fry into the waters.

Finally, he found the somewhat older Dragonite and the gigantic Dragonite on Dragonite Island and said to them:

"I've already released the fry into the sea water, it won't be long before you can eat fish here."

The older Dragonite was very pleased, and the gigantic Dragonite was also filled with gratitude.

D smiled, continuing: "However, it's important to remember, you cannot eat them before they've grown up, otherwise if they're all eaten, there won't be any more fish left in this area of sea."

---

The elder Dragonite, rich in life experience, nodded, indicating it knew about this matter.

Even as Dragonites, when hunting in the wild, they wouldn't eat the small fish that haven't grown up yet because if they did, there would be no fish left in the sea.

"And the other Dragonites too, I'll leave it to you to remind them," D said.

"Roar." The elder Dragonite nodded, agreeing to the request.

Great, then next, all that's left is the development of the Berry Garden!

With great anticipation, D returned to that piece of open land.

The Dugtrio had already cultivated the land and were squeaking about something under the watchful eyes of a group of Dragonites.

D didn't ask, as he led Koraidon and Cyclizar to plant those Berry seedlings into the dug pits.

The last step was up to Calyrex.

Under the gaze of the group of Dragonites, Calyrex floated gently forward.

It raised its right hand slightly, and in the next second, a bright light shone on the land, and the Berry seedlings grew at a visibly rapid pace.

Before long, a Berry Garden filled with fruit appeared before everyone.

The Dragonites were stunned at the sight.

Their gazes towards Calyrex were filled with admiration.

This Pokémon is amazing!

Calyrex was already accustomed to this and merely smiled gently before retreating to D's side.

"Alright, now you can pluck fruits from these trees to eat!" D said with a smile to the Dragonites in front of him.

"Roar~"

The Dragonites felt very happy; they'd never thought it was possible to grow Berries like this.

So magical!

D stayed a while longer on Dragonite Island.

Soon, he saw Dragonites flying in one after another, picking Berries from the trees and eating them.

Some Dragonites would pick Berries to give to the young Dragonair and Dratini.

The Dragonite clan is like this, as communal Pokémon, once they grow up, they consciously take on the habit of caring for the young until the young also grow into dragons.

Watching the harmonious scene, D felt that Dragonites are truly cute Pokémon.

Compared to the quasi-God Hydreigon from a neighboring region, the Dragonite clan from top to bottom seemed so harmonious and friendly.

Plump paws, rounded bodies, big eyes, when they look at you, it feels like the whole world brightens up.

Before leaving the World Tree, D found the Dragonites working on the ranch and asked them to take some Berries to Giratina outside.

Though the Dragonites didn't understand, they still obediently complied.

Each Dragonite held a pile of Berries in their arms, flying to Flower Sky Island where Giratina was and happily gave them to it.

Giratina looked approvingly at this group of Dragonite followers he'd just acquired.

Not bad, they are sensible!

From now on, these Dragonites will be under its care!

D stood beneath the World Tree, watching that scene.

This way, he wouldn't have to worry about the Dragonites facing any danger when delivering goods outside.

*

March 15, Year 200 of the League.

This morning, D was browsing the internet at home, searching for information on the King's Rock, a tool for evolving Slowpoke into Slowking.

King's Rock, it's said online to be a token of a king, not commonly seen, and occasionally appearing on the trade market.

But this item is often in short supply, usually being bought up as soon as it's listed.

This tool is quite popular on the market.

Not only can Slowpoke evolve into Slowking with it, but Poliwhirl can also evolve into Politoed using it.

Upon seeing this, D stopped, took out his Rotom Phone, opened the online shopping app, and entered [King's Rock] into the search bar.

Unfortunately, there wasn't a store selling the King's Rock on the app.

Just then, a video call from Geeta came in.

Seeing the name, D was a bit surprised; what did Geeta want?

He pressed to answer, and the next second, Geeta's face appeared on the screen.

The chief champion greeted D with a cheerful smile: "Long time no see, D, how have you been lately?"

"Everything's fine." D noticed Geeta's expression and suddenly had a bad premonition: "What's up?"

Geeta smiled a little: "Well, there's something I need to ask you for a favor with."

Hearing this, D thought to himself, as expected, he knew it!

Geeta continued: "In the past few months, the Paldea League has welcomed several champion-level trainers. As you know, reaching this level of strength, unless something unexpected happens, it's hard to move forward."

D: "...So?"

Geeta nodded with a smile: "So, I recommended your ranch to the champion-level trainers in the Paldea League. They might come to challenge you, so please take care of them!"

D: "..."

He couldn't help but complain: "It feels like I'm working for the Paldea League in another way, but without pay."

"Oh, don't say that!" Geeta said with a smile: "If you need anything, feel free to ask the Paldea League for it!"

"And even though you haven't joined the Paldea League, everyone in the League considers you one of us, they all call you [The Unofficial Champion]."

Having become familiar with Geeta, D didn't hold back.

He boldly stated: "Find me a King's Rock, and I'll host these champion-level trainers!"

---

"A King's Rock? Isn't that the item used to evolve Poliwhirl into Politoed?" Geeta exclaimed in surprise.

D nodded: "Recently, a Slowpoke joined my ranch, and I want to use a King's Rock to evolve it into a Slowking."

"I see." Geeta nodded slightly: "Then it's settled! I'll help you find a King's Rock, and you help me with those Trainers."

"No problem!" D secretly began to rub his hands together in anticipation.

A Champion-level Trainer, huh?

The trials on his ranch were quite the hit earlier; if someone has the intention, it's easy enough to find the forum post and follow the guide to challenge the trials.

Like sending out a Pokemon with a type advantage over the Gogoat, letting it build up power, and then delivering a fatal blow to Gogoat to pass the Grass Trial.

For a Champion-level Trainer, this kind of thing isn't difficult.

Because every Champion has achieved their status through countless Pokemon battles, facing the Grass Trial, they only need to clash once to find a strategy.

But it doesn't matter; even if someone passes the Gogoat's Grass Trial, there are still double battles with Koraidon and Flutter Mane waiting for them.

The call ended, and Geeta immediately dialed Larry's number.

Soon, the call connected, and Geeta smiled and said,

"Larry, there's a new task I need you to work hard on! Once it's done, you can have a three-day paid vacation."

*

Meanwhile, in front of Paldea Academy in Mesagoza City.

A boy, around fourteen or fifteen years old, was looking at his Rotom Phone, at a special task posted by the Chief Champion.

——[Go to D Ranch in Porto Marinada Town and challenge and pass any trial. Depending on your completion level, the Paldea League will issue corresponding rewards.]

The boy's name was Yang, and he had become a newly minted Champion-level Trainer in the Paldea League a week ago.

Since joining the ranks of Champions, he hadn't received any special tasks.

Now, seeing a task personally issued by their Chief Champion, Yang was very surprised.

D Ranch...? What's that place?

Is there a mistake, asking him to go to a countryside ranch for trials?

The fourteen-year-old Champion-level Trainer couldn't help but frown.

Although puzzled, Yang decided to go anyway.

Just then, his Rotom Phone rang again.

Checking it, he saw a message from a friend asking what he was doing.

Yang didn't hide it, telling his friend he had received a task to go to a ranch for trials.

However, he quickly received a few exclamation marks from his friend in response.

[Koyano: D Ranch?!!!]

[Yang: Yes, do you know the place?]

[Koyano: Of course! You've always been traveling and not on the school forum, so you might not know, but that place is famous!]

With that, Koyano sent him a forum link.

Yang clicked in and saw several large characters.

——The Hidden Boss of Campus Legends.

Hidden Boss?

Yang couldn't help but frown, continuing to read.

[Every Trainer in the Paldea Region should know that challenging all the Gyms and obtaining all badges, and then passing the Paldea League's Champion battle does not mean your journey is over.

It is said that in some place in the Paldea Region, there is a mysterious and powerful Trainer. Only by challenging and defeating this person can you become the strongest Trainer in Paldea.]

The person referred to as the Hidden Boss of the Paldea Region in this post is the Rancher, D of D Ranch.

He is a mysterious and powerful Champion Trainer, rumored to have over twenty years of travel experience with combat experience that is exceptionally rich and powerful.

After retiring, D returned to his hometown of Porto Marinada Town, where he operates a ranch and conducts trials with Pokemon, offering challenges to passing Trainers to help them practice battle skills and improve their battle experience.

The trials include: Gogoat's Grass Trial, Pawmot's Thunder Trial, and Dragonite's Storm Trial.

In addition to these, there was an added trial testing both sides' luck through Metronome.

The more Yang read, the more serious his expression became.

This rancher doesn't seem to be an ordinary rancher.

Gogoat, which makes Grassy Terrain only effective for itself; a massive Vine Whip like a python, and Grass Sacred Sword... are those self-created moves?

Then there's Pawmot's Thunder Trial.

The Pawmot can actually fly, wielding a weapon like Tinkaton's Hammer, and can inject electricity into the hammer, controlling it to attack from behind, catching opponents off guard.

Dragonite's Storm Trial is even more exaggerated.

A Dragonite that can create typhoons... opponents must battle amidst extreme storm conditions, and that's when the Dragonite will attack with terrifying Thunder.

In stormy weather, the accuracy of Thunder moves increases greatly, and once hit, even Elite-level Pokemon will be fried inside and out.

If these descriptions are true...

Yang grew more and more alarmed, immediately abandoning the idea of challenging the Storm Trial.

His trump card is a Venusaur, completely countered by Hurricane.

Now, that leaves the Thunder Trial and the Grass Trial...

Between the two, Yang felt both were suitable for him to challenge.

Venusaur has Poison Type, countering the purely Grass Type Gogoat.

And as a Grass Type Pokemon, Venusaur is also resistant to Electric Type, and can use Vine Whip to transfer electricity into the ground, greatly reducing the power of Electric Type moves.

Then it's decided, he'll choose the Thunder Trial!

Yang has a seventy percent chance of passing, because his Venusaur has undergone special training specifically against Electric Type.

The Chief Champion said that if he could pass a trial, they would issue generous rewards.

Being rewarded by the Chief Champion, who is respected and admired by all of Paldea, is a very honorable thing!

---

---

---

---

# 439: Chapter 436: The Hidden Trial of D Ranch, Unlocked!

League year 200, March 16, Spring.

After receiving the task arranged by the chief Champion, Yang set off from Mesagoza City, heading towards Porto Marinada Town.

However, after arriving at the place, he accidentally got lost and couldn't find the location of D Ranch no matter how hard he tried.

At this moment, Yang remembered the content from that guide post on the school forum.

——[D Ranch is located on the outskirts, a bit away from the town. If you can't find it, you can ask the local residents as D Ranch is very well-known around there.]

To complete the chief's task, he decisively stopped an uncle driving by and asked him which way to go to D Ranch.

The uncle was wearing blue overalls, with a towel around his neck. His head, like a light bulb, popped out of the car window, and he replied with a hearty smile:

"Oh! You mean D Ranch? You're going in the wrong direction; D Ranch is to the east of the town, and you've headed south!"

The uncle pointed in the direction of the town and added, "Look, return to the town and take the road from Porto Marinada Market straight east. You'll see a small road extending into the outskirts, and D Ranch is at the end of that road!"

"Alright, I understand." Yang replied politely, "Thank you very much for your help, uncle."

"Hey, no need to thank me. I've met quite a few people like you wanting to visit D Ranch!" The uncle showed a warm smile on his face.

As Yang was about to turn and leave, his heart was moved by what he heard.

Hmm?

Are there many others besides him challenging D Ranch?

No, this was apparent from the post on the school forum.

Those people must all be students from the Paldea Academy, which might be why D Ranch is so famous in the academy.

At that moment, Yang was filled with curiosity about D Ranch. Seeing that the uncle seemed familiar with D Ranch, perhaps he could get more information about that person from him.

With these thoughts in mind, Yang asked.

"Uncle, do you know D?"

Upon hearing this, the uncle was in no hurry to leave, as if he had a lot to say on this topic.

"You mean D? Of course, I know him! He's the most famous Rancher in our town!"

"He hasn't been in the town for long, but in just over a year, he turned an abandoned ranch into a thriving one."

"He also led the town's farmers to believe in Calyrex. All farmers blessed by Calyrex have bountiful crops!"

The uncle couldn't stop talking.

"Let me tell you, D is amazing! A year ago when there was a forest fire, it was D and his very powerful Dragonite who put it out! They saved many Pokemon, and I heard the Paldea League even awarded him a medal."

"Not only that, D also founded Dragonite Delivery. His dairy products are of high quality as well; because of his arrival, our fishing-dependent seaside town is becoming busier."

"Almost every day, tourists and Trainers are drawn here! D? He's the big lucky star of our entire town!"

The uncle's eyes shone brightly, as if he felt immensely proud of D being in Porto Marinada Town.

Yang: "..."

Isn't this a bit exaggerated?

Yang pondered. If all this were true, just how legendary could this person named D be?

Thinking of this, Yang became increasingly curious.

He bid farewell to the uncle and, to reach the ranch quickly and see D with his own eyes, Yang immediately took out a Poke Ball.

"Come on out, Girafarig!"

With a flash of white light, a giraffe-like Pokemon with a long neck, standing over three meters tall, and a head that seemed to wear a hood appeared on this country road.

Yang mounted to ride it and made his way toward the destination.

Before long, a ranch appeared at the road's end.

There seemed to be an intense battle going on there.

From a distance, Yang could see a storm gathering above the ranch. As the fierce wind surged, heavy rain poured down from the sky, forming a terrifying storm.

Yellow lightning brewed in the clouds above, and Yang immediately recognized it as the Electric Type Thunder move.

Although the thunder hadn't struck yet, just hearing that rumbling sound brought a strong sense of pressure.

"How terrifying! Is that the true strength of a Champion-level Trainer?" Yang couldn't help but mutter.

Although they've reached Champion-level Trainers according to the Paldea League's strength ranking, everyone knows that a Champion-level Trainer is not the true Champion.

Compared to the top chief Champion Geeta, their strength is still a notch lower.

"Girafarig, speed up!"

With Yang's command, Girafarig quickened its running pace beneath him.

Soon, they arrived at the ranch.

Sure enough, a fierce battle was underway.

It seemed someone had arrived before him.

Yang looked closely and saw a young girl commanding a Meowscarada in battle with a Dragonite.

And standing opposite the girl was a man with a calm and steady demeanor.

That must be the Rancher, D.

At this point, the battle was reaching its conclusion.

The fierce storm raged across the battlefield, and the Hurricane formed by Flying Type energy engulfed Meowscarada completely.

---

This move Hurricane made the already battered Meowscarada lose its ability to battle.

The storm gradually subsided.

The young Indeedee came forward holding a bottle of Life Sheep Milk and fed it to the Meowscarada.

Not long after, the injuries on Meowscarada were healed.

"Unfortunately, you failed the Storm Trial." D spoke to the girl while noticing another person approaching.

"Wow, that was strong!" The girl seemed to still be in shock from the battle.

Yang recognized this girl who had arrived before him and said in astonishment, "Nanako? How did you get here so quickly?"

"Yang?" The girl called Nanako turned around. Seeing Yang didn't surprise her, and she explained, "I was just nearby in Porto Marinada Town, so I came by for a challenge. Are you here for the trial too?"

Yang nodded, noting from Nanako's expression that she had apparently failed.

D looked at the two with interest, realizing they seemed to know each other.

In terms of the internal workings of the Paldea League, they probably counted as colleagues, right?

Nanako shifted her attention from Yang, checked the injuries on Meowscarada, and then raised her hand to ask:

"Mr. D, can I try some other trials?"

"Of course." D replied with a smile, "Which trial would you like to challenge this time?"

Nanako glanced around. There were quite a few Pokemon from the ranch gathered around the battle area, some looking even stronger than Dragonite, Pawmot, and Gogoat.

Thinking it over, Nanako asked, "Mr. D, are there only the Grass, Storm, Thunder, and Metronome Trials here on the ranch?"

D could see right through the girl's thoughts.

With a mysterious smile, he said, "Not only those. Besides the four main trials, there are some hidden trials on the ranch. But only by passing at least one of the four can you qualify for the hidden ones."

Like the Double Battle Trials of Koraidon and Flutter Mane...

If challengers still feel unsatisfied, then he might invite Calyrex and Giratina to show what the true gods are capable of.

Nanako's eyes lit up instantly upon hearing this.

"Hidden trials?!"

Yang, standing nearby, was also quite surprised.

Based on what he learned from the school forum, the existing trials were already exaggerated. He couldn't even imagine how difficult those hidden trials could be...

D nodded slightly, "Yes, but you guys are not yet qualified to challenge them. To do so, you must first defeat these Pokemon of mine."

Dragonite, Pawmot, and three Gogoat stood behind him, looking at the two challengers along with D.

Nanako was extremely curious, "Mr. D, can we have a preview? Please!"

However, D was adamant, choosing to keep it a secret: "No way! If you want to know, then come and challenge the trial!"

Given the situation, a battle was inevitable! Yang immediately thought.

"Nanako, if you're not challenging the trial, let me battle Mr. D!" Yang couldn't wait any longer.

Hidden trials? He must become the first Trainer to unlock the hidden trials of D Ranch!

"Yang, that's so rude." Nanako said disdainfully.

She initially wanted to challenge the Grass Trial again, but Meowscarada needed to rest for a while after the fierce battle to relieve stress.

So, Nanako stepped aside.

Yang stepped onto the battle ground, realizing that the rancher before him was from the same generation as their chief Champion.

Perhaps because the chief was acquainted with D, he had entrusted D to give special training, trials, and tempering to these Champion-level Trainers to further their training.

He realized the chief's great intention!

"Mr. D, please guide me!" Yang said earnestly.

D nodded and asked, "Do you need an introduction?"

Yang shook his head, "No need, I've already studied in advance."

The strategy guide recorded every detail of the trials, covering each Pokemon's special abilities, custom moves, battle styles, and points to note.

One could say that the trials Mr. D created were completely open to challengers, based on the experiences of countless predecessors.

Though having all the cards exposed is unfair in normal Pokemon battles, not one person has passed the trials so far.

What does that tell you?

There are only two possibilities.

One is that Mr. D is incredibly strong; the other is that all challengers are weak.

Yang: "..."

D was unaware of what this challenger was pondering.

He nodded and asked, "Alright, since that's the case, I won't say more. Your friend just challenged Dragonite's Storm Trial, which trial do you intend to take on?"

"Thunder Trial!" Yang replied without hesitation.

"Thunder Trial, huh?" D was a bit surprised but nodded, "Pawmot, it's your turn!"

"Pamo!"

With a crisp call, a pink-furred Pawmot flew out from behind D and landed on the battlefield.

---

Yang looked in the direction.

The Pawmot had a red cape tied behind it, holding a small silver hammer that made it look both adorable and awe-inspiring.

A flying Pawmot... Is it using electromagnetic levitation?

This ability isn't uncommon; Pokemon like Magnemite and Electrode use electromagnetic levitation to float.

However, it's quite rare to see it on a Pawmot.

"In that case, I'll send you out! Go, Venusaur!"

Yang took out a red and white ball and threw it.

With a flash of white light, a Pokemon with a giant flower on its back, toad-like in appearance and covered in bumps, appeared on the battlefield.

Venusaur, the final evolved form of the Grass Type Starter Pokemon, Bulbasaur, from the Kanto Region.

This was Yang's ace Pokemon.

Starting from Bulbasaur, Yang had been training it towards being an all-rounder.

It could both attack and defend, disrupt opponents and launch frontal assaults, with as many as three different tactics at its disposal.

To counter opponents specializing in reverse type matches, Yang had even trained Venusaur for Thunder Shock.

Plus, Grass Type Venusaur naturally resists Electric Type attacks, so in this battle, victory is assured!

"Whoosh!"

The Indeedee acting as referee blew the whistle hanging around its neck.

Pawmot vs. Venusaur, the battle officially began!

"Venusaur, use Sleep Powder!" Yang immediately commanded, trying to seize the initiative.

Hearing the words "Sleep Powder," D's heart skipped a beat, instinctively thinking that Yang planned to use a sneaky tactic to pass the Thunder Trial.

Venusaur also has a Poison Type, is it going to start poisoning Pawmot next?

A cloud of hypnotic powder started drifting from Venusaur's flower.

In response to this situation, D chose to meet the trick head-on: "Pawmot, fly upward to avoid the range of the Sleep Powder!"

"Pawmo!"

Pawmot immediately flew to a higher position.

Seeing this, Yang quickly ordered Venusaur to pursue.

"Razor Leaf!"

A flurry of sharp leaves immediately followed behind Pawmot.

Pawmot noticed it, tightened its grip on the small hammer, and electric sparks flickered around it as a powerful Electric Type Energy instantly enveloped Mjolnir.

Pawmot threw with force, and the hammer met the Razor Leaf head-on.

The rapidly spinning leaves shattered instantly upon contact with the hammer, but the hammer didn't stop and continued flying towards Venusaur below.

"Venusaur, dodge quickly!" Yang swiftly ordered.

"Charna!"

Venusaur sensed the danger in advance and started to move sideways simultaneously with the trainer's command.

Luckily, reacting quickly, the hammer just grazed its leaves as it landed behind it.

Seeing this, D nodded slightly.

The next second, floating in the sky, Pawmot reached out its hand as if in sync.

The hammer on the ground started shaking violently and then levitated as if being attracted by some force.

It's the Hammer Arm!

Yang immediately recognized it and shouted without hesitation: "Venusaur, watch your back, and use Vine Whip!"

"Charna!"

Venusaur noticed the small hammer flying up from behind.

Two thick vines quickly lashed out from its sides, moving to intercept the hammer.

Then, they caught it in midair.

The two vines tightly wrapped around Mjolnir, stopping its advance and rendering it immobile.

Caught it! That move, which had defeated countless challengers, had its flaw found!

Just thinking about it made Yang feel incredibly excited.

But soon, his eyes widened as he suddenly realized he had overlooked something.

The hammer was just a hammer! Losing it didn't mean the Pawmot had lost its means of attack!

Sure enough, in the next second, Yang saw Pawmot abandon the effort to retrieve the hammer, attacking Venusaur head-on together with the hammer for a pincer attack.

Brilliant electric sparks erupted with both Pokemon at the center.

The intense current flooded the entire battlefield instantly.

In the electric light, Venusaur's vine whips were forced to retract as Pawmot gripped the hammer tightly, electric sparks coursing over its body.

It raised the hammer high, delivering a final strike at the Venusaur below.

"Boom!"

A massive explosion echoed from the center of the battlefield.

Venusaur didn't even have the chance to transfer the electric power into the ground through its vines before losing its ability to fight.

"That Pawmot is so fast!" Nanako exclaimed in surprise.

If it were her, could she pass this Thunder Trial?

The answer was no.

When the smoke on the battlefield completely cleared, Pawmot hovered in the air unscathed, while Venusaur lay on the ground, its eyes becoming swirly like spinning spirals.

Its condition clearly indicated it had lost its ability to fight.

It bore scorch marks from the electric attack and showed signs of heavy bruising, demonstrating the immense strength of Pawmot.

What incredible power!

Initially, Yang thought the content in that post was exaggerated by the netizens, but after experiencing the Thunder Trial firsthand, he realized there was no exaggeration at all...

---

He lost, defeated by the Pawmot despite having a type advantage, and many strategies had not even been put into action.

"I'm sorry, you didn't pass the Thunder Trial."

D was used to this scene; he smiled, watching his sister Indeedee deliver Life Sheep Milk to the opponent.

"Well done, Venusaur, you did very well." Yang comforted the Venusaur, who was feeling down after losing the battle.

The opponent was too unusual.

That Pawmot was so small, so cute, yet it had such immense strength in its body. Moreover, its speed far exceeded Venusaur's.

"Do you want to continue?" D looked at the two Trainers sent by Geeta in front of him.

Nanako instinctively shook her head, having witnessed the entire battle; she felt she had no chance of winning.

However, Yang nodded: "Mr. D, I want to continue the challenge, please let me think for a moment."

"Okay." D had no objections.

Yang fell into deep thought.

Venusaur had already fallen; with the strength of his remaining Pokemon, he feared they were no match for that Pawmot either.

So what if he started with the other two Trials?

Yang suddenly thought of the various descriptions about the Grass Trial mentioned in the strategy guide.

The endlessly recovering Gogoat, the special "Grass Sacred Sword," the powerful Vine Whip...

Regarding the Grass Trial strategy, there was currently only one possible way to pass.

That was to use moves like Meditation, Dragon Dance, and Sword Dance to maximize the Pokemon's abilities, and then attack the Gogoat with the strongest move.

If you can defeat it with one move, then Gogoat's endless Grassy Terrain and super recovery lose their effect.

Besides that, there's another way, which is to use Taunt to put Gogoat into a state of taunt, preventing it from using Grassy Terrain and Synthesis moves.

But those Gogoats are very special because, according to the forums, their Grassy Terrain is almost instantaneous; the moment the battle starts, the entire battleground turns into Grassy Terrain.

The opponent's Pokemon has no opportunity to use Taunt.

In that case, the first method is the only option.

And right beside him, there was a Pokemon that met the requirement.

With this in mind, Yang opened his eyes and firmly said to D, "Mr. D, I've made up my mind, I want to challenge the Grass Trial next."

Grass Trial? D looked at the Gogoat behind him and asked, "Are you sure?"

"Yes." Yang nodded.

"Alright then." D said, "But unlike the other two trials, the battle mode for the Grass Trial is 3v3, and you can use three Pokemon. Once all the Gogoats lose their combat ability, you pass."

"Three Pokemon?" Yang pondered thoughtfully and nodded, "I understand, please start!"

Seeing the expression on the youth's face, D guessed he had thought of a counter-strategy.

He was somewhat curious about how Yang would challenge the Grass Trial.

The battle officially began.

D's Gogoat walked onto the battleground.

For the first battle, Yang sent out a Pokemon that surprised D greatly.

A small body, covered in large, fluffy, soft cotton.

It was a Whimsicott, a Grass and Fairy Type Pokemon.

The battle began; Gogoat instantly released a Grassy Terrain that covered the whole battlefield.

Meanwhile, Yang ordered the Cottonee to use Tailwind.

Tailwind: A Flying Type move, which creates a tailwind for a period of time, increasing the speed of all allied Pokemon.

Seeing this, D squinted his eyes. Tailwind? Interesting.

"Gogoat, Vine Whip!" In response, D chose to break the enemy with force.

"Meee!"

Gogoat let out a cry, and two thick vine whips, as large as pythons, lashed out at incredible speed toward Whimsicott.

Witnessing this terrifying move, Whimsicott let out a startled cry, and the next moment, the two vine whips ensnared it, crossing around it.

Just as the vine whips were about to crush Whimsicott, it took advantage of its small size to squeeze out from the gap.

Seeing this situation, Yang seized the opportunity to command, "Whimsicott, use Sunny Day!"

Whimsicott exerted its efforts; with Tailwind's effect, its body wavered around the field like a dandelion.

At first, D didn't know what Yang intended by sending out Whimsicott.

But with Sunny Day's activation, he instantly realized.

Yang was setting up an advantageous battle environment for the upcoming Pokemon!

Realizing this, D immediately ordered, "Gogoat, don't give it the chance, use Leaf Blade!"

"Meee!"

Upon receiving the instruction, Gogoat unleashed a torrent of Grass Energy from its mouth, and almost instantly, a massive Grass Sacred Sword formed.

In the next second, Gogoat shot out like an arrow, defeating the Whimsicott using Sunny Day in an instant.

Yang's heart jumped to his throat; he looked nervously towards the sky.

The sunlight gradually intensified, the temperature began to rise, and golden rays of sunlight showered the ground.

Seeing this, Yang's heart leaped for joy.

Yes! Sunny Day succeeded!

---

"Come back! Whimsicott, your task is complete, thank you for your hard work!" Yang retrieved Whimsicott with a Poke Ball.

At this moment, he felt a glimmer of hope for victory in his heart.

"Are you planning to use a Fire-Type Pokemon to defeat Gogoat in one move?" D nodded, "Nice idea. If that's the case, then show me!"

So far, only Leon has passed the Grass Trial.

D was very curious if today would witness the emergence of the second trainer to pass.

Otherwise, he might think that the difficulty of the Grass Trial is too exaggerated and plan to lower it a bit.

"Yes!" Yang nodded seriously. To live up to the expectations of the leader, he planned to give his all in today's battle!

In the second match, Yang sent out a Leafeon.

The battle began once again.

Though he anticipated a second person passing the Grass Trial, D hadn't deliberately gone easy.

Thus, as soon as the battle started, he immediately commanded an attack on Gogoat.

"Once more!"

Gogoat began its initial move.

Yang watched the Grass Energy surging in Gogoat's mouth, his palms started sweating, but he remained calm, issuing commands to Leafeon:

"Leafeon, listen carefully, don't confront Gogoat head-on. Once there's a chance, use Sword Dance, then use Substitute to dodge attacks. When you can't hold on any longer, immediately use Baton Pass!"

"Leafeon!"

Leafeon also sensed its opponent's strength at this moment.

And right then, Gogoat charged at Leafeon with Leaf Blade in its mouth.

Following the trainer's instructions, Leafeon instantly used Substitute, leaving a small dinosaur doll behind while its true form disappeared.

The moment Substitute ended, Leafeon seized the opportunity and used Sword Dance.

Meanwhile, Gogoat's Grass Sacred Sword instantly shattered Leafeon's Substitute.

"What is that guy planning to do?" Nanako watching the battle couldn't help but frown.

Meanwhile, D had already realized Yang's plan.

Substitute combined with Sword Dance, is it also laying the groundwork for what's next?

If he recalled correctly, Leafeon could use the move Baton Pass.

Baton Pass, a Normal Type move, allows a Pokemon to switch out directly with a reserve Pokemon.

Even more amazingly, the incoming Pokemon can inherit its ability changes directly.

Be it Tailwind, Sunny Day, or Sword Dance, all were groundwork laid for the last Pokemon to come.

"Interesting." It was the first time D had encountered a trainer using such tactics in reality.

No wonder Paldea Region is full of talented individuals!

Thinking so in his mind, he mercilessly ordered, "Gogoat, use Vine Whip to sweep the surroundings with full force!"

After using Substitute, a Pokemon doesn't disappear into thin air but appears elsewhere on the battlefield.

The Substitute dinosaur is merely an item tool created by them using energy to confuse opponents.

Upon receiving the command, Gogoat unleashed a terrifying burst of Grass Energy.

The energy transformed into two gigantic Vine Whips, and from the center of Gogoat, began indiscriminately sweeping across the entire battlefield.

Witnessing this scene, both Yang and Nanako couldn't help but open their eyes wide.

"What's going on?" Nanako murmured to herself.

Yang felt his heart leap into his throat and shouted, "Dig, use Dig!"

"Leafeon!"

On the field, upon seeing the fan-like Vine Whips, Leafeon almost froze.

Only after hearing the trainer's voice did it hastily use Dig, burrowing underground.

D: "Gogoat, use Bulldoze to force it out!"

Gogoat retrieved the Vine Whip, its gaze became sharp, and then it stomped heavily on the ground with its front feet.

In the following second, the entire battlefield began to shake violently.

"Boom!"

Leafeon underground felt the pressure from the soil and was forced out to the surface.

Waiting for it on the ground was Gogoat's massive Vine Whip.

Leafeon looked up at the giant Vine Whip in front of it and jumped in fright.

"Leafeon!"

Seeing the Vine Whip about to strike down, Leafeon used its last Substitute.

At this moment, having used Substitute twice consecutively, Leafeon was close to exhausting its energy.

What did the trainer say again?

Use Baton Pass when you can't hold on any longer!

"Leafeon!"

Without hesitation, Leafeon used Baton Pass.

Its body turned into a light and returned to its Poke Ball, switching with the Pokemon inside.

Leafeon: Now, it's up to you!

In that light, a Pokemon draped in purple armor, with burning purple flames on its blade-like arms, appeared at Leafeon's last position like a specter.

That was... Ceruledge!

Yang immediately tossed a bead toward Ceruledge.

The bead fell onto Ceruledge, instantly bursting into a dazzling light.

Within the light, Ceruledge's body became engulfed in Tera Energy.

On its head appeared a candelabrum and flame.

That was the mark of Tera Flare.

---

The current Ceruledge is no ordinary Ceruledge.

It is a complete Fire Tera Ceruledge that has inherited Leafeon's two consecutive times, Whimsicott's Tailwind, and Sunny Day.

Seeing this, D couldn't help but raise his eyebrows.

"Truly remarkable!" he exclaimed.

Although he also has a Tera Orb to Terastalize Gogoat, that Tera Orb is not with him but left at home.

It's impossible to interrupt the battle to fetch it now.

"This is our final power, Mr. D, please help us test it!" Yang's eyes burned with fiery determination: "Ceruledge, use Bitter Blade!"

"Gogoat, let's go too!" D softly said.

With the command given, both Pokemon moved simultaneously.

Gogoat gathered a massive Grass Sacred Sword in its mouth.

Ceruledge gently touched its blade with its forehead, as if focusing its attachment to the world at the sword's tip.

A fierce purple flame ignited on its body, and then it swung the blade, slashing towards Gogoat.

The mighty fiery energy and Gogoat's Grass Energy collided and neutralized, finally reaching a critical point and resulting in a tremendous explosion.

The entire battlefield was engulfed in dazzling light, with scorching waves of fire spreading in all directions.

Yang was extremely tense inside.

Can we win?

A moment later, he got his answer.

On the battlefield, the powerful Gogoat had fallen to the ground, while his Ceruledge stood calmly not far away, gazing at the Gogoat.

Victory!

Yang was extremely excited inside.

However, the next second, a surge of Grass Energy emerged from the Grassy Terrain.

This energy entered Gogoat's body, and in the blink of an eye, Gogoat got back up from the ground.

Yang: ?!!

How could this be? Yang's mouth hung open, such an exaggerated recovery effect!

D: "..."

How could a Gogoat, blessed by nature, fall so easily?

Chosen by the World Tree, they, like the grass of this world, have life that is endless and abundant.

But there was no need to continue.

"Come back, Gogoat!" He recalled Gogoat: "We lost this battle."

Yang was slightly stunned.

D smiled and continued: "There are still two more!"

Yang, slightly stunned, seemed to understand something, then nodded heavily.

"I understand!"

Gogoat and Ceruledge collided once more.

Five minutes later, with the fall of the last Gogoat, the second Trainer to pass the Grass Trial was born.

Nanako widened her eyes in shock: "You actually passed!"

Yang tried to maintain composure and calm: "This is the result of my efforts with my Pokemon! Whimsicott, Leafeon, Venusaur, Ceruledge, thank you!"

Having defeated strong opponents, both the Pokemon and their Trainer were very happy.

D, meanwhile, smiled at the young man and said: "Congratulations on successfully passing the Grass Trial. As a reward, you can receive the Magical Berry Wine brewed by me."

However, Yang wasn't very concerned; he turned to D, ready to push forward and attempt the hidden trial at the ranch.

"Mr. D, can you tell me what the hidden trial in the ranch is now?"

Upon hearing this, D was slightly taken aback, then inquired with interest: "Do you really want to know?"

"Yes!" Yang quickly nodded.

"I can't refuse you, but since you've passed the Grass Trial, it means you have the qualification to challenge it." D looked back at Koraidon.

"Ah gah-ss." Koraidon, in sync, stepped forward.

D introduced: "This is Koraidon. The next trial is the Ancient Trial, where the challenge is to battle Koraidon."

"However, unlike other trials, in this trial you can find teammates to form a double battle, and Koraidon will also have a teammate, following a 2v2 format."

"Of course, you can also direct your two Pokemon alone for a double battle."

Looking at the Pokemon named Koraidon in front of him, Yang's eyes were full of excitement as he decided to take on the challenge.

"I want to try!"

"Alright."

D smiled slightly, not saying much more.

He returned to the battlefield, and Yang sent out Ceruledge once again.

The battle officially began.

Ceruledge tried to use Bitter Blade to attack Koraidon, but before it could strike, Koraidon was already in front of it.

With one claw, Ceruledge was instantly rendered incapable of battling.

Yang: ??!

"So, so strong!" Nanako opened her mouth in disbelief, her expression shocked.

This Pokemon was even stronger than the three Gogoats and Dragonite just now!

At this moment, both Yang and Nanako understood what D meant earlier, about not having the qualification to challenge the hidden trial before passing the three main trials.

That Koraidon was stronger than they had imagined, its strength likely reaching another level!

"Mr. D, is this the hidden trial in the ranch?" Nanako asked with a trembling voice.

D thought carefully, then shook his head: "No, besides Koraidon's Ancient Trial, there are a few other trials, but those... we'll talk about them after you defeat Koraidon!"

---

Defeat Koraidon?!

Doesn't that mean that the Pokémon left in the trial are stronger than Koraidon?

Koraidon's strength already surpassed their expectations, and the remaining Pokémon's strength is unimaginable, they dared not even think...

The two looked around, trying to find a Pokémon among those that might be stronger than Koraidon.

D saw through their thoughts, but didn't explain, instead, he smiled and waved his hand.

Brother Indeedee understood and turned away, soon returning with three servings of fruit wine.

"Take it! This is your reward for passing the trial, and as the second Trainer to pass, the other two bottles are my extra reward for you. I'm delighted to see such an exciting match, your wisdom and courage are truly commendable!" D said.

Yang lowered his head to look at the three bottles of fruit wine.

While Nanako noticed the keyword "second,"

she asked in surprise, "Has someone passed the trial before this guy?"

D nodded slightly: "That's right, there was a very strong Trainer."

"Was it the chief?" Nanako asked in surprise.

In the Paldea Region, the chief is the strongest Trainer.

D shook his head: "No, the chief is too busy with work to challenge the trial."

Nanako nodded, quite shocked.

D then introduced the effects of the fruit wine to Yang.

Initially, Yang and Nanako thought it was just ordinary fruit wine.

Until they heard from D that the fruit wine could enhance a Pokémon's resistance to abnormal states, their faces instantly showed shock.

"Really?"

"Of course." D said: "I've used a family secret method to brew this fruit wine, merging the essence of berries to improve a Pokémon's physique and help them grow better."

"How amazing!" Nanako exclaimed, looking at the fruit wine with envy.

She wanted it too, but she had no way to pass the trial yet...

Besides, her Pokémon are all Grass Type, unable to use the same tactics as Yang.

Ah! How frustrating!

Compared to Nanako's frustration, Yang was much more calm.

"Thank you very much, Mr. D, but this is too precious and I can't accept it, besides the chief has already prepared a reward for me."

D was quite surprised to hear this.

Oh? This young man is interesting.

"This is the reward you deserve," D said: "Every Trainer who passes receives it, it's my gift to the brave."

Hearing this, Yang opened his mouth and then seriously accepted the fruit wine: "Thank you, Mr. D!"

Mr. D is indeed as the town rumors say, a great person!

*

Soon after sending off the two challengers, D received a video call from Geeta.

"How was it?"

D smiled: "That trainer named Yang is quite interesting, apart from Leon, he's the first to pass the Grass Trial."

D detailed the events of the day.

After listening, a peculiar light shone in Geeta's eyes.

"So, this Trainer's talent is quite good!"

"So where's my King's Rock?" D asked.

"Don't worry," Geeta smiled: "I've already entrusted Larry to look for it for you!"

D: "…Don't wear Larry out, when I saw him earlier he seemed exhausted."

Hearing this, Geeta smiled and said: "Larry has been quite energetic lately! I heard he's been buying that mysterious milk from your ranch often."

"I've heard from the sales department that when Larry drinks the milk, his work efficiency increases several fold!"

Said this, Geeta suddenly remembered something: "Oh right, I almost forgot, D, do you want to collaborate with me?"

D was stunned for a moment, suddenly sensing an ominous feeling: "What kind of collaboration?"

Geeta: "Sell the milk from your ranch to the Paldea League, we can purchase it at a high price!"

D: "…"

"Ahem, let's just forget about this." D decisively rejected Geeta's proposal, he did not want to become an accomplice: "Boosting energy temporarily with external things might be fine, but over time, it'll be harmful to the body!"

Hearing this, Geeta was quite surprised: "Really? It'll be harmful? I thought the milk from your ranch was like caffeine without side effects!"

Sheep's milk indeed has no side effects, but everyone needs a proper break. If you're working every day relying on sheep's milk, then that's way too mechanical.

Thinking about this, D nodded seriously and said, "Anything can be addictive, so it's better to be natural unless you really need it or you're planning to boost your health."

"Alright then." Upon hearing that long-term consumption of sheep's milk could be addictive, Geeta gave up on the idea.

"Well, if there's nothing else, I'll hang up now!" D was worried that Geeta might try sneaking in a conversation about squeezing the poor workers.

However, Geeta brought up another topic instead.

"By the way, D, are you and Lono pretty close?"

D was a bit surprised: "Why? Did something happen?"

"Nothing!" Geeta shook her head. "I just feel like you two seem quite familiar."

She occasionally went to see what the Gym Leaders she managed were up to.

Upon hearing Lono had started a live stream, Geeta was curious enough to check it out.

At that time, in the streaming room, she saw Lono discreetly advertising D Ranch.

Thinking it over, D said, "It might be because Lono visits the ranch quite often!"

Most of the other Gym Leaders had only come once, but Lono had visited the most.

"I see..." Geeta nodded thoughtfully.

A woman's intuition told her that there was something unusual about this situation.

As far as she knew, Lono wasn't the type of Gym Leader to eagerly improve their skills.

After a brief moment of thought, Geeta asked, "Did Lono challenge the trial when she visited?"

D thought for a moment and nodded, "She did."

This made it even stranger, Geeta thought to herself.

Looking at D across from her, an incredible suspicion popped into her mind.

Could it be that Lono...

Seeing Geeta's expression, D found it a bit odd: "What's up?"

Geeta snapped back to reality and shook her head with a smile, "Nothing big. The upcoming challengers will rely on you, okay? I have things to do here, so I'll catch up with you later!"

After the call ended, Geeta had an intrigued look on her face.

She planned to look into Lono's intentions later.

If it were just as she suspected, she wouldn't mind playing matchmaker.

If those two ended up together, D would stay in the Paldea Region, and she wouldn't have to worry about him moving elsewhere.

When the time comes for them to wed, she could attend as the officiator.

In that moment, Geeta thought about many things.

A talented person like D would surely have exceptional offspring.

Those would be the future talents of the Paldea Region!

*

Three days later, at the Paldea League Headquarters.

Yang walked into this tall building clad in a suit.

Three days ago, he reported to the chief about passing the trial.

The chief, as promised, had allocated his reward.

A generous reward, along with a promotion to work in the Paldea League's operations department.

Yang felt immense pride in this matter.

With anticipation for the new job and dreams for the future, he headed into the operations department building.

In the office, Yang noticed a very famous figure.

It was none other than his senior, Larry, the Gym Leader of Medali Gym, and also the Flying Elite!

Yang was thrilled; was he about to become colleagues with his senior?

He walked up happily and greeted Larry:

"Good morning, senior! I'm Yang, newly transferred to the operations department. Please guide me well!"

Larry: "..."

He looked wearily at the young man.

Young people always full of energy and spirit... it's truly nostalgic!

Larry nodded at the young man, saying, "Hmm, hello. I have some tasks to attend to, let's chat next time."

"Ah, alright, senior!" Yang quickly stepped aside, watching his senior's figure gradually depart.

---

---

---

---

# 440: Chapter 437: Wishiwashi Shoal Under the Sea, Slowpoke and Shellder

League Year 200, March 21, D once again met Larry.

"This is the King's Rock that Chief asked me to give you, I've delivered it."

Larry was dressed in a black suit, his black shoes slightly dusted and no longer shiny, and there was a trace of fatigue on his young face, making him look quite spiritless.

Upon seeing Larry, D remembered Geeta mentioning how Larry bought goat milk online from his family to stay awake for overtime work.

At this moment, seeing Larry like this, D couldn't help but say: "Thank you for your trouble, Mr. Larry, would you like to come in and have a cup of tea to rest for a bit?"

Larry shook his head wearily: "No, I've delivered what I needed to, my work is done, now it's time to head home."

After completing this task, he received a three-day paid leave, and next, Larry planned to use these days to rest well at home.

"Alright then."

D nodded, only able to watch as Larry turned and left.

After all, for a working person like Larry, home is the most comfortable place.

It wasn't until Larry's figure disappeared at the end of the road that D withdrew his gaze.

He looked down at the essential item in his hand for Slowpoke's evolution - the King's Rock.

The King's Rock in reality wasn't much different from how it appeared in games, it was a golden crown, though it seemed quite ancient with marks of time erosion.

This item, likely the crown of an ancient king, serves as proof of a king. Perhaps because it had been shrouded by a king's aura for a long time, it possessed a hint of a king's power.

Slowpoke and Poliwhirl can transform into the rulers of their respective groups by absorbing this power.

Now with the King's Rock, he only needed to find a Shellder willing to coexist with Slowpoke, and Slowking could be born.

Compared to the King's Rock, finding a Shellder was much easier.

Because Shellder is a very common Pokemon, usually residing in the sea.

D had previously checked with his Rotom Phone; Shellders could be spotted along any coastal area of the Paldea Region.

Conveniently, Porto Marinada Town is near Paldea West Sea.

So next, let's head to the beach to find a Shellder and help Slowpoke evolve into Slowking!

D found Slowpoke's Poke Ball and then went to the World Tree to bring out Slowpoke.

"I'm going out for a bit, I'll be back soon." D, with the diving equipment he previously bought, informed the Indeedee Sibling about his trip before departing.

"Indeedy~" The siblings nodded, indicating they would take good care of everything.

"I'm counting on you." D patted the heads of the siblings.

He turned to look around, checking what the other Pokemon were up to.
