```html
The Eevees were playing hide and seek in the pasture, Pawmo's parents were watching over the hens at the Chicken House, Farfetch'd was working out in the Scallion Garden, and the Dragonites had gone out to work.

Most of the remaining Pokemon were playing across the river in Snorlax Park.

After a word with the Indeedee Sibling, D headed to Porto Marinada Town beach with Koraidon and Cyclizar, searching for traces of Shellder.

The sky blue, the water clear, the sun bright.

The vast sea surface was calm, occasionally a few Wingulls flapped their wings across the sea, then folded their wings to rest on the beach.

"Come out! Slowpoke!"

Accompanied by a flash of white light, Slowpoke's figure appeared on the beach.

It still had its eyes open with a blank gaze, staring ahead in a daze.

After a while, Slowpoke seemed to realize that its surroundings had changed, and it opened its cream-colored mouth.

"Slow?"

D placed the King's Rock sent by Larry on Slowpoke's head.

The crowned Slowpoke continued to stare in a daze.

D observed for a while, feeling it was quite perfect.

Indeed, the King's Rock seemed quite fitting for Slowpoke.

Aside from the somewhat perceptive eyes of Slowpoke, everything else was great.

Next, it was time to find a Shellder compatible with Slowpoke.

D, with Koraidon and Cyclizar, looked around, scouring the beach but unable to find any Shellder.

On the golden sands, apart from a few Wigletts and resting Wingulls, there were no other Pokemon.

Seeing this, D had Koraidon switch to Wave Riding Form again.

Instantly, Koraidon, originally in its Riding Form, transformed once more.

It spread its hands to make them suitable for swimming; its previously curled-up tail unfolded, and the tire at its chest acted as a float.

In its Wave Riding Form, Koraidon stood before the sea, now appearing as a water motorcycle ready to be ridden.

"Cyclizar, wait here for us, okay!"

D embraced Slowpoke, mounted Koraidon, and looking back at the waiting Cyclizar, spoke to it.

"Garrr."

Cyclizar nodded obediently, envious of how Koraidon could help D with anything.

In comparison to Koraidon, it felt utterly useless.

Even with hard work to get stronger and practice moves, it couldn't help D.

For a moment, Cyclizar felt uncertain about the purpose of its actions.

---

"Gaurgh..."

Cyclizar sat on the beach, letting out a sorrowful sigh.

Meanwhile, D and Koraidon had gradually moved away from the shore.

Though Koraidon could swim, it couldn't dive; staying underwater for a minute or two was fine, but even a Legendary Pokemon would drown if submerged for too long.

So, D patted Koraidon's head and said to it, "Wait here for me, I'll be back soon!"

"Aga-sizz!"

Koraidon knew it couldn't be of much help, so it nodded its large head.

D donned a diving suit and, with Slowpoke, dove into the sea.

The underwater world was a vast blue, with sunlight streaming through the surface, creating a sparkling scene below.

The seabed was filled with various corals and seaweed, with countless Water Type Pokemon swimming leisurely among them.

Love-shaped Alomomola, schools of Wishiwashi, white Wigletts, and Finneons emitting a gentle glow...

D's eyes swept over this underwater wonderland.

Shellder, a type of Pokemon often residing in coastal seabeds, cleverly used its ever-extended tongue to dig into the sand in search of food.

At night, they would hide in the holes they dug and sleep inside them.

So... to find Shellder, one should focus on the sand of the seabed.

D carried Slowpoke on his back and continued to dive deeper.

During this time, he passed by a spectacular school of Wishiwashi.

The school, sensing an unfamiliar presence, scattered in fright.

Though large in number, they hadn't reached the critical mass needed to form the "Demon of the Sea," so they still opted to flee when faced with danger.

Speaking of Wishiwashi, they are indeed intriguing Pokemon.

Individually, they are quite weak, considered food by many Pokemon, like Wailord, which is said to eat thirty tons of Wishiwashi in a day.

But when their numbers are sufficient, they organize into a massive formation to fend off enemies.

Legend has it that when Wishiwashi unite into this form, they wield a power worthy of the Sea Demon.

Even fierce Pokemon like Gyarados would flee in their presence.

D took out his Rotom Phone and captured footage of the Wishiwashi school swimming in the azure waters.

After a while, realizing there was no threat, the school swam back, drifting around the area.

"Rotom, ask these Wishiwashi if there are any Shellders living around here," D instructed Rotom.

When Rotom Technology Company first developed the Rotom Phone, they specifically incorporated waterproof features, thus allowing it to function normally even underwater.

"Got it, Roto!"

Rotom responded, flying forward to find a Wishiwashi and relayed D's words to it.

The Wishiwashi were initially frightened but, upon hearing Rotom, they carefully examined the human and cautiously replied:

"&%@#..."

The Rotom Phone auto-decoded the response: [Yes, there are Shellders here, they're all in the sand, Roto...]

Shellders typically wouldn't harm Wishiwashi; the two were not natural enemies and maintained a relatively harmonious relationship, so the Wishiwashi had some awareness of the Shellders dwelling below.

"In the sand?" D looked down at the seabed and nodded, "Got it, thank you."

He retrieved two raw eggs from his pocket and cracked them open.

The transparent egg whites floated away in the seawater.

Drawn by the scent, a few Wishiwashi cautiously approached and began to eat the egg whites and yolks.

Soon, more Wishiwashi were attracted to the scene.

The two eggs weren't enough, so D took out a small bottle of milk, opened it, and offered it to the Wishiwashi.

Now the Wishiwashi were no longer afraid of D.

They gathered at the bottle's opening, sipping the milk that flowed from it.

"Eat up, eat up!" D smiled broadly as he watched.

Used to a diet of plankton and seaweed, the Wishiwashi had never tasted something so delicious, and their eyes sparkled.

Once they finished the bottle of milk, the Wishiwashi's gaze toward D changed dramatically.

Now, a few clever Wishiwashi started to lead the way.

The other Wishiwashi followed suit, forming a large school, gesturing for D to follow.

Seeing this, D was quite surprised: "Are you going to lead me?"

The Wishiwashi school made sounds like the swirling of water.

Rotom: [Yes, Roto!]

D hadn't anticipated this turn of events, showing a look of amazement.

He followed the Wishiwashi school deeper, until reaching the seabed.

Here, the sand was white, and scattered light danced gently, casting a kaleidoscopic display.

Some Wigletts lived here, an alternate form of Diglett. They had long, thin white bodies, resembling sea eels.

As D approached, the Wigletts timidly retreated into their hiding stones.

The Wishiwashi school didn't stop, so D continued to follow them further.

---

After passing through this white sand area, a coral reef made up of rocks and corals appeared ahead.

On a large underwater rock, D finally spotted a Shellder.

Found it!

D was very excited, with this, Slowking could be born!

He turned to look at the Slowpoke on his back, preparing to approach a Shellder and ask for its opinion.

If the other agrees, then they could evolve first, and if it feels inappropriate after evolving, they could revert back.

The relationship between Slowpoke and Shellder is very special, and their evolution is different from other Pokémon.

This "symbiotic evolution" can be reversed at any time.

Just by removing the Shellder biting on the Slowpoke, the evolved Slowking or Slowbro can revert back to a Slowpoke and Shellder.

A school of Wishiwashi stopped not far away.

They neither approached nor left, but stayed there curiously observing the human's actions.

D, carrying Slowpoke, swam forward.

The Shellder was half-opened, with a pair of eyes in the dark shell, and a large pink tongue sticking out from the shell.

However, before D could approach, the alert Shellder noticed him.

With a muffled "pop" sound, Shellder immediately clamped its shell shut, and with the shells constantly opening and closing, it quickly swam backward.

And during its escape, it seemed to have spit out something.

D looked closely and found that it was a beautiful pearl!

D: "..."

It seems that the Pokedex was right... Shellder tends to spit out its pearl because it finds it bothersome.

That Shellder escaped, so D had to focus on other Shellders.

Just then, D suddenly felt the Slowpoke on his back move.

He turned around in confusion, only to see Slowpoke jump down itself, its normally sluggish eyes seemingly drawn by something, slowly moving its limbs and swimming toward a target.

Following Slowpoke's gaze, D saw a Shellder blowing bubbles on a protruding rock.

The pearl that the previous Shellder dropped happened to land on this bubble-blowing Shellder's shell.

Slowpoke swam to the front of the Shellder and stopped there, its eyes moving up, staring blankly at the beautiful pearl.

The Shellder, which had been sleeping with its shell closed, seemed to sense something unusual.

It slowly opened its shell, and upon opening its eyes, saw a pink unfamiliar Pokémon suddenly appear in front of it.

!!!

Shellder was startled, and instinctively used Shell Clamp on Slowpoke.

Slowpoke's head was immediately bitten by the Shellder.

The next second, countless bubbles appeared in the seawater, and a dazzling light burst from the connection of the two Pokémon.

The light of evolution instantly engulfed the two Pokémon, and within the light, Shellder and Slowpoke's bodies gradually merged into one.

When the light dissipated, a new Pokémon appeared before D and the school of Wishiwashi.

It was a bipedal Pokémon with an orange and white umbrella-like structure around its neck, crowned with a Shellder crown.

Its eyes were full of wisdom, yet it still looked quite silly.

D: "..."

Is this Slowking? Why does it still look so not-so-smart?

Slowking, however, turned its gaze toward him and opened its mouth, emitting a stream of bubbles.

"&@#%."

D was slightly taken aback. Was that Slowking speaking?

"Rotom, could you help translate?" He looked at his Rotom Phone.

However, the Rotom Phone translated a garbled message: "Unable to translate, Roto!"

"Scan Slowking." D asked a different question.

Rotom: "Slowking, known as the sage of the sea, is always calm and composed.

There is a saying in some regions, [If lost, ask Slowking], indicating it is well-known for its intelligence.

Although Slowking can solve any problem posed to it, no one can understand its language, Roto!"

D: "..."

So after evolving, did Slowking create its own language system?

Anyway, as long as Slowking has evolved successfully.

Although the method of evolution was a bit strange, it seems Slowking is getting along quite well with Shellder now.

Perhaps?

"Slowking, do you still recognize me?" D swam in front of Slowking, pointed at himself, and asked.

Slowking looked at him with wise eyes, then nodded gently.

Of course, it remembered that it was this human named D who brought it here and helped it evolve into Slowking.

However...

Who is it? Where is it? Why does this world exist?

Slowking did not understand, and it inevitably fell into pondering the world.

D looked up at the surface and said to Slowking: "Shall we leave here first? We can talk more when we get back to the ranch."

Slowking thought of everything about the World Tree and nodded, speaking another string of words D couldn't understand.

As D swam upwards with Slowking, he recalled the plot about Slowking in the anime.

If he remembered correctly, in the movie "Revelation Lugia," there was a Slowking living on Shamouti Island.

That Slowking was very special; it could not only foresee the future but also speak the human language.

So here's the question: is that something all Slowking can learn, or is that particular Slowking the only one in this world who can learn human speech?

D wasn't sure, and he decided to have a good chat with this Slowking after returning to the ranch.

Since the Phone Rotom couldn't translate, he'd try using Translation Nuts Cake instead.

---

---

---

---

# 441: Chapter 438: The Clueless Great Sage and Cyclizar's Inner Woes

Before surfacing, D looked at the school of Wishiwashi leading the way for him.

Wishiwashi, this kind of Pokemon, has moist, glowing eyes, making them irresistibly endearing and very cute.

Plus, due to their very weak power, they naturally have a pitiful and lovable demeanor.

D reached into his pocket, took out the last bottle of MooMoo Milk, opened the cap, and let the milky white liquid flow into the sea.

Seeing this, the school of Wishiwashi were overjoyed, eagerly swimming forward to drink the milk.

Looking at these adorable little Fish-type Pokemon, D was moved and then asked:

"Wishiwashi, would you like to come live with me?"

The school of Wishiwashi quickly finished sharing the milk, and upon hearing these words, they all turned their gaze toward D.

"To my place, where no Pokemon will harm you, and I'll teach you how to plant seaweed underwater, so that as long as you spend a little time tending to it, you'll never have to worry about being hungry." D said with a smile.

Wishiwashi, these Water Type Pokemon, are indeed so cute, and more than that, they can guide him.

D wanted to invite them back to settle on Dragonite Island, as a way to thank them for helping him find Shellder, and to bolster the nearby marine ecosystem, creating a complete ecological system.

The Wishiwashi at this moment had fully trusted this human in front of them, and upon hearing this, they happily agreed.

Seeing this, D happily took out a Poke Ball and captured the Wishiwashi.

But the number of Wishiwashi was really too large, with at least a hundred in front, and the Poke Balls were simply not enough.

After thinking it over briefly, D asked the Wishiwashi to wait here for a bit, then surfaced.

Koraidon was idly patting the sea water with its claws.

Hearing the commotion from the sea surface not far away, it quickly lifted its head and looked over in that direction.

"Ah-gah-sis!"

Koraidon swiftly swam over.

D reached out and patted its large head, then used the Rotom Phone to call Calyrex, intending to ask for his help in using Psychic to directly transfer these Wishiwashi into the World Tree.

Soon, the call connected, and upon hearing D's plan, Calyrex said nothing.

He directly used Teleport to arrive at the beach of Porto Marinada Town and then used Psychic again to transfer all these Wishiwashi, Slowking, D, Koraidon, and Cyclizar into the World Tree.

On Dragonite Island, the Dragonites, who were sitting on the beach eating Berries, were all startled by the sudden occurrence.

It wasn't until they clearly saw D's figure that they all breathed a sigh of relief.

So it was D!

D greeted the Dragonites and then released the school of Wishiwashi into this sea area.

He jumped into the water and said to the school of Wishiwashi: "From now on, this place is your new home. There are no dangers here, so rest assured and settle down!"

The school of Wishiwashi looked around and didn't find any dangers in this sea area, which meant that what D said was true.

A sea without dangers... The school of Wishiwashi also saw lush seaweed and algae growing on the seabed.

So much food, is this paradise?

The faces of the Wishiwashi showed expressions of happiness.

"So that's settled then, I still have some things to do, I'll come back later to teach you how to cultivate algae and create a bustling underwater farm." D said with a light laugh.

The school of Wishiwashi nodded repeatedly and then began forming a large pack to explore this unfamiliar sea area.

Next, D planned to take Slowking to the top layer of the World Tree to have a proper conversation with it using Translation Nuts Cake.

Before leaving, he specifically instructed the Dragonites not to harm those newly joined Wishiwashi.

Seeing D say this, the Dragonites, already having an abundance of seabass and Berries, nodded their large heads, indicating they wouldn't harm the little fish.

"I believe in you." D said with a smile while patting the Dragonites on their heads.

The school of Wishiwashi could care for the algae, and their appetites are so small that they couldn't possibly finish off all the algae.

In such a situation, the excess algae would be eaten by those edible ordinary seabass.

Then the Dragonites could start enjoying this delicious fish meat.

*

After leaving Dragonite Island, D took a few Pokemon and rode the World Tree elevator all the way up.

During this time, Slowking maintained a wise gaze, seemingly deep in thought.

It wasn't until they reached that lake that Slowking snapped back to reality.

D ate a piece of Translation Nuts Cake, trying to use this method to communicate with Slowking.

"Slowking, can you hear what I'm saying now?"

Slowking looked at him and nodded: "I can hear you, hello, D."

Indeed, the Translation Nuts Cake worked! D felt a relief in his heart.

He continued to ask: "Can you speak human language or Pokemon language? Previously, we couldn't understand what you were saying, not me nor other Pokemon."

Slowking nodded slightly: "I can't speak human language, but I can speak Pokemon language. The language I was speaking was one I invented myself."

D: "..."

As expected of a Pokemon renowned for its high intelligence!

---

Just after evolving, I already created a language system that no one else can understand, belonging solely to myself.

D took a deep breath and continued: "You should already know what this place is, right?"

Slowking raised its head, glanced around, and its eyes finally rested above.

"If I'm not mistaken, this should be the inside of a giant tree, right?"

"That's right." D nodded and then introduced: "This is the World Tree, the place with the strongest natural aura. I plan to build an Explorer Team Guild here, specifically for Pokemon to live and reside."

Slowking listened quietly.

D continued: "I invited you over earlier because I wanted your help to look after the shop here."

After all, Slowking's intelligence is very high. Running a shop, making dishes should be quite easy for it, just like it is for humans.

However, upon hearing this, Slowking looked troubled: "I'm sorry, but I'm not sure how to run a shop because I'm just a Slowking."

D: "..."

It seems Slowking isn't interested in matters related to running a shop?

This swallowed all the words D originally prepared to say.

He hesitated, looking at the confused Slowking before him, and eventually gave up the idea of asking it to be the tavern owner.

"Alright, but that's no problem. You can settle down here at the World Tree peacefully."

Apparently, the Heroic Expedition Team will have to find an Oranguru interested in cooking and running a shop.

Slowking looked apologetic: "It's really bothering me!"

"It's no big deal." D began to comfort Slowking: "Don't overthink it, I'm not going to force you to do anything for me. As long as you can settle here, I'll be very happy."

For D, a collector, watching Pokemon live and play on his farm, in the World Tree, is the happiest thing in the world, even if they do nothing.

"Thank you, D." Slowking said: "Give me some time to think, there are many unresolved questions in my mind, they have been troubling me, I need to research methods to solve these puzzles."

Hearing this, D was very surprised.

"Puzzles troubling you? Can you tell me what it is?"

Slowking nodded gently.

It looked up to the sky and spoke with confusion: "Why is the sky blue? Why is this planet born? What's the meaning of our existence? Why can't past events change? Who am I? Where am I? What should I do?"

D: "..."

Slowking repeated again, troubled: "It's really bothering me!"

D immediately gave up the idea of helping Slowking solve the puzzles troubling it, because those things are considered only by the Wise.

And he wasn't a Wise One, he was just a common man.

Thinking of this, D bid farewell to Slowking, leaving it alone by the lake, contemplating life.

After a while, Treecko, Milotic, Tatsugiri, Tropius, Arboliva, and other Pokemon were attracted over.

They surrounded Slowking standing there motionless, contemplating life.

The Treeckos were puzzled: "Kefka?" (What is it doing?)

Tropius shook its head: "Wo..." (Don't know...)

All the other Pokemon looked confused too.

After a while, the Pokemon slowly dispersed, each going about their own business.

But Slowking raised its foot, walked next to a giant rock.

It raised its short leg, clumsily climbed onto it, and sat cross-legged, continuing its contemplation.

Who knows how much time passed.

Three Treeckos carrying berries passed by.

One Treecko looked at the berries wrapped in leaves, troubled:

"Kefka..." (It's really bad, the berries have been there for too long and are rotten. Such a waste. If there was a way to prevent them from rotting, it would be great...)

The three Treeckos didn't notice Slowking on top of the rock.

They leaned against the rock sitting down to rest.

The results from the Berry Garden came too fast, and with just a few stomach-hungry Pokemon, it was impossible to finish them.

Treecko initially planned to send more to Dragonite and others, but D seemed to worry they would be hungry and cleared a large Berry Garden on Dragonite Island too.

The berries couldn't be sent out, the extra berries stored in the tree hole, those stacked at the bottom had already gone bad.

This made Treecko very troubled.

Right then, a voice suddenly rose from behind them.

"If you're worried about berries going bad, dry the berries to make fruit preserves, store them in barrels to make berry wine or juice, make berry candy, then the berries can be stored longer and won't go bad."

Hearing this, the three Treeckos were shocked, quickly got up and turned back.

Then, they noticed Slowking sitting cross-legged on the rock.

The Treeckos were very surprised: "Kefka?!"

Could those really be true? But why did this originally silly Slowpoke know?

Slowking seemed to see through their thoughts and explained: "I just thought of those."

How, how amazing!

The three Treeckos widened their eyes in disbelief.

The Treecko most concerned with the berries quickly asked: "Kefka Kefka?" (How can we make berries into fruit preserves, make juice or wine, make berry candy?)

---

Slowking looked up at the sky.

There should have been a sun there.

But now, for some unknown reason, the sun was gone, and the sky had become quite strange, with some broken sky islands floating above.

"The sun, with adequate sunlight, can dry the berries into dried fruit," Slowking explained.

The Treeckos looked up with it, but there was no sun in this part of the sky.

To verify the feasibility of this method, the Treeckos went to find Arboliva.

Upon hearing their idea, Arboliva thought for a moment and then led the three Treeckos to a Sunflower radiating bright sunlight.

"Li." (This should work.)

So, the Treeckos placed the berries next to the Sunflower to start drying them.

*

After returning to the ranch, it was almost noon.

D hummed a little tune as he went to the kitchen to prepare lunch, but at that moment, he noticed a Cyclizar looking distressed.

Seeing Cyclizar in this state, D was very surprised.

"Cyclizar?" he called out.

Cyclizar turned to look at him.

"What's wrong? Are you feeling down?" D walked over to Cyclizar and reached out to touch its forehead, finding that Cyclizar wasn't sick.

"Gao..." Cyclizar lay on the ground looking listless.

Not far away, Koraidon noticed this scene, blinked, and understood.

But it didn't say anything or go over to stir up trouble, competing for attention like it used to.

"Strange, it's not sick?" D was a bit puzzled.

He felt Cyclizar's emotions again and found its mood very low and disheartened.

D furrowed his brow and then softly asked, "Can you tell me if you've encountered anything recently?"

"Gao..." Cyclizar shook its head, indicating no.

It just felt useless...

Scenes from the past flashed in Cyclizar's mind.

Back then, when D first came to the ranch, the day before it was sent to the ranch, Mayor Thomas specifically found it and said:

"Little Cyclizar, a new owner is about to come to that abandoned ranch, a very poor child who lost his parents and grandfather and is all alone in this world. I hope you can go and accompany him."

At that time, hearing these words, Cyclizar only felt that the human was so pitiful...

So, the kind-hearted Cyclizar immediately agreed, deciding to go to the ranch, to be there with that human, and become his most capable assistant.

But later, that formidable Koraidon arrived, and at first, Cyclizar didn't think much of it, aside from being a bit afraid of that frightening Pokemon.

Back then, Koraidon showed hostility towards it, seemingly not liking it being with D.

D worked hard to mediate their relationship, and gradually, that Pokemon named Koraidon was no longer so mean to it and seemed to start accepting it.

Later on, it even proactively taught Cyclizar new moves.

But what followed was D's increasing lack of need for its help.

That Koraidon was too capable.

It could fly, run, and swim.

Not only that, but working in the fields, digging and planting fruit trees, harvesting crops—Koraidon was proficient in all of it.

In comparison, it felt terribly clumsy.

It couldn't run as fast as Koraidon, couldn't fly, and couldn't swim.

Sometimes, Cyclizar was really envious of Koraidon, envious of how powerful and strong it was, how it could help D with so many things.

From the beginning wanting to train hard to become strong, like the powerful Koraidon, Cyclizar suddenly lost confidence in itself.

Since Koraidon was always by D's side, what was the point of doing these things? D didn't need its protection at all.

No meaning.

But it also really liked D, didn't want to leave D.

These two conflicting emotions tangled with each other, causing Cyclizar to feel extremely distressed.

Cyclizar suddenly fell ill.

This really worried D.

Cyclizar always lacked energy, not even training, nor answering D's questions, languishing all day by the fireplace, motionless.

Initially, D fed Cyclizar Life Sheep Milk for illness, but Cyclizar still showed no signs of improvement.

Then, D asked Calyrex for help, but Calyrex shook its head, indicating that it had no solution.

D was worried, and so were the Pokemon close to Cyclizar.

Alcremie, Glaceon, Ogerpon, Dragonite, and Pawmot all came to comfort it, with no effect.

Even Dragon Power couldn't sense Cyclizar's thoughts.

This Pokemon seemed to have suddenly shut down its inner self.

D took Cyclizar to the Pokemon Center in town for a checkup.

Soon, Miss Joy came out of the treatment room with the examination report.

Her expression was very serious, looking as if Cyclizar had some grave illness.

D's mood immediately sank.

Miss Joy frowned and said, "This Cyclizar is in poor mental state, and its body seems to be affected. Has it not had much appetite recently, not eating well?"

D nodded, the food he prepared for Cyclizar was left uneaten.

Miss Joy continued, "This is not due to physical reasons. If I'm not mistaken, Cyclizar has something on its mind, a knot that's trapping it."

A mental issue? D was slightly startled, trying to recall recent events—he didn't seem to have neglected Cyclizar?

The Pokemon on the ranch were increasing, and he spent time with them daily to take care of each one's emotions.

Miss Joy asked D, "Mr. D, has Cyclizar recently encountered something that makes it sad? I can sense that it likes you a lot but tries not to approach you."

"During the checkup, it kept looking at you outside. I think you're very important to it, Mr. D?"

D's mood instantly plunged to the bottom.

What exactly happened to Cyclizar?

With no results from the checkup, D took the examined Cyclizar back to the ranch.

At this moment, Pawmot's parents came to D, saying two roosters had gotten into a fight and fallen into the river.

D frowned slightly, turning to Cyclizar and said: "Cyclizar, wait here for a bit, I'll make something special for you later."

Cyclizar lay on the grass, half-closing its eyes, looking very unenergetic.

It watched D leave with Pawmot's parents to rescue the roosters that fell into the river, then closed its eyes.

But just then, a shadow suddenly appeared in front of it.

Cyclizar sensed a familiar presence.

It opened its eyes and saw Koraidon, backlit, looking down at it.

---

---

---

---

# 442: Chapter 439: Cyclizar and Koraidon

In the meadow, Koraidon lowered its head, looking at its past self.

It knew well why Cyclizar was feeling sad, because it had once gone through the same thing.

At that time, another Koraidon had said something to it, words that it hadn't forgotten even today.

Thinking of this, Koraidon opened its mouth: "Gaa hiss." (I have things I can do, and you also have things you can do.)

Cyclizar was slightly stunned, its gaze toward Koraidon filled with bewilderment.

"Gaa row?" (What do you mean?) Cyclizar couldn't help but ask.

Koraidon gazed at Cyclizar with its pair of golden eyes, its expression serious, questioning:

"Gaa hiss?" (Haven't you always wanted to be the most reliable Pokemon by D's side? Are you going to give up here now? In this state, can you still help D?)

Cyclizar was taken aback, it hadn't expected Koraidon to talk to it about these matters.

But when the topic was brought up, its emotions instantly became even lower.

Cyclizar drooped its head depressingly, its voice very small: "Gaa row..." (You can do all those things, even without me it's okay, D doesn't really need me...)

Cyclizar looked at the Koraidon in front of it, its form was so grand and strong, its height so tall, its strength so mighty.

It also wanted to become such a Pokemon.

But it was impossible because Cyclizar simply couldn't evolve, it couldn't undergo a transformation like Slowpoke evolving into Slowking.

Upon hearing Cyclizar's words, Koraidon couldn't help but sigh helplessly.

It shook its head, saying: "Gaa hiss." (In the future, you'll definitely be able to help D, becoming his most reliable assistant, just like me.)

Seeing Koraidon's certainty, Cyclizar was very surprised, its eyes filled with confusion and puzzlement.

"Gaa row?" (How do you know?)

However, Koraidon did not answer, simply gazing at Cyclizar, thinking to itself: that's because I am your future self.

There never was any Koraidon; from beginning to end, it was always that kind-hearted Cyclizar.

After a pause, Koraidon added: "Gaa hiss." (In the future, I will leave here, and at that time D will be left in your care.)

Since it and Cyclizar can only exist as one, let its past self accompany D!

When the time comes, it will prevent Cyclizar from going to that place, so Cyclizar won't fall into the Time Machine, nor have to watch the moon for so many years in that ancient space.

Upon hearing this, Cyclizar was stunned.

At this moment it couldn't dwell on feeling bad anymore, it hurriedly asked: "Gaa row?" (Leave? Where are you going?)

Koraidon didn't know either. If the event of the future doesn't happen, it's likely to be corrected by time and disappear.

"Gaa hiss." Koraidon said, until then, let it accompany D a bit longer!

Cyclizar was very heavy-hearted, was Koraidon leaving the meadow because of it?

But Cyclizar knew that Koraidon had come from a very distant time, because D had once told it tales of Koraidon.

Koraidon lived in a very ancient world, full of dangers and fierce, terrifying Pokemon, experiencing countless battles and training there before growing into a powerful Pokemon.

And now, Koraidon was here alone in its world, it had no mother and no friends here.

How pitiful...

If Koraidon leaves, where can it go?

Cyclizar felt both unbearable and guilty inside.

After a while, it looked at Koraidon, saying: "Gaa row!" (If you leave, D will be very sad, I could share my mom with you, please stay!)

Koraidon was speechless internally, what sharing mom, wasn't that its mom too...

Koraidon didn't directly answer but continued: "Gaa hiss!" (Let's make a promise!)

"Gaa row?"

"Gaa hiss." (Today's affair is a secret, don't tell D, next I'll keep teaching you my skills, one day you'll use them to protect D.)

This was the promise it made with its past self.

Cyclizar dumbly looked up at Koraidon before it, although it didn't quite understand, it could still sense that Koraidon seemed to have made some determination.

Koraidon promised: "Gaa hiss." (You will definitely use them in the future, if you remain like this, when I'm not here and D faces difficulties, you won't be able to assist him.)

These words touched Cyclizar's heartstrings.

It had always wanted to help D, to become his most reliable Pokemon.

Both Pokemon gazed into each other's eyes.

Koraidon's expression was so earnest, Cyclizar was dazed, could it really use those skills to help D in the future?

In response, Koraidon's answer was affirmative.

After a good while, Cyclizar slowly regained its composure.

It earnestly looked at Koraidon, this mighty Pokemon.

"Gaa row, gaa row!" (Don't go, I'll eat well, I won't do this again!)

This Pokemon thought it was because of its envy of Koraidon as D's most reliable assistant that Koraidon decided to leave the meadow.

---

"Gwah..."

Koraidon sighed helplessly in its heart.

The past me was so immature and annoying, I even refused to eat well because of such things, how foolish it was.

So thought a certain Pokemon that was once jealous of itself.

Thus, when D pulled two fighting roosters from the river, he saw in the distance that Koraidon was saying something to Cyclizar.

D's heart stirred, and at this moment, a sudden realization came to him.

Could it be that his Little Cyclizar had become like that because of Koraidon?

At the beginning, he would invite both Pokemon to help, acting as his assistants.

But later, as the Pokemon on the ranch increased, D exerted a lot of effort, and for convenience, most of the time, he rode Koraidon directly for travel, and inadvertently neglected Cyclizar.

Under such circumstances, Cyclizar might feel unneeded, useless, leading to low spirits and emotional knots.

Is it because of me?

D silently started to reflect and made a decision in his heart: better late than never, he must spend more time with Cyclizar in the future.

D called Flareon and asked it to use Heat Wave to dry the two fighting roosters.

Afterward, D handed the two limp roosters to Pawmot's parents, informing them that the roosters were fine.

However, to prevent these chickens from getting flu, D had prepared medicine for them in advance.

When everything was done, he returned to Koraidon and Cyclizar, ready to spend an entire day with Cyclizar, helping it unwind.

Thinking of this, D smiled as he approached the two Pokemon, asking, "What are you talking about?"

Cyclizar instinctively looked at Koraidon.

Koraidon transformed into its goofy riding form, made a "Gwah" sound, turned, and ran off, seemingly wanting to leave time for Cyclizar and D.

D noticed this scene and sensed an unusual vibe between the two Pokemon.

Watching Koraidon run off, D investigated, "What did Koraidon just say to you? It didn't bully you, right?"

Upon hearing "bully," Cyclizar quickly shook its head: "Gah-o!" Koraidon didn't bully it!

D had already guessed, seeing Cyclizar's reaction, that it seemed Koraidon's words had just opened Cyclizar's heart.

D smiled and asked, "So what were you guys whispering about just now?"

Cyclizar thought of the promise it made with Koraidon and immediately closed its mouth, acting as if they hadn't said anything.

"Let me guess, was Koraidon just comforting you?" D asked.

Cyclizar widened its eyes: "Gah-o?"

That expression seemed to ask how he knew.

D smiled slightly, "We've lived together for so long, how could I not know you guys?"

Cyclizar looked adorable, seemingly realizing it's true...

And at that moment, it heard D sigh.

Cyclizar immediately looked up.

D reached out, stroked its head, and apologized seriously, "I'm sorry, Cyclizar, it's my fault for not noticing your feelings."

"Gah-o..."

Hearing D apologize, Cyclizar immediately looked teary-eyed like a wronged child.

"Did you feel sad because you thought you weren't needed anymore, that you felt useless?" D asked.

Cyclizar tearfully nodded: "Gah-o!"

"No it's not." D shook his head, saying seriously, "I don't need you to specifically do anything for me, or help me out. Just living together with you guys makes me very happy."

"Gah-o..."

"You know, Cyclizar," D asked, "for a Human Trainer, the first Pokemon is always very special. You are my first Pokemon here."

He had long been accustomed to Cyclizar's presence.

Just seeing it chasing butterflies in the flowers, sleeping peacefully by the warm fireplace, or clumsily helping harvest crops, D felt very at ease.

If one day Cyclizar left and he couldn't see it on the ranch, D felt he would definitely be very sad.

Sometimes, Pokemon's emotions are not as nuanced and intricate as humans', so when expressing feelings to them, it's best to do so in a straightforward manner to avoid misunderstandings.

Therefore, D directly said to Cyclizar, "You are very important to me. If one day you ran away and didn't want to be my Pokemon, I would be very sad."

"Gah-o..."

Cyclizar listened tearfully.

"Do you understand now?" D asked.

"Gah-o!" Cyclizar nodded earnestly.

"Very good." D smiled, rubbing its head, and stood up, "Now let's go home! You haven't eaten well for several days, let's go, I'll make you something special to eat."

"Gah-o!"

Cyclizar quickly stood up and followed D back to the house.

The man and Pokemon went into the kitchen, and D, while opening the fridge to check the variety of ingredients inside, asked, "Is there anything you want to eat?"

Cyclizar: "Gah-o!"

Rotom Phone helped translate: "[Hot noodle soup!]"

"Hot noodle soup?" D was somewhat surprised. Wasn't that the dish he had to make when he first arrived in this world, due to limited ingredients?

---

D's thoughts inadvertently returned to that chilly spring morning two years ago, picturing himself and Cyclizar huddled in the dilapidated old house, drinking steaming hot noodle soup.

At that time, Cyclizar really disliked the taste of hot noodle soup, so D rarely made it for him again. Cyclizar mostly ate sandwiches, fried chicken patties, and roasted meats after that.

However, seeing Cyclizar wanting to give it another try, D cheerfully agreed: "Alright, I'll make you hot noodle soup again."

It's not like the past anymore; their lives are no longer tight, and the refrigerator is stuffed with a variety of ingredients. They can buy whatever they want to eat.

D spent quite a bit of effort to finally find a pack of noodles in the cupboard.

Adding clean water into the pot, waiting for the noodles to soften, and then adding scallions and lard, letting the noodles soak up the broth.

Recalling the scene of making hot noodle soup for the first time, Cyclizar had just arrived at the ranch, barely a few months old.

Now Cyclizar has grown into a large Pokemon.

A smile appeared on D's face as he felt happiness from meeting Cyclizar, while he added a drop of sesame oil into the pot and placed a prepared soft-boiled egg inside. With a poke from the chopsticks, the golden yolk slowly flowed out, merging with the noodle soup.

Finally, D turned off the heat and served the noodles into a clean porcelain bowl. Bright green scallions floated on the surface of the broth, making it look very appetizing just by looking.

Hot noodle soup is ready!

D placed the bowl of hot noodle soup in front of Cyclizar and habitually glanced at its effect.

However, in the next second, D suddenly noticed something incredible.

Because the effect of the hot noodle soup had changed compared to the familiar bowl he made in the past!

[Blessing Soup Noodles (A): A special dish infused with deep emotions, legend holds that those who drink it can achieve their wish.

Cooking Effect: Wish, it is said that eating this can fulfill a wish.

Review: Looking to the future while reminiscing about the past, hoping tomorrow will be better; wealthy life may be happy, but those past poor days might be more worthy of reflection.]

Hmm?

D stared blankly at the unusual bowl of hot noodle soup before him.

As if sensing D's oddness, Cyclizar looked over with puzzlement.

"Grr?"

D finally came to his senses and said to Cyclizar: "Eat up! After you finish, make a wish; maybe this bowl of noodles will help you fulfill it."

Cyclizar was a bit surprised: "Grr hiss?"

"It's true," D nodded: "You can give it a try."

Upon hearing this, Cyclizar immediately lowered his head, lying down by the food bowl and devouring the special hot noodle soup.

Hmm, the taste is much better than the first time he ate it, Cyclizar happily ate it, and when he finished the last sip of broth, he started to make a wish.

What wish should he make?

At that moment, Cyclizar thought about a lot of things, he thought about D, the folks at the ranch, and finally Koraidon.

Recalling the words Koraidon had said to him just now, Cyclizar had the answer in his heart.

He wished within himself—for Koraidon not to leave and to always accompany D.

Now, he no longer feels bad about not being as powerful as Koraidon.

Because D said that he and Koraidon are different, that he is important to D too.

Having made the wish, Cyclizar opened his eyes.

In that case, Koraidon probably won't leave anymore, right?

"What wish did you make?" D curiously looked towards Cyclizar.

Thinking of the promise with Koraidon, Cyclizar shook his head: "Grr!"

He must keep the secret!

"So mysterious, alright, I won't ask then. May your wish come true!" D stood up and started cleaning the pots and bowls.

Cyclizar stayed to the side.

During the time, D noticed the Glaceon, Ogerpon, Alcremie, Pawmot, and other Pokemon secretly watching from the living room.

He said to Cyclizar: "Glaceon and the others were very worried about you before, would you like to say something to them?"

Upon hearing this, Cyclizar looked back and indeed saw Glaceon and the others.

Recalling his own state over the past couple of days, Cyclizar felt somewhat embarrassed, but then remembered that everyone was concerned about him and summoned his courage to walk over to them.

D washed the dishes alone.

Sister Indeedee noticed the scene, approached to ask if he needed help.

D shook his head: "No need, you all should take a rest! I've got all the chores covered today."

The Indeedee Sibling work every day for everyone, cleaning, sorting chores, helping feed, all work laboriously.

D intended to let them take a break, he would handle today's chores himself.

The Indeedee Sibling felt D's intentions deeply, and they were very pleased inside.

Although it was their job, being cared for and understood by their owner makes them among the most fortunate among all Indeedee in the world.

Sister Indeedee happily ran to the room to rest, but Brother Indeedee stayed, looking out to see if D needed any help.

And D's thoughts turned to Koraidon.

Today, Koraidon's behavior was really unexpected.

Because it actually comforted Cyclizar!

Although he didn't know exactly what the two Pokemon talked about, D could feel that after speaking with Koraidon, Cyclizar's mood was not as bad as it was initially.

---

In that case, Koraidon not only has completely integrated into modern society, but has also undergone significant changes.

If Professor Sada found out about this, she would definitely be very surprised.

D could almost imagine Professor Sada's expression and her astonished tone when she hears about this.

"What?! Koraidon, a Paradox Pokemon with such a fierce nature, actually took the initiative to comfort other Pokemon? That's truly amazing!"

However, speak of the devil and the devil appears.

D just finished washing the dishes when he received a video call from the Paldea Giant Pit.

The two professors deep in Zone Zero had also kept up with the times, switching to the more convenient Rotom Phones.

As the call connected, Professor Sada's heroic face appeared on the screen.

She smiled and greeted D: "Long time no see, D!"

"Long time no see, Professor!" D replied with a smile: "Is the research over at the Giant Pit going well?"

"Everything's going well." Professor Sada nodded.

D looked behind Professor Sada but only saw Professor Turo, so he couldn't help but ask: "Where's Arven? Has Arven returned to the surface?"

Professor Sada nodded slightly: "Arven has gone to study at Paldea Academy. There are many peers there, and Arven will surely make new friends there. Even without our company, he won't feel lonely."

"Arven has already started attending school?"

D suddenly felt that time flew by. He still remembered when he first met Arven; Arven was a little boy who was always silent and spoke very little.

"Yes!" Professor Sada nodded with a smile: "But Arven is not very good at Pokemon battles. He is more interested in cooking than battling, perhaps influenced by you."

Saying this, Professor Sada looked at D, remembering how D loved cooking, and Arven had stayed there for a while when he was younger.

D: "..."

This has nothing to do with him, because even without him, Arven would have been interested in cooking.

"By the way, Professor, why did you contact me today?" After a bit of small talk, D began to ask.

Professor Sada shook her head and said: "It's nothing special, just checking if Koraidon and Flutter Mane are doing fine."

Speaking of this, D remembered how Koraidon had just comforted Cyclizar.

He told Professor Sada about what had just happened.

And as D anticipated, Professor Sada showed a surprised and unexpected expression:

"What...?" After the shock, Professor Sada looked thoughtful: "Interesting, Koraidon actually took the initiative to comfort other Pokemon. It seems it has fully integrated into the world we live in."

D nodded in agreement.

He recalled the descriptions and relationships of Koraidon and Cyclizar in the games.

Some say Koraidon is what Cyclizar looked like in the past, and Miraidon is what Cyclizar will look like in the future.

As for this theory, D had yet to see any evidence of it in the world.

He pondered for a moment and curiously asked: "By the way, Professor, have you noticed that Koraidon, Cyclizar, and Miraidon have similar appearances? Is there some connection between them?"

Hearing this, Professor Sada nodded thoughtfully: "It's possible. According to the research by me and Turo, it's very likely that Cyclizar, living in modern times, is the result of Pokemon like Koraidon evolving naturally."

"The appearance of Pokemon changes according to different environments. I think you know this, with the different regional forms of Pokemon."

"Based on my investigation and some literature, the era Koraidon lived in had a high oxygen level in the atmosphere, and the air was filled with primitive violent energy. Perhaps it's because of this that Koraidon, Flutter Mane, and Scream Tail Pokemon are so fierce, like untamed beasts."

"Of course, your Koraidon and Flutter Mane are not like that."

D nodded thoughtfully.

Filled with primitive violent energy?

This reminded him of a specialized term—[Primal Reversion].

Primal Reversion is a special phenomenon that only occurs with the Legendary Pokemon Groudon and Kyogre.

It refers to the process where they revert to their original forms after absorbing sufficient natural energy.

Professor Sada continued:

"And as time progressed, the concentration of oxygen in the atmosphere decreased and the violent energy in the air began to dissipate. Those Pokemon then started to change towards the appearance of modern Pokemon.

Moreover, the birth of Berries provided them with enough nutrition, allowing these Pokemon to survive without hunting each other.

Afterwards, except for some enemy Pokemon, the relationships between different Pokemon gradually became harmonious."

Upon hearing this, D understood.

A bold idea suddenly popped into his mind.

"Professor, is there a possibility that Cyclizar could undergo Primal Reversion to become Koraidon?"

Just like Groudon and Kyogre.

"Primal Reversion?" Professor Sada noticed this unique term: "A fitting description. I can only say that there's a certain possibility, but I'm not very certain about the specifics since I've never formally encountered the world Koraidon lived in."

```

---

Speaking of this, Professor Sada showed a look of longing. It would be wonderful if she could travel to that time and space and see the Pokemon living in the past herself.

This has been her dream all along.

But she couldn't do that, because she still had family here. She couldn't abandon them and leave.

There is a certain possibility, but it's really difficult to achieve, because currently Professor Sada knows nothing about the past world unless she can investigate it herself.

D heard what Professor Sada meant and regretfully abandoned the idea.

Then he thought of Miraidon, Iron Thorns, those Paradox Pokemon from the future.

"What about those Pokemon from the future?" D asked.

Recalling the descriptions of those Pokemon, could it be that someone has modified the Pokemon's bodies, allowing them to achieve mechanical ascension?

However, regarding the future, Professor Sada isn't very sure either.

At this moment, Professor Turo chimed in from behind.

"Future Pokemon are likely born in the era of technological explosion. According to my research, traces of artificial manufacturing can be found on the Pokemon Iron Valiant."

"It may have been created by a mad scientist from the future who likes Gardevoir and Gallade as a bionic Pokemon."

Listening to Professor Turo, is he establishing a connection with Iron Valiant?

D was surprised. Iron Valiant's personality is quite violent!

Seeming to understand what D meant, Professor Turo smiled and said:

"Using some special methods, if you're interested, D, you can always come to the Area Zero Research Institute, and we'll give you a detailed introduction then."

With Koraidon here, D can walk freely in the Paldea Giant Pit.

Moreover, someone from the Paldea League contacted them earlier.

Upon learning that D had entered the Paldea Giant Pit before, the person immediately declared that from now on, D could freely enter the Giant Pit without needing to apply for qualification from the Paldea League.

As for who that person was... of course, it was none other than the Paldea Region's Chief Champion, Geeta.

After Geeta and D met, Professor Sada and Professor Turo felt very surprised.

Therefore, they specifically made this call to tell D the news that he can freely enter and exit the Paldea Giant Pit from now on.

D: "..."

Geeta trusts him that much? Isn't she afraid he'll abduct all the Pokemon in the Paldea Giant Pit?

Alright, he indeed won't.

"Okay then, I'll personally thank the Chief later." D nodded.

"Alright, that's it for today. Next time if we get the chance, we can chat more about ancient Pokemon!" Professor Sada smiled and hung up the phone.

*

At the same time, Paldea League.

Geeta dialed Lono's phone using her Rotom Phone, preparing to probe her thoughts.

While Lono was sitting at home in front of her computer, concentrating on editing a video.

The sudden ringing of the Rotom Phone placed beside her startled her.

When Lono saw the name of the caller, she was startled once more.

It was the Chief!

The Chief calling now—is she going to inspect her work?

However, Lono quickly realized it's after hours.

Oh, no problem then.

The call connected, and Lono greeted Geeta cheerfully: "Hello, hello, good afternoon! Chief!"

Geeta smiled slightly: "Hello, Lono. Has your Gym challenge been going smoothly recently? Have you encountered any difficulties?"

"Smoothly!" Lono shook her head and said: "Difficulties? Nope, everything is fine, the popularity of the Levincia Gym is soaring up like Eelektross leaping over the dragon gate!"

"That's good." Geeta then talked about the main topic: "By the way, I heard from Grusha that you have a good relationship with D, is that true?"

"D?" Lono didn't expect the Chief to suddenly switch to this topic. She didn't react for a moment and didn't know how to answer.

Geeta: "Hmm? What's wrong?"

"Nothing, nothing!" Lono hurriedly waved her hand: "D and I are indeed friends!"

"Friends indeed..." Geeta's face showed a peculiar smile.

Lono: "?"

The Chief seems a bit off today.

Just as she was about to ask what happened, Geeta shifted the topic abruptly.

"By the way, Lono, you're almost nineteen this year, right?" Geeta asked.

Lono was a bit puzzled but still replied: "Yeah, I just recently celebrated my eighteenth birthday, heading towards nineteen."

"Time flies so fast! I remember when you first became the Gym Leader, you were only fourteen. Nineteen... less than four years younger than D. Have you ever met someone you like?" Geeta asked.

Lono looked completely dumbfounded, a big question mark appeared above her head, the Chief was indeed acting strange.

"Chief, it's still too early to talk about this!"

"Too early?" Geeta smiled and said: "That's true, it's indeed a bit early for you, but it's time for D. I plan to introduce some suitable candidates to him for matchmaking. What do you think?"

"What?" Lono was taken aback, her face immediately showing a surprised expression, she instinctively said: "D probably isn't that desperate yet, right?"

Geeta: "Just trying out, besides, there are indeed a few people in the Paldea League who are quite suitable for D, so I called today to ask for your opinions since you and D are friends."

---

Lono felt a bit flustered inside. She said so, but...

Watching the distracted Lono on the screen opposite her, Geeta had already gotten the answer she was probing for.

"Just as I thought, you do have feelings for him, right?"

Hearing this, Lono immediately widened her eyes, looking at Geeta in disbelief.

Geeta gently laughed: "As far as I know, you're not the type of Gym Leader who's ambitious or vigorously enhancing their skills."

"Grusha and Larry went once, and yet you've gone several times. You can't possibly have suddenly awakened a desire for intense training to become the strongest Trainer in the Paldea Region, right?"

Lono: "...Is it that obvious?"

Since things had been said to this extent, how could Lono not see Geeta's intentions today?

Geeta just smiled without saying a word.

Lono admitted helplessly: "Alright, alright... Maybe a little bit!"

"Since you like him, be a bit proactive, muster the courage to pursue him; otherwise, D, with his tight-lipped personality, would never know in his lifetime." Geeta encouraged.

But Lono seemed a bit hesitant.

"I always feel D and I are not quite a fit..."

That has been the reason she hasn't been proactive.

Geeta raised an eyebrow: "Hmm?"

Lono listed: "D and I, one is in the east, the other in the west. Compared to bustling big cities, D prefers the quiet countryside, and we're different in personality and lifestyle habits..."

Geeta nodded while listening.

After a while, she spoke: "Love is a two-way street; both need to walk towards each other. Also, that's not what you should be considering now. First, you should express your feelings to him, and those other things can be considered later."

Lono now fully reacted, looking at the Chief with a strange gaze, different from her usual self.

"Chief, why did you suddenly become concerned with these matters?" Lono asked.

Geeta smiled slightly: "All for the Paldea League. Alright, let's leave it here for today! Speak about it more once you've confirmed your feelings!"

Lono: "..."

For the Paldea League, what does that even mean?

Could her falling in love revitalize the Paldea League?

Lono was dumbstruck.

But on second thought, the Chief was indeed right.

She ought to express her feelings first; otherwise, thinking too far ahead and then facing rejection would indeed be awkward.

Thinking like this made Lono feel pretty apprehensive.

Anyway, she decided to visit D Ranch to see D first!

After the call ended, Geeta sat in her chair, lost in thought.

Rika, who had listened to the entire conversation, said speechlessly: "Chief, when did you start moonlighting as a matchmaker?"

Geeta chuckled softly: "No, I'm just trying to give them a hand."

But Rika easily saw through her.

Clearly, it's about keeping D in the Paldea Region...

Geeta didn't mind being seen through by Rika.

Instead, she started imagining the future.

"By the way, Rika."

"What?" Rika looked at her.

Geeta said: "Do you think, if D had a child, at what age would they break into Champion Level?"

Upon hearing this, Rika's face instantaneously showed an incredulous expression.

Chief, plotting to keep D in the Paldea Region is one thing, but you're even planning for his child!

"What kind of expression is that?" Geeta chatted casually: "I just believe his child would definitely be amazing."

Rika thought silently for a moment.

Koraidon, Calyrex, Dragonite, Spectrier, Glastrier, Gogoat...

A Trainer capable of capturing/raising these Pokemon is surely not lacking in skill.

If the child decides to become a Trainer, D would likely impart all his lifetime knowledge to him/her.

In that ranch filled with so many Pokemon, D might pick a starter Pokemon for the child from birth, allowing them to grow up together.

This way, their bond and level of teamwork would far exceed those Trainers who only receive their Pokemon at age ten.

At age three, when the child starts walking and talking, D might already be teaching them how to raise and train Pokemon.

Training up to age ten, that child could directly challenge Gyms, clear the Paldea League, and become the youngest Champion.

Geeta seemed to see the vision already, a glorious new star rising in their Paldea Region.

"Truly something to anticipate!" Geeta murmured gently.

*

Paldea League Year 200, late March.

That morning, D was steaming Grassy Seed dumplings for the Gogoat, when he suddenly received a call from Mayor Thomas.

"Mayor Thomas?"

On the other end, Mayor Thomas nodded: "Good morning, D! This year's Spring Horse Racing Festival will be starting in two days, do you want to bring your Horse-type Pokemon to participate?"

Spring Horse Racing?

Last year, Spectrier competed in the race and won the championship, so logically, it should be Glastrier's turn this year.

But Glastrier's interest in this race has diminished significantly.

Last time, D worried Spectrier's trophy might deform its head, so he had Calyrex negotiate to see if the trophy could be removed.

Eventually, Calyrex told Spectrier directly that the trophy wasn't a crown.

Spectrier immediately took off the trophy then, while nearby Glastrier overheard.

Glastrier, who initially yearned to win a crown and become the Horse King, instantly lost interest in the horse racing festival.

Thinking of all this, D hesitated slightly and replied: "I'm not really sure, maybe ask those two Horse-type Pokemon first; if they're willing, then I'll take them to participate."

"Alright." Mayor Thomas nodded, then hung up the phone.

---

---

---

---

# 443: Chapter 440: The Horse King Showdown and the Viral Giratina

D ended up not going to this year's horse racing festival.

Because Glastrier knew that wasn't a Crown, just an ordinary Gold trophy, completely unworthy of its noble bearing as the King of Horses.

While Glastrier was talking, Spectrier stood quietly to the side listening, just not saying a word the whole time.

Because the one who'd put that trophy on its head as a Crown was it.

Not only did it wear it, it wore it for nearly a whole year.

Awkward.

Spectrier emptied its mind, pretending it had never done such a thing in its life.

Seeing that neither horse wanted to take part in the horse racing festival, D turned and left the stable, planning to go tell Mayor Thomas about it.

After he left, Glastrier thought back on Spectrier's behavior and couldn't help but secretly snicker off to the side.

Spectrier couldn't see, but its hearing was extremely sharp, and it immediately picked up on Glastrier's sneaky laughter.

Spectrier instantly flew into a shame-fueled rage, ghostly light flickering all over its body as it launched an attack at Glastrier.

Double Kick!

Glastrier took a kick to the belly and immediately let out a pained whinny.

Seeing this stupid horse Spectrier dare raise a hoof against it, the hot-tempered and straightforward Glastrier couldn't hold back anymore and struck back with a kick of its own.

Spectrier was thoroughly enraged and directly formed a Shadow Ball, hurling it at Glastrier.

The next second, with a boom, the perfectly fine stable was flipped over by this horse just like that.

The Eevee playing out on the grass saw Spectrier and Glastrier going at it and immediately cried out in alarm.

Soon, Umbreon ran into the room, biting the hem of D's pants and tugging him outside.

"Bui!"

"What's going on?" D was a bit surprised; seeing how anxious Umbreon looked, he said to Mayor Thomas on the other end of the phone, "Something seems to have happened at the ranch, I've got to go check it out. Let's talk next time."

Mayor Thomas nodded kindly. "It's fine, go on!"

Hanging up, D hurriedly followed Eevee outside.

On that open stretch of grass, Spectrier and Glastrier were charging at each other, murderous intent in their eyes.

Slamming with their bodies, kicking with their hind legs...

And the stable they used to live in had already been turned completely into a pile of rubble.

D: !!!

"Glastrier, Spectrier!" he shouted, trying to stop them.

But the two furious horses didn't stop at all.

Seeing this, D directly pressed the button to summon Calyrex.

Right after, Calyrex's figure appeared in the ranch.

It too immediately noticed the two horses that were fighting.

Calyrex frowned, its expression stern.

A vast surge of Psychic power poured out from Its body, thrusting itself between the two horses and forcibly separating them.

"What art thou doing?" Calyrex's tone was filled with majesty.

At this, the already-calmed-down Spectrier was the first to complain about Glastrier.

"Heeiiih~ss!" (This guy Taunted me!)

Glastrier was in no way cowed. "Briiih~ss!" (It struck first!)

Spectrier glared at Glastrier. "Heeiiih~ss!" (You're the one who Taunted me first!)

Glastrier said directly, "Heeiiih~ss?" (Telling the truth counts as Taunt? You're just too dumb, wearing that trophy on your head like a Crown!)

Calyrex: "..."

D: "..."

Hearing this, Spectrier was instantly blown up with anger.

Two streams of purple smoke uncontrollably rose from its ear holes.

A powerful Ghost Type Energy surged wildly over its body, finally taking shape as a pitch-black demonic shadow that came crashing down on Glastrier like a dark tide.

Glastrier sensed the danger and, unwilling to back down, formed a large icicle, sending it hurtling toward Spectrier.

Seeing this, Calyrex's face was full of speechless exasperation.

Icy blue light flashed in Its eyes, and in the next second, a surge of mighty Psychic power erupted from Its body, directly erasing the Moves of Spectrier and Glastrier out of thin air.

"Enough, both of you, stop!"

Looking at Its two beloved steeds, Calyrex only felt a pounding headache.

"Glastrier, thou art the one who erred first. Thou shouldst not have mocked Spectrier."

Glastrier snorted in displeasure; clearly Spectrier was just too stupid, and it had merely spoken the truth.

Hearing Calyrex's words, D immediately understood what had happened.

Glastrier had laughed at Spectrier for wearing the trophy as a Crown, Spectrier got embarrassed and angry, and then the two of them started fighting.

D was utterly speechless.

No denying it, Glastrier's emotional intelligence was really low...

Seeing Glastrier like this, Calyrex didn't know what to do for the moment.

This whole thing was Glastrier's fault, but Glastrier didn't look the least bit convinced.

Meanwhile, the wronged Spectrier stood silently off to the side, keeping its mouth shut and saying nothing.

Given Calyrex's temperament, there was no way It could bring Itself to slap Glastrier's horse face.

Both palm and back of the hand are flesh; both of them were Its beloved steeds. Calyrex was in a real bind and finally could only cast a pleading look at D.

D instantly understood what Calyrex meant.

He thought it over briefly, then stepped forward and said to the two horses, "In the end, this all came from the issue of the Crown, right?"

Spectrier and Glastrier turned their gazes on him.

D went on, "In that case, why don't you settle it with a duel? Whichever Pokemon wins gets to become the King of Horses. When the time comes, I'll have a King of Horses Crown made for it—a real Golden Crown. Then Calyrex and I will help it get crowned as Horse King. How does that sound to you?"

---

Sure enough, upon hearing the words "real Golden Crown," both Spectrier and Glastrier's attention was instantly drawn.

Spectrier snorted.

If it could obtain that crown, Glastrier would no longer have the right to mock it; by then, it would truly become the King of Horses!

Glastrier also widened its eyes, very excited.

This crown, it must get!

Calyrex looked at D with a helpless expression.

But then, what about the Horse-type Pokemon that loses?

D gestured for Calyrex to calm down, then continued:

"But keep in mind, in the future, every year, the horse that doesn't get the crown will have the right to challenge the King of Horses. After all, the King of Horses should be the most powerful Horse-type Pokemon in the world, not only the fastest but also the strongest. If it isn't strong enough, it doesn't deserve to hold the position of King of Horses."

Calyrex could not help but nod.

That's right! This way, there's no need to worry about any Horse-type Pokemon being disappointed. Even if it loses this year's match, it can still challenge the crown next year.

What a good idea! Calyrex looked at D gratefully.

After hearing these words, both Spectrier and Glastrier fell into contemplation.

What D said was right, the King of Horses should be the fastest and most powerful Horse-type Pokemon!

Only such a Horse-type Pokemon deserves to be called the King of Horses.

"Very well." D nodded and continued, "Next, you should rest a bit, and when you're in your best condition, I'll start the King of Horses competition for you."

"But remember, at that time, Calyrex will not ride with you in the battle; you can only rely on your own strength to fight for the crown."

"Neigh~ hiss!"

"Ice~ hiss!"

Both Glastrier and Spectrier were full of fighting spirit.

"Then you both should go rest now!" D said.

The two horses, obsessed with the crown, each found a place to rest and prepare.

It didn't matter that the stable was gone, when they were in the Crown Tundra, they used to sleep directly out in the open anyway.

Watching their backs, D and Calyrex were momentarily at a loss for words.

Well, he should go find Keri to rebuild the stable.

Such troublesome horses.

But Keri was still over at the World Tree building the Explorer Team Guild; she probably didn't have time to come rebuild the stable here.

In that case, let the two horses deal with some hardship first, sleeping directly on the grass, and see if they dare to destroy the ranch facilities again in the future.

"Where do you plan to find the crown?" Calyrex asked, looking a bit worried.

"That's simple." A Pokemon's image floated into D's mind: "Just find Gholdengo and ask for some gold coins, then take them to the town and give them to the craftsmen to help forge a Golden Crown."

Gholdengo had given him many coins before, all of which D had collected, likely enough to make a Golden Crown.

This way, he wouldn't have to borrow coins from Gholdengo again.

That afternoon, D took a big bag of coins to Porto Marinada Town.

He found a gold goods specialty shop, handed over the coins, and asked them to help make a crown.

"No problem!" The master craftsman in the shop confidently assured him: "My craftsmanship is renowned throughout Porto Marinada Town. I only need a day to create the item you want. Come back tomorrow at this time!"

"Okay." D nodded, indicating that a Pokemon would pick it up tomorrow, so the crown should be handed directly to it by then.

"No problem!" The master craftsman agreed.

After returning to the ranch, D started cleaning up the ruins of the stable destroyed by Spectrier and Glastrier.

Wood pieces and planks were everywhere; throwing them away felt wasteful. D planned to keep them and use them for burning in the fireplace for warmth later when it got cold.

The Pokemons were all playing around on the ranch.

After that day's incident, Koraidon and Cyclizar's relationship had improved a lot.

D often saw the two of them together, with Cyclizar consulting Koraidon and Koraidon teaching Cyclizar the moves it mastered.

Seeing this harmonious scene, D was very pleased.

Thinking back, the relationship between the two Pokemons was quite terrible at the beginning! Koraidon was full of hostility toward Cyclizar, and Cyclizar was also afraid of Koraidon.

No one expected that they would become good friends now.

It really confirmed the lyric "At first sight, I didn't like you, who knew our relationship would later become so close."

A good thing indeed!

D waved and spoke to the two Pokemons from afar: "Keep up the good work, I'm off to check on the World Tree!"

Who knows what Slowking is thinking about its life right now.

"Ah gah hiss!"

"Gah ooo!"

Koraidon and Cyclizar replied cheerfully.

D smiled slightly, then took his Rotom Phone and Translation Nuts Cake and headed for the World Tree in the Reversed World.

During this time, D glanced at his Rotom Phone and saw that Giratina was complaining in the Lagendary Beast chat group.

[World's Greatest Giratina: So annoying, the two guys next door are stirring up trouble again. They better not interfere with my Reversed World, or I'll end them both!]

---

D: "..."

He glanced at the message and found it was sent five minutes ago.

Calyrex had already replied below.

[Farmer: Stay calm, and you'll feel much better.]

[The Most Powerful Giratina in the World: Hmph, you don't know those two annoying guys! They just can't stand each other. Every time they meet, they start fighting, and their battles always mess up my Reversed World. Those two outdated geezers, their minds are always stuck in the past.]

D: "..."

The two outdated geezers, does that refer to Time God Dialga and God of Space Palkia?

The current timeline seems to have reached the Hoenn Region, with only a year left until those major events happen in the Sinnoh Region.

If he remembers correctly, by then, the space-time occupied by Dialga and Palkia will collide, and the two Pokemon will fight fiercely in a place called Alamos Town.

And all of this is the precursor to the Creator God Arceus waking up from its slumber.

Who'd have thought that signs of it were starting to appear already?

But speaking of which, it's rather funny that Giratina referred to Dialga and Palkia as two outdated geezers.

Heh...

D couldn't hold back a laugh, and he opened the Pokemon Forum with great interest, clicking on Giratina's personal homepage.

Only to see its follower count has impressively reached seven digits.

[The Most Powerful Giratina in the World (Annual VIP 10 — Followers: 5,550,384)]

Wow! Giratina's followers have surpassed 5.5 million!

D was quite shocked, he then clicked on Calyrex's personal homepage and found Calyrex only had five million followers.

Compared to Giratina, who made a fortune by taking photos every day, Calyrex was much more laid back about the Pokemon Forum.

Aside from occasionally replying to private messages and queries from netizens, it never made any posts.

But what's the deal with the annual VIP 10 on Giratina's homepage?

Where did it get the money to buy membership?

D was quite surprised, and on his way to the World Tree, he made a stop at Giratina's Flower Sky Island to ask about it.

Giratina proudly replied: "Hmph, all of that is donated by my fans! I have a lot of money now, Rotom, show D my savings!"

"Received, Rotom!" Rotom quickly displayed Giratina's personal account balance.

D leaned in to count: "Ones, tens, hundreds, thousands, ten thousands, hundred thousands, millions... 1.6 million?"

D's eyes widened in disbelief; is being an influencer this lucrative?!

Giratina was extremely proud: "Sometimes humans can be quite considerate."

At that moment, D noticed that there were many new items on the Sky Island.

He checked closer and found that they were all specially made food for Electric Type Pokemon.

"What are these?!" D asked in surprise.

Before Giratina could answer, Phone Rotom chimed in with a happy electronic sound:

"These are treats Giratina bought for me, Rotom!"

Giratina gave D an oblique glance and said with grandeur: "I have money now. Is there anything you want? I can buy it for you online!"

D: "..."

It's evident that Giratina has made rapid progress recently, even learning to use shopping apps.

It's no wonder it called Dialga and Palkia outdated geezers.

"Never mind, keep your money for yourself. I don't want anything," D said.

However, D was quite interested in Giratina's fan base.

He wanted to find out where all those Giratina fans came from.

So, D got Rotom to check the locations of Giratina's fans from the back end.

"Received, Rotom!"

Phone Rotom immediately conducted a background check on Giratina's personal homepage.

Soon, a detailed interface popped up on the screen.

[Fan Sources — Sinnoh Region: 58%, Kanto Region: 8%, Johto Region: 2%, Hoenn Region: 6%, Kalos Region: 4%, Unova Region: 7%, Galar Region: 3%, Alola Region: 2%, Paldea Region: 8%, Others: 2%]

D instantly widened his eyes.

Wow, Giratina has so many fans in Sinnoh?!

Wait! D suddenly noticed something: Could it be that the people in the Sinnoh Region revere Giratina as a great god?

Thinking of this, D immediately searched online.

Soon, he found several news articles about people in Sinnoh building Giratina statues in city parks.

Moreover, the Sinnoh League had also allocated a significant amount of money to support the construction of these statues.

Seeing this, D was at a loss for words for a moment.

Because he suddenly recalled something.

In the movie "Arceus and the Jewel of Life," when Arceus awakens, it plans to destroy humanity out of anger.

By then, Giratina, along with Dialga and Palkia, will unite to stop Arceus.

If humans were to witness such a thing, wouldn't they think that Giratina is fighting against the god to protect humanity?

D couldn't even dare imagine such a scenario.

It's really bizarre!

The commotion from the Legendary Beast battle would undoubtedly attract some humans to live stream it, and Giratina's fans would watch its heroic figure in action during the stream.

Thinking about this, D couldn't help but visualize a scene—

The angry god punishing humanity, countless meteors falling from the sky, brave Giratina rushing to the battlefield to protect humanity and the planet, but ultimately falling short.

---

The humans in the underground shelters cheered for Giratina, placing all their hopes on it.

Finally, all humanity transformed into light and fought alongside Giratina.

D: "..."

That was truly a wonderful misunderstanding.

At this moment, Giratina noticed D's expression and curiously asked: "What are you thinking about?"

D snapped back to reality, quickly shook his head, and said: "It's nothing, nothing. You go on playing, I have something to do over by the World Tree."

"Go on, go on!"

Giratina replied carelessly, then focused on surfing the internet.

D turned to look back.
