He saw Giratina lying on Flower Sky Island, basking in the warm sunshine, smiling while talking to Phone Rotom.

In that moment, a thought suddenly emerged in D's mind.

If that's the case, does it mean Giratina will never feel lonely again?

At first, D was a bit worried that Giratina and Phone Rotom wouldn't get along.

But now, it appeared they had become the closest companions.

Rotom diligently taught Giratina how to surf the internet, and Giratina would buy food online for Rotom.

Undoubtedly, Giratina viewed Rotom as a little follower.

But D believed that if Rotom were in danger, Giratina would certainly do its best to protect it.

For friends and followers, the often irritable Giratina was indeed a reliable and trustworthy elder brother.

D smiled slightly, then withdrew his gaze from Giratina, turning toward the World Tree.

Riding the World Tree elevator, he arrived at the top floor unimpeded.

After more than half a month, several new buildings had sprung up on what was once a bare grassland.

The first to be completed was the warehouse used by Pokémon to store food.

Upon seeing D arrive, Keri stepped forward to introduce these places to D.

"So far, the café, warehouse, Pokémon dormitory, and restaurant have all been completed. The remaining facilities like the guild headquarters, item shop, Pokémon hospital, and training room are under construction, expected to be finished around late April or early May."

D nodded, pushing open the warehouse door, discovering it was already filled to the brim with berries.

"These were brought over by Treecko and Arboliva. They pick berries from the orchard every day and store them here," Keri explained.

Just then, D noticed a pile of dried berries and berry juice in a corner of the warehouse.

He was momentarily stunned and then asked in surprise: "What's that?"

Keri shook her head: "I didn't make those. Apparently, the Pokémon made them themselves, but I don't know where they learned how to make dried berries."

"Huh?" This news left D quite surprised.

Did the Pokémon come up with this method using their own intelligence?

Turning berries into dried berries certainly extends their storage life and makes them easy to carry, suitable for eating during travel or adventures.

Interesting, D decided to go find those Pokémon to see.

Keri continued with the construction work, while D turned around, preparing to find Arboliva to inquire about the situation.

He ate a few pieces of Translation Nuts Cake, and eventually found Arboliva in a clearing in the forest.

Not just Arboliva; Treecko, Milotic, Tropius, Tatsugiri, and other Pokémon had all gathered there.

Even several Dragonites had run over!

What surprised D even more was that these Pokémon were actually lining up.

At the end of the line, Slowking sat on a large rock, looking thoughtful, like a philosopher contemplating life.

At the front of the line was Milotic.

It looked at Slowking, inquiring: "Muu? (When I use Water Gun to water the berry trees, I often need to use it dozens of times to finish watering all the trees, but it feels so tiring and my energy can't support using Water Gun anymore. Besides this watering method, is there a better way?)"

Slowking on the stone showed a thoughtful expression, and after a while, it replied: "Why not transfer your Water Type energy to the sky and turn it into rain to fall down?"

In this way, all the berry trees could be watered at once.

Upon hearing this, Milotic's eyes brightened.

It immediately began to try, and after a few failures, Milotic finally found the knack and performed Rain Dance.

Soon, a rain cloud appeared above the forest, and drizzling raindrops started falling.

Some Pokémon below were drenched, hurriedly seeking shelter from the rain.

However, Tatsugiri, Slowking, and Milotic, being water-type Pokémon, loved environments full of water.

So, instead of avoiding the rain, they happily danced in it.

Next, three Tatsugiri jumped to the front of the big rock.

"Mii! Mii? (How can we properly manage a sushi shop?)"

Slowking began to think deeply again.

After a long time, it slowly opened its mouth: "Treat your customers sincerely. When they feel your sincerity, they will visit your sushi shop frequently."

Tatsugiri excitedly bounced a few times, indicating they remembered!

D was quite amazed watching this scene.

Goodness, has Slowking become the Great Sage?!

At this moment, Arboliva noticed D arriving.

---

It stepped forward happily, greeting D.

"What's happening over here?" D asked.

Arboliva glanced at the line-up of Pokemon consulting Slowking and softly replied:

"Olé~" (They're asking Slowking questions~)

Through Arboliva's narrative, D gradually understood what had transpired on the World Tree during his absence.

A week ago, three Treecko, overwhelmed by a surplus of Berries, passed through this place.

They sat under a large rock, expressing their frustration over the abundance of Berries they couldn't finish, which were bound to rot, such a waste.

At that time, Slowking was perched on the rock pondering philosophy.

Upon hearing the Treecko's woes, Slowking came up with a solution for them.

The idea was to use sunlight to craft Berries into dried fruits, thus allowing long-term storage.

So, the Treecko attempted to make dried fruits.

They picked a large leaf, then used Razor Leaf to slice the Berries into thin pieces.

Finally, they placed these Berry slices on the leaf and carried them next to the Sunflower to dry.

Soon, under the sunlight provided by the Sunflower, the dried fruits were completed.

Witnessing this, the three Treecko were astonished.

They hadn't expected the seemingly clueless Slowking to be so clever.

From that day forward, Slowking became a sage of the World Tree, and other Pokemon would come to consult Slowking with their problems.

No matter the challenge, Slowking would help solve it.

After hearing this, D was incredibly surprised.

The information recorded in Slowking's Pokedex was actually true!

If he recalled correctly, Slowking is dubbed the Sage of the Sea in the Pokedex.

They are very smart, and in some areas, there's even a saying that if you're lost, just ask Slowking.

Slowking would also help guide lost people to the right direction.

Not only that, if anyone, or a Pokemon encountered a difficult problem and consulted Slowking, it would help solve it too.

Apparently, the description in the Pokedex about Slowking is quite fitting!

Initially, D had worried that Slowking might seem out of place on the World Tree.

But now it seems it has integrated in another way.

Though not cut out to be the Pokemon Tavern owner, being a Great Sage isn't bad either.

In the future, if Pokemon living here encounter any issues, they can come to consult with it.

Especially since once the Explorer Team Guild is completed, the number of Pokemon on the World Tree will increase, leading undoubtedly to a swell of challenges.

With Slowking, this high IQ Pokemon around, perhaps those challenges can be resolved just like the Pokemon coming today to consult Slowking.

*

Early the next morning, a Dragonite set off from D Ranch, heading to Porto Marinada Town, and retrieved the Golden Crown crafted for D from the Gold Shop Owner.

Under the startled gaze of the shopmaster, Dragonite flew back to the ranch with the Golden Crown.

D Ranch, 9:00am.

By the Gogoat pen, on the adjacent battle arena, Glastrier and Spectrier stood steadfastly at opposite ends, gazing firmly at each other.

With the Golden Crown in hand, D stood beside Calyrex, explaining today's Horse King competition rules to the two horse-type Pokemon.

"Today's rules for the Horse King competition follow a 1v1 format, Glastrier vs Spectrier. You two Pokemon will battle, and whoever wins will earn the right to wear this crown that symbolizes the honor of the King of Horses!"

"Nay~ Hiss!"

"Ice~ Hiss!"

Upon hearing this, the two horse Pokemon neighed simultaneously.

Glastrier anxiously scraped the ground with its foreleg, eager to start the competition!

Spectrier maintained its serene composure.

Though its vision is impaired, its other senses, like hearing and smell, are exceptionally keen.

These senses help it accurately explore its surroundings, correctly assessing the terrain and its opponent's position.

Thus, despite its rough opponent Glastrier, Spectrier remains unfazed.

D noticed the starkly different pre-match states of the two horse-type Pokemon.

To prevent the defeated Pokemon from feeling dejected, he continued:

"Winning this battle doesn't mean the victor gets to remain the King of Horses indefinitely. The Pokemon that loses can challenge the winner tomorrow. If you can win, you can take the crown."

"And the victorious Pokemon must stay vigilant to guard the crown. Continuous improvement and winning each Horse King competition will make you the true King of Horses!"

With the rules explained, D stepped aside.

Calyrex floated to the center of the battle arena, declaring to his two beloved horses:

"The battle begins!"

An Indeedee blew the whistle.

The next second, the two horses on the battlefield began to move.

Glastrier pounded the ground galloping forward, a frigid aura emanating from its hooves, freezing the surrounding earth.

With an astonishing momentum, it charged towards Spectrier.

Charge!

Spectrier remained stationary, its purple haze quietly drifting.

---

Glastrier's domineering and fierce body suddenly collided forward.

However, in the next moment, Spectrier's form seemed to blur slightly, allowing Glastrier to pass right through it.

Glastrier:!!!

Seeing Glastrier's actions, Spectrier let out a neigh that sounded like a mocking laugh.

Using sound to locate Glastrier's position, Spectrier suddenly raised its hind legs and launched a Double Kick at Glastrier.

Kicked hard, Glastrier felt as if its very soul was being shaken loose, widening its eyes and letting out a furious neigh.

A beam of cold light burst forth from its hooves.

But Spectrier seemed to have anticipated this, stretching its legs, surrounded by flashing blue light, galloping across the battlefield.

Using Agility, Spectrier's speed increased dramatically.

Glastrier attempted to give chase, trying to use its powerful body to bring down its opponent, but Spectrier was simply too fast; it couldn't keep up at all.

The more it chased, the more agitated Glastrier became.

Rage transformed into power as Glastrier lifted its front legs high and stomped heavily onto the ground.

In the next second, the earth began to tremble, sending a shockwave through the ground, aiming at Spectrier, whose speed had slowed down due to the impact.

Hit by the blast, Spectrier let out a muffled whimper, watching as Glastrier charged forward, releasing a peculiar light from its body.

Confuse Ray hit successfully, bewildering Glastrier, causing it to start running in the wrong direction.

Affected by the Confuse Ray, it thought Spectrier was in the opposite direction!

Spectrier gazed towards the distance, starting to plot a strategy in its mind.

Then, a pitch-black Shadow Ball was launched towards the confused Glastrier!

With the sound of a boom, Glastrier fell to the ground, losing its ability to battle.

Thus, victory was decided.

D looked with interest at Spectrier in the distance.

The last move, did Spectrier use Nasty Plot to enhance the power of its Shadow Ball?

Nasty Plot: a Dark Type move, after its use, the Pokemon plots a strategy, activating its brain, greatly boosting its Special Attack.

What a clever Spectrier!

In contrast, Glastrier seemed a bit less intelligent.

D glanced at the fallen Glastrier.

That guy attempted to use a Normal-type Tackle to attack the Ghost-type Spectrier.

Glastrier and Spectrier, one simple-minded and brutish, the other clever and cunning, Calyrex's two favorite steeds are indeed interesting.

With the battle ended, Calyrex used Heal Pulse to aid Glastrier's recovery from its injuries.

Soon, Glastrier awoke.

"It seems the outcome of this battle is clear." Calyrex floated forward, bringing the Crown to Spectrier.

Looking at the real Golden Crown before it, not some useless trophy, Spectrier's heart felt immensely excited.

Moreover, this was personally bestowed by Calyrex!

Images spanning thousands of years appeared in Spectrier's mind.

It remembered when, at the Crown Shrine, Calyrex crowned Zacian and Zamazenta like this!

Then, the King of Swords and King of the Shield were born.

And now, it's about to become the one and only King of Horses in the world!

Such an honor, how could it not be thrilled?

Spectrier excitedly snorted.

Calyrex spoke with a gentle tone: "My beloved steed, accept this Golden Crown! From now on, you shall be the King of Horses."

Under the control of Psychic, the Crown slowly floated to the top of Spectrier's head.

Feeling the weight of the object atop its head, Spectrier let out an exhilarating neigh towards the sky.

"Neigh~!"

In the distance, Glastrier snorted two plumes of white breath.

Sneaky fellow! To use such sly, despicable means!

It was not content!

At this moment, Calyrex turned to look at it.

"My beloved steed, do not be angry. On this day next year, you may challenge Spectrier, and should you win, I shall crown you as well."

Hearing this, Glastrier glared at Spectrier, thinking: Just wait, I will snatch that Crown away!

It should be the true King of Horses, the world's strongest Horse-type Pokemon!

Spectrier remained indifferent, ignoring Glastrier, for it has now become the true King of Horses, mere ordinary steeds don't even qualify to anger it.

Spectrier, adorned with the Crown, went off to survey its "territory."

Meanwhile, Glastrier secretly gathered its strength, preparing for the upcoming Horse King championship next year.

Watching the peaceful rest of the two Horse-type Pokemon, D and Calyrex both breathed a sigh of relief.

Phew... It's finally over.

Such healthy competition might even enhance the strength of those two Horse-type Pokemon, thereby indirectly boosting Calyrex's overall power.

After all, Calyrex can combine with them, and they even possess fusion moves, Astral Barrage and Glacial Lance.

These moves not only sound impressive but are also powerful beyond measure.

With Calyrex's current power, D feels that besides Sinnoh Region's Hydreigon, no Dragon-type Pokemon can withstand one of its Glacial Lances.

Not even that Black Rayquaza could endure.

Given that Flying + Dragon Type Rayquaza is four times weak to Ice Type, under such circumstances, Calyrex's Glacial Lance is nothing less than a true dragon-slaying lance.

It's a pity Calyrex hasn't had the chance to use this move; otherwise, he'd really like to see it.

Oh no, maybe, after all, there's some chance.

D suddenly remembered Hoopa's movie, the plot about Unbound Hoopa summoning and controlling Legendary Pokemon from its rings to brawl.

If he remembers correctly, Giratina will also be summoned over there, possibly allowing him to join the excitement and witness the Legendary Pokemon showdown.

Perhaps even film a documentary.

Because D really wants to know how long it takes Groudon to walk back from Kalos to Hoenn.

---

---

---

---

# 444: Chapter 441: Guiding Lono, The Worm in the Apple

In League Year 200, April 1st, as the temperature rose, the Paldea Region officially welcomed the sweltering summer.

The temperature outside was already a bit hot during the day, so while it was still cool in the early morning, D finished milking the Gogoat and then fed the other Pokemon on the ranch.

Once everything was done, D planned to go down to the wine cellar and check on the progress of those fruit wines.

But before he could enter the house, he received a call.

D took out his Rotom Phone and saw that it was actually Lono calling.

He raised an eyebrow, stopped in his tracks, and pressed accept.

"Hello?"

"Haro-haro, D, what are you doing right now?" Lono's energetic voice came from the other end of the line.

"I just finished the work on the ranch and was about to rest for a bit." D answered as he loosened his grip on the Rotom Phone, planning to head to the faucet in front of the house to wash his hands.

The Rotom Phone automatically switched into flight mode and Levitate beside D's ear.

"What about you? Don't you have work today?" D asked. At this time of year Gym Leaders usually weren't on vacation yet; under normal circumstances they would stay in their Gyms to receive challenges from Trainers.

If it was Lono… she should be over in Levincia City livestreaming her Gym battles right now, right?

There was a silly little chuckle from the other end, followed by, "Guess!"

At the ranch entrance, Lono put a finger to her lips in a shushing gesture toward Koraidon and Cyclizar, who had spotted her from not far away.

Koraidon, who'd been about to let out a growl, immediately shut its mouth.

"Hm?" D was a bit surprised. "Are you livestreaming outside right now?"

"Wrong!"

D: "…"

After washing his hands, D stood up. Thinking about Lono's personality, a sudden idea struck him, and he asked:

"Don't tell me you're already on your way to the ranch?"

"Hehe, bingo!" Lono giggled. "Turn around and look!"

D turned around and saw Lono standing on the patch of grass not far away, waving vigorously in his direction.

And Koraidon and Cyclizar were standing a short distance behind her, watching this way together with her.

Seeing this scene, D could only give a helpless smile.

This kind of stunt really did fit Lono's personality.

He hung up the call. The weather outside was getting hotter by the minute, so after thinking for a moment, D invited Lono to sit in the living room.

"Then I'll intrude~!" Lono followed along with a bright grin.

"Are you here to challenge the Trial again today?" D asked.

Lono nodded repeatedly. "Yup! After last time's challenge, I've already figured out a way to deal with Pawmot's Hammer!"

In reality, she just couldn't think of any other excuse to come over to the ranch.

"I see…" D's interest was piqued at once, though something still felt a bit off in his heart.

Because as far as he knew, Lono was not the type to grit her teeth and work hard to improve her strength.

Back in the games, she focused more on show effect than on winning Gym battles.

Whatever, overthinking it wouldn't help. Maybe Lono had just suddenly decided to put in some effort?

If that was the case, then he had to give her a proper hand.

The two of them walked into the house, while in the ranch not far away, Koraidon and Cyclizar were squatting on the grass, watching the backs of the two leaving.

Seeing Koraidon's expression, Cyclizar glanced at it, then at Lono, and asked in confusion, "Gya-ow?" (Do you know that human?)

It kept feeling like Koraidon seemed really familiar with that human.

Though it itself also had a pretty good impression of that human; last time when it was feeling down, she'd gone out of her way to encourage it.

Hearing Cyclizar's question, Koraidon nodded. "A-ga-ss." (You'll know her too.)

It thought to itself that their little master would one day hatch from Lono's egg.

In the future their little master would ride on their backs and play together with them!

Cyclizar didn't understand; it was thoroughly confused by Koraidon's words.

"Gya-ow…"

In the living room, the younger Indeedee brought Lono a glass of refreshing iced tea.

"Thank you! You worked hard!" Lono thanked the younger Indeedee, then took a sip. Her eyes immediately lit up. "Mmm, so tasty!"

Hearing the guest praise the tea she'd brewed, the younger Indeedee was delighted.

Hugging the tray, she showed a small smile. "Ai~ii!"

The younger Indeedee turned and left, and before long, she brought over a few small snacks she'd made herself to treat the guest.

"Ah! Thanks for the trouble!" Lono hurriedly thanked the younger Indeedee.

"Ai~ii~" The younger Indeedee indicated that it was no trouble at all.

D watched this scene with a smile on his face. After the younger Indeedee turned and left, he finally asked:

"Has the streaming been going smoothly lately?"

Lono nodded again and again. "Yeah! I tweaked my streaming style a bit, and the popularity of Levincia Gym has been climbing higher and higher!"

D actually followed Lono's personal page on Pichu TV, so he had a pretty good grasp of her situation.

Although she hadn't reached the level she had in the games yet, she was already very popular.

Hearing Lono say this, he nodded and smiled. "That aside, it doesn't seem to be vacation time at Levincia Gym yet, right? Is it okay for you to come all the way out here?"

As far as D knew, the Paldea League also had performance requirements for Gym Leaders and would even send people to inspect the Gyms and evaluate their strength.

Lono nodded. "Don't worry! Before I came here, I already requested three days off from the Paldea League, and the chairwoman herself approved it!"

"I see!" Looked like the Paldea League and Geeta were actually pretty humane.

---

When Lono finished her snack and drank the Indeedee's tea made by her sister, she decided to challenge Pawmot's Thunder Trial once again.

D found Pawmot, and the two of them arrived at the battleground in the pasture.

Looking at Lono, who was full of fighting spirit on the opposite side, D couldn't help but click his tongue in amazement.

"I didn't expect it!"

Lono was slightly taken aback: "Didn't expect what?"

D: "I didn't expect you to be so hardworking, it's quite different from what I imagined."

Unlike Grusha and Larry, who basically only came once, Lono often visited the ranch to challenge the Trials.

Could it be that Lono in this world is different from the one in the game?

Since Lono wanted to improve so much, he had to seriously lend a hand, after all, Lono could be considered a friend of his.

However, when Lono heard this, she blushed slightly, she wasn't coming for the Trial!

"Although I'm not very good at cultivating and training Electric Type Pokemon, I think someone like you who specializes in Electric Type Pokemon might get some inspiration from Pawmot."

"So, in the upcoming battle, we won't be showing our full strength, it's more of a demonstration, so make sure your Pokemon watch carefully!"

"Eh?" Lono didn't quite understand for a moment, but after a while, she finally got D's meaning and quickly nodded: "Okay!"

In the first battle, D sent out Pawmot, while Lono sent out her Electivire.

The battle began, and Pawmot swung its hammer, charging ferociously at Electivire.

A powerful current burst from the hammer, and Electivire sensed the strong electricity, quickly crossing its arms to try to block it.

Pawmot's hammer pounded down fiercely, and a burst of yellow current erupted from Electivire's body, absorbing all of Pawmot's electricity into its body.

Motor Drive ability!

D immediately recognized the ability; a Pokemon with this ability would not take damage from Electric Type moves but would instead gain a speed boost.

However, Pawmot wasn't an ordinary Electric Type Pokemon, its hammer not only carried Electric Type Energy but also dealt physical damage.

Electivire's arms sank, and at this moment, Lono's voice came from behind it.

"Electivire, use Brick Break!"

The strong Electivire instantly raised its hand and chopped at Pawmot in front of it.

Pawmot sidestepped to avoid it, and seeing that the attack failed, Lono immediately commanded Electivire to pursue.

"Thunderbolt!"

A dazzling electric flash burst violently from Electivire, transforming into an electric beam blasting at Pawmot mid-air.

Pawmot hovered there motionless.

D said calmly: "Charge."

"Pawmi!"

The next second, Thunderbolt engulfed Pawmot, but surprisingly, the terrifying Electric Type Energy didn't deal much damage to Pawmot, instead, it absorbed it into its body using Charge!

"How could this be?!" Lono was quite surprised.

D explained with a smile: "For Electric Type Pokemon, electricity is also their food. When Charge is used, Pokemon naturally accumulates electricity in their body."

"But if at this time, they can consume the opponent's Electric Type moves, although they will take some damage, it will greatly enhance the effect of Charge."

"Turning the opponent's attack into food?" Lono was slightly taken aback, she had never thought it could be like this!

"Do you want to try it?" asked D.

Lono nodded: "Electivire, get ready!"

Having heard their conversation, Electivire nodded and began to prepare.

"Pawmot, use Discharge," D commanded.

"Pawmi!"

With a shout, a strong current surged from Pawmot's small body, eventually enveloping the entire battlefield.

As Discharge was about to engulf Electivire, Lono's voice rang out simultaneously: "Electivire, use Charge!"

"Roar!"

Electivire let out a loud roar, standing still in place, and entered a charging state.

Turning electricity into food... Electivire recalled D's words, and it began to devour the electricity.

I eat, I eat, I eat, eat, eat!

The electricity in its body climbed steadily upwards, and in the blink of an eye, Electivire felt its stored Electric Type Energy reach a new level.

In this state, if it used other Electric Type moves, their power would be greatly enhanced!

D nodded at Lono and Electivire: "Come on!"

Lono was completely absorbed in the battle at this moment.

She nodded forcefully and then shouted loudly: "Electivire, use Supercell Slam!"

"Pawmot, let's do it too, use Double Shock!"

On command, the two Pokemon on the field moved simultaneously.

Electivire's strong body was completely covered by electricity, looking like it was wearing a suit of electric armor, it charged forward fiercely.

Pawmot, on the other hand, poured all of its electricity into the small silver hammer, fearlessly charged toward the Electivire.

The two Pokemon moved at an incredible speed, and in the blink of an eye, they collided.

---

A dazzling spark of electricity erupted instantly, ultimately forming into a yellow, spherical glow enveloping the two Pokémon within it.

Immediately following, a fierce explosion occurred on the battlefield.

The intense shockwave forced Pawmot and Electivire to retreat, but neither lost their ability to continue fighting.

"What a powerful strike!"

Seeing the scars on Electivire, Lono couldn't help but widen his eyes.

D smiled and said, "It seems your Electivire has mastered it. Let's consolidate this for now. There's no need to continue this battle further."

"Got it!" Lono nodded.

Lono's older brother Indeedee brought some Life Sheep Milk to restore the Pokémon's energy.

Due to the battle, combined with the weather, both Pokémon had sweated quite a bit.

Seeing this, D invited them to rest in the living room.

"How do you feel?" D asked with a smile.

Lono nodded, praising, "As expected of D!"

"Once you return, you can use this method to train Bellibolt and the others. If all your Pokémon master it, the strength of the Levincia Gym will surely become even more formidable," D said.

"Yeah, I got it!" Lono listened very attentively.

Looking at the powerful Pokémon surrounding D, Lono suddenly became curious about his past.

According to the leader, D had arrived in the Paldea Region three years ago. Before that, he had traveled to various regions.

Thinking of this, Lono asked, "By the way, D, where did you travel before coming to the Paldea Region?"

D was slightly taken aback, not expecting Lono to ask this question.

He pondered for a while before answering, "A lot of places! Like Kanto, Johto, Hoenn, Sinnoh, and Kalos. I've been to them all."

"Wow!" Lono exclaimed, "I've never left the Paldea Region."

D smiled gently, "When you have the time, you can explore other places. Each region has its unique culture and Pokémon habitats."

Lono nodded and asked, "And don't you live with your parents?"

"Parents?" D shook his head, "They're both gone. I lived here in the Paldea Region as a child. Later, I moved with them to the Hoenn Region for their treatment. After they passed away, I traveled to other places."

"After getting tired of traveling, I returned to my hometown, Porto Marinada Town, and inherited this ranch from my grandfather."

Hearing this, Lono opened her mouth, apologetic, "I'm sorry, I didn't mean to bring this up."

D grinned, signaling Lono not to worry, "It's alright, it's all in the past."

They looked at each other, Lono's eyes glimmering as she earnestly nodded, "Yeah, D is a very strong person!"

D didn't continue the topic, instead asking, "And what about you?"

"Me?" Lono pointed to herself, seeing D nod, she spoke, "I grew up around Levincia City like any other normal kid. After turning ten, I left home to attend Paldea Academy."

"Then, I met Bellibolt and Mismagius. We challenged gyms together, collected badges, and eventually joined the Paldea League, becoming the Gym Leader of Levincia City."

"City life must be lively, right?" D asked.

Lono paused, unsure how to answer, "I've never lived in the countryside."

"That's too bad, isn't it?" D chuckled, "Though the countryside isn't as bustling as the city, the life here is wonderful."

"In spring, you can see hills covered with flowers, with many wild Floette dancing among them."

"On summer nights, you can see many fireflies gathering on the riverbanks, like stars. Some Water Type Pokémon will even peek out from the water to watch the fireflies."

"In autumn, the hills turn golden. If you go into the mountains, you can find a variety of mountain goods: mushrooms, hazelnuts, berries, and occasionally, you see wild Pokémon storing food for the winter."

"In winter... it's too cold to go out much, but sometimes I build snowmen and stargaze with the Pokémon on the ranch."

"During those times, the night sky is clear, every star and the moon are visible."

These words left Lono looking forward eagerly.

"Sounds wonderful!"

"Right?" D also began to look forward to this year's summer.

Just then, he suddenly remembered something.

"Hey, it's already summer. There should be fireflies tonight. Would you like to see them?"

Lono was slightly startled, "Eh? Can I?"

D nodded, "Of course, there's a room upstairs. You can stay the night and head back to Levincia City tomorrow."

It's a guest room meant for hosting visitors.

Alder, Professor Oak, and others have stayed upstairs before.

"Hehe!" Lono chuckled shyly, "Then I'll impose on you!"

"Indeed~" D's little sister Indeedee happily offered to prepare the room for their guest.

*

By early evening, as dusk was approaching, the hills behind echoed with cicada chirps, distant yet near.

---

The firefly colony residing in the grass began to gradually become active.

By the time the sky became completely dark, the firefly colony gathered at the lakeside, illuminating the night.

D spread a picnic blanket on the grass by the river and filled it with a variety of snacks and foods.

Fresh milk pudding, honey cake, Poffins, Pokeblocks, Berry Juice, MooMoo Milk...

The Pokemon from the ranch all gathered there, happily chasing and playing around the picnic blanket.

On the other side of the river, the Psyducks sat obediently by the riverbank, holding their heads with their hands, vacantly watching the fireflies dancing above the river.

Lono also released all of her Pokemon.

Bellibolt, Kilowattrel, Mismagius, Electrode, Luxray, Electivire.

Their Pokemon had eaten together before, so they were somewhat familiar with each other.

Therefore, upon meeting, Pawmot flew up and released a bit of current, using the specific communication method of Electric Type Pokemon to greet Luxray and the others.

Flutter Mane was still very curious about Mismagius, floating mid-air, staring at it without blinking.

Mismagius, feeling scared under its gaze, turned around and hid to the side.

Lono looked at this dreamy scene, and a feeling never felt before suddenly surged in her heart.

Before this, she had visited many attractions.

The Million Volt Skyline of Levincia City, the Casseroya Waterfall of Casseroya Lake, the Grand Olive Orchard in the southwest of Paldea...

As for Glaseado's Grasp on Glaseado Mountain, Lono hadn't been there yet because she was afraid of the cold.

But none of those places could give her this kind of feeling.

By the slowly flowing riverbank, the firefly colony gently glowed, slowly flying, their figures reflected on the surface of the clear water, gently rippling with the waves.

The serene night, filled with the chirping of cicadas in summer, the gentle evening breeze...

All of this is something you can't experience in a big city.

"So beautiful!" Lono murmured, then a smile appeared on her face: "Thank you, D!"

D, with his hands on the grass, looked up at the firefly-lit riverside in front of him and said softly:

"I should also thank you."

"Eh?" Lono was taken aback.

D smiled: "You helped promote my ranch shop in your livestream, right?"

Lono was completely confused: "How did you know?"

She had been so cautious!

"Because your fans all came to my shop's message board and said so." D opened his Rotom Phone and found those initial messages.

[Recommended by Lono's livestream! The milk here is delicious, sweet, and fresh. The Dragonite delivery is also fast, highly recommended!]

[It's indeed good, thanks to Lono, I found this hidden gem shop. The vegetables are very fresh, now my grandma doesn't have to go out to buy vegetables anymore. I'll definitely come again!]

And so on.

Lono's face turned red as she read, how embarrassing.

"Thank you." D said softly.

Lono truly is a warm-hearted person! If he remembered correctly, in the anime, Lono, as a livestreamer, often encouraged her fans to lift their spirits.

Lono scratched her head sheepishly.

At this moment, D suddenly remembered something.

"By the way, did you get here by taking the Squawkabilly Flying Taxi?" he asked.

Lono nodded: "That's right! There are flying taxi stations in both Levincia City and Porto Marinada Town, it's very convenient to take them directly here!"

"How long does the flight usually take?" D asked.

"Not too long, about six hours or so." Lono calculated carefully.

After all, Porto Marinada Town and Levincia City are so far apart, one in the far west of Paldea and the other in the far east, six hours is quite fast.

D asked incredulously: "Does it really take six hours by flying taxi?"

Lono, puzzled: "Huh? What's wrong?"

D: "..."

Too slow, no, the round trip is too troublesome, isn't it?

"Tomorrow, let Dragonite take you back." D suddenly felt a headache.

At Dragonite's speed, it can reach Levincia City in less than half an hour, much faster than Squawkabilly.

Now it was Lono's turn to be confused.

She felt a bit embarrassed, "Isn't that a bit too much trouble?"

"It's fine, Dragonite has to deliver goods to Levincia City anyway, you can just ride along with it." D said.

"Alright then." Lono giggled: "Thank you very much!"

Not far away, Koraidon and Cyclizar were squatting on the grass.

Cyclizar looked towards Koraidon, puzzled, and asked: "Gao?" (Why can't we go bother D?)

Koraidon, with a serious expression: "Gagg." (Because D has something very important to do.)

"Gao?"

Something important?

Cyclizar immediately perked up, paying full attention.

Koraidon looked at the two by the riverbank and the Pokemon surrounding them, memories surfacing in its mind.

Its young owner, D's child, pitifully tugged at it, asking when it would return.

At the time, when it was still a Cyclizar, it said it would return soon.

Yet it couldn't go back, its young owner never waited for its return.

Remembering this, Koraidon's mood inevitably turned somber.

But then, it pulled itself together once more.

Because it had returned, and when it meets its young owner again, it must say: I've come back.

*

The next morning, Lono rode the Dragonite back to Levincia City.

---

Half an hour after Dragonite left, D received a message from Lono.

[Lono: Thank you, I had a great time yesterday!]

D chuckled, sent a smiling emoji back, and then put his phone away.

It's another fresh day, and there's plenty of work awaiting him at the ranch.

Just as D was planting summer crops in the fields, Keri arrived at the ranch with the construction team.

She greeted D and then looked around, gossiping: "Where's your little girlfriend?"

D was dumbfounded on the spot: "What little girlfriend?"

But then D suddenly realized something.

Could Keri be talking about Lono?

Sure enough, the next second, D heard Keri saying: "It's the girl who came to the ranch yesterday!"

D: "..."

"Don't talk nonsense, she came for the ranch's challenge trial." D shook his head.

However, Keri smiled slightly, "Oh? Is that so? But I noticed she looked at you differently!"

D: "..."

What kind of look? Did Lono look at him differently?

No way?

Keri didn't say any more, and after those few words, she took Machamp to continue the construction.

Leaving D standing there, utterly confused.

But after pondering for a while and getting nowhere, he decided not to think about it.

Moreover, the videophone in the house started ringing, so D put the matter aside and went inside to answer the call.

Even though Rotom phones are now widespread in this world, videophones haven't disappeared.

Because some trainers don't have Rotom phones, or they lose their phones in the wild, they can go to the local Pokemon Center and use the videophone there to contact family and friends.

The videophone at D Ranch is still there, but it's used as a public phone for the ranch.

Guests often call to order vegetables, milk, berries, and whatnot.

Once the call connected, a gruff voice was heard on the other end.

"Hello, is this D Ranch? I'm the sweet shop owner from the neighboring city. The city is hosting a dessert competition, and I need fresh apples to make apple pie and apple candy. I plan to order a batch of apples from you!"

"Sure, no problem." After understanding the man's needs, D asked him to send over his location and contact information, assuring him the apples would soon be delivered.

Soon, D received the guest's information.

Name: Cliff

Contact number: 6937394

Address: Cascarrafa City, Azure Bay Street, Shop No. 3

After hanging up, D used the map app to check the distance between Cascarrafa City and Porto Marinada Town.

If driving, it would take about an hour and a half to get there.

Once the location was confirmed, D began to call upon his Pokemon helpers to head to the apple garden across the river to pick apples.

This season, the apples in the garden were large and round, almost every one of them was red and tempting.

Picking apples is a simple task, so D initially only called Koraidon and Cyclizar to help.

But upon hearing about this, Ogerpon and Gholdengo also eagerly ran over wanting to assist.

D handed them a few boxes, instructing them to pack the unpicked apples into them.

Ogerpon and Gholdengo were very clever; just a glance was enough for them to learn how to do it.

They adeptly picked apples from the trees and placed them into the boxes.

Seeing their deftness, D trustingly turned his attention away, continuing his tasks.

Initially, the two Pokemon worked smoothly, but soon D heard Ogerpon cry out, seemingly startled by something.

D and the other Pokemon immediately turned their heads to look.

"What happened?" D asked.

Ogerpon pointed at an apple on the fruit tree.

"Ponnie!"

D looked in the direction it was pointing, and saw a peculiarly shaped "apple".

At first glance, it looked very much like an apple, but upon closer inspection, the differences from a real apple were apparent.

Big eyes replaced the usual apple stem, a pair of caterpillar tails protruded from holes in the back, and two fang-like patterns appeared on the lower half of the apple.

Clearly, a bug had gotten inside that apple.

While observing that strange apple, a Pokemon's name immediately flashed through D's mind.

Applin?

Apparently frightened by Ogerpon's shout, the Applin immediately shrunk into the apple, hiding motionlessly.

Seeing Applin, D's first reaction was: It's finally here!

He'd been waiting for Applin to come to his apple garden for so long.

Because Applin with Ripen ability can help the berries and apples mature!

With them, the orchard on the World Tree can grow even better.

Thinking of this, D paused his actions and said to Ogerpon: "Don't worry, it's also a Pokemon, its name is Applin, a Pokemon that lives specifically inside apples."

Upon hearing this, a look of curiosity crossed Gholdengo's face.

Could Pokemon really live inside apples?

Ogerpon, on the other hand, was filled with dread.

It was greatly concerned about accidentally eating an Applin inside an apple in the future!

---

"Bonnie..." Ogerpon suddenly didn't look well, wondering what came to mind.

D noticed the expressions of the two Pokemon and explained:

"Don't worry, Applin doesn't live inside the apple from the beginning. It hides in the apple after birth and spends its entire life there."

"Ogerpon, you don't have to be afraid of it, just pay more attention when you eat apples in the future, you can tell the difference between Applin and a regular apple."

With that, D stepped forward and placed his hand over the apple inhabited by Applin.

Dragon Power, activate!

As a Dragon Type, Applin immediately felt a friendly aura.

It was slightly surprised, then extended its eyes from the apple to curiously observe.

Soon, Applin noticed the human had approached.

Applin instinctively wanted to avoid, but quickly realized the friendly aura emanated from this human.

"Hello, Applin, I'm D, the owner of this orchard. You don't need to be afraid, no Pokemon will harm you here." D released his good intentions to Applin.

Applin cautiously looked at this human, initially full of vigilance, worried it might be eaten.

Since unevolved Applin is fragile, various incidents can lead to their demise.

For instance, losing their hiding apple causes their body's moisture to slowly dissipate, becoming progressively weaker. If they don't find an apple, they will perish in weakness.

Or if the apple falls from a height, it might force Applin out.

Besides these accidents, they also occasionally fear predator Bird-type Pokemon.

Sometimes, even a Pokemon like Noibat might mistake them for food and take them back to the nest.

As they look too much like apples.

Yet soon, Applin felt a special power from the human before it.

The person seemed to be caring for it, even conveying their thoughts to it.

[Don't be afraid, no one and no Pokemon will harm you here.]

Applin gradually let down its guard.

He knew, no Dragon Type Pokemon can refuse Dragon Power.

"Alright, from now on, feel free to live here!" D said.

When Applin evolves into Appletun or another form, by then the World Tree will be completely grown. He will send Applin to live by the World Tree.

To Pokemon, it's an absolutely safe place.

But before that, D had one more thing to do.

"Come, Applin, let me see your ability."

He conveyed his intention to Applin.

Applin seemed to half-understand, it took a while to grasp D's meaning.

Applin jumped next to a slightly green apple.

The next second, a unique wave was released from its body.

Affected by the wave, the green apple began reddening at a visible speed, eventually transforming completely into a big red apple.

Seeing this, the word 'ripen' instantly flashed through D's mind.

This Applin's ability perfectly suited the orchard's ripening needs!

Such Pokemon can care for fruit trees, aiding berries to grow better and faster.

D was delighted, but immediately felt Applin's thoughts through Dragon Power.

[I ripened all the apples on this tree!]

"Hmm?" D was quite surprised: "Really?"

Applin gave an affirmative response.

Seeing this, D turned and observed the apples' growth on the apple tree, then other trees' apples.

Comparing, he found this tree's apples had matured for a period. The remaining unripe apple was because Applin hadn't had time to ripen it.

"Why are you doing this?" D curiously asked.

Applin's thoughts quickly conveyed through Dragon Power.

[Because I ate an apple here, I need to ripen a few apples to return to you.]

D hadn't expected that to be the reason.

"Have you been here for a long time?" he asked.

Applin indicated it hadn't been here long, but it saw D watering and caring for trees, knowing this was the human's territory.

"I see." D nodded, thinking this Applin was quite considerate.

"In that case, stay here for now. After a while, I'll take you to another place to live, where it's safe, and you can help care for the orchard there." D said.

Applin agreed.

D placed it back on the tree and continued working with his Pokemon assistants, boxing apples, loading them onto the truck.

Once everything was done, D sat in the driver's seat, driving apples and a truckload of Pokemon down the road to deliver to customers.

Meanwhile, in the Hoenn Region, a vast mountain forest.

Mewtwo, leading a group of cloned Pokemon, sought a haven in this world and its reason for existence when it suddenly sensed a familiar aura.

It stopped flying, using Psychic to slowly descend with Meowth, Pikachu, Venusaur, and other cloned Pokemon.

Upon landing, Mewtwo saw two familiar figures in the dense forest.

One was Teddiursa, with a heart of justice.

And the other was the Mythical Pokemon, Mew, once treated as its mother, having fought an intense battle.

---

---

---

---

# 445: Chapter 442: As If an Old Friend Returns

It's been several years since Mewtwo last saw Mew.

They met on New Island, when Mewtwo was still lost in chaos, seeking an answer.

To find the meaning of its existence, Mewtwo transformed New Island, converting the ruins of the Team Rocket Research Institute into a castle, inviting powerful humans to a banquet.

Mew trespassed into its castle at that time.

Clone Mewtwo, made from Mew's genes, fought a great battle with Mew to prove that a copy could be stronger than the original.

The battle affected the Pokémon and Trainers attending the banquet, and that's when Mewtwo saw the good side of humanity.

A human named Ash tried to stop their fight but was petrified, and the Pokémon present awakened him with their tears.

Mewtwo, moved by everything it witnessed, finally abandoned its plan and led the group of cloned Pokémon away from New Island.

The second meeting was at a place called Mount Quena.

Then, Mewtwo first formally learned about the Heroic Expedition Team and deeply understood the Mew it once considered a mother.

Ultimately, Mewtwo accepted Mew and Teddiursa's invitation, leading the cloned Pokémon to a place called D Ranch.

A human named D hosted Mewtwo and the cloned Pokémon.

In his eyes, these cloned Pokémon appeared no different from regular Pokémon, all beings existing in this world.

To thank the host, or perhaps for some other indescribable reason, Mewtwo didn't use its Psychic powers to erase his memories of it.

After that, it began traveling the world with the cloned Pokémon like humans, searching for a paradise of their own.

Yet, to this day, Mewtwo still hasn't found that place.

But Mewtwo doesn't feel lost, because it met many Pokémon along the way and had ample time to reflect.

The human genes within it offered richer emotions and wisdom compared to ordinary Pokémon.

This journey has been quite rewarding for it.

Mewtwo's mindset has undergone a tremendous transformation compared to its days on New Island.

Having experienced so much, met so many Pokémon, observing how they treated it like an ordinary Pokémon.

Those moments, it seemed as if it became a part of the vast realm of this world, coming into the world humbly and departing just as humbly.

Reuniting with Mew here, a wave of unprecedented emotion suddenly surged in Mewtwo's heart.

Perhaps it was anticipation for meeting Mew, sharing their current situations.

Mewtwo subdued the terrifying Psychic energy fluctuations within itself, approached with the cloned Pokémon, calling out through Telepathy.

"Mew."

Mew, taking a nap under a towering tree, suddenly heard a familiar voice calling it.

Confused, it opened its eyes and looked ahead.

Soon, Mew saw several familiar figures in the clearing ahead.

It was... Mewtwo, Meowth, Venusaur, Pikachu, and the others!

"Miu!"

Mew let out a joyful growl.

The joy of reunion washed over its heart, and it happily flew forward, circling around Mewtwo and the Pokémon, greeting them.

Teddiursa and Zapdos were awakened by Mew's voice.

Teddiursa's eyes brightened, and it ran forward joyfully, greeting Mewtwo and the others: "Ema Ema!"

Zapdos, having never met Mewtwo, felt puzzled seeing its teammates' reactions.

But soon, Teddiursa began introducing Mewtwo to it: "Ema, Ema Ema!" (It's Mewtwo, a good friend we met before!)

Upon hearing this, Zapdos realized that this Pokémon, like it, might have also been helped by the Heroic Expedition Team.

Mewtwo glanced at the bird Pokémon with yellow feathers.

It nodded slightly towards it, then looked at Mew and Teddiursa.

"It's been a while." Mewtwo's deep voice resonated through Telepathy in the two Pokémon's minds.

"Miu!" Mew nodded its little head, indeed, it had been a long time since they last met.

It tilted its head, looking at everyone following Mewtwo, and couldn't help but ask: "Miu?" (Are you traveling too?)

Mewtwo nodded.

A group of Pokémon sat in this woodland clearing.

Teddiursa took out snacks prepared by D from the pouch on Zapdos, treating this group of Pokémon.

Mewtwo glanced at those delicately crafted, cute human food and then said to Mew:

"Yeah, I'm exploring the world, and also looking for a sanctuary for us cloned Pokémon."

Mew asked: "Miu?" (Did you find it?)

Mewtwo shook its head: "Not yet."

This world is vast, with traces of human presence in many places.

Compared to places near human cities, often disrupted by Human Trainers, Mewtwo's heart yearns more for unseen valleys, those serene and beautiful with abundant resources.

The location with abundant resources is the most crucial among the three conditions.

Initially, Mewtwo did not feel much, but as they traveled, gradually, Mewtwo realized something.

Pokemon need to eat.

It alone didn't really mind and could just make do with anything, but that wouldn't work for the group of companions following it.

So over this past year or so, every time they stopped along the road, Mewtwo would first settle them in a safe place, then go out alone to look for food.

Because of this, Mewtwo often found itself sighing. Life, huh… Is this what life is like for humans and for those ordinary Pokemon that were originally born into this world?

Hearing Mewtwo's words, Mew and Teddiursa exchanged a look.

In fact, the two of them were very fond of Mew as well, and already regarded Mewtwo as a good friend.

On quiet, late nights in the past, Mew would occasionally think of Mewtwo, wondering where it was now and how it was doing.

And after the World Tree grew, that feeling only became stronger.

—If only Mewtwo and Pikachu and Meowth and the others could also come live at the World Tree.

Then everyone would never have to part again!

Whenever they wanted to meet, they could see each other anytime, anywhere; they could eat Berries together, watch the stars, or chase each other around and play tag, and even invite everyone into the secret base to play.

Just thinking about it felt so blissful!

With that thought, Mew and Teddiursa made up their minds.

Teddiursa opened its mouth and extended an invitation to Mewtwo: "Yimo!" (Mewtwo, come live at the World Tree with everyone!)

Hearing this unfamiliar word, Mewtwo was slightly taken aback.

"World Tree?"

Its tone was filled with confusion.

"Yimo!" (That's right! The World Tree!)

Teddiursa nodded again and again.

Beside it, Mew covered its mouth and laughed happily: "miu～" (That's right, it's a very, very big tree, D grew it, and we've already invited lots of good friends to go live there～)

Teddiursa's eyes were shining: "Yimo!" (That way, from now on everyone can always be together!)

D… that human again.

In Mewtwo's mind surfaced the scene from last time, when that human had treated it to fruit wine.

"He's the one who grew the World Tree?" Mewtwo asked.

Teddiursa nodded: "Yimo!" (And Gogoat!)

Gogoat—sounded like a Pokemon as well.

Mewtwo couldn't help falling into thought; it didn't agree right away.

These days it was no longer alone. Besides itself, there were many Pokemon by its side waiting for its care.

Thinking of this, Mewtwo turned around and looked at the group of cloned Pokemon that followed it.

Pikachu, Meowth, Charizard, Blastoise, Venusaur, Sandslash, Gyarados, Golduck, Ninetales, Nidoking, Vaporeon, Vileplume, Rapidash, Rhyhorn, Wigglytuff.

Aside from these cloned Pokemon, over this year their group had also continued to grow.

Two Mareep, a Skitty, a Patrat, a Buneary, as well as a Skiddo and a Mudbray.

These Pokemon had once suffered cruelly at the hands of humans; Mewtwo could not stand by and watch such things happen, so it stepped in and rescued them.

From then on, this group of Pokemon joined their team.

Mewtwo was planning to take them to find a hidden place where they could live together.

Mewtwo gazed at this group of Pokemon, and this group of Pokemon gazed back at Mewtwo.

"miu?"

Off to the side, Mew tilted its head as it watched this scene.

It blinked, and seemed to sense the hesitation and wavering in Mewtwo. A thoughtful look appeared on Mew's small face.

Teddiursa's little face was scrunched in distress, utterly puzzled by Mewtwo's hesitation.

"Yimo..." (It'd be fine if everyone just went over there to live together...)

Mewtwo stayed silent, saying nothing.

Just then, Mew suddenly thought of a good idea.

It flew excitedly up in front of Mewtwo and called out to it: "miu!" (Mewtwo!)

Mewtwo blinked and lifted its head to look at Mew in puzzlement.

The next second, Mew curled up its body as if gathering strength, and then a blue, mystical halo spread out from it in all directions.

The scenery around them began changing rapidly.

From an open clearing in a wild forest, they arrived above an unbelievably gigantic, towering tree.

In almost the blink of an eye, all the Pokemon noticed this super-massive tree.

Their faces showed expressions of shock.

"Pika!"

"Meow?"

"..."

Mewtwo was also very surprised, but soon realized that Mew was showing them the World Tree from its memories!

"So this is the World Tree?" Mewtwo looked forward with a hint of curiosity in its eyes.

Compared to this tree, they were so small and insignificant.

"miu!"

Mew nodded repeatedly.

Then it used Telekinesis to carry the surrounding Pokemon and fly forward.

Passing through the lush canopy at the top of the World Tree, a vast region appeared below.

Mewtwo swept its gaze over it and found that this place was actually as large as a huge human-built metropolis!

The tree held all kinds of terrain: dense, verdant forests, vibrant grassy fields, streams winding through the woods and finally gathering in an open area to form a round lake.

These were the scenes they could see for the moment.

In places their sight couldn't yet reach, there must be even more expansive regions.

Mew flew along the tree with everyone while introducing the place:

"miu～" (This is the World Tree. Don't worry, it's very quiet here. Other than D occasionally coming up, there are no other humans at all. There are only Pokemon in the tree. D said he's going to help us build the headquarters of an Explorer Team Guild here!)

---

Teddiursa chimed in from the side: "One-e one-e!" (That's right! Besides the guild, there are also the Pokemon Tavern, Pokemon Cafe, Pokemon Restaurant, and Pokemon Hospital, all run by Pokemon!)

"Miu!" (And D will plant lots and lots of fruit trees, so everyone won't have to worry about going hungry!)

Teddiursa's eyes were full of determination.

"One-e!" (We want the name of the Heroic Expedition Team to be known throughout the world! Helping every Pokemon in need!)

Hearing the words of the two Pokemon and witnessing the scenes on the World Tree, even before Mewtwo could speak, the group of Pokemon behind it showed expressions of longing.

If possible, they really wished for a home of their own, where they could eat Berries full every day and wake up to play with their companions.

Instead of wandering around like this... homeless, like stray Pokemon, roaming everywhere.

Most importantly, with Mewtwo together.

Living happily in a paradise just for them.

After the introduction, Mew and Teddiursa didn't speak again.

They quietly looked at Mewtwo and the group of Pokemon in front of them, leaving the remaining time for everyone else.

Although they really wanted to invite everyone to settle on the World Tree, ultimately, it was a decision everyone had to make themselves.

Mewtwo silently looked at the Pokemon behind it.

When it saw their eyes, it already had the answer in its heart.

"Do you want to live in that place?"

The second Pikachu, cloned from Ash's Pikachu, was the first to answer: "Pikachu!" (Mewtwo will come with us too!)

The clone of Team Rocket's Meowth showed longing: "Meow." (No matter where you look at the moon, it's always round, meow.)

Mewtwo was slightly startled.

Its gaze swept over the surrounding Pokemon.

Venusaur, Charizard, Blastoise and other Pokemon nodded to it in unison.

Mewtwo closed its eyes and softly said: "I understand."

A moment later, it opened its eyes and turned to look at the waiting Mew and Teddiursa.

"Thank you, Mew, Teddiursa." Mewtwo's deep voice echoed in the ears of the two Pokemon: "Next, we will set off together to see the place you mentioned."

"Miu!" Mew let out a happy cry.

Teddiursa also nodded happily: "One-e!" (Yes, yes, you will definitely fall in love with that place! There, you won't have to wander around outside anymore!)

Seeing these two Pokemon so thoughtful for them, Mewtwo was touched.

"Mm." It nodded, then asked: "Will you come with us to that place?"

Mew and Teddiursa shook their heads.

"One-e, one-e one-e!" (Not for now, D gave us some tasks, we're going to help a Pokemon named Jirachi!)

"Miu!" (We'll go back in the winter, and then I'll take you to play in my secret base, there are lots of toys!)

Seeing Mew so innocent and childlike, Mewtwo couldn't help but smile.

Was it cloned from such a Pokemon?

Now, it seems their personalities are incredibly different.

The human genes within it told Mewtwo that, in a sense, Mew was its mother.

And throughout its years traveling this world, Mewtwo had long understood what a mother was.

But seeing Mew in front like a child, Mewtwo suppressed that thought.

It nodded and agreed: "Mm, we'll be there waiting for you to return."

*

In the afternoon, after D successfully delivered the apples ordered by a customer, he started heading back to the ranch.

On the dirt-scented countryside path, D drove his old pickup truck leisurely along.

A bunch of Eevees crowded the passenger seat, pressing their faces against the window, watching the scenery on the sides of the road.

While Koraidon and Cyclizar sat in the back of the pickup truck.

Occasionally, trainers with travel backpacks could be seen running by, and sometimes, you'd see them take out a Poke Ball and battle with Pokemon in the grass.

Watching that scene, D, driving the pickup truck, felt like a background NPC in a game, living a simple life of driving around and traveling back and forth with Pokemon.

Wasn't that what a Normal person in the Pokemon World was like?

Suddenly, D noticed a trainer with an anxious expression standing by the roadside, waving at him.

Raising an eyebrow, D parked the car by the road, leaned out the window, and asked: "Hey, what's up?"

The young boy, looking anxious, replied: "Sir, please help us and take us to the nearest town! This Litleo is hurt and needs treatment quickly!"

Upon hearing this, D looked at the Litleo in the boy's arms.

The Litleo had its eyes shut tight, full of wounds, with messy fur, looking very weak, as if it was seriously injured.

"Don't worry, hang on!"

Saying this, D took out a bottle of Life Sheep Milk from the truck bed and handed it over: "Feed it to that Litleo. After drinking this milk, its body will get better."

The boy was momentarily dazed, but quickly realized and fed the bottle of fresh milk to the severely injured Litleo.

The next second, a miraculous scene unfolded.

The milk transformed into magical energy, beginning to heal the wounds on Litleo's body at a visible speed.

In the blink of an eye, the Litleo had recovered health, and then slowly opened its eyes.

---

---

# 446: Chapter 443: Explorer Team Guild Completed!

The Litleo looked confused.

While the boy holding it had a look of surprise on his face.

Eh?

Litleo's injuries have healed?!

The boy looked up at the man driving, his face full of disbelief.

Only then did he notice the group of Pokemon in the man's car.

At this moment, a large group of Eevee were crowded behind the car window and beside the man, peeking at him.

And in the back of this pickup truck, there were also two big Pokemon sitting there, one of which he recognized—it was a Cyclizar, a transportation Pokemon from the Paldea Region.

They seemed too big to fit in the driver's seat, so they were placed in the back.

"You're welcome." The man's voice snapped the bewildered boy back to his senses.

D smiled at the Litleo and casually asked, "Is it your Pokemon?"

The boy finally came back to himself, hurriedly shaking his head, "No, it isn't! I just encountered it in the wild. It was attacked by a Scovillain, and I helped to rescue it."

"I see." D nodded.

He looked at Litleo, who also realized that this human had saved it, and it gratefully licked the boy's cheek with its tongue.

"Looks like it likes you very much!" D said with a smile.

Seeing Litleo was fine, the boy was in a good mood too. He reached out, petting Litleo's head.

Watching the harmonious interaction between the boy and the Pokemon, D's lips curled up, thinking to himself that there were indeed many kids like Ash in the Pokemon World.

Without saying a word, he stepped on the gas, ready to move forward.

As the car started, the boy realized he hadn't thanked the gentleman.

He ran behind the car with Litleo, shouting, "Hey, wait a minute!"

However, the man's voice came from the front.

"No need for thanks, just take good care of yourself and Litleo!"

With these words, the car drove off completely.

Leaving the boy and Litleo standing by the road in a daze.

After a while, the boy withdrew his gaze.

He looked down at Litleo beside him, happily saying, "Alright, your body is fine now, you can go back!"

But Litleo was unwilling to leave.

Because it remembered how the boy anxiously rescued it from Scovillain, bringing it to seek help.

Litleo looked up at the boy and gave a growl.

"Ah-woo!"

The boy blinked in surprise.

Then Litleo jumped up, using its paw to pull a red and white ball from the boy's waist.

The red and white ball fell to the ground, and Litleo looked down at the Poke Ball, then back at the boy.

"Ah-woo!"

Seeing this, an incredible thought popped into the boy's mind.

He pointed at himself, asking incredulously, "You want to leave here with me?"

Litleo nodded affirmatively, "Ah-woo!"

The boy paused, seemingly understanding Litleo's intention, and his eyes lit up. He used the Poke Ball to capture Litleo.

The Poke Ball gently rocked twice and then fell silent.

This meant success in capturing Litleo.

"Litleo, please take care of me from now on!"

*

After helping the young trainer, D was in a great mood.

He hummed a little tune while driving leisurely on the country road leading to Porto Marinada Town.

Just then, the Rotom Phone he had in his pocket suddenly rang.

D drove with one hand, retrieving the phone with the other, only to find that it was an incoming video call from the Heroic Expedition Team.

"Huh?" Have Teddiursa and the others called back?

He pressed to connect, and the next moment, the faces of Teddiursa and Mew appeared on the screen.

"Yippee yippee!"

"Miu～"

As soon as the call connected, D heard some Pokemon cries from the other end.

Before he could speak, the intents of the two Pokemon were translated and read out by Phone Rotom.

Teddiursa: "D, more new companions are going to live at the World Tree!"

Mew: "That's right, a lot of new companions are joining this time!"

"Hmm?" Listening to this, D became interested: "What Pokemon are they?"

Not only him but several Eevee next to him also craned their fluffy heads over.

Teddiursa: "It's Mewtwo!"

Mew: "And Charizard, Golduck, Wigglytuff, Venusaur, Pikachu, Meowth and the others!"

"Pff!" D almost spit out a mouthful of water, asking incredulously, "What did you say?"

Teddiursa repeated itself.

"Mewtwo and Pikachu and the others are coming to live at the World Tree with us!"

Then, D saw the camera on the other end of the screen move to another direction.

There, a human-like Pokemon with a long tail looked calmly at the Rotom Phone.

...Mewtwo!

And behind Mewtwo stood a large group of ordinary Pokemon.

Pikachu, Meowth, Charizard...

Those Pokemon bore distinct dark patterns, which D immediately recognized as markings.

They were the marks of the Cloned Pokemon!

D's mind flashed with the plot from the movie "Mewtwo Strikes Back."

If he remembered correctly, that Pikachu was the clone of Ash's Pikachu.

And that Meowth was the clone of the talking Meowth from Team Rocket's trio.

Wait a minute! Wasn't Teddiursa and Mew traveling in the Hoenn Region? How did they encounter Mewtwo there?

---

At this moment, the Rotom Phone's lens returned to Mew and Teddiursa.

Mew said, "Mewtwo will soon take Pikachu and the others to the ranch!"

D snapped back to attention, calmly nodded and said, "I understand, no problem, I will take good care of them."

Mew was very happy about this and let out a meaningless sound of joy: "miu～!"

"What about you all?" D asked, "Are you going back to the ranch with Mewtwo?"

Mew shook its head, "No! Because we have things to do, it's the task you entrusted to us, D!"

D felt a surge in his heart, "Is it Jirachi?"

Mew nodded its little head, "That's right! We've already heard news about Forina from the Pokemon along the way!"

"Next, Teddiursa, Zapdos, and I plan to head there to find Jirachi and bring it back to the ranch. D, have you found a way to help Celebi?"

D shook his head and said, "Not yet, I don't have any ideas right now, I'll need to formulate a plan based on the actual situation when the time comes."

Upon hearing this, Mew and Teddiursa nodded.

The plan was left to D to think over; what they needed to do first was to find Jirachi!

That Pokemon is truly pitiful, sleeping for a thousand years but only waking for seven days, just thinking about it makes one feel lonely and isolated.

The Heroic Expedition Team must continue to set out.

After simply explaining the current situation to D, they hung up the call.

Mew and Teddiursa looked towards Mewtwo, and Mew nodded slightly at them.

After a brief reunion, they parted again.

Taking with it the group of cloned Pokemon and those harmed by humans, Mewtwo began to fly towards the other end of the planet.

Meanwhile, the three Pokemon of the Heroic Expedition Team continued their journey.

They watched Mewtwo's figure recede, looking forward to their next meeting.

*

The old pickup truck stopped in the ranch.

The Pokemon lined up to jump off the truck, and only after they had all gotten off did D close the door.

To be honest, D was quite confused about Mewtwo coming to settle at the World Tree.

Although he had previously mentioned this to Teddiursa and Mew, he hadn't expected them to encounter Mewtwo so quickly.

And then they directly extended an invitation to Mewtwo.

Not only did Mewtwo come itself, but it also brought a large group of Pokemon to the ranch.

D couldn't help but worry whether Mewtwo and the Pokemon would get along well with those at the World Tree.

But worrying was of no use, D resolved to pay more attention to the situation and try to ease relations between the two groups.

For instance, having Arboliva and the others organize a welcome event for the new Pokemon, using a gathering to bring the two sides closer.

In the evening, Keri and the Machamp construction team left the World Tree.

She found D to report on the construction situation.

"Alright, this project is finally completed, all the facilities you mentioned are now built."

This fast? D was somewhat surprised, but recalling the construction speed of the Machamps and Conkeldurrs, he felt it was quite normal.

"Good, thank you for your hard work!"

D settled the final payment with Keri, though this construction cost a large sum, D felt everything was worth it for the dream of creating an Explorer Team Guild.

After sending off Keri and her Machamp construction team, D left dinner preparation to his sister Indeedee.

He then went to the World Tree to check on the situation.

Riding the World Tree elevator to the top floor, D jumped into the passage from the stump emitting a faint green glow.

The area on this floor had completely transformed.

On the grass-covered open space, various wooden buildings sat, their shapes and decorations were unique, making them easily recognizable at a glance for their functions and purposes.

For instance, the sign of the Pokemon Item Shop was painted with cartoon images of MooMoo Milk, various Berries, and various small items.

MooMoo Milk can help Pokemon recover stamina, different Berries have different functions.

And those small items can help Pokemon enhance their strength or exert other effects.

For instance, the Everstone prevents evolution, the Fire Stone filled with Fire Energy, Miracle Seeds that boost the power of Grass Type Moves, and so on.

These images can be understood by Pokemon at a glance, and then they can come here to purchase what they need.

Aside from these, there were also a restaurant, sushi shop, cafe, dormitory, training room, and other facilities.

The sign of the restaurant was painted with various food and cooking images, like sandwiches, Poffins, Pokeblocks, and so on.

The dormitory's sign was painted with an image of a bed, indicating to the Pokemon, "Oh! This is where you sleep!"

Out of curiosity, D walked into the Pokemon dormitory and was surprised to find it different from the conventional meaning of a dormitory.

Keri crafted unique nests according to the various living environments of different Pokemon.

For example, Greedent liked tree holes, Charizard preferred stone slabs, Eevees enjoyed cozy fluffy nests, and so on.

Not only did Pokemon like it here, but it was also hard for humans not to be charmed.

These small nests, filled with a sense of safety and natural ambiance, looked so comforting!

---

If he were a Pokemon, he would definitely choose that tree hole to be his nest!

Because inside that tree hole, there's a fluffy blanket that looks both safe and comfortable.

After touring the dormitory, D went outside again.

These buildings are arranged in a ring around this open space, and at the center lies a Pokemon Park, complete with benches, street lamps, and a fountain.

Paths connect the facilities and the park, forming a cohesive whole.

However, what attracted D the most was the Exploration Team Guild Headquarters located at the center.

It's grand and spacious. As soon as you step inside, you see a request board, where in the future you can post your own requests to seek the help of other Pokemon.

It's just that Pokemon can't read...

But with Phone Rotom, it shouldn't be a problem either.

D began pondering on ordering a batch of Rotom Phones.

At this moment, the Pokemon originally dwelling on this level also noticed the scene over here.

Soon, a large group of Pokemon curiously ran over.

Treecko, Tatsugiri, Tropius, Arboliva, Milotic...

Arriving here on Tropius, Tatsugiri immediately noticed the sushi shop, as the pattern on the shop's sign is almost identical to them!

"Mi?" Tatsugiri looked at D, seemingly asking what that place was.

D replied, "That's a sushi shop."

"Mi?!"

Hearing this, Tatsugiri was very delighted.

They immediately hopped over to it, jumping around the shop's counter.

D followed them inside.

Looking around, there was a kitchen, a counter, and tables and chairs for customers to dine in.

Ingredients for making sushi require seaweed, rice, carrots, cucumbers, and so on.

Nori for seaweed can be found in the waters around the lower Dragonite Island, and rice, carrots, and cucumbers can be grown through planting.

Next, all that's needed is to hire some Pokemon staff and teach Pokemon how to plant crops and process ingredients, and the sushi shop can officially start operating.

But can Pokemon really plant wheat themselves and then process it into rice?

Moreover, for the continuous development of the sushi shop, Pokemon must also learn about seed preservation and seedling cultivation.

D couldn't help but feel a little worried.

Besides these, he also has to teach Pokemon how to make sushi, how to clean dishes, just thinking about it feels so difficult!

Making sushi requires Pokemon with hands who are also meticulous and clever, and cleaning dishes requires Water Type Pokemon.

All aspects rely on the help of Pokemon. In short, there just aren't enough hands on the World Tree right now!

The few Pokemon here simply can't support the operation of the entire Exploration Team Guild.

Fortunately, the World Tree here will soon welcome a group of new residents to bolster the workforce.

Charizard, Ninetales, and Rapidash can help with lighting fires; Blastoise and Golduck can use Water Gun to clean dishes; Venusaur and Skiddo can cultivate crops.

Sandslash, Patrat, and Mudbray can dig and till the fields.

Pikachu and Mareep can help generate electricity, providing power for the guild.

And Arboliva can provide everyone with fresh and tasty olive oil.

Oh right, there's also Wigglytuff!

D remembers there's a Wigglytuff in Mewtwo's team!

He thought of the game "Pokémon Mystery Dungeon: Explorers of Time" where Wigglytuff is the guild master.

Perhaps he could replicate that and have that Wigglytuff serve as the guild master.

D: "..."

It really feels like a real-life playhouse.

Alright, Keri wasn't wrong at all; he just wants to play house with the Pokemon.

However, while imagination is beautiful, reality is unknown.

All of this has to wait until Mewtwo and the group of Pokemon arrive to be discussed further.

But with Mewtwo's flying speed, they should be able to reach the ranch by noon tomorrow, right?

Before they arrive, D still has some things to prepare.

The next morning, after finishing the work on the ranch, D drove to the town.

He constantly shuttled through the streets, going in and out of various shops, only finishing purchasing what he wanted near noon.

There were various types of crop seeds, saplings of various fruit trees, pots, pans, blankets, mats, quilts, clocks, large power generation equipment, various processing machines, and more.

Keri has already established a mill next to the food barn, where Pokemon just need to put the harvested wheat in to get a heap of flour.

Then with an oven, these ready doughs can be baked into bread.

"Hmm, it seems that besides ordinary Pokemon, the World Tree needs some production-type Pokemon to join," D thought to himself.

Like Garganacl, Shuckle, Miltank, Alcremie, and Combee kind of Pokemon.

Shuckle, also known as an automatic juicer, just requires handing over berries to it, and Shuckle will help brew a magical Berry Juice with restorative effects.

A sticky liquid is secreted from Shuckle, and if this liquid mixes with the Berry Juice, the brewed juice has a better effect than regular Berry Juice.

While pondering, the Rotom Phone suddenly rang.

D glanced down and saw a message from Calyrex, saying Mewtwo and the others had already arrived at the ranch.
