What incredible speed, truly befitting Mewtwo!

After a brief moment of amazement, D started the car and began the journey back to the ranch with a cargo full of goods.

Ten minutes later, the pickup truck stopped on the grassy field of the ranch.

D hadn't even gotten out of the car when he saw Mewtwo, touted as the strongest Pokemon in history, in the not-so-distant area.

Behind Mewtwo stood a large group of Pokemon.

Pawmot, Cyclizar, Koraidon, Gogoat, Garganacl, Alcremie, and Eevees all gathered around, curiously watching the Pokemon who had come to the ranch.

And facing D's group of Pokemon.

The cloned Pokemon and those hurt by humans seemed a bit uneasy.

Mewtwo, however, remained much calmer.

Upon seeing D get out of the car, its gaze followed and turned towards him.

---

---

---

---

# 447: Chapter 444: [Mewtwo Joins the Chat Group]

"Long time no see, Mewtwo."

D chuckled lightly as he greeted Mewtwo from a short distance away.

He still remembered the time Mewtwo came over and got drunk at the Pokemon Tavern.

"Hm."

Mewtwo responded in a low voice and then shifted its gaze.

"Where is the World Tree?" it asked.

D glanced at the restless Pokemon behind Mewtwo, then put down what he was holding, ready to take them over to see the World Tree first.

"Follow me!"

Mewtwo nodded, a blue light appearing in its eyes.

Powerful telekinesis instantly enveloped the Pokemon beside it, gently lifting them off the ground to follow D as they flew forward.

Through the farmlands and into the lake, the group entered the Reversed World.

Looking at this space filled with a sense of destruction, Mewtwo's eyes showed a hint of astonishment.

Almost as soon as it entered here, it sensed a terrifying aura in this realm.

Mewtwo's gaze locked immediately onto a distant, flower-filled, fragmented Sky Island.

A bizarrely shaped Pokemon, exuding a fierce aura, was resting there.

D noticed Mewtwo's gaze and explained:

"That's Giratina, the master of this space. Due to some special reasons, the World Tree can only be placed here for now, but don't worry, it won't affect you."

Hearing this, Mewtwo nodded calmly and redirected its gaze in another direction.

There stood a towering, magnificent tree.

The lush leaves emitted a glowing green light, continually releasing a rich aura of nature.

"Is that the World Tree?" Mewtwo muttered to itself.

D affirmed with a hum: "Yes, what we're seeing now is just its exterior. Inside, there's an extensive space. Let's go and check it out!"

With that, D walked towards the World Tree.

They quickly arrived on the massive Sky Island where the World Tree stood.

Looking up at this giant tree, the Pokemon with Mewtwo were all amazed.

This tree is so big!

Mewtwo also looked up at the sky, and the enormous canopy seemed to stretch endlessly, its green leaves blocking out the sun.

For the first time, it felt so small.

To the surprise of the Pokemon, the air here was so fresh, and the breeze so pleasant—they liked this place!

In this Secret Realm, Pokemon like Skiddo, Venusaur, Blastoise, and Wigglytuff showed expressions of delight.

Is this the "paradise" they have been searching for?

Mewtwo kept observing these Pokemon's expressions, and seeing that they seemed to like it here, it made a decision internally.

"Don't rush, let's go inside and take a look!" D said with a smile, "Follow me, I'll take you in!"

He stepped through the entrance, with a large group of expectant Pokemon following behind him.

Mewtwo walked at the back.

Before officially entering the Sky Island, it turned back to glance in Giratina's direction.

As D led the way, he kept an eye on the Pokemon, noticing Mewtwo's actions and realizing that Mewtwo seemed very concerned about Giratina over there?

Come to think of it, a powerful Pokemon like Mewtwo would naturally have a strong sense of territory, right?

As he pondered, Mewtwo's voice rang in his mind.

"Will this tree always stay in this place?"

D was slightly taken aback but then understood Mewtwo's meaning.

He shook his head and said, "Of course not. In about a month, by June, the World Tree will be fully grown."

"At that time, Giratina will use its power to help lift the tree into the sky, where only you will live, without human disturbance."

Mewtwo remained silent, as if in thought.

After a while, it asked, "And what about you? Will you go live on the World Tree?"

"Me?" Did Mewtwo not want him to go?

However, possessing human genes, Mewtwo quickly understood his thoughts.

It explained, "No, I don't mean anything else, just asking simply. After all, you planted this tree, and we should thank you for providing us with such a place."

"I see!" D realized.

He shook his head and said, "No, I won't go live there, because I have my own things to do."

Like tending to the ranch, for example.

If he were to abandon the ranch to live on the World Tree, he would find it quite hard to part with.

Mewtwo nodded.

D added, "But occasionally, I'll also go up to check on everyone living there and see if there's anywhere I can help."

As they spoke, the group had already wound through the twisting tree tunnels, arriving at the location of the World Tree elevator.

D taught the Pokemon one by one how to use it: all they had to do was jump onto those green bubbles, and they'd be lifted up.

Some playful Pokemon were immediately drawn to this toy.

Like Pikachu, Skitty, and Buneary.

Although they were cloned, they were no different from ordinary Pokemon, their bodies still retaining that innocent and playful nature of Pokemon.

Perhaps repressed for too long, these Pokemon were interested in everything.

When D finished explaining, they eagerly began playing on the bubbles.

---

D and Mewtwo stood below, watching the scene.

After a while, D asked, "Do you want to give it a try?"

Mewtwo: "..."

It turned around and said, "Let's keep moving forward."

The Pokemon who were playing around stopped when they heard this and obediently followed behind Mewtwo.

Seeing this, D didn't say much.

He led the group of Pokemon to the top layer of the World Tree.

Soon, a vast grassland appeared before the eyes of the Pokemon.

And on the grassland, there were many uniquely designed buildings.

They looked somewhat like human-constructed houses.

From a distance, Mewtwo saw several Pokemon using large leaves to carry Berries into a house.

"This is the headquarters of the Heroic Expedition Team Guild," D said. "As you can see, those buildings are specially built for the expedition team."

"That over there is the dormitory for Pokemon to sleep in, the grandest building is the Explorer Team Guild Headquarters, that's the Item Shop, over there is the cafeteria and sushi bar... and in the center is the park for Pokemon to relax."

Hearing these words, Mewtwo couldn't help but show surprise.

D noticed its thoughts and smiled, saying, "That's right, all the clerks and service personnel here are Pokemon, not a single human!"

Mewtwo murmured in disbelief, "Such a thing actually exists..."

"Don't underestimate the power of Pokemon."

D walked to the group of Pokemon beside Mewtwo and introduced them one by one:

"Charizard can use Fire Moves to help start fires and keep warm, Blastoise can use Water Gun to help water the plants, Venusaur can use Grass Energy to care for crops."

"Pikachu and Mareep can provide power for everyone, Mudbray can till the land, Patrat and Buneary can also serve as messengers within the World Tree."

"The Pokemon live on their own power, planting Berries here, running the guild, completing tasks, and never having to worry about going hungry again."

The Pokemon listening to D's words showed expressions of longing.

Every time D mentioned a Pokemon's name, that Pokemon's eyes lit up brightly.

That's right! The ones that were cloned can also do things just like the ordinary Pokemon of this world!

The Pokemon that hadn't been mentioned yet looked expectantly at the human.

Could they really live together here, just as D described?

Pokemon that had been persecuted by humans were a bit insecure.

One Skiddo even looked fearful.

D noticed this Skiddo and couldn't help but think of the three Skiddo at his home that only knew how to dance all day.

But, if he remembered correctly, Skiddo didn't appear in the movie "Mewtwo Strikes Back," did they?

Skiddo are Pokemon from the Kalos Region; they shouldn't appear in the Kanto Region.

Not only Skiddo, but also Mareep, Mudbray, Buneary, Patrat, and Skitty nearby.

D looked curiously at Mewtwo; did it take these Pokemon in?

Mewtwo's voice resonated directly in D's mind through telepathy.

"They were once Pokemon persecuted by humans."

Hearing this, D instantly understood.

No wonder these Pokemon appeared so anxious and uneasy...

He reached out to gently stroke the forehead of the Skiddo, calming its emotions:

"Don't worry, it's okay, no one will hurt you here, you can live here peacefully."

Skiddo looked at him with a bewildered expression: "Maa?"

D: "You know, I also have three Skiddo on my ranch; they used to be just like you, but now they've all grown into reliable Gogoat. When I have time, I'll bring them to the World Tree to make friends with you."

"Maa..." The Skiddo sensed the kindness radiating from the human and was momentarily stunned, then finally showed a happy, expectant smile: "Maa!"

Mewtwo silently watched the scene.

When it saw the usually downcast Skiddo smile, it felt a sense of relief.

It seemed that this place was indeed where they belonged.

At that moment, the original inhabitants of the World Tree noticed the Pokemon.

Since D had told them yesterday that new companions would be joining, the Pokemon had gathered early in anticipation.

Tatsugiri, Milotic, Treecko, Tropius, Arboliva.

Almost all the Pokemon except for Slowking were present.

Arboliva was the first to welcome the new Pokemon, and Tropius also let out a joyful cry, offering the banana fruit hanging from its neck.

Meanwhile, the three Tatsugiri bounced up and down excitedly in place.

With new Pokemon joining, does this mean they can hire employees for the sushi bar?

Who should they choose?

Looking at the large group of Pokemon in front of them, the Tatsugiri became afflicted with choice paralysis.

After a while, one of the Tatsugiri shouted.

"Mi?! (Is there any Pokemon willing to help at our sushi bar?!)

Sushi bar? Help?

Mewtwo and the group of Pokemon were slightly taken aback.

D couldn't help but feel exasperated.

He stepped forward, picked up the three Tatsugiri, and said to them:

"Don't be so hasty. The most important thing now is to hold a welcome party for everyone. Once the new companions are familiar with the place, you can invite them to help at the sushi bar."

---

D thought to himself that the sushi shop didn't even have pots, bowls, or utensils; even if there were employees, they couldn't open the shop!

"Mii..."

The three Tatsugiri murmured quietly, implying that in that case, they might as well recruit workers later!

It was already noon, and Mewtwo along with other Pokemon who traveled all night hadn't eaten yet; their stomachs were growling with hunger.

D even heard some Pokemon's stomachs making rumbling sounds.

Seeing this, Arboliva instantly understood.

Arboliva whispered something to Treecko, and then a few Pokemon ran to the storage warehouse filled with Berries, carrying out various Berries and placing them in front of all the Pokemon.

"Go ahead and eat!"

D smiled and told the group of Pokemon.

Although they were very hungry, the Pokemon didn't pounce on the pile of Berries like ravenous beasts.

They walked up politely, picked up a Berry with their paws, and happily started eating.

Mewtwo stood still and didn't move.

D glanced at it and asked, "Aren't you eating?"

Mewtwo shook its head, remaining still; it wasn't very hungry.

"Alright." D thought carefully, guessing perhaps Mewtwo could replenish its energy through absorbing the natural energy around?

It seems that Legendary Pokemon can do this, not needing to eat specifically.

The group of Pokemon ate lunch joyfully.

After lunch, D took them to explore other parts of the Explorer Team Guild.

Through the woods, there appeared a garden full of Berries ahead.

The group of Pokemon was amazed; there were so many Berries here!

D introduced, "As you can see, this is the orchard specifically for growing Berries; the Berries you ate today at noon were picked from here."

"These trees will grow a batch of Berries every so often, so you don't need to worry about not having enough to eat or running out of Berries."

Because this is the World Tree, it provides constant energy for the fruit trees, allowing them to bear fruit.

In a sense, the World Tree is perhaps the best place to farm in the entire world.

The group continued forward, passing through the garden, arriving at a spacious farmland region.

This area is located to the north of the Explorer Team Guild, the terrain is flat, the soil is fertile, light is ample, and a small creek runs through beside it.

At the end of the creek sits a watermill, where flour is processed.

D planned to transform this area into farmland for cultivating crops.

Such as rice, wheat, carrots, pumpkins, watermelons, soybeans, and corn.

"Pika, Pikachu!" Pikachu looked around the area, looking at the land ahead, it questioned D.

The Rotom Phone auto-translated Pikachu's words.

It asked, "What will this land be used for in the future?"

D answered, "It will also be used for growing crops."

"Meow?" Meowth seemed confused; there's already a big Berry garden over there, what kind of crops will be grown here?

D smiled and said, "There is more to eat in this world than just Berries; you would get tired of eating only Berries soon, wouldn't you?"

"So, I plan to plant some other crops here, like rice, wheat, corn, potatoes, etc."

"Rice can be processed into rice, which is the raw material for fried rice and sushi."

"Wheat can be milled into flour, which can be used to steam buns or make fluffy, soft, sweet bread."

"Corn can be ground into cornmeal or turned into popcorn with specific machinery, paired with Alcremie's cream, making caramel cream-flavored popcorn, with perfectly combined caramel and cream flavors, which must be delicious."

"Besides, there are carrots, pumpkins, potatoes, and watermelons, each can be made into many delicious dishes."

Upon hearing D's words, some Pokemon began to drool.

Mewtwo remained calm, however.

Mewtwo silently listened to D speak, a question arose in its heart.

Can the Pokemon truly, like humans, complete such complicated operations relying only on their strength?

Mewtwo had already figured out D's intention.

It seems he wants to build this place into a sustainable Pokemon Paradise, where Pokemon of different attributes and species live together.

Guild, Heroic Expedition Team Guild...

"Is it for Mew?" Mewtwo murmured in its heart.

Recalling the delicious snacks made by humans that Mew carried, Mewtwo suddenly thought this human really treated Mew well.

No wonder Mew liked him and chose him.

By comparison, the humans it encountered were hard to describe.

Regardless of the Pokemon's will, the human scientist who created it tried to use it as a weapon, wanting to exploit it like Giovanni did.

Only the young man named Ash showed Mewtwo another side of human beings.

Mewtwo came to understand that not all humans are greedy, evil, and unscrupulous.

There are still many humans in the world maintaining a kind heart, treating Pokemon sincerely, and even willing to give up their lives to save Pokemon.

---

D led a group of Pokemon on a brief tour of the World Tree, then let them decide how to spend their time on their own.

Instead of telling them more, it's better to let them experience life on the World Tree firsthand.

Before leaving, D said to Mewtwo, "This tree has more than just this one level. Below, there's an ocean area perfect for Water Type Pokemon to reside."

"I understand," Mewtwo nodded.

It had already sensed with telekinesis that the internal space of the tree far exceeded its expectations.

"If any Water Type Pokemon have trouble adjusting here, you can take them over there," D continued.

He noticed that there were several Water Type Pokemon in Mewtwo's group.

Gyarados, Blastoise, Golduck, and Vaporeon.

Mewtwo's response remained succinct.

"Okay."

Having explained everything, D turned and left the world, planning to return tomorrow or the day after.

Since he had to help the Explorer Team Guild with preparing various furnishings.

Pots and pans, kitchenware, excess ingredients, various tools, and processing machines.

D planned to finish all the shopping and then ask Calyrex to use psychic power to send everything up in one go.

*

D left.

Immediately, only a group of Pokemon remained on the World Tree.

Arboliva and the Treeckos continued working in the orchard, with Milotic occasionally visiting to use [Rain Dance] and help with watering.

Those Pokemon who had just joined the World Tree stood on the sidelines watching.

Initially, Arboliva was worried these Pokemon wouldn't be used to life on the World Tree.

But soon, it realized these Pokemon seemed endlessly curious about everything.

Some Pokemon even actively joined in the planting work.

This quietly eased Arboliva's worries.

And when the Pokemon being cared for by Mewtwo learned from the Treeckos that the berries they ate today were personally grown by them, they all showed surprised expressions.

Soon after, some Pokemon thought of something.

Previously, during their travels, it was Mewtwo who helped them find berries to fill their stomachs.

Now, could they grow berries together and give them to Mewtwo to eat?

Thinking of this, the Pokemon immediately became full of energy.

They couldn't wait to plant their own berry trees and then offer the berries to Mewtwo.

Gradually, the relationship between the two groups of Pokemon grew more harmonious.

After some struggle, the three Tatsugiri finally succeeded in hiring Pokemon employees for the sushi restaurant.

They were Vaporeon, Golduck, and Venusaur.

After being arranged by the three Tatsugiri, Venusaur became the head chef, Vaporeon went out to attract customers, and Golduck became the server delivering dishes.

What? You ask what the boss does?

The boss, of course, stays at the counter to watch the store!

Mewtwo did not participate in any of this.

It floated in mid-air, watching the scenes below, and seeing those Cloned Pokemon living here like ordinary Pokemon, a peculiar feeling arose in its heart.

Sometimes, Mewtwo also felt Meowth's words were right.

Back then, they were still hiding at Mount Quena.

Pikachu and Meowth didn't want to stay there, feeling that living in a cave all the time was no different than imprisonment.

Meowth said: The moon looks round no matter where it's seen; whether you're a Pokemon or not, the moon shining there is round.

Precisely.

What does it matter if they're cloned?

Everyone lives in this world and sees the same round moon, no difference at all.

Everyone is a Pokemon.

It... also has the right to live in this world and enjoy the scenery with everyone else.

Wasn't this peaceful life exactly what it had always yearned for?

Thinking of this, Mewtwo descended into the area of the Pokemon Park, ready to explore this place with its companions.

Whether it's farming, using human tools, or running a shop.

If any help is needed later, Mewtwo is more than willing to lend a hand.

For example, using its human genes to make meals for Charizard and the others.

Working hard together with everyone, planting crops in the fields, waiting for them to mature, then preparing them into delicious meals to enjoy with everyone.

This simple yet fulfilling life is what it dreamed of.

As night fell, Arboliva prepared to host a welcome banquet for everyone.

Seeing Mewtwo coming over, Pikachu and Meowth hurriedly waved at Mewtwo, signaling it to come over!

Mewtwo nodded slightly, then flew over.

Pikachu and Meowth stood on either side of it, excitedly recounting the events of the afternoon.

"Pika Pikachu!" (We went to plant berries with Arboliva and the others!)

"Meow~" (We planted them ourselves. When the berries grow, we'll give them to you to eat, Mewtwo~)

"For me?" Mewtwo was slightly taken aback.

Pikachu and Meowth looked at it, happily nodding.

"Pika Pi!" (That's right! Because Mewtwo used to help us find berries, now we also want to grow berries for Mewtwo!)

What?!

Mewtwo was very surprised.

It had never encountered anything like this before.

Meanwhile, a group of Pokemon had already arrived at the location of the banquet.

---

Arboliva asked Charizard to use Flamethrower to help start the fire.

Such a thing was a piece of cake for Charizard, who readily agreed, delighted that Arboliva treated it like a Normal Pokemon.

Opening its mouth, it spat out a flame, and the pile of wood instantly blazed up.

The firelight illuminated the faces of the surrounding Pokemon, reflecting in the eyes of every single one.

Everyone quietly watched the fire, eating Berries, recalling the amazing experiences of the day, as if they were dreaming.

They had found a paradise of their own and were warmly accepted by the Pokemon there.

Not only that, those Pokemon shared Berries with them and held a feast to welcome their arrival.

It truly felt like being in a dream!

Cloned Meowth blankly looked up at the sky, yet the round moon was nowhere to be seen here.

It could only murmur: "Meow..." (I hope this dream never ends, meow...)

The other Pokemon didn't speak either.

Clearly, many Pokemon shared the same thought as Meowth.

Tropius, Milotic, and Arboliva looked at them in confusion.

After a while, Treecko asked blankly: "Treeko?" (What's bothering you?)

The Pokemon were slightly stunned.

Before they could speak, Tatsugiri added: "Mi!" (Why not ask the wise Slowking?)

Tatsugiri Two followed up: "Mi!" (If you have a problem, go to Slowking; it'll help us find a solution!)

Tatsugiri Three nodded repeatedly: "Mi!" (That's right! Slowking is our Great Sage; it knows everything!)

Slowking?

Hearing the name of the Great Sage, the Pokemon present were slightly stunned.

Is that also a Pokemon?

Seeing the confused expressions on their faces, Arboliva thought for a moment and decided to take everyone to meet Slowking.

Thus, a group of Pokemon extinguished the fire, passed through the forest, and arrived at a clearing in the woods.

In the clearing sat a large stone, with Slowking perched on it, gazing at the sky, deep in thought.

In front of it were several Berries.

Those were what Arboliva and the others had brought for Slowking.

Arboliva and the others stood aside.

Tatsugiri was carried on Tropius's back.

They looked at the new companions and introduced: "Mi!" (Just line up and ask Slowking your questions one by one!)

The Cloned Pokemon exchanged glances, lining up curiously and dreamily.

Mewtwo joined in too.

It gazed at the Pokemon on the stone, pondering like a human philosopher, and thought to itself:

"The Great Sage who knows everything? I wonder if it can answer my question."

Soon, the first Pokemon asked its question.

It was the Cloned Meowth and Cloned Pikachu.

They asked: "What is the meaning of life?"

This was a question they had been pondering for a long time, but had yet to find an answer.

Upon hearing this question, the Slowking on the stone looked at the two unfamiliar Pokemon.

It blinked its eyes, thought for a long while, and finally answered: "The meaning of life is in yourselves."

"Pika pi?"

"Meow?"

Meowth and Pikachu were slightly taken aback.

Slowking: "Do what you want to do, follow your heart. When you feel happiness and joy in what you do, that is the meaning of your life."

Hearing this, the two Pokemon pondered.

What they wanted to do... now, they just wanted to plant Berries with their companions and live well here.

Recalling the anticipation and happiness of planting Berries this afternoon, Pikachu and Meowth suddenly seemed to understand.

So that's their purpose, after all?

Having found the answer, the two Pokemon happily stepped aside.

From now on, they would achieve more of their life's value, living happily with everyone in this place!

Mewtwo, listening, pondered these words.

Then, it was the second Pokemon's turn.

It was the Cloned Charizard.

Looking at Slowking, it asked puzzledly: "Roar?" (Why do Pokemon fight each other? Wouldn't it be better to live together?)

Slowking: "Because not all Pokemon have such thoughts. Where there are Pokemon, there will be conflicts, possibly over food or territory."

"Roar..."

Charizard heard this half-understandingly; it didn't like it that way, it just wanted to live in peace with everyone here.

Next came the third, fourth, fifth...

Every Pokemon asked the questions that bothered them.

Some asked about the meaning of battles, others about the purpose of becoming stronger, and some sought advice on making delicious dishes.

Soon, it was Mewtwo's turn to ask its question.

It looked at the sage-like Slowking, then solemnly asked the question that had been bothering it for a long time.

"Why do I exist?"

Slowking gazed blankly, its face full of wisdom, looking at the unique Pokemon before it.

Hearing this inexplicable question, it couldn't help but show a slightly troubled expression.

"That's a head-scratcher!"

Mewtwo remained silent.

Slowking looked at it for a while, then asked: "Who are you?"

"Mewtwo." Mewtwo replied: "I am Mewtwo."

---

"Mewtwo..." Slowking asked again, "How did you end up here?"

Mewtwo: "We received an invitation from Mew, so I brought them here."

Upon hearing this, Slowking turned his gaze to the Pokemon, then slowly said:

"You wish to protect these Pokemon and live here with them."

Mewtwo paused slightly, then appeared to realize something.

A trace of wisdom flashed in Slowking's eyes as he spoke gently:

"Looks like you've already found the answer to this question yourself."

*

Three days later, D had finished purchasing the necessary machines and materials for constructing the Explorer Team Guild from the town and online shopping apps.

Nearing noon, he asked Calyrex to use Psychic to teleport him and the pile of items to the World Tree.

"Hold on."

Calyrex put down the Rotom Phone, blue light flashed in its eyes, and the next second, D and the pile of items were teleported to the World Tree.

During these three days, D had been busy outside purchasing things.

Additionally, he intentionally let the Pokemon interact by themselves here, so he hadn't often been to the World Tree during this time.

After three days, the overall situation at the World Tree hadn't changed much.

The sushi shop couldn't operate, and three Tatsugiri were lying in the Slowpoke Fountain at the Pokemon Park, blowing bubbles.

Skitty and Buneary were lounging on the park bench, leisurely enjoying the breeze.

Seeing D coming, those two Pokemon immediately turned their curious gazes toward him.

"Hello there!" D greeted them and asked, "Where are the other Pokemon?"

"Meow~"

Skitty straightened its body, opened its paw pads, and stretched lazily.

It jumped off the bench and led the way ahead.

Buneary followed along.

And the three Tatsugiri also excitedly looked towards D.

"Tats!"

Having eaten Translation Nuts Cake beforehand, D instantly understood what they meant.

These three were asking if he had brought materials for making sushi, as they were eager to start business!

D said helplessly, "Of course I brought them, but have you found your employees?"

Tatsugiri nodded excitedly and then informed D about the current situation of the sushi shop.

The sushi shop currently had three Pokemon employees.

They were Vaporeon, Golduck, and Venusaur.

Venusaur is the head chef of the shop. It can use Vine Whip to handle sushi and Razor Leaf to chop carrots.

Vaporeon and Golduck are the waitstaff of the shop; however, Vaporeon's job is to attract customers and clean dishes, while Golduck's job is to serve the customers.

Upon hearing this, D couldn't help but twitch his mouth slightly.

Well, the job allocation is quite organized, perfectly matching each Pokemon's abilities and traits.

"Alright, let me discuss some matters with Mewtwo, and then I'll come to teach you how to open the shop." D said.

"Tats!" Tatsugiri started bouncing in the water excitedly.

D turned to Skitty and Buneary: "I'll need you to help guide me next."

The two Pokemon happily agreed.

With their guidance, D left the guild area and entered a forest.

There he saw Charizard curled up, dozing under a tree, and Venusaur resting on a small hill far away.

Passing through this forest, ahead were the Berry Garden and farmland areas.

Only a few Pokemon were sporadically seen in the Berry Garden, and not finding Mewtwo, D continued forward.

This time, he finally saw Mewtwo's figure in the vast plain.

Not only was Mewtwo there, but also Mudbray, Skiddo, Mareep, Sandslash, Vileplume, Rhyhorn, and Tropius.

Mewtwo was leading a group of Pokemon, cultivating and tilling the land there.

Mudbray and Sandslash used Dig and Tillage Moves.

The other Pokemon didn't know how and were watching on the side, ready to help later in crop cultivation.

Mewtwo was just more exaggerated.

At that moment, it was forcibly tilling the ground using the terrifying Psychic power within its body!

Under the stirring of that Psychic power, every grain of soil on the plain was forcibly moved and lifted into the air, then through stirring and rotating, it fell back to the ground.

As a result, the soil on the plain became very soft, perfect for farming.

Seeing this scene, D was completely dumbfounded.

Oh dear, is Mewtwo farming?!

At this moment, Mewtwo also noticed his arrival.

So it stopped its work and turned to look at D.

"I've come to deliver things. Just as you've cultivated the fields, you can plant the crop seeds I've brought." D said.

There's no need to concern about seasonal crops at the World Tree; any crop can be directly planted.

Upon hearing this, Mewtwo nodded. It estimated that it was almost noon, so it prepared to finish up and go to eat.

The group returned to the guild, and then D witnessed an even more shocking sight.

He saw Mewtwo using Psychic to hold several Berries, then found a dozen wooden cups, and then squeezed those Berries into juice using Telekinesis.

Is Mewtwo... cooking?!

D immediately widened his eyes; no, what intrigued him more was where those wooden cups came from?

Could it be Mewtwo made them itself?

D fell into deep thought.

If he remembered correctly, besides overwhelming combat power, Mewtwo possessed many abilities.

For instance, it could freely use various Psychic abilities such as remote object moving, weather control, creating objects through mind, memory modification, and thought control.

In the movie "Mewtwo Strikes Back", it even used its powers to construct a high-tech castle and rewrite the program of an automatic copying machine.

It's so terrifying; how could there be such a powerful Pokemon in this world?!

Both farming and cooking, if Giovanni knew about this, it's hard to imagine his reaction.

Because Mewtwo was created by Team Rocket as a biological weapon.

A powerful Mewtwo was naturally suited for combat.

Yet Mewtwo's power was so immense that Giovanni had specially crafted a force-restraining armor for it once.

And now, Mewtwo, which Team Rocket had high hopes for, was farming and cooking here...

This is truly unimaginable!

Mewtwo turned around and handed him a cup of Berry Juice.

"Thank you!" D politely expressed his gratitude.

Mewtwo felt more like a human than a Pokemon to him.

Perhaps it's due to the human genes within its body?

D suddenly remembered he also had something to give Mewtwo.

He took out the pre-prepared Rotom Phone from his pocket and tossed it over.

Mewtwo instinctively caught it, looking down at the peculiar machine with a puzzled expression.

"Rotom Phone, with it, you can contact me anytime. If you need anything, just tell me, and I'll bring it to you."

With that said, D added Mewtwo to the Legendary Beast chat group.

Before handing the phone to Mewtwo, D had already registered a communication account for Mewtwo and added it as a friend.

Meanwhile, Calyrex and Giratina received a notification simultaneously.

[Mewtwo joined the chat group!]

---

---

---

---

# 448: Chapter 445: United as One, Building Our Home Together

Seeing that a new Pokemon had joined the group chat, Giratina immediately came out and asked:

[Is this Pokemon also a god?]

The Phone Rotom in Mewtwo's hand automatically read the message out loud for it.

"A god? No, I'm not a god." Mewtwo thought it was this machine speaking to it, so it shook its head and said, "I'm... a Pokemon."

Watching this scene, D couldn't help but facepalm.

Ever since he'd accidentally let it slip to Giratina last time and blurted out the name of the [Legendary Beast Group Chat], Giratina had started taking itself for a god.

And based on the abilities Calyrex had displayed, that clever fellow had concluded that Pokemon seemed to be a "god" as well.

That made Giratina realize this group chat was only open to Legendary Pokemon like itself.

And now, another Pokemon named [Mewtwo] had joined.

Mewtwo's words were automatically sent through the Rotom Phone.

[Mewtwo: A god? No, I'm not a god, I'm... a Pokemon.]

[Giratina: Then how did you get in?]

Mewtwo: "..."

D stepped in to explain, "I invited him. Giratina, you go do your thing first, I need to show Mewtwo around the World Tree."

Giratina was only curious about Mewtwo; hearing D say that, it didn't press further.

Only then did D get the chance to explain the Rotom Phone to Mewtwo.

"This is a kind of smart machine developed by humans. There's a Pokemon called Rotom living inside it, so humans call it a Rotom Phone."

"With this, even if you're in the Kanto Region and I'm in the Paldea Region, we can still talk to each other. And besides that, it has lots of other functions."

"Later the Rotom inside the phone will give you a detailed introduction."

Mewtwo nodded thoughtfully.

It lowered its head to look at the Rotom Phone in its hand, and then saw a smiling face icon appear on the screen.

Next, the voice of the Pokemon named Rotom sounded from within:

"Hello, Mewtwo, I'm Rotom, it's great to meet you. Please take care of me from now on, roto!"

Mewtwo had dealt with human machines before; it had even built some with its own hands.

So it quickly accepted the existence of the Rotom Phone.

Today's lunch for the Pokemon on the World Tree was still Berries.

The variety was a bit limited, but the Pokemon still ate happily.

D felt a little sorry seeing that, so after lunch he called all the Pokemon on this floor over and led them to the pile of stuff he'd brought up.

A bunch of Pokemon lined up in several rows in front of D, the tall ones standing in back, the small ones in front.

All of them were staring curiously at the pile of items behind D.

"These are things I brought you from the human world," D said with a smile, introducing them.

"There are seeds for crops you can plant, lots of different kinds of Berries meow, pots and pans and bowls for cooking and eating, little quilts and blankets for Sleep, machines for processing crops and power-generating machines, plus some ingredients and food."

"Mii!" (Sushi!)

At those words, the three Tatsugiri sprang up first.

Seeing how excited and eager they looked, the other Pokemon all turned to look at the three of them as well, smiles blooming across their faces.

Because right now, everyone was feeling exactly the same.

Nothing was more worth being happy about than building, with their own hands, the home they'd depend on to live from this day forward.

D looked at the three Tatsugiri and couldn't help but be amused by them.

"Don't worry, I brought it all! But there's a lot to do, so let's take it one step at a time."

D glanced at the power storage device at hand and asked, "Who's willing to help everyone generate electricity?"

"Meow?" Meowth asked in confusion. What did "generate electricity" mean?

"Generating electricity means providing energy for the Explorer Team Guild." D pointed at the sushi shop and the park's streetlights, explaining, "With electricity, these lights can turn on and light up the road home for you at night."

Hearing that, Clefable's eyes lit up. "Pishii?"

So that meant they wouldn't need to be afraid of the dark anymore?

D nodded with a smile. "That's right. But we need Electric Type Pokemon to help."

"Pika Pikachu!" Pikachu dropped to all fours and ran over excitedly, volunteering to help!

"Meee!" The bulbs on the tails of the two Mareep lit up as well; they could use Electric Type Moves too.

The other Pokemon couldn't generate electricity and could only stand aside, watching in embarrassed envy.

"Then I'll be counting on you for this!" D said with a smile. "But you don't have to come every day to generate electricity. Once the machine is fully charged, it can power things for over a month."

"When the World Tree finishes growing and flies up into the sky later, we'll be able to use solar and wind power to generate electricity. But for now, I'll have to trouble you for a while!"

"Pikachu!"

"Meee!"

Pikachu and the Mareep nodded casually—no problem! Their bodies had endless electricity!

Especially Pikachu. As if to prove its point, it crouched on the ground and let out a powerful Growl.

Then, two streams of yellow current shot out from its cheeks.

The intense Spark instantly lit up everything around them.

"That's amazing!" D praised without holding back.

This Pikachu really deserved to be a clone of Pikachu the god! It hadn't even gone through much training, yet the electricity it released was already quite impressive.

---

"Pika~chu~"

Hearing D's praise, Pikachu looked a little shy.

"Then we'll settle it like this for now," D said.

A few Pokemon nodded.

Mewtwo, who had silently watched all of this, suddenly spoke:

"After we leave here, I can modify these machines and build a power plant."

D was slightly stunned. "You can build one?"

Mewtwo gave a soft grunt of acknowledgment; it had done this kind of thing before.

Maybe it was because of the human genes in its body that it possessed wisdom and emotions far beyond ordinary Pokemon, and with its brain developed to the extreme by Psychic power, it could easily learn human knowledge.

The castle on New Island from before was built by it after absorbing the memories and data left behind by those Team Rocket researchers.

So D wasn't too surprised that Mewtwo could do this kind of thing, since he'd already expected it.

Still, thinking about it, Mewtwo really had put a lot of skill points into all sorts of weird stuff…

House building, machine modification, laying concrete floors, it could even hypnotize humans.

In the movie, Mewtwo hypnotized a Miss Joy and had her come to New Island to be a housekeeper.

"Then I'll leave it to you," D said.

He was quite happy inside. Since Mewtwo had brought it up on its own, that meant it had started to see this place as "home."

Pokemon working together with one heart to build a shared home and all that—it sounded pretty touching.

Perfect. Later he could have Mewtwo build a power plant, and when Zapdos retired and came back, it could just settle down right there at the plant.

With the energy problem solved, the next thing was crop planting.

D dug out the seeds and saplings he'd bought from that pile of stuff, getting ready to go over to the farmland area and teach the Pokemon how to plant crops.

But there was too much stuff, and he couldn't carry it all by himself.

Seeing this, the Pokemon nearby all ran over to help.

Charizard picked up a bundle of saplings with its claws, Venusaur unleashed Vine Whip to wrap around the taller fruit trees, and Mudbray and Tropius each carried two sacks of seeds on their backs.

As for smaller Pokemon like Buneary, Clefable, Vaporeon, and Meowth, they each took a small bag of seeds.

Everyone ran toward the farmland area amid laughter, full of expectations for a better life.

Walking at the front, D glanced back and suddenly felt like he was leading a group of kindergarten kids on a spring outing.

Before long, they passed through Slowking's Wise Man's Forest and arrived at that stretch of fertile field.

Mewtwo and the others had already opened up a large area of farmland here.

D sorted the seeds into categories, putting vegetables with vegetables and grains with grains.

Finally, D began explaining to this group of Pokemon how to plant, the harvest time, harvesting methods, and how to save seeds and raise seedlings.

Planting and harvesting were very simple for these Pokemon, but saving seeds and raising seedlings was a bit difficult for them.

D talked for quite a while, but not many Pokemon could understand.

Seeing that, D wasn't anxious. The crops hadn't even been planted yet, let alone matured; he could always teach again after they ripened later.

By then the Pokemon could follow along with him and try it themselves, and it would surely be much easier.

After the introduction, D led everyone to start planting the fields together.

They scattered the seeds into the cultivated fields, then covered them with soft soil.

At first, this group of Pokemon moved very awkwardly.

But soon they slowly mastered the technique of sowing, and their speed and efficiency improved dramatically.

Meanwhile, Mewtwo wasn't idle either.

It flew up into the air, blue light flashing in its eyes as a vast Psychic power radiated from its whole body.

The next second, Mewtwo swooped down from the sky.

The untilled fields below immediately began, at a speed visible to the naked eye, to be forcibly transformed by Psychic into farmland suitable for planting.

The sight left D dumbfounded.

Mewtwo's ability was just way too over the top!

In no time, Mewtwo had finished turning over this entire stretch of field.

It even carefully left ridges between each plot for the Pokemon to walk on, making it convenient for them to check on how the crops were growing.

After finishing this, Mewtwo handed that small section of land over to Venusaur and the others to plant.

As for itself, it used Psychic to carry the remaining seeds and sped across the sky.

Under the precise control of its Telekinesis, the seeds seemed to be attracted as they each fell into the dug holes.

Then, soft soil automatically covered them.

By the time Mewtwo had finished planting all the fields, D and the other Pokemon had also completed the areas they were responsible for.

D looked around and felt he ought to put a little sign in front of each plot, so the Pokemon could tell what crop was planted there.

But he hadn't brought any materials today.

After thinking for a moment, D decided he could make them later and bring them up to the World Tree.

The first batch of crops planted by the Pokemon was very meaningful; letting them personally watch the crops they planted grow and bear fruit would feel completely different from just harvesting directly.

So D didn't have Calyrex come over to instantly ripen these crops.

"What's next?" Mewtwo asked.

"Next is watering," D said, looking toward the surrounding Water Type Pokemon.

---

"Woo!" This time, Milotic stepped forward voluntarily.

Its slender and graceful body gently swayed, a magical force surged above the fields, gathering into a cloud.

Then, drizzle fell from the clouds, nourishing the fields below.

"That's right, just like that." D nodded: "Use Water Type moves to irrigate the fields, the seeds in the soil will absorb the water, helping them grow better. Besides Rain Dance, you can also use Water Gun to water them."

For this, Blastoise, Gyarados, Golduck, and Vaporeon felt they had a strong say.

They all knew how to use Water Gun, so they could come over and help water the fields in the future!

"That's right, just like that." D nodded with a smile.

With that, the matters concerning the fields could be temporarily concluded.

He glanced around, finally locking his gaze on the mill by the river.

D took out a bag of homegrown rice and wheat from his waist, and said:

"Next, I'll take you to the mill over there to see how the harvested rice and wheat are turned into rice and flour."

Through the fields, it took about three minutes for the group to arrive at the water-powered mill.

The mill was built by the river, utilizing the power of the water flow, the windmill above slowly turned.

The mill had two openings, and D took out the rice from the bag, placing it into one of the openings.

"This is where the grains are crushed and turned into rice."

With the flow of water, a series of squeaky sounds emitted from the mill, and after a few minutes, the mill's task was completed.

D walked to the other side, took out the polished rice, and told the Pokemon who followed:

"This is rice; it can be used to make meals or sushi."

"Mi?!" (Are we going to make sushi now?!)

The three Tatsugiri exclaimed excitedly.

D gave a helpless smile: "Don't hurry, there's still one more thing."

He took out the bag of wheat and placed it into the other opening of the mill.

With the milling process, the wheat quickly turned into snow-white fine flour.

"This is the flour I mentioned before." D said: "It can be used to steam buns or make bread. You just need to mix it with eggs and milk, then put it in the oven, and you can bake soft, sweet bread."

"Pikachu..."

Pikachu imagined the scene and felt like it was already drooling just by thinking.

"Okay, the function of the mill has been introduced. Next, let's go back to the guild!" D said.

Upon hearing this, the three Tatsugiri immediately realized D was going to teach them how to make sushi, and quickly hopped onto D.

One jumped onto his head, while the other two ran left and right onto D's shoulders.

"Now let's make sushi with you." D turned to Mewtwo: "Mewtwo, would you like to come along too?"

Mewtwo nodded.

It could sense that this human named D genuinely loved Pokemon.

After some detours, the group finally returned to the Explorer Team Guild.

Since there were no chickens near the World Tree, D could only bring a few eggs from the ranch.

He placed the kitchenware, tableware, and electrical appliances in their respective positions and began demonstrating to the group of Pokemon.

Since the chef at the sushi restaurant was Venusaur, this demonstration was mainly for Venusaur to watch.

This cloned Venusaur had patterns different from regular Pokemon, resembling those who paint camouflage on their faces like savages...

Despite the wild look of this Venusaur, its personality was very attentive and gentle.

"To make sushi, you need several ingredients first." D laid out the required ingredients on the chopping board.

Venusaur and Tatsugiri, among other Pokemon, immediately turned to watch.

"Rice, seaweed, fish fillet, eggs, carrots."

"First, we need to cook the rice using a rice cooker. If there's no rice cooker, we'd have to rely on Fire Type Pokemon to ignite, but I brought a rice cooker this time, so Charizard and others won't need to help."

"Plug in the power, and the rice cooker will operate on its own. Then, we clean the rice thoroughly and put it in, finally adding the appropriate amount of water, cover, and start steaming."

"Meanwhile, we can deal with other ingredients."

Venusaur and Tatsugiri watched attentively.

D started cutting the salmon fillet on the chopping board with a knife.

Looking at the orange-red fish fillet, Tatsugiri was startled and let out a shrill scream: "Mi!"

Then it started trembling in fear.

Isn't that Tatsugiri? How scary! Making sushi means using Tatsugiri as ingredients?

D: "..."

D immediately got what Tatsugiri was thinking.

He explained speechlessly: "No, that's not Tatsugiri, it's salmon, a food Dragonite love to eat."

"Mi?" Tatsugiri was somewhat incredulous.

D nodded to them: "Why would I eat you?"

"Mi..." Tatsugiri thought carefully and realized it was indeed the case.

After seeing it was merely ordinary fish fillet, not Tatsugiri, a sigh of relief settled in its heart.

---

"It's called salmon," D introduced to the several Pokemon in front of him, "This type of fish usually lives in the ocean, but don't worry, you don't need to go too far to catch them."

"You just need to take the World Tree elevator to the next floor, the Dragon's Holy Sanctuary, and you can find these fish in the sea there."

"While you're there, you can also find another ingredient for making sushi, seaweed. Seaweed also grows in the sea, and once dried, it looks like this."

Saying this, D showed the thin piece of seaweed to the Pokemon in front of him.

"Both these ingredients are sourced from the sea, so if you ever run out, you'll have to rely on yourselves to find them in the ocean."

D looked at Vaporeon and Golduck beside him.

They worked at the sushi shop and were also Water Type Pokemon.

But thinking they might not have much experience in finding fish,

after a brief consideration, D added, "If you can't find them yourselves, you could ask Dragonite living there to help you. You just need to give them some sushi or other food in return."

Three Tatsugiri excitedly put their hands on their hips, looking at Vaporeon and Golduck, and said to them:

"Mi!" (The task of finding ingredients is up to you!)

D helplessly said, "You can't give all the work to Vaporeon and Golduck, they'll be too busy and exhausted."

"Mi..."

Tatsugiri fell into a dilemma; who should they send to gather supplies?

They thought about it carefully and suddenly came up with a good idea.

"Mi!" (I've got an idea!)

They could just find their former subordinate!

"Hmm?" D was quite surprised, "What idea?"

Tatsugiri: "Mi!" (Go find Dondozo!)

D immediately understood.

Dondozo would indeed be suitable for the job of supply gatherer.

When the time comes, Tatsugiri could accompany them to the ocean to get supplies.

But to find Dondozo, they would need to go to Casseroya Lake.

However, D found it difficult to leave at the moment.

At this moment, Mewtwo suddenly spoke up, "Let me take them!"

"Hmm?" D was slightly taken aback, he looked at Mewtwo, then nodded, "Alright, I'll leave it to you then."

Mewtwo nodded slightly, then used Teleport to take the three Tatsugiri away from the World Tree.

They were heading toward Casseroya Lake to find Tatsugiri's subordinate, Dondozo.

Meanwhile, D stayed behind to continue teaching Venusaur how to make sushi.

Venusaur was a very smart Pokemon. After watching D's sushi-making process a few times, it fully learned the method.

Seeing Venusaur indicate that it had learned, D stepped aside and asked, "Want to give it a try?"

Venusaur then extended two Vine Whips, scooped a spoonful of rice from the pot with a spoon, made a rice ball, placed a slice of cut salmon on top, and finally wrapped it with seaweed.

And with that, the first piece of salmon sushi was completed,

"Very good, it seems you have fully mastered it," D nodded in satisfaction.

Venusaur was very happy, "Dana~"

"Next, I'll teach you the second type of sushi, rolled sushi," D added.

This method primarily involves using seaweed to roll up the rice balls, then cutting them into small pieces for easy consumption by guests.

This sushi-making method is also very simple, and Venusaur quickly mastered it.

"There are some more ingredients here, do you want to make some sushi for everyone to try?" D asked.

"Dana!" Venusaur nodded happily.

Then, Golduck and Vaporeon entered their work mode.

They skillfully invited guests to sit down and then served the sushi made by Venusaur.

A group of Pokemon happily enjoyed themselves immediately.

Venusaur did not forget to set aside a portion for Mewtwo.

Seeing the Pokemon getting along so well, a smile naturally appeared on D's face.

He quietly turned and left, starting to place the remaining furniture and tableware in their respective places.

Pokemon Tavern, Pokemon Restaurant, Explorer Team Guild...

"The Item Shop doesn't have any items yet, hmm, maybe I can teach the Pokemon to recognize Berries and have them bring the Berries to the Item Shop for sale later."

Berries have various effects; some can restore health, some can heal paralysis, and others can cure freezing, akin to status healing potions in role-playing games.

According to D's plan, Pawmot's parents were supposed to come to the World Tree to run the Item Shop.

But right now, they had a very important task to accomplish.

The World Tree didn't have eggs, so to get eggs, they would need to raise hens.

The two Pawmot had already gained considerable experience in raising chickens at the ranch, making them the perfect choice to come to the World Tree to serve as Chicken House managers.

And there's also the Exploration Team Guild Headquarters.

The Heroic Expedition Team trio were still out traveling, and D planned to wait for them to return to discuss the guild's operations with them in detail.

Unknowingly, the items D had brought in from outside had all been placed throughout the World Tree.

Today's tasks were finally completed; next, it was time for him to return to the ranch!

At this time, Mewtwo and Tatsugiri also returned from Casseroya Lake to the World Tree.

Along with them came three somewhat clumsy-looking Dondozo.

---

There's no doubt, those three Dondozo are surely Tatsugiri's little brothers.

However, the Water Type Dondozo aren't very good at moving on land, so after Tatsugiri briefly explained the situation around the World Tree to them, they were sent to the sea within the Dragon's Holy Sanctuary.

This way, if one day the sushi restaurant runs out of ingredients, the clever Tatsugiri can just head to the sea and stock up together with its little brothers.

Unnoticed by them, time had already come to evening.

D bid farewell to the group of Pokemon.

"I have to head back first. I might come over again in a few days. If there's anything you don't understand, you can go ask..."

Before D could finish, he heard Tatsugiri's response.

"Mii!" (I know, I know, if there's anything I don't understand, I'll ask Slowking!)

D: "..."

Well, that works too!

He then looked at Mewtwo: "If there's any problem you can't resolve, use the Rotom Phone to contact me. I'll come over to help then."

"Alright." Mewtwo nodded.

D left, and before he did, he looked at Mewtwo standing there, suddenly feeling that Mewtwo now seemed like the chief security officer of the World Tree.

Hmm...

This title indeed suits Mewtwo.

With Mewtwo around, there's no need to worry about bad guys reaching the World Tree.

Once the World Tree takes to the skies, such a massive tree will certainly attract some human attention.

After all, with satellites hanging in space, a tree the size of the World Tree will surely be noticed immediately.

By then, there might be large numbers of humans forming expedition teams to explore.

This is also a problem.

Moreover, he can't always keep the World Tree in the Reversed World, otherwise the Pokemon living there won't see the sun or moon for years.

That would be too depressing.

Even with the presence of a Sunflower, such a small Sunflower cannot possibly illuminate every corner of the World Tree.

D diligently thought of a solution.

So here comes the question, could he ask the Dragonite to create a storm cloud around the World Tree to deter outsiders from approaching?

Or have Mewtwo use its Psychic powers to warp space and artificially create a Secret Realm, preventing others from locating the World Tree from outside?

Thinking of this, D, who had returned to the ranch, sat on the sofa, took out his Rotom Phone, and sent a message to Mewtwo.

Meanwhile, Mewtwo at the World Tree was exploring the functions of the Rotom Phone.

It's not unfamiliar with human writing. During its time building the high-tech castle on New Island, it had already learned human writing and could even craft programs.

At this moment, Mewtwo opened a webpage and entered some text into the search box.

[Pokemon, why do they exist?]

Though it already knew the answer, Mewtwo was still curious about how humans in this world viewed the question.

Suddenly, a message popped up from the Poke Communications in the background.

It was sent by D, who called himself "King Wing".

[King Wing: Hey Mewtwo, can you use your Psychic powers to warp space? The World Tree will return to the real world soon, and such a big commotion is bound to attract human attention, then draw a large group of explorers.

So I want to ask if you could warp the space around the World Tree, creating a Secret Realm so that no one outside can see the World Tree inside.]

Warping space, creating a Secret Realm...

It hadn't tried before, but D's words gave it a new idea.

Mewtwo thought of the moves it mastered.

If it could combine Trick Room, Psybeam, and Light Screen, maybe it could achieve this.

First, use Psychic to create a Trick Room, then erect Light Screen, and finally use Psybeam to create an illusion, erasing the World Tree within the space.

In this way, ordinary humans wouldn't be able to detect the World Tree from outside.

After a moment of calm contemplation, Mewtwo replied.

[Mewtwo: I can give it a try.]

Upon receiving Mewtwo's answer, D finally felt relieved.

With this, he wouldn't have to worry about the World Tree becoming a hot tourist spot, flooded by a vast number of visitors.

Even if some humans accidentally stumbled into it, like Trainers similar to Ash, it would just be a fortuitous encounter gifted to them!

If that person had malicious intent...

The completed form of Calyrex riding Spectrier and Glastrier, Giratina lurking in the shadows, Mewtwo with its high intelligence capable of reading and erasing memories.

Once he grows old, he'll send Gogoat to the World Tree to guard its entrance, just like the Guardian Pillars of the World Tree in the Kanto Region.

Along with Koraidon, Pawmot, Dragonite, and the Dragonite Legion led by the gigantic Dragonite.

To get through the World Tree, they'd first have to defeat the three Gogoat at the entrance, then face attacks from Giratina and Koraidon.

As they continue upwards, Mewtwo and Calyrex would be waiting there.

With these Pokemon teaming up, there should be no one in this world who could conquer the World Tree.

Oh right, there's also Rayquaza!

D thought of the shiny Rayquaza invited by his Dragonite to the ranch as a guest.

He needed to have a chat with Dragonite and Rayquaza later to see if he could invite it to reside on the World Tree as well, giving the World Tree another layer of protection.

---

Everything is ready except for the east wind.

Now all that's left is for the World Tree to fully grow!

*

April 20th, 200th year of the League calendar.

It's been nearly half a month since Mewtwo's arrival.

During this time, D traveled to the World Tree several times, bringing the Pokemon there some other daily necessities, and also delivered a few books and a Rotom Phone to the Great Sage Slowking.

Because D noticed that whenever the Pokemon in the World Tree had a problem, they would go to the big rock where Slowking lived to consult with him.

For example, how to get along with friends, how to grow seedlings, how to transport flour better, and so on.

D was worried that Slowking might also encounter something he didn't know, so he bought a few books on agriculture, animal husbandry, and an encyclopedia in Porto Marinada Town, and gave them to Slowking.

Just in case, he also gave Slowking a Rotom Phone so it could use the internet whenever it encountered something it didn't understand.

It's worth mentioning that D built a brand new Chicken House on the World Tree.

Pawmot's parents also moved to the World Tree, becoming the chicken coop managers there, responsible for taking care of the chickens.

With them, the Pokemon on the World Tree now have an endless supply of eggs to eat.

However, more than chickens, D actually wanted to invite a Pokemon like Chansey to live on the World Tree.

Because Chansey's eggs are extremely nutritious, and they lay larger and more eggs than chickens, sometimes even several eggs a day.

However, such Pokemon are very rare, especially in modern times.

It's said that a long time ago, there were many Chansey living in this world, but people began to capture them in large numbers to obtain their nutritious eggs.

After a period of overhunting aimed at taking the eggs from Chansey's abdomen, the number of Chansey in modern times has become very scarce.

It's basically very hard to see in the wild.

Aside from that, D also purchased three Miltanks and two Alcremies from the ranch store in town and sent them to the World Tree.

Now, the Explorer Team Guild basically won't run out of milk, eggs, cream, or berries to eat.

On usual days, Arboliva also shares her highly nutritious olive oil with everyone.

Some Pokemon interested in cooking have started following Slowking's advice and using an oven to bake cakes and desserts.

Although there was a fire caused by an oven explosion due to mistakes in the middle, fortunately, a Water Type Pokemon was present at the time and quickly put out the flames.

Upon hearing this, D sent them a new oven.

And half a month ago, the crops that D and Mewtwo planted were also almost ready for harvest.

Perhaps it's because the Natural Energy on the World Tree is too abundant, but the crops planted there grow very fast and are of high quality.

D has already received photos from Mewtwo and plans to go up in a few days to help with the harvest.

It's safe to say that the World Tree side is gradually getting on the right track.

After being busy building the World Tree for more than half a month, D finally got a few days to rest.

With the arrival of summer, the temperature is starting to heat up.

It's only cool for a while in the morning and afternoon.

D only goes out during these two periods to sell vegetables or to repair the wooden fences at the ranch that have been damaged by wild Pokemon.

For the rest of the time, he basically stays at home cuddling Pokemon and playing on his phone.

Lately, D has been following the news from the Hoenn Region through the internet.

Because if the timing is right, the Hoenn incident will erupt this year.

To awaken the Super Ancient Pokemon Groudon and Kyogre, Team Magma and Team Aqua have started their plans.

They want to use the power of these two Super Ancient Pokemon; one wants to expand the land area, and the other wants to cover the whole world in seawater.

And this year, Groudon and Kyogre will be awakened by those two extremist organizations, leading to a cataclysmic battle in the Hoenn Region.

Since the Pokemon World entered the internet age, all regions seem to be connected.

D quickly found information related to the Hoenn incident.

An internet user named [King of the Sea] posted a thread arrogantly taunting the Hoenn League, saying their Team Aqua's great plan would soon be completed.

D took a look at the posting date and saw that it was from the day before yesterday afternoon.

Tsk, this member of Team Aqua seems to be quite bold!

Out of curiosity, D clicked into the comments and saw the official announcement from the Hoenn League.

[Hoenn League: The member has been successfully arrested by Hoenn police this morning. After investigation, he was found to be a member of the evil organization Team Aqua. Team Aqua is currently plotting an evil plan in the Hoenn Region, so all citizens please stay vigilant. If you spot any suspicious individuals, please contact the local police department immediately!]

D: "..."

What a reckless underling, don't they know that popping the champagne early is never a good idea?

Their boss Archie from Team Aqua might just get infuriated by his underlings.

Such a foolish person would surely become the laughingstock among their peers if word got out.

However, Team Magma can actually pop their champagne.

Because the Hoenn League's attention is entirely drawn by Team Aqua, their action plan might proceed much more smoothly.

Groudon and Kyogre...

D couldn't help but fall into deep thought.

Just then, a notification sound came from the Rotom Phone.

It was a message from a friend.

Huh? Who could be messaging him now?

D unlocked the screen and saw a little red dot appear next to Geeta's avatar.

...It's Geeta.

[Geeta: By the way, D, summer is almost here. To thank you for hosting the Paldea League members these past few days, we've prepared a vacation trip for you, complete with private jet and all expenses covered, including hotel and meals.

The destination is Levincia City, which is a great tourist spot this season. I hear summer matches perfectly with the beach!]

D: "..."

Why does Geeta seem a bit off, so attentive?

But he still needs to go to the World Tree to help with the harvest, and he doesn't have time to leave the ranch.

After a brief moment of thought, D replied.

[Wing King: Thank you very much, but the ranch has been quite busy lately, and I can't get away, so I'll pass.]

---

---

---

---

# 449: Chapter 446: Paradise

Paldea League Headquarters.

Geeta looked at the reply on her phone screen, and her feelings were a bit complicated for a moment.

Not far away, Rika was leaning against the wall with her arms crossed. Seeing Geeta like this, she stayed silent for a while: "…He didn't accept it?"

"Sigh." Geeta let out a breath and laid out the plan she'd originally had: "I was going to have Lono give him a proper tour of Levincia City, then follow it up with a seven-day beach vacation over there!"

She had even bought the tickets already, but D said he wasn't going.

Rika thought about it carefully, then said: "All the farmers are busy harvesting crops right now. Maybe D just doesn't have the time!"

Geeta was scrolling on her phone, just about to say something, when she suddenly saw a trending topic online.

The trending topic said that Galar's strongest Champion Leon had left the Galar Region and was currently on a cruise ship headed for the Paldea Region.

The post was by a Paldean who had recently gone traveling in the Galar Region to see its local specialty: Dynamax battles.

Now that the trip was over, that person was planning to take a cruise back to Paldea, and ended up running into Leon right on the ship, even taking a photo together.

In the photo, Leon was grinning wide, showing his white teeth, his smile looking incredibly hearty.

Leon? What's he coming to the Paldea Region for again? Is he looking for D?

Geeta suddenly remembered how Leon had come by last time asking them about D.

An alarm bell went off in her heart on the spot.

Could Leon be planning to invite D to join the Galar League?

Absolutely not!

Geeta's expression suddenly turned serious.

She solemnly laid out her suspicions to Rika.

After listening, Rika opened her mouth, completely speechless: "Chairwoman, that's a bit much…"

"Rika, help me pack up, I'm going with you to visit D!" Geeta made up her mind.

Just right, she'd been planning to head to the ranch anyway to see what D was busy with, and at the same time sound him out a bit, see what he was thinking.

So he wouldn't end up like her, putting major life events like marriage behind work.

Rika: "…"

She sighed and said: "Alright, I got it."

*

Meanwhile, over at the ranch, D had also received the news that Leon was coming by the ranch to visit him.

Leon said he was currently on his way to the Paldea Region and was expected to arrive in about two days.

Two days… D thought it over carefully and realized that would be just about when he finished everything he needed to do on the World Tree.

He organized his wording in his head, then replied.

[Wing King: Alright, then once you get ashore remember to take a Squawkabilly Flying Taxi straight here, don't go getting lost again.]

[Leon: Haha, sure, don't worry, I'll definitely arrive on time this time!]

On the other end of the screen, Leon replied, a bit embarrassed.

Ever since learning his lesson from getting lost last time, he had simply given up on traveling by Charizard and chose a cruise ship, a form of transportation with a fixed route.

Leon figured that way he probably wouldn't get lost, right?

But there was nothing he could do about it—he'd had a terrible sense of direction since he was a kid.

Which was actually kind of embarrassing to talk about.

When he first started traveling, he would often get lost, to the point that even his Charmander was forced to learn how to read maps to guide him.

Later, that Charmander evolved into a Charizard and became the most reliable partner at his side.

*

The next day, D brought the Pokemon from the ranch and Calyrex along to the World Tree to speed up the World Tree's Growth.

With the blessing of Calyrex's tremendous power, a thick green radiance burst forth from the World Tree.

A moment later, the light faded away, and the World Tree, having absorbed a huge amount of energy, returned to its original appearance.

Although its exterior hadn't changed much, D knew that now there were only the last two months left before this World Tree fully matured!

D looked up at the World Tree in front of him, and in the next second, the World Tree's information automatically surfaced in his mind.

[World Tree (Mature Stage): A seedling tree that has absorbed a vast amount of Power of Grass. Because of Gogoat, an aberration occurred during its Growth. Boundless Life Force is brewing within, eventually breaking through the earth. It is said to be able to support an entire world and use Natural Energy to nourish all living things. Time remaining until fully grown: 19,850 years.]

"Next up, once in May, and when June comes, we can officially launch the World Tree Ascension Plan!" D was filled with excitement.

It wasn't just him—Calyrex standing to the side was also feeling something hard to put into words right now.

It had never imagined that in Its lifetime, It would actually be able to cultivate such a great existence!

"It truly makes Us look forward to it." Calyrex murmured softly.

Just then, a flash of white light appeared around them, and a moment later, Mewtwo's figure appeared on the Sky Island at the base of the World Tree.

It looked at D and the group of Pokemon with suspicion and uncertainty, then turned its gaze to the World Tree in front of it.

From the look on its face, it was obvious that it had also just sensed the change in the World Tree.

"Don't worry, that was Calyrex helping the World Tree with its Growth." D cut straight to the point: "That's Calyrex's power. It can accelerate the growth of flowers, grass, and trees, making them mature in an instant. It's just that the World Tree needs too much energy, so we have to do it bit by bit."

"I see." Mewtwo nodded calmly and turned its gaze toward that big-headed Pokemon over there.

It remembered that the other party was in that group chat as well.

These days, Mewtwo had been quietly lurking in the group chat, watching the other two Pokemon talk.

---

After a period of observation, it discovered that Giratina had a rather brutish and aggressive personality, enjoying trading insults with humans on the internet.

Sometimes, when Giratina couldn't out-argue humans, it would actually travel through the internet cables to scare those humans.

At first, Mewtwo thought Giratina was full of malice towards humans.

But later on, it realized that's just how Giratina was.

Compared to the always swearing Giratina, the Pokemon named Calyrex was much more peaceful.

He would occasionally speak up to soothe Giratina's irritable mood, calming it down.

Besides that, Mewtwo also noticed that these two Pokemon were very popular on the human internet, having many human fans.

Giratina once tried to persuade it to register an account as well, but it refused.

Because Mewtwo did not want to reveal its current situation, nor did it want to be disturbed by humans, and it wasn't accustomed to that kind of life.

Thinking of this, Mewtwo glanced at Calyrex and nodded slightly.

Calyrex returned a gentle smile.

Then, he turned around and said to D: "In that case, I shall not disturb you any longer. I'm going back to play with my phone."

D: "..."

"Alright, off you go!" After sending Calyrex off, D turned his gaze to Mewtwo and said, "The crops in the fields should be ripe by now, right?"

Mewtwo nodded.

"Great, then let's go over there together and help you harvest the crops!" D looked back at the Pokemon beside him and said, "Koraidon, Cyclizar, Pawmot, and you Alcremie and Garganacl, shall we go help out?"

"Ah gah hiss!"

"Ga-ow～"

"Bamo～"

"..."

The group of Pokemon happily agreed.

D led them into the World Tree, following Mewtwo.

Mewtwo flew ahead, its long tail gently swaying, seemingly in a very good mood.

D watched for a while until they reached the area where the World Tree elevator was located, then withdrew his gaze, leading the Pokemon onto a green bubble and ascending to the top level of the World Tree.

The Explorer Team Guild was situated there.

After a few days, the guild had become lively again, with Pokemon visible everywhere.

In the sushi shop, Venusaur, Vaporeon, Golduck, and the three Tatsugiri managers were working hard.

On a bench in the Slowpoke Park, Skitty and Ninetales were leisurely enjoying the breeze.

In the café, Buneary and Patrat were making coffee.

In the tavern, Pikachu and Meowth were working as waiters.

And in front of the Explorer Team Guild, a Rhyhorn was sleeping soundly.

D even saw a few Dragonite that came to play here.

The two Dragonite were sitting in the sushi shop, mouths wide open, tasting the salmon sushi made by chef Venusaur.

Wow, these Pokemon really look relaxed and at ease!

D was full of wonder, and the Pokemon beside him were also intrigued by this place.

At this moment, Alcremie, through the display window of the cake shop, spotted two other Alcremie and let out a gasp of surprise.

"Mama!"

Those two Alcremie were brought here by D a few days ago from the town.

One was a cream vanilla flavored Alcremie and the other a lemon flavored Alcremie.

Upon hearing Alcremie's call, they immediately lifted their heads, looking over through the display window.

D noticed this scene and said to Alcremie, "Do you want to go and play with them?"

"Mama!" Alcremie nodded enthusiastically; it loved making friends!

"Then go ahead!" D smiled. The World Tree was very safe, and he wasn't worried about anything harming the Pokemon.

So, Alcremie happily ran off.

D then turned to the other Pokemon beside him and said:

"This is the Pokemon Paradise I've created for you. You can move freely here and do whatever you like."

Upon hearing these words, Gholdengo and Ogerpon couldn't help but look longingly.

Like children seeing a new toy, they excitedly ran towards the sushi shop.

Pawmo also made its way to the Chicken House to visit its parents.

The Eevee at D's home scattered, enjoying themselves around the Explorer Team Guild.

Only five large Garganacl stood there dumbly, appearing somewhat at a loss.

At this time, Alcremie waved them over, prompting the five Garganacl to happily run after it.

Suddenly, D was left with only Koraidon and Cyclizar.

Mewtwo stood silently by, watching the scene.

"Let's go check out the fields!" D suggested.

Hearing this, Mewtwo nodded.

Soon the group arrived at an area where the fields had been transformed from a plain.

By this time, various crops had grown in those fields.

Green watermelons, golden pumpkins, dark green cucumbers, rosy apples, golden wheat...

A breeze stirred, rippling through the wheat fields, the air filled with the joy of harvest.

"Remember how I told you to harvest the crops before?" D asked.

Mewtwo nodded.

The next second, a powerful psychic force enveloped the wheat field.
