Immediately, grains of wheat rose from the field, gathering into a dark cloud and floating into the air before being deposited into the mill.

---

Charizard and Nidoking were already there with wooden barrels.

As the mill turned, flour ground from wheat fell into the barrels, quickly filling them to the brim.

Seeing this, Nidoking picked up the full flour barrel and moved it aside, then carried over a second empty barrel.

After several such rounds, a dozen barrels filled with flour were neatly arranged beside the mill.

D: "..."

Pokémon with Psychic type are really convenient, aren't they?

But D didn't come today to watch Mewtwo perform magic, because Mewtwo can't take care of them forever. Eventually, those Pokémon need to learn how to harvest by themselves and pass it on.

So, he called over the other Pokémon watching nearby, along with Koraidon and Cyclizar, to demonstrate how to harvest crops.

The Pokémon watched carefully, and one particularly clever Sandslash soon learned the skill.

Using its sharp claws, it began following D to harvest crops.

D glanced over and nodded, "That's right, just like that, keep it up!"

Seeing this, the other Pokémon joyfully joined in.

Mewtwo levitated nearby, puzzled as it watched everyone in the fields.

D stopped, standing next to it, as if he had noticed Mewtwo's confusion, and explained:

"They need to learn to harvest on their own, because you can't take care of them forever. If one day you suddenly want to leave the World Tree and travel somewhere else, these Pokémon can live here relying on their own strength."

Farming, watering, harvesting...

Different Pokémon working together, hand in hand, to build a farm that truly belongs to them.

Hearing this, Mewtwo looked thoughtfully at the fields.

D didn't say much more, just smiled and said, "Let them try a lunch made from ingredients they grew themselves this afternoon!"

"Alright." Mewtwo nodded.

From then on, Mewtwo didn't interfere much. It simply stood and watched D lead those Pokémon in their diligent labor.

Until the group of Pokémon collapsed tiredly on the ground, did it finally step in to harvest the remaining crops, using Teleport to move them to the granary.

D watched and thought: manpower is still lacking!

If there were a few more Pokémon like Haxorus and Scyther, these fields wouldn't need Mewtwo to intervene.

"Oh well, let's wait for Mew and Teddiursa to slowly invite them!" D thought.

As noon approached, they returned with their full harvest to the Explorer Team Guild.

D personally cooked, using the fruits and vegetables they harvested today, to prepare a sumptuous lunch for the Pokémon.

The meal took place at the restaurant next to the Explorer Team Guild.

A large group of Pokémon crowded the tables in the restaurant, with each table covered with exquisite dishes and desserts.

Some Pokémon tasted the food and immediately showed expressions of shock.

So delicious!

Seeing its expression, the other Pokémon began to feast heartily.

D stood aside smiling with Koraidon, asking: "How does it feel to eat food made from crops you grew yourself?"

Meowth looked utterly content, as if drunk: "Meow~" (Such a blissful feeling meow~)

Pikachu's eyes also lit up: "Pika Pika!" (This is the life I love!)

"Da na!"

"Awoo!"

"Ooh~"

The surrounding Pokémon echoed, and for a moment, the entire restaurant was filled with the taste of happiness.

D looked at the scene with a smile.

"From now on, live life well in this place!"

*

After lunch, D began to explore the life of Pokémon on the World Tree.

He found that these Pokémon did their jobs flawlessly.

After filling their bellies, the Pokémon gathered together gradually dispersed and went to do the things they enjoyed.

As owner of the sushi bar, Tatsugiri returned to the sushi restaurant with its three employees to continue running it.

But everyone had just eaten, and weren't hungry, so no customers went in.

At that moment, chef Venusaur reported to Tatsugiri that ingredients ran out.

Upon hearing this news, Tatsugiri immediately set off to the next level in the Land of Dragons, where it found its buddy Dondozo, and together they went to the sea to search for salmon and seaweed growing on the seabed.

D didn't follow, as he wanted to see other Pokémon living in this place.

Hence, he went to the coffee shop beside the sushi bar.

When D arrived, Buneary was at the bar brewing coffee adeptly.

It stirred the spoon in the cup, mixing the coffee grounds evenly.

Seeing D come, Patrat immediately brought over three cups of coffee prepared by Buneary.

One for D, one for Koraidon, one for Cyclizar.

D took a sip and found the coffee was not as bitter as he expected, as if Buneary had improved its flavor.

"Great skill!" D gave Buneary a thumbs up.

He noticed that Koraidon and Cyclizar, who didn't normally drink coffee, were drinking a lot, showing this improved coffee suits the Pokémon's taste.

---

"Mimi mi!" Buneary let out a joyful growl.

At this moment, the three Treecko guarding the Berry Garden arrived at the café.

Buneary immediately got busy.

D didn't stay to disturb them; after finishing his coffee, he left with Koraidon and Cyclizar.

Next up was the Item Shop.

The current owner of the Item Shop was a Vileplume. Various kinds of Berries were displayed on the shop counter, including those that restore stamina and those that cure various status conditions, offering a wide variety.

D took a walk around the store, and the Vileplume happily followed him to offer service.

"Yes, just like that, serve your customers with enthusiasm, and you'll attract more patrons!" D said to Vileplume.

Vileplume nodded vigorously, got it!

Next to the Item Shop was the Pokemon Hospital, but there was currently no one inside because no Pokemon at the World Tree could provide treatment yet.

Beside the hospital was a dormitory for Pokemon to sleep.

At this moment, two Mareep and a Ninetales were lazily napping in their cozy nests.

D didn't disturb them, got up, and left the place, heading to the Pokemon Tavern.

Pikachu and Meowth were busy there.

Gholdengo was also there, clearly enjoying the atmosphere of the place.

Pikachu and Meowth, using the methods taught by Slowking, were extracting Berry Juice from the Berries.

Once a glass was filled with Berry Juice, Meowth would bring it over to Gholdengo.

Seeing D, Gholdengo happily greeted him.

D shook his head with a smile, "You have fun here, I'm going to check out other places!"

Next was the turn of the Exploration Team Guild Headquarters.

However, the place hadn't officially started its operations yet, and it was empty, with just a dozing Rhyhorn at the entrance.

D didn't go in but walked over to the Slowpoke Fountain, sat on a bench, and contentedly looked at everything around him.

Skitty was enjoying the breeze on the bench. Seeing D sit down, it affectionately jumped onto his lap and dozed off with a gentle purr.

Looking just like an ordinary "cat."

D chuckled and gently stroked Skitty's pink fur.

With that, the issues at the Exploration Team Guild had all been resolved!

Even if D didn't come by, the Pokemon here could rely on their own efforts to live.

D glanced around and asked the two Pokemon beside him, "What do you think of this place?"

Koraidon and Cyclizar looked around.

Cyclizar's face lit up with joy, "Gyao!" (It's a paradise for Pokemon!)

D raised his eyebrows, repeating Cyclizar's words, "A paradise... quite a fitting name. Let's call this place [Paradise] from now on!"

Just as Koraidon is the Paradise Guardian Dragon, although its title's Paradise refers to the Area Zero Research Institute established by Professor Sada and Professor Turo.

But D felt the World Tree was more fitting for the name Paradise than that place.

"What about you, Koraidon?" D looked at Koraidon beside him.

"Agaa!"

Koraidon nodded its big head, showing its agreement with Cyclizar's description.

"Then you must protect this place well in the future, don't let any bad guys come and ruin it!" D grinned.

"Gyao!" Cyclizar nodded seriously.

However, Koraidon sensed something different in D's words.

It looked at D with confusion but couldn't figure it out.

*

On April 24th, year 200 of the League calendar, after several days of sailing, Leon finally arrived in the Paldea Region.

This time, Leon was smart about it.

To prevent getting lost, he stuck with the main group the entire way.

The moment he got off the ship, he immediately looked for the local Squawkabilly Flying Taxi station, where he took a flying taxi to D's place.

At the ranch, D sat in the air conditioning, holding a cup of Life Sheep Milk, inquiring about Leon's current itinerary.

Last night, he discovered a fun game on his phone and ended up staying up late, leading to him feeling groggy when waking up in the morning.

At any other time, D might have gone back to bed for a nap and woken up naturally.

But today was a day for visitors; he had to entertain them.

After drinking all the Life Sheep Milk in the cup, D instantly felt all his exhaustion and weariness vanish.

Just then, the call to Leon was connected, and the sound of rushing wind could be heard from the other end, sounding like it was high in the sky.

"Hey, have you arrived in the Paldea Region?" D asked.

Leon's voice came from the other side: "Yes! I'm about to arrive in Porto Marinada Town!"

Hearing the wind whistling by, D couldn't help but ask, "Did you ride Charizard over here yourself?"

If so, he might end up flying in the opposite direction to Levincia City, right?

Leon immediately understood D's implication and reassured him, "Don't worry! This time I came by flying taxi!"

"That's good. What time do you expect to arrive?" D breathed a sigh of relief and asked.

"Around 10:30 in the morning."

"10:30?" D reminded, "Alright, don't wander around town. I'll drive into town to buy groceries later and pick you up from there."

---

Leon: "..."

He wanted to say that he wasn't a child anymore, but thinking he might get lost, he swallowed those words.

Finally, he replied, "Okay."

At 10:20 in the morning, D finished buying groceries and headed to the Squawkabilly Flying Taxi station in Porto Marinada Town.

After waiting for about ten minutes, a Squawkabilly Flying Taxi descended from the sky onto the platform.

The door opened, and Leon emerged from inside.

D spotted him right away, rolled down the window, and patted the passenger seat, gesturing, "Get in!"

Leon didn't mind that D's little pickup was old and basic; he just opened the door and got in.

"Long time no see!" Leon beamed with a cheerful smile.

"Indeed, it's been a while." D started the car, made a turn, and drove out of Porto Marinada Town.

It had been a year since D last met Leon. Back then, it was because of Leon that Alder came to the ranch and managed to save his Volcarona's life.

"Isn't work busy in the Galar region?" D asked.

He often saw news about Leon online and noted he had taken on many commercial endorsements, with his cloak covered in various brand labels for each battle appearance.

"Busy!" Leon admitted helplessly. "If I weren't busy, I'd have been here challenging you long ago."

D: "..."

"Especially recently." Leon chuckled, looking at D, teasing, "That whole Calyrex situation caused quite an explosion in the Galar region!"

Initially, D didn't think much of it since Calyrex wasn't a resource of any particular region; it was a being with independent thought.

But after Leon's mention, he suddenly felt a bit embarrassed.

"Was it that impactful?" he asked.

"How to put it?" Leon pondered briefly before saying, "It greatly affected the Galar Royal Family; their actions faced public criticism, disgrace, and their influence in the Galar region diminished significantly, though it didn't impact the Galar League much."

After all, the public was quite rational, as the Galar League wasn't responsible for what happened.

The Galar League had always been devoted to developing and building the Galar region, much better than the Galar Royal Family who did nothing but steal credit.

"I see..." D nodded, sinking into thought.

From Leon's reaction, it seemed the Galar League didn't regard Calyrex with much importance.

Compared to Calyrex, Chairman Rose clearly prioritized Dynamax battles more now.

To completely solve the Galar region's energy problem, he secretly established an underground energy plant, but didn't foresee that the leaked energy would cause Pokémon everywhere to start Dynamaxing uncontrollably, eventually releasing the rampant Eternatus.

If Eternatus were entirely under Chairman Rose's control, what would the Galar region look like?

D didn't know, but he sensed that the current Galar region had quite a "commerce-over-agriculture" vibe.

Not that it suppressed agriculture; it wasn't that extreme yet, just gave a sense of being neglected.

"But to think that such events happened in the Galar region in the past is unimaginable," Leon marveled.

The Legendary Pokemon saved the Galar region from the Darkest Day, which for modern-born humans like them, seemed unbelievable.

Not to mention that the Pokémon was once the ruler of the Galar region.

"It's not just the Galar region; many regions have their own ancient legends, though Galar's are a bit complex and were misconstrued by later generations," D said.

"Let's not talk about that!" Leon shook his head, spiritedly saying, "I'm here today to battle you; last time I challenged the Grass Trial, and this time I want to challenge the Storm Trial and Thunder Trial!"

"There's more than just those three trials at the ranch now!" D laughed.

Instantly intrigued, Leon exclaimed, "Hmm?!"

"It's a hidden trial at the ranch, only available to Trainers who have passed any preliminary trial. Since you completed the Grass Trial last time, you can now take on this trial," D explained.

"What is it?" Leon eagerly asked.

"I call it the Ancient Trial," D introduced. "Although, unlike the previous trials, in this one, you can choose between a single or double battle."

"For singles, you only need to defeat one Pokémon, Koraidon, to pass, but for doubles, you need to defeat both Pokémon."

D was curious to see how Koraidon's strength compared to Leon, the strongest Champion.

Ancient Trial, Koraidon...

Leon immediately envisioned the robust, crimson Pokémon.

His eyes sparkled with anticipation: "Then I'll take on this trial!"

"No problem, I'm also looking forward to our battle," D smiled.

During the chat, the pickup truck arrived at the ranch.

From afar, D saw Geeta at the entrance of the ranch.

Hmm? Geeta?

Why is she here? Isn't the Paldea League busy?

D was a bit surprised, and as they got closer, he stopped the car.

Geeta also noticed them and cheerfully said, "Long time no see, D. I'm here for a visit; hope I'm not interrupting?"

D: "...Not at all."

Is this a leader's inspection?

---

If he remembered correctly, in the game, Geeta would regularly inspect those Gym Leaders to assess their strength.

Although he was not a Gym Leader, now he could probably be considered a semi-official Elite, right?

"Come on in and chat!" D drove the car into the ranch.

Leon jumped out of the car.

Geeta raised an eyebrow, a hint of surprise showing on her face.

"Lord Leon? I didn't expect to see you here."

Leon looked at Geeta and shook hands with her, grinning: "Yeah, long time no see, Chief. I'm here to visit D and have a sparring match with him!"

"I see!" Geeta realized, clasping her hands together in excitement, "It seems I also have a chance to witness a battle between two powerful Champion-level Trainers!"

"It's just a simple sparring match, not an all-out battle!" Leon said with a grin.

Leon loved to smile, and when speaking with others, he always wore a bright, friendly smile, making him seem very approachable.

Geeta shook her head: "Such matches are rare! Besides the World Championship and the Eight Great Masters, it's hard to see two Champions battle elsewhere."

Speaking of the World Championship and the Eight Great Masters, Leon remembered something.

He asked: "By the way, Geeta, aren't you participating this year?"

Geeta chuckled and shook her head: "There are still a lot of things waiting for me at the Paldea League, I might not have the time."

Leon was quite disappointed: "I was hoping to battle you on that stage!"

Geeta shifted the topic to D.

"D, are you not considering participating in the World Championship and the Eight Great Masters?"

"Me?" D was a bit surprised, shaking his head he said: "I think I'll pass. I'm not a teenager anymore, those stages are for the young to challenge!"

Geeta was in her mid-twenties, around twenty-six or twenty-seven, with Leon being the only one under twenty, still very young.

For a moment, D and Geeta both looked at Leon, remarking, "Ah, the youth!"

Leon: "...You're not much older than me anyway!"

"Enough talk, I'll go make dinner, you two go inside and have some tea!" D invited.

The three entered the living room, where the Indeedee sibling offered them tea.

Geeta and Leon noticed that many new Pokemon were at D Ranch, and as Trainers, their eyes were naturally drawn to them.

Leon even took out his Rotom Phone to scan Ogerpon and Gholdengo.

"Unable to find relevant information; these are not yet recorded in the Pokédex!"

"Gholdengo, the Coin Entity Pokémon, is said to be composed of 1000 coins, and is friendly to everyone, instantly forming good relations!"

Geeta explained: "That's a unique Pokémon from the Paldea Region! It evolved from a Pokémon named Gimmighoul, and currently, there's only this one in the entire Paldea Region."

"So rare?!" Leon was slightly surprised.

"Yes!" Geeta nodded: "D put in a lot of effort to make this Pokémon evolve!"

"What about that Pokémon?" Leon looked at Ogerpon.

However, Geeta shook her head: "That one I'm unsure of, it's a Pokémon D brought back from outside."

Hearing them discussing it, Ogerpon bounced around, showing its starry eyes.

"Bonnie!"

D raised his head from the kitchen to help with the introduction: "That's Ogerpon, a Pokémon from the Johto Region."

"Ogerpon..." Leon repeated the Pokémon's name, "Looks so lively!"

"Bonnie~"

Leon greeted it with a smile: "Hello, Ogerpon!"

Ogerpon happily responded: "Bonnie~"

Then it ran off with Gholdengo.

Leon then pointed the phone at Koraidon, which was sleeping on the floor.

However, the Pokédex still lacked information on Koraidon.

This left Leon very surprised, how could this be?

Geeta had a clearer understanding of this matter.

The research at the Area Zero Research Institute was initially funded by the Paldea League.

As a result, they were well aware of the research and experiments conducted by the two Professors.

And this Pokémon was summoned from ancient times by Professor Sada through the Time Machine.

Its name, Koraidon, was also given by Professor Sada.

But so far, the research on Paradox Pokémon by the Area Zero Research Institute hasn't entered the public eye.

Thus, neither Koraidon nor any other ancient or future Pokémon has been recorded in the Pokédex.

D, after cooking a few simple dishes, left the rest of the meal preparation to the Indeedee sibling.

He returned to the living room to chat with the two guests.

However, seeing him, Geeta suddenly brought up the topic of traveling to Levincia City.

"That expense-paid trip opportunity is very precious, if you don't go, someone else will grab the spot!"

D thought for a moment, then said: "Let's leave it for someone else!"

Geeta: "..."

Leon was quite surprised: "Travel? D, are you planning a trip?"

"I don't have that plan for now." D shook his head.

"Alright then." Leon offered: "If you have time in the future, you can visit the Galar Region, I can take you to bathe in hot springs in Circhester, listen to metal music in Spikemuth Town, and visit the Battle Tower in Wyndon City!"

D smiled and nodded: "Sure!"

Speaking of which, he has never been to hot springs before!

It's a pity as there seem to be none in the Paldea Region; otherwise, he would definitely take Koraidon and the others to experience it.

Leon nodded seriously: "Yes, I'll definitely entertain you well when the time comes."

Geeta: "!!!"

Geeta was internally shocked.

Sure enough, she guessed it right; Leon was here to invite D to settle in Galar!

She quickly changed the topic; to hide her own purposes, she leisurely sipped some tea first.

After the two ended their conversation, she casually asked:

"Speaking of which, D, aren't you overwhelmed managing the ranch alone? Have you considered getting married, running the ranch with your wife, and taking care of these Pokémon?"

D: "It's alright, the Pokémon on the ranch are well-behaved, and the Indeedee sibling helps out too, so it's manageable."

"As for marriage, Chief, it seems you're not married either?"

Geeta: "..."

She pretended to calmly take a sip of tea: "There are still many things at the Paldea League that need my attention; work is a priority, so I'm not planning to marry for now."

But D's situation was different.

If D decided to get married now, and have a child in a couple of years, she could help raise them personally.

Once the child grows up and becomes an adult, it would coincide with her retirement, just in time to take over her role and lead Paldea to greatness.

The more Geeta thought about it, the more it seemed like a perfect plan.

D couldn't help but tease: "Workaholic!"

---

---

---

---

# 450: Chapter 447: Leon Challenges the Ancient Trial, Rumors of the Darkest Day

After lunch, Leon couldn't wait to have a Pokemon battle with D.

"Alright," D nodded, leading him to the battlefield in the pasture.

Some Pokemon attracted by the battle also gathered around, cheering for Koraidon.

Geeta also stood aside, hands behind her back, watching the two on the field with anticipation.

"Do you want a double or a single battle?" D asked with a smile.

Koraidon walked onto the battlefield, ready for action, while Flutter Mane quietly blended in with the crowd of Pokemon to watch the upcoming battle.

"Let's have a single battle this time!" Leon's eyes were filled with excitement, "We'll try double battles next time!"

Since Koraidon was D's strongest Pokemon, he would use his strongest Pokemon to battle as well, to make it thrilling!

"No problem," D smiled faintly.

Leon took out a Poke Ball from his waist, and with a flash of white light, a Charizard, impeccably trained and full of power, appeared on the battlefield.

The flames on its tail burned intensely, indicating that this Charizard was at peak condition.

D observed the state of the Charizard and said, "Without Dynamax, the battle might not be as exciting!"

Because Leon was at his strongest when he could use Dynamax.

"No worries!" Leon seemed indifferent, "Dynamax is something you can experience when you come to the Galar Region!"

"I'll give you a Dynamax Band so Koraidon can undergo Dynamax, and when that happens, I'll let Charizard Dynamax to battle you!"

Now that he's the Galar League Champion, he could gift Dynamax Bands to excellent Trainers he favored.

Dynamax Koraidon...

D was instantly intrigued because after Sword and Shield, the Dynamax system wasn't retained, and this led to the Paldea Region Pokemon being unable to Dynamax.

But that's just in the game; in reality, all Pokemon in the world could Dynamax, including Koraidon.

The prerequisite was the need for a Dynamax Band and being near an Energy Point.

"Alright," D was excited to see what a super-sized Koraidon would look like.

Now, it's time to commence the battle!

The brother Indeedee, with a whistle hanging around its neck, stepped to the edge of the battlefield.

It glanced at the two battle-ready Pokemon and then blew the whistle hard.

"Whew!"

The battle officially began.

Crimson energy slowly flowed from the scales of Koraidon.

In the next moment, the weather changed, the floating white clouds dispersed, revealing a clear blue sky.

A golden sun hung high in the sky, and the sunlight intensified!

Leon noticed this scene and grew interested.

"Is it the Pokemon's ability? Amazing, it can actually alter the weather."

D slightly smiled and instructed, "Koraidon, use Ancient Power to probe the opponent!"

"Agh-hiss!"

Koraidon stomped the ground fiercely, and a resounding roar spread from beneath its feet, primitive energy quickly flowed, and several stones coalesced beside Koraidon, flying towards Charizard.

However, seeing this, Leon didn't retreat but advanced, his desire to attack was very strong.

"Charizard, Iron Tail!"

"Roar!"

Charizard roared, wings opened, and its tail was shrouded in a layer of silver light, resembling metal.

Facing the flying stones, Charizard rushed forward, its tail, as hard as steel, swept from left to right, instantly shattering those stones.

"Let's go!" Leon grinned, "Charizard, use Dragon Claw!"

Based on his judgment, the Koraidon seemed to be a Dragon+Fire Type Pokemon.

In this case, Koraidon would resist Fire Type moves but be vulnerable to Dragon Type moves.

Of course, even if he's wrong, it doesn't matter; this is just a simple probe.

With the command given, Charizard accelerated rapidly towards Koraidon.

Its right claw raised high, purple energy swirling quickly, forming a giant Dragon Claw, striking towards Koraidon.

Koraidon stood still, its wild face calmly watching Charizard.

"Let's do this too, let's see whose power is stronger between the two of you."

D's voice came from behind, and Koraidon's aura suddenly began to surge, it swung Dragon Claw, instantly forming a huge Dragon Claw, meeting Charizard head-on.

In the clash of swords and shadows, the two Pokemon collided.

The massive Dragon Claws clashed, releasing a searing energy, subsequently triggering a strong shockwave.

But despite the impact's power, Koraidon stood firmly and unmoving.

Its golden pupils met Charizard's.

The wild primal aura instantly swept over, and for a moment, Charizard felt like it was locked onto by a top predator in the ecosystem.

"Agh-hiss..."

Koraidon let out a low growl.

The force in its hand suddenly increased, and then directly sent Charizard flying.

The massive Dragon Claw left several scars on Charizard, its body recoiled, stumbled back, barely stabilizing.

---

"What a powerful force!" Geeta's eyes sparkled as she watched the battle.

The power of that Koraidon clearly surpassed that of Charizard!

Seeing this, Leon grinned: "Indeed strong! But it's not over yet, right? Charizard, use Heat Wave!"

Leon decisively abandoned competing in strength with Koraidon and opted for a long-range attack.

Charizard began flapping its dragon wings, unleashing a scorching wave of heat.

Under the brilliant sunlight, the temperature in the air rose even higher.

D instantly understood Leon's strategy.

He shook his head and smiled: "It's not that easy. Koraidon, it's our turn, use Collision Course!"

The Heat Wave surged forward, but Koraidon ignored it, leaping high with its golden eyes locked on Charizard.

Then, an ancient aura emanated from Koraidon as it violently transformed and charged towards Charizard.

With such speed, Charizard couldn't evade at all, and was hit by Koraidon almost instantly.

"Boom!"

The ancient aura triggered a massive explosion upon impact, instantly pinning Charizard beneath Koraidon.

The next moment, D's voice rang out: "Now, use Ancient Power!"

Primal energy rose from the earth, turning into massive stones that hovered around Charizard and Koraidon.

Danger!

Leon instantly felt alarms ringing in his mind, as Koraidon's speed and strength far exceeded his Charizard. His brain raced, trying to find a way to respond.

But Charizard was pinned underneath Koraidon, unable to move.

"I have no choice but to go all out!" Leon gave his final command: "Charizard, use Inferno!"

Intense flames erupted instantly, enveloping both Charizard and Koraidon.

Simultaneously, the stones conjured by Ancient Power came crashing down.

The two contrasting moves clashed fiercely, causing a violent explosion that engulfed both Pokemon's figures.

The intense flames ignited the grass at the edge of the battlefield, prompting Vaporeon to quickly use Water Gun to extinguish the fire.

The firelight was reflected in their eyes.

Geeta appeared amazed.

Leon seemed a bit tense.

D, however, remained calm because he knew Koraidon would not lose.

Sure enough, as the explosion's aftershocks faded on the battlefield,

Koraidon slowly emerged from the flames.

Its scales were charred black by the fire, but its expression remained unchanged.

While Charizard had already collapsed on the battlefield, its eyes swirling, clearly having lost its ability to fight.

Brother Indeedee stepped forward to check on Charizard, then blew the whistle signaling the end of the battle.

Geeta applauded: "What a spectacular battle!"

This was her first time witnessing the battle involving those Paradox Pokemon.

"Whew..." Leon sighed deeply, watching his sister Indeedee feed Charizard some Life Sheep Milk, and remarked: "As expected of a hidden trial! Looks like I still have a ways to go in my training."

He decided that upon returning, he would train a bit more on the Isle of Armor under Master Mustard.

D also fed Koraidon a bottle of Life Sheep Milk, and hearing Leon's words, he smiled and said: "You've already done very well."

Hearing this, Leon became interested: "Has anyone else challenged Koraidon besides me?"

D nodded: "A Champion-level Trainer from the Paldea League, but he lost in just one encounter."

Upon hearing Paldea's Champion, Leon couldn't help but glance at Geeta.

Geeta: "..."

D explained: "Not the top Champion, just another Trainer."

"Oh oh!" Leon then recalled that the Paldea League was somewhat different from other regions, having many Champion-level Trainers.

But their abilities varied greatly—some Champions were very strong, while others were quite weak, and only the strongest among them could become the top Champion of the Paldea League.

In the meantime, Leon's Charizard had already recovered its combat capabilities, and Koraidon's injuries had fully healed as well.

With the battle over, D patted Koraidon on the head and said, "Go have some fun!"

"Arghh~"

The group of Pokemon dispersed in all directions.

The weather outside was a bit hot, so D invited the two to rest for a while in the living room.

At this moment, Geeta suddenly received a phone call.

"Hello? This is Geeta. Alright, I understand, I'll return right away."

Upon hearing this, D and Leon looked back at her.

Geeta's face was filled with apology as she explained awkwardly: "I'm very sorry, but it looks like I have to leave earlier. There's an issue at the Paldea League that requires my attention."

By now, D was used to Geeta being busy all the time.

After all, the Paldea League was full of workaholics from top to bottom.

He nodded and said goodbye to Geeta.

Geeta smiled warmly, nodding: "Yes, see you next time."

Then, she turned to Leon: "Lord Leon, if you encounter anything in Paldea, you can contact the Paldea League. I hope you have fun here."

Leon waved his hand: "This visit is purely personal to meet D, no need for formality!"

Geeta smiled gently: "In that case, gentlemen, I hope we meet again next time!"

After speaking, Geeta turned and left the ranch.

"Geeta is truly dedicated!" Leon remarked.

D nodded in agreement, for it was indeed true.

---

The Paldea Region, unlike other regions, does not have a League Chairman, so Geeta, the Chief Champion, in addition to her duties as the regional Champion, must also take on the role of Chairman.

Unlike in the Galar Region where Chairman Rose and his secretary Oleana handle various affairs, Champion Leon can relax a bit and focus solely on Pokemon battles.

"Alright." D turned around, "Why don't you rest in the living room for a bit? If you're not in a rush, you can rest overnight at the ranch and return to Galar tomorrow."

"Hehe, that's exactly what I was thinking, so I'll be imposing on you!" Leon sheepishly scratched the back of his head.

The two entered the room and sat down on the sofa.

His sister, Indeedee, brought over two cups of tea and a plate of snacks.

Leon took a sip and then said a bit awkwardly, "Um, D..."

"Hmm?" D looked up at him.

Leon continued, "Actually, the reason I came this time is because I want to ask for your help with something."

This statement took D by surprise.

He put down his tea and asked, "What is it?"

Leon straightened his face and said seriously, "To be precise, I want to learn more from Calyrex about past events."

"Calyrex?" D was slightly taken aback, but after a brief moment of contemplation, he quickly guessed Leon's intent.

Sure enough, Leon continued, "Whether it's the legends recorded in the history of the Galar Region or the truth revealed by Professor Oak, there's no clear record of the disaster known as the 'Darkest Day' being completely annihilated."

"So someday, it will descend upon the Galar Region again, and by then, the region will once again be in danger as it was in the past."

The ancient texts from Galar document that disaster from three thousand years ago.

At that time, a black vortex appeared in the sky, covering the entire Galar Region. The sky turned dark, rendering the scene reminiscent of an apocalyptic scenario.

Not only that, the texts also document that the Darkest Day triggered many wild Pokemon to undergo uncontrollable Dynamax or Gigantamax transformations.

Because of the havoc caused by these giant Pokemon, the Galar Region was on the brink of destruction.

"I am very worried that that disaster might happen again." Worry filled Leon's eyes.

The Galar Region currently thrives on Dynamax battles, but where does the energy supporting Pokemon's Dynamax transformations come from?

Moreover, Chairman Rose and the Macro Cosmos group have established energy stations in major cities.

If the Dynamax energy there were to leak, what kind of consequences would it trigger?

"I understand." D nodded gently.

Leon is also a Champion worthy of respect.

In the anime, he was severely injured trying to stop Eternatus.

If not for Zacian and Zamazenta arriving in time, Leon would probably have lost his life there.

This act is quite reminiscent of Steven's willingness to sacrifice himself to save the Hoenn Region in the special episodes.

Thinking of this, D took out his Rotom Phone and sent a message to Calyrex.

In no time, the figure of Calyrex floated in from outside the door.

Meeting this legendary King of Galar for the second time, Leon suddenly had an indescribable feeling inside.

Recalling the feats it accomplished for the Galar Region in the past, Leon solemnly thanked Calyrex.

"Greetings, Calyrex, I am Leon, the current Champion of the Galar Region."

Upon hearing the name "Galar Region," Calyrex displayed a surprised expression.

With a gentle gaze fixed on this young human, it slowly floated over to D's side, elegantly sitting on the sofa.

Next, Leon heard the gentle voice of Calyrex resonate in his mind.

"What brings you here?"

Leon straightened his expression and asked, "Regarding the Darkest Day from three thousand years ago, what more can you tell us? From what I gathered from ancient texts, those two heroes did not completely defeat the Darkest Day, but merely sealed it. I am very worried that it will descend upon the Galar Region again in the future."

Leon slowly voiced his inner concerns.

After listening, Calyrex showed an enlightened expression, "So that's what brings you here!"

Looking at Leon before it, and at the mention of the Darkest Day, its tone changed, transforming back into the once majestic, noble, and elegant King of Galar.

"Since you have this in your heart, I shall recount to you everything from the past!"

The next moment, a vast spiritual power emanated from Calyrex.

The energy enveloped the entire room in an instant.

Soon after, the surroundings underwent an overwhelming transformation.

The room, sofa, and walls disappeared without a trace.

What came into view was an endless vast plain, nowhere in sight stood a human town, only scattered Pokemon could be seen.

"This is the world twenty thousand years ago." Calyrex began slowly, "At that time, the Darkest Day descended from the skies."

"Twenty thousand years ago?" Leon was astonished.

D, however, wasn't surprised because he knew Calyrex had the ability to see the past and the future.

Calyrex nodded slightly.

As soon as it finished speaking, the entire sky turned red, and an incomparably massive meteorite streaked across the sky, crashing down to earth with a blazing trail of flames.

---

In an instant, the earth trembled, and everything on this prairie was utterly destroyed, as wildfires began to blaze, and countless Pokemon started to flee in all directions.

Leon's gaze was irresistibly shaken by this scene.

And next, he witnessed a sight he would find hard to forget for the rest of his life.

From within the gigantic meteor that could destroy this land, a hideous, dragon-like mysterious creature slowly crawled out!

The creature's body was made of a strange material emanating a red glow, with a deep purple substance resembling an exoskeleton or armor attached to it, resembling a dragon's skeleton in appearance.

Its head was triangular, a semi-transparent red membrane-like structure, adorned with five long spines extending backward, a pair of eye-like spots formed by three white circles at its front, and a slender, toothed jaw made of deep purple material.

Its body and tail were thin and long, covered with spine-shaped, spiked deep purple material, resembling a vertebral column.

Its limbs and claws were made of deep purple material, thin and sharp, with each claw having three pairs of tips.

In the midsection of its body, encased by its skeletal frame, there was an area resembling a ribcage.

A pink, heart-like glowing core was pulsating slowly within it!

"What on earth is that creature?" Leon's voice was shocked; its size far surpassed even the largest Pokemon in the world, Wailord.

"That is the [Darkest Day]." Calyrex's voice slowly resonated.

"Eternatus." D silently added the name of that Pokemon in his heart.

Leon looked surprised, incredulous, "The Darkest Day? Is that creature the Darkest Day itself?"

Calyrex nodded gently.

Then, the scenes before them continued to unfold.

The Pokemon that emerged from the meteor, for reasons unknown, was in poor condition, extremely weak.

Just after breaking free from the meteor, it collapsed due to exhaustion, its massive body falling back into the meteor and slipping into a slumber, with only the pink core in its chest pulsing at an extremely slow pace.

Eternatus had fallen asleep.

But the matter wasn't over.

Calyrex's thoughts stirred slightly, and time began to accelerate automatically.

Spring came and went, the earth was covered in frost, and then the snow melted, and everything began to revive.

After countless cycles of the seasons, that creature awoke from its slumber.

But its condition still seemed poor.

Apparently in an attempt to sustain its life, it began to massively absorb the land's energy from the Galar Region.

As the creature's condition gradually improved, abnormalities began appearing throughout the Galar Region.

First, the sky.

Affected by the terrifying energy unleashed by that creature, the space above Galar was twisted, creating large, swirling black whirlpools.

Then, a red light enveloped the nearby Pokemon, causing them all to enter Dynamax and Gigantamax states, losing their sanity and becoming nearly frenzied.

These giant Pokemon began to wreak havoc throughout the Galar Region, leaving devastation in their wake, with forests set ablaze and human towns reduced to ruins.

The royalty ruling over the Galar Region cowered in the palace, summoning heroes from all around to prevent the disaster.

But the heroes' strength was far from enough, as at that time, the entire Galar Region was almost destroyed.

The scene cut off abruptly here.

In an instant, everyone returned to the living room.

Leon was in a daze, seemingly yet to recover his composure.

D's mind recalled the subsequent scene.

With the King's Power, Zacian and Zamazenta went to challenge Eternatus, shattering it in battle.

Eternatus' body fragments cascaded down in the form of Wishing Stars, Wishing Pieces, and Wishing Chips.

Its core, gravely injured, also descended into slumber.

Zacian and Zamazenta saved the Galar Region from disaster, and that event became a legend passed down within Galar.

Until later, when Chairman Rose and his secretary Oleana began collecting Wishing Stars, attempting to awaken Eternatus...

"This is the truth of the Darkest Day."

Calyrex gazed solemnly at the descendants of Galar before him.

"Your guess is correct, in the near future, the Darkest Day will awaken, and by that time, it will once again shroud the entire Galar Region."

Upon hearing these words, Leon's expression became grim.

He firmly and seriously said, "I will definitely prevent this from happening."

Calyrex sensed the determination in this descendant from Galar.

He appreciatively looked towards Leon, nodding, "Since that is the case, at that time you may go to the Slumbering Weald, seeking the aid of those two heroes, which is the final help I can provide to the Galar Region."

The Slumbering Weald? Two heroes?

Leon blinked in slight surprise.

His gaze met D's.

D nodded, "Indeed, they were the two kings, Zacian the King of Swords and Zamazenta the King of the Shield who, three thousand years ago, repelled the Darkest Day, saving Galar."

"Of course, you can also rely on me when the time comes, I have a Pokemon here that can provide some assistance in the battle against the Darkest Day."

"Come to think of it, it could probably be considered a junior to Zacian and Zamazenta!"

Such a case was too dangerous, and Leon instinctively did not wish to involve others, as it was the responsibility of the Champion to protect the Galar Region.

---

But with D willing to lend a Helping Hand, Leon was moved and earnestly said, "Thank you."

After saying these words, Calyrex flickered and went to the Glass Greenhouse to play on the phone.

Leon originally planned to stay another day at the D Ranch, but the truth he learned from Calyrex made it impossible to linger.

Because the Galar Region is constantly facing a tremendous crisis.

Leon also decided to leave: "Thank you very much for you and Calyrex's help. I won't stay at the ranch today, but when Galar's crisis is resolved, I'll come back to visit properly."

D was slightly taken aback but immediately understood Leon's thoughts.

"No need to be so nervous, there's always a way to solve it," D comforted.

"Hmm."

Leon grinned again, returning to his cheerful demeanor.

"I'm off!"

He stood up and waved to D.

D escorted him to the ranch, and to prevent Leon from getting lost, he directly called for his family's Dragonite delivery rider to help take him to the port.

The Rotom Phone can help Dragonite with navigation and location, so there's no worry about Dragonite getting lost.

Dragonite gladly agreed, landed on the ground, signaling the human to climb aboard; it just needed to deliver this human to the destination like a package, which wasn't any different from usual.

Knowing beforehand about Dragonite delivery at D's side, Leon wasn't overly surprised.

He thanked D once more, climbed onto Dragonite's back, waved goodbye to D, and then left.

With Geeta and Leon gone, the ranch returned to its usual calm.

D went back home and started tidying up the house, which had been messed up by Pokemon.

There's nothing at the World Tree needing his attention for now, allowing him to focus entirely on the ranch.

League Year 200, April 25th.

Early in the morning, D went to the Apple Garden, trying to find a way to help the Applin evolve.

It's well known that Applin has multiple evolution methods.

They evolve into different forms depending on the flavor of apples consumed.

If Applin eats a sour apple, it evolves into Flapple, capable of spitting strong acidic liquid.

Applin that consume sweet apples evolve into Appletun, exuding a sweet fragrance.

The apples grown at the ranch are very sweet, but seem not sweet enough for Applin's evolution.

Looking at Applin disguised as an apple on the apple tree, D fell into deep thought.

"Have you tasted all the apples here?" he asked.

Applin shook its head quickly, indicating it hadn't, but felt that these apples weren't sweet enough for evolution.

"I see..." D murmured, having no choice but to purchase apples specifically for Applin's evolution online.

However, D had one more thing to do before that.

He took out the Rotom Phone, opened the Pokedex, and searched [Applin].

Soon after, information about Applin was displayed, with Rotom's electronic voice automatically introducing:

"Applin, the Apple Core Pokemon, spends its entire life inside apples. When faced with bird-type Pokemon predators, it disguises as an apple to protect itself, possessing four evolution forms, Rotom!"

Upon hearing "possessing four evolution forms," D was stunned.

What's going on? Doesn't Applin have only two evolution forms?

He quickly opened the Pokedex and found two completely new forms aside from Flapple and Appletun.

These were [Dipplin] evolved through honey apples.

And [Hydrapple], evolved from Dipplin.

D clicked on Hydrapple, listening as Rotom introduced:

"Hydrapple, the Apple Snake Pokemon, with seven snake bugs living inside honey-made apples. The central snake bug commands, and occasionally, when they synchronize their thoughts, they release true power, Rotom!"

"Dipplin and Hydrapple?" D was a bit surprised, "Could these two new forms be those announced in the Indigo DLC?"

When he traveled through, the Indigo DLC hadn't been released.

It seems now, whether it's Ogerpon or Dipplin and Hydrapple, all are content from the DLC.

What surprised D even further was the new move named [Dragon Cheer].

He searched online for this move and found out that this new Dragon Type move was developed by a teacher at Blueberry Academy.

Pokemon can't learn it naturally; it requires Trainers to guide and assist in development.

Blueberry Academy...

D felt this name sounded familiar.

He recalled carefully, realizing he heard it from Geeta.

Back then, Geeta said the Paldea League and Unova League collaborated to establish a new academy for Pokemon battles over the sea.

Additionally, Blueberry Academy and Paldea Academy conduct exchange activities each semester, sending students to mutually visit and learn.

"Interesting," thought D.

After a brief understanding of Applin's new evolution forms, he focused his attention on the Applin before him.

---

Opening the Pokedex, he displayed the three evolutionary forms to Applin.

"Which form would you like to become?" D asked.

Applin's round eyes rolled around as it listened to what Rotom had just said, quickly eliminating Dipplin and Hydrapple from consideration.

It didn't want other Applin vying for apple habitats with it, and if it evolved into a Hydrapple, seven bugs would have to live in one apple—just thinking about it felt cramped.

So it was left with Flapple and Appletun.

For Flapple, it needed to eat a Tart Apple, but Applin disliked the sour taste and preferred the sweet taste of Sweet Apples.

Thus, it made its decision quickly.

"Do you want to evolve into Appletun?" D noticed where Applin's gaze lingered.

"Applin!" Applin let out a growl, confirming.

"In that case, I'll go buy you a Sweet Apple," D said.

He opened his online shopping app and searched for the evolution item 'Sweet Apple' for Applin.

Soon, the app displayed a list of Sweet Apples, ranging from 1000 to 2000 League Coins.

Compared to other evolution items, these magical apples were quite cheap.

D found a store, added it to the cart, chose the fastest Pelipper Delivery, and placed the order.

Compared to Dragonite Delivery, Pelipper deliveries from other stores were considerably slower.

After waiting about two days on the farm, a Pelipper with a satchel finally arrived leisurely.

It landed outside the farmhouse window, knocking on the glass with its large beak.

D opened the window, and the Pelipper settled on the windowsill, pulling a rosy apple from its bag with its beak and placing it in D's hand.

Then, Pelipper handed D a delivery note.

D glanced at it, signed his name to confirm receipt, and the Pelipper turned away, flying off leisurely.

"So laid-back!"

D couldn't help but remark, then turned around to the Apple Garden to give the Sweet Apple to Applin.

Applin took a bite of the sweet apple.

The next moment, a burst of white light emerged from its body.

Within the glow, Applin's form quickly began to change.

From a tiny apple, it gradually transformed into a brand-new Pokemon standing about half a meter tall.

Appletun was born!

Appletun's appearance resembled a turtle, with its high, arched back covered in lattice-like confections, retaining the eyelids from its Applin days.

It looked chubby and incredibly cute.

Especially as Appletun was enveloped in a layer of sweet nectar, smelling wonderfully fragrant.

D looked on, satisfied with the scene.

An Appletun with the [Ripen] ability, a perfect match.

"Hello, Appletun!" he greeted the Pokemon before him.

"Awoo!" Appletun lifted its head slightly clumsily to respond, its nature appearing as docile as a cow.

"Next, I'll take you to a new Apple Garden, but don't worry; it's as safe as the farm," D said with a smile.

"Awoo~" Appletun made a gentle growl, eagerly looking forward to the place.

Thus, D brought Appletun to the World Tree.

That Apple Garden had been planted with Mewtwo and others a few days ago, and blessed with Calyrex's power, it was already producing large apples.

As soon as Appletun arrived, it was captivated by the orchard teeming with fruit.

It gazed at the red apples hanging from the branches, its eyes sparkling with excitement.

The apples were big and round, and just looking at their exterior, one could imagine how sweet and juicy the flesh was.

The mere thought made one's mouth water.

"Alright, from now on, you'll live here! Use your Ripen ability to help nurture more apples here," D said.

"Awoo~"

On the way here, Appletun listened to D introducing the place.

It knew many Pokemons lived here, all great friends.

Appletun loved the place and decided to stay, using its abilities to cultivate more delicious apples for everyone.

Thus, another endearing 'old farmer' was added to the World Tree's residents.

When D returned to the farm, it was already noon.

"Indee?" His younger sibling Indeedee came forward, asking D what to have for lunch.

"Let's eat whatever!" D replied casually.

The Eevees, Ogerpon, Gholdengo, Butterfree, Farfetch'd, Flutter Mane, Alcremie, and Garganacl had all gone to play at the World Tree.

These Pokemon weren't coming back for lunch, and D wasn't worried about them going hungry since the World Tree had plenty for them to eat,

leaving only Koraidon, Cyclizar, Pawmot, and Dragonite at home, along with Combee, Gogoat, Miltank, and Wooloo.

Aside from Koraidon, Cyclizar, and Dragonite, D had prepared the food for the other Pokemon ahead of time; he just needed to feed them during mealtimes.

Such as Grassy Seeds for Gogoat, fresh pasture and Pokeblock for Miltank, honey for Combee, and carrots for Glastrier and Spectrier.

As for himself, he'd eat something simple; it was summer, hot, and he didn't have much appetite.

But before that, D still went to cook a few dishes for Koraidon and the others.

He grabbed a sandwich and sat on the sofa, browsing Hoenn Region-related news.

Since the recent incident of a Team Aqua member provoking and getting captured by the Hoenn League,

Red Leader and Archie might have restrained their men, as there were no signs of Team Magma or Team Aqua members online.

Thinking about the consequences of Groudon and Kyogre's battle, D switched to his forum account and posted a thread online.

[Predictions on Team Magma and Team Aqua's objectives and actions, potential crisis in Hoenn Region.]

---

---
